na nyūnasya hetur bhavati | tadyathā duḥkhe dharmajñānakṣāntis tasyā evānāgatāyāḥ sabhāgahetur viśiṣṭasya ca yāvad anutpādajñānasya | anutpādajñānam anutpādajñānasyaivānyaviśiṣṭasyābhāvāt | darśanabhāvanāśaikṣyamārgās tridvyekeṣām | tatrāpi mṛdvindriyamārgo mṛdutīkṣṇendriyamārgasya hetuḥ | tīkṣṇendriyamārgas tīkṣṇendriyamārgasyaiva | tadyathā śraddhānusāriśraddhādhimuktasamayavimuktamārgāḥ ṣaṇṇāṃ caturṇāṃ dvayoś ca | dharmānusāridṛṣṭiprāptāsamayavimuktamārgāḥ trayāṇāṃ dvayor ekasya | kathaṃ punar ūrdhvabhūmikasyādhobhūmiko mārgaḥ samo vā bhavati viśiṣṭo vā | indriyato hetūpacayataś ca | tatra darśanādimārgāṇāṃ mṛdumṛdvādīnāṃ cottarottarahetūpacitatarāḥ | yady apy ekasantāne śraddhādharmānusārimārgayor asaṃbhavaḥ utpannas tv anāgatasya hetuḥ | 
[AKBh.II.53.a] 
	kiṃ punaḥ mārga eva samaviśiṣṭayoḥ sabhāgahetur bhavati | nety āha | laukikā api hi 
		prayogajās tayor eva (53a) 
samaviśiṣṭayoḥ sabhāgahetur bhavanti, na nyūnasya | yathā katame ity āha 
		śrutacintāmayādikāḥ | (53b) 
ete hi prāyotikāḥ śrutacintābhāvanāmayā guṇāḥ samaviśiṣṭayor eva hetur na nyūnānām | tadyathā kāmāvacarāḥ śrutamayāḥ śrutacintāmayānāṃ cintāmayāś cintāmayānām | bhāvanāmayābhāvāt | rūpāvacarāḥ śrutamayāḥ śrutabhāvanāmayānāṃ cintāmayāabhāvāt | bhāvanāmayā bhavanāmayānām eva | ārūpyāvacarā bhāvanāmayā bhavanāmayānām eva | teṣām api navaprakārabhedāt mṛdumṛdavaḥ sarveṣāṃ [p.88] mṛdumadhyā aṣṭānām ity eṣā nītiḥ | upapattipratilambhikās tu kuśalā dharmāḥ sarve navaprakārāḥ parasparasabhāgahetuḥ | kliṣṭā apy evam |ānivṛtgāvyākṛtās tu caturvidhāḥ | vipākajā airyāpathikāḥ śailyasthāniāḥ nirmāṇacittasahajāś ca | te yathākramaṃ catustridvyekeṣāṃ sabhāgahetuḥ nirmāṇacittam api kāmāvacaracaturdhyānaphalam | tatra nottaradhyānaphalam adharadhyānaphalasya | na hy ābhisaṃskārikasya sabhāgahetor hīyamānaṃ phalaṃ bhavati | tadyathā śālir yavāadīnāṃ mā bhūn niṣphalaḥ prayatna iti | ata eva cāhuḥ syād utpanno 'nāsravo 'nutpannasyāsravasya na hetuḥ syāt tadyathā duḥkhe dharmajñānam utpannam anutpannānāṃ duḥkhe dharmajñānakṣāntīnām | sarvaṃ ca viśiṣṭaṃ nyūnasya na hetuḥ | syād ekasantāniyataḥ pūrvapratilabdho 'jāsravo dharmaḥ paścād utpannasya na hetuḥ | syād anāgatāḥ duḥkhe dharmajñānakṣāntayo duḥkhe dharmajñānasya yāsmān na pūrvataraṃ phalam asty anāgato vā sabhāgahetuḥ | syāt pūrvotpanno 'nāsravo dharmaḥ paścād utpannasyānāsravasya na hetuḥ | syād adhimātro nyūnasya | tadyathottaraphalaparihīṇasyāvaraphalasaṃmukhībhāve duḥkhe dharmajñānaprāptiś cottarottara kṣaṇasahotpannānāṃ duḥkhe dharmajñānakṣāntiprāptīnāṃ nyūnatvād iti | uktaḥ sabhāgahetuḥ | 
		saṃprayuktakahetus tu cittacaittāḥ (53cd) 
evaṃ sati bhinnakālasantānajānām apy anyonyasaṃprayuktakahetutvaprasaṅgaḥ |ekākārālambanās tarhi | evam api sa eva prasaṅgaḥ | ekakālās tarhi | evaṃ tarhi sati, bhinnasantānajānām api prasaṅgo navacandrādīni paśsyatām | tasmāt tarhi 
		samāśrayāḥ ||53d' || 
samāna āśrayo yeṣāṃ, te cittacaittāḥ anyonyaṃ saṃprayuktakahetuḥ | samāna ity abhinnas tadyathā ya eva cakṣurindriyakṣaṇaś cakṣurvijñānasyāśrayaḥ, sa eva tatsaṃprayuktānāṃ vedanādīnām eva yāvan manaḥ | indriyakṣaṇo manovijñānatatsaṃprayuktānāṃ veditavyaḥ | yaḥ saṃprayuktakahetuḥ sahabhūhetur api sa | atha kenārthena sahabhūhetuḥ kena saṃprayuktakahetuḥ | anyonyaphalārthena sahabhūhetuḥ | sahasārthikānyonyabalamārgaprayāṇavat | pañcabhiḥ samatābhiḥ saṃprayogārthena saṃprayuktakahetuḥ | teṣām eva sārthikāṇāṃ samānānnapānādiparibhogakriyāprayogavat | ekenāpi hi vinā sarveṇa na saṃprayujyanta ity ayam eṣāṃ hetubhāvaḥ | uktaḥ saṃprayuktakahetuḥ | [p.89] 
	sarvatragahetuḥ katamaḥ | 
		sarvatragākhyaḥ kliṣṭānāṃ svabhūmau pūrvasarvagāḥ | (54ab) 
svabhūmikāḥ pūrvotpannāḥ sarvatragaḥ dharmāḥ paścimānāṃ kliṣṭānāṃ dharmāṇāṃ sarvatragahetuḥ | tān punaḥ paścād anuśayanirdeśakośasthāna eva vyākhyāsyāmaḥ | kliṣṭadharmasāmānyakāraṇatvenāyaṃ sabhāgahetoḥ pṛthak vyavasthāpyate | nikāyāntarīyāṇām api hetutvād eṣāṃ hi prabhāveṇānyanaikāyikā api kleśā upajāyante | kim āryapudgalasyāpi kliṣṭā dharmāḥ sarvatragahetukāḥ | sarva eva kliṣṭā dharmā darśanaprahātavyahetukā iti kāśmīrāḥ | tathā prakaraṇeṣūktaṃ darśanaprahātavyahetukāḥ dharmāḥ katame | kliṣṭā dharmāḥ yaś ca darśanaprahātavyānāṃ dharmāṇāṃ vipāka iti | avyākṛtahetukā dharmāḥ katame | avyākṛtāḥ saṃskṛtā dharmā akuśalāś ceti | duḥkhasatyaṃ syāt satkāyadṛṣṭihetukaṃ na satkāyadṛṣṭer hetur iti svistaro yāvan na satkāyadṛṣṭeḥ teṣāṃ ca dharmāṇāṃ jātiṃ jarāṃ sthitim anityatāṃ ca sthāpayitvā yat tad anyat kliṣṭaṃ duḥkhasatyam iti | idaṃ tarhi prajñaptibhāṣyaṃ kathaṃ nīyate, syād dharmo 'kuśalo 'kuśalahetuka eva | syād āryapudgalaḥ kāmavairāgyāt parihīyamāṇo yāṃ tatprathamataḥ kliṣṭāṃ cetanāṃ saṃmukhīkarotīti | aprahīṇahetum etat saṃkhāyoktam | darśanaprahātavyo hi tasyā hetuḥ prahīṇatvān noktaḥ | uktaḥ sarvatragahetuḥ | 
	vipākahetuḥ katamaḥ | 
		vipākahetur aśubhāḥ kuśalāś caiva sāsravāḥ ||54cd || 
akuśalāḥ kuśalasāsravāś ca dharmā vipākahetuḥ | vipākadharmatvāt | kasmād avyākṛtā dharmāḥ vipākaṃ na nirvarttayanti | durbalatvāt | pūtibījavat | kasmān nānāsravāḥ | tṛṣṇānabhiṣyanditatvāt | anabhsyanditasārabījavat | apratisaṃyuktā hi kiṃpratisaṃyuktaṃ vipākam abhinirvarttayeyuḥ | śeṣās tūbhayavidhatvān nirvarttayanti | sārābhsyanditabījavat | katham idaṃ vijñātavyaṃ vipākasya hetur vipākahetur āhosvid vipāka eva hetur vipākahetuḥ | kiṃ cātaḥ | yadi vipākasya hetur vipākahetuḥ, vipākajaṃ cakṣur iti etan na prāpnoti | atha vipāka eva hetur vipākahetuḥ, karmaṇo vipāka ity etan na prāpnoti | naiṣa doṣaḥ | ubhayathāpi yoga ity uktaṃ prāk | atha vipāka iti ko 'rthaḥ | visadṛśaḥ pāko vipākaḥ | anyeṣāṃ tu [p.90] hetūnāṃ sadṛśaḥ pākaḥ | ekasyobhayatheti vaibhāṣikāḥ | naiva tu teṣāṃ pāko yuktaḥ | pāko hi nāma santatipariṇāmaviśeṣajaḥ phalaparyantaḥ | na ca sahabhūsaṃprayuktaka'hetvoḥ santatipariṇāmaviśeṣajaṃ phalam asti | na cāpi sabhāgahetvādīnāṃ phalaparyanto 'sti | punaḥ punaḥ kuśalādyāsaṃsārphalatvāt | kāmadhātāv ekaskandhako vipākahetur ekaphalaḥ pratyayas tajjātyādayaś ca | dviskandhaka ekaphalaḥ kāyavākkarma tajjātyādayaś ca | catuḥ'skandhaka ekaphalaḥ prathame dhyāne vijñaptiḥ saha jātyādibhh | catuḥskandhaka ekaphalaḥ kuśale cetasy asamāhite | pañcaskandhaka ekaphalaḥ samāhite || ārūpyadhātāv ekaskandhako vipākahetur ekaphalaḥ prāptir nirodhasamāpattiś ca saha jātyādibhiḥ | catuḥskandhakaḥ kuśalāś cittacaittāḥ tajjātyādayaś ca | asti karma yasyaikam eva dharmāyatanaṃ vipāko vipacyate, yasya jīvitendriyam | yasya manāyatanaṃ, tasya dve manodharmāyatane | evaṃ yasya spraṣṭavyāyatanaṃ yasya kāyāayatanaṃ, tasya trīṇi kāyaspraṣṭavyadharmāyatanāni | evaṃ yasya rūpagandharasāyatanāni | yasya cakṣurāyatanaṃ tasya catvāri cakṣuḥkāyaspraṣṭavyadharmāyatanāni | evaṃ yasya śrotraghrāṇajihvāayatanāni | asti tat karma yasya pañca ṣaṭ saptāṣṭau nava daśaikādaśāyatanāni vipāko vipacyate | vicitrāvicitraphalatvāt karmaṇo bāhyabījavat | tadyathā bāhyāni bījāni kācid vicitraphalāni bhavanti | tadyathā padmadāḍimanyagrodhādīnām | kācid avicitraphalāni | tadyathā yavagodhūmādīnām | ekādhivikasya karmaṇas traiyadhviko vipāko vipacyate | na tu dvaiyadhvikasyāpy ekādhiviko mā bhūd atinyūnaṃ hetoḥ nāpy anantaraṃ samanantarapratyayākṛṣṭatvāt samanantarakṣaṇasya | pravāhāpekṣo hi vipākahetuḥ | 
		atha ka eṣāṃ hetūnām adhvaniyamaḥ | ukta eṣām arthato 'dhvaniyamaḥ, na tu sūtrita ity ataḥ sūtryate, 
		sarvatragaḥ sabhāgaśca dvyadhvagau (55ab) 
atītapratyutpannāv eva anāgatau na staḥ | uktaṃ cātra kāraṇam, 
		tryadhvagās trayaḥ | (55b') 
sahabūsaṃprayuktakavipākahetavas trikālāḥ | kāraṇahetus tu kālaniyamānupadarśanāt | sarvādhvakaś cādhivaviprayuktaś ca veditavyaḥ | uktā hetavaḥ || 
		kiṃ punas tat phalaṃ, yasyaite hetavaḥ | [p.91] 
		saṃskṛtaṃ savisaṃyogaṃ phalaṃ (55cd) 
phalaṃ dharmāḥ | katame | saṃskṛtā dharmāḥ pratisaṃkhyānirodhaś ceti śāstram | evaṃ tarhi phalatvād asaṃkṛtasya hetunā bhavitavyaṃ, yasya tat phalaṃ, hetutvāc ca phalena bhavitavyaṃ yasya tad dhetuḥ | saṃskṛtasyaiva dharmasya hetuphale bhavataḥ | 
		nāsaṃskṛtasya te ||55d || 
kiṃ kāraṇam | ṣaḍvidhahetvasaṃbhavāt pañcavidhaphalāsaṃbhavāc ca | kasmāt mārgo visaṃyogasya kāraṇahetur neṣyate | yasmāt sa utpādāvighnabhāvena vyavasthāpito na cāsaṃskṛtam utpattimat | kasyedānīṃ tat phalaṃ kathaṃ vā mārgasya phalam | tadbalena prāpteḥ | prāptir eva tarhi mārgasya phalaṃ prāpnoti | ytasyām eva tasya sāmarthyān na visaṃyogaḥ | anyathā hy asya prāptau sāmarthyam anyathā visaṃyhoge | katham asya prāptau sāmarthyam | utpādanāt | kathaṃ visaṃyoge | prāpaṇāt | tasmān na tāvad asya mārgaḥ kathaṃcid api hetuḥ | phalaṃ cāsya visaṃyogaḥ | athāsaty adhipatiphale katham asaṃskṛtaṃ kāraṇahetuḥ | utpattyanāvaraṇabhāvena kāraṇahetuḥ | na cāsya phalam asty adhvavinirmuktasya phalapratigrahaṇadānāsamarthatvāt | naiva hi kvacid asaṃskṛtaṃ bhagavatā hetur ity uktam | uktaṃ tu paryāyeṇa hetur iti sautrāntikāḥ | katham uktam | 
		ye hetavo ye pratyayā rūpasyotpādāya te 'py anityāḥ | 
		anityān khalu hetupratyayān pratītyotpannaṃ rūpaṃ kuto nityaṃ bhaviṣyati 
evaṃ tāvad dhi vijñānam iti | evaṃ tarhi vijñānasyālambanapratyayo 'py asaṃskṛtaṃ na prāpnoti | utpādāyety avadhāraṇāt | prāpnoti | 
		ye hetavo ye pratyayyā vijñānasyotpādāya te 'py anityāḥ 
ity uktaṃ na tūktaṃ ye vijñānasya pratyayāḥ te 'py anityā iti | nanu ca hetavo 'pi ya utpādāya ta evānityā iti vacanād asaṣmskṛtasyānāvaraṇabhāvamātreṇa kāraṇahetutvāpratiṣedhaḥ | ukta ālambanapratyayaḥ sūtre na tv anāvaraṇahetur iti na sūtre sidhyaty asaṃskṛtasya hetubhāvaḥ | yady api nokto na tu pratiṣiddhaḥ | sūtrāṇi ca bahūny antarhitānīti katham etan nirdhāryate, nokta iti | 
		atha ko 'yaṃ visaṃyogo nāma | nanu coktaṃ prāk, pratisaṃkhyānirodha iti | tadānīṃ pratisaṃkhyānirodhaḥ katamo, yo visaṃyoga ity uktam, idānīṃ visaṃyogaḥ katamaḥ | yaḥ [p.92] pratisaṃkhyānirodha ity ucyate | tad idam itaretarāśrayaṃ vyākhyānam asamarthaṃ tatsvabhāvadyotane | tasmād anyathā tatsvabhāvo vaktavyaḥ |āryair evatatsvabhāvaḥ pratyātmavedyaḥ | etāvat tu śakyate vaktuṃ nityaṃ kuśalaṃ cāsti dravyāntaram | tadvisaṃyogaś cocyate pratisaṃkhānirodhaś ceti | sarvam evāsaṃskṛtam adravyam iti sautrāntikāḥ | na hi tad rūpavedanādivat bhāvāntaram asti | kiṃ tarhi | spraṣṭavyābhāvamātram ākāśam | tadyathā hy andhakāre pratighātam avindanta ākāśam ity āhuḥ | utpannānuśayajanmanirodhaḥ pratisaṃkhyābalenānyasyānutpādaḥ pratisaṃkhyānirodhaḥ | vinaiva pratisaṃkhyayā pratyayavaikalyād anutpādo yaḥ so 'pratisaṃkhyānirodhaḥ | tadyathā nikāyasabhāgaśeṣasyāntarāmaraṇe | nikāyāntarīyāḥ punar āhuḥ |anuśayānām anutpattau prajñāyāḥ sāmarthyam ato 'sau pratisaṃkhyānirodhaḥ | yas tu punaḥ duḥkhasyānutpādaḥ sa utpādakāraṇānuśayavaikalyād eveti na tasmin prajñāyāḥ sāmarthyam asty ato 'sāv apratisaṃkhyā nirodha iti | so 'pi tu nāntareṇa pratisaṃkhyāṃ sidhyatīti pratisaṃkhyānirodha evāsau | ya evotpannasya paścādabhāvaḥ sa eva svarasanirodhād apratisaṃkhyānirodha ity apare | asyāṃ tu kalpanāyām anityo 'pratisaṃkhyānirodhaḥ prāpnoty avinaṣṭe tadabhāvāt | nanu ca pratisaṃkhyānirodho 'py anityaḥ prāpnoti | pratisaṃkhyāpūrvakatvāt | na vai sa pratisaṃkhyāpūrvako na hi pūrvaṃ pratisaṃkhyā paścād anutpannānām anutpādaḥ | akiṃ tarhi | pūrvam eva sa teṣām anutpādo 'sti | vinā tu pratisaṃkhyayā ye dharmā utpatsyante, tadutpannāyāṃ pratisaṃkhyāyāṃ unar nopapadyanta iti | etad atra pratisaṃkhyāḥ sāmarthyaṃ yad utākṛtotpattipratibandhānām utpattipratibandhabhāvaḥ | yadi tarhi anutpāda eva nirvāṇam idaṃ sūtrapadaṃ kathaṃ nīyate, pañcemānīndriyāṇi āsevitāni bhāvitāni bahulīkṛtāny atītānāgataprtyutpannasya duḥkhasya prahāṇāya saṃvarttanta iti | prahāṇaṃ hi nirvāṇam anāgatasyaiva cānutpādo nātītapratyutpannasyeti | asty etad evam | kiṃ tu tadālambanakleśaprahāṇāt duḥkhasya prahāṇam uktaṃ bhagavatā, yo rūpe cchandarāgas taṃ prajahīta | cchandarāge prahīṇe evaṃ vas tadrūpaṃ prahīṇaṃ bhaviṣyati, parijñātaṃ vistareṇa yāvad vijñānam iti | evaṃ traiyadhivikasyāpi duḥkhasya prahāṇaṃ yujyate | athāpy atītānāgatapratyutpannasya kleśasya prahāṇāyety ucyeta | atrāpy eṣa nayaḥ | athavā 'yam abhiprāyo bhaved atītaḥ kleśaḥ paurvajanmikaḥ pratyutpannaḥ kleśa aihajanmiko yathā tṛṣṇāvicariteṣv aṣṭādaśa tṛṣṇāvicaritāny atītam adhvānam upādāyety atītaṃ janmādhikṛtyoktam evaṃ yāvat pratyutpannam | tgena ca kleśadvayenāsyāṃ santatau bījabhāva [p.93] āhito 'nāgatasyotpattaye | tasya prahāṇāt tad api prahīṇaṃ bhavati | yathā vipākakṣayāt karma kṣīṇaṃ bhavati | anāgatasya punar duḥkhasya kleśasya vā bījābhāvāt atyantam anutpādaḥ prahāṇam | anyathā hy atītapratyutpannasya kiṃ prahātavyam | na hi niruddhe nirodhābhimukhe ca yatnaḥ sārthako bhavatgīti | yady asaṃskṛtaṃ nāsty eva, yad uktaṃ bhagavatā, ye kecid dharmāḥ saṃskṛtā vāsaṃskṛtā vā virāgas teṣāṃ agra ākhyāta' iti katham asatām asann agro bhavitum arhati | na vai nāsty evāsasṃskṛtam iti brūmaḥ | etat tu tad īdṛśaṃ yathāsmābhir uktam | tadyathā asti śabdasya prāgabhāvo 'sti paścād abhāva ity ucyate | atha ca punar nābhāvo bhāvaḥ sidhyati | evam asaṃskṛtam api draṣṭavyam | abhāvo 'pi ca kaścit praśasyatamo bhavati, yaḥ sakalasyopadravasyātyantam abhāva ity anyeṣāṃ so 'gra iti praśaṃsāṃ labdhum arhati | vineyānāṃ tasminn upacchandanārtham | yady apy asaṃskṛtam abhāvamātraṃ syān nirodha āryasatyaṃ na syāt | na hi tat kiṃcid astīti | kas tāvad ayaṃ satyārthaḥ | nanu cāviparītārthaḥ | ubhayam api caitad aviparītaṃ dṛṣṭam āryair yad uta duḥkhaṃ ca duḥkham eveti duḥkhābhāvaś cābhāva eveti ko 'syāryasatyatve nirodhaḥ katham abhāvaś ca nāma tṛṭīyaṃ cāryasatyaṃ syāt | uktaṃ yathāryasatyaṃ dvitīyasyānantaraṃ dṛṣṭam uddiṣṭaṃ ceti tṛtīyaṃ bhavati | yady asaṃskṛtam abhāvamātraṃ syād ākāśanirvāṇālambanavijñānam asadālambanaṃ syāt | etad atītānāgatasyāstitvacintāyāṃ cintayiṣyāmaḥ | yadi punar dravyam evāsaṃskṛtam iṣyeta, kiṃ syāt | kiṃ ca punaḥ syāt vaibhāṣikapakṣaḥ pālitaḥ syāt | devatā enaṃ pālayiṣyanti, pālanīyaṃ cet maṃsyate | abhūtaṃ tu parikalpitaṃ syāt | kiṃ kāraṇam | na hi tasya rūpavedanādivat svabhāva upalabhyate, na cāpi cakṣurādivat karma | amuṣya ca vastuno 'yaṃ nirodha iti ṣaṣṭhīvyavasthā kathaṃ prakalpyate | na hi tasya tena sārdhaṃ kaścit saṃbandho hetuphalādibhāvāsaṃbhavāt | pratiṣedhamātraṃ tu yujyate amuṣyābhāva iti | bhāvāntaratve 'pi yasya kleśasya prāptivicchedād yo nirodhaḥ prāpyate, sa taseyeti vyavadiśyate | tasya tarhi prāptiniyame ko hetuḥ | dṛṣṭadharmanirvāṇaprāpto bhikṣur ity uktaṃ sūtre | tatra katham abhāvasya prāptiḥ syāt | pratipakṣalābhena kleśapunarbhavotpādātyantaviruddhāśrayalābhāt prāptaṃ nirvāṇam ity ucyate | āgamaś cāpy abhāvamātraṃ dyotayati | evaṃ hy āha | yat khalv asya (Pradhan:svalpasya) duḥkhasyāśeṣaprahāṇaṃ pratiniḥsargo vyaṅgībhāvaḥ kṣayo virāgo nirodho vyupaśamo 'staṃgamaḥ anyasya ca duḥkhasyāprati[p.94]sandhir anutpādo 'prādurbhāvaḥ | etat kāntam etat praṇītaṃ yad uta sarvopādhipratiniḥsargas tṛṣṇākṣayo virāgo nirodho nirvāṇam iti | kim evaṃ neṣyate nāsmin prādurb havatīty ato 'prādurbhāva iti | asamarthām etāṃ saptamīṃ paśsyāmaḥ | kim uktaṃ bhavati | nāsmin prādurbhavatīti yadi satīty abhisaṃbadhyate, nityam evāprādurbhāvaprasaṅgo nirvāṇasya nityatvāt | atha prāpta ity abhisaṃbadhyate, yata eva tatprāptiḥ parikalpyate, tasminn eva saṃmukhībhūte prāpte vā duḥkhasyeṣyatām aprādurbhāvaḥ | ayaṃ ca dṛṣṭānta evaṃ sūpanīto bhavati | 
		pradyotasyeva nirvāṇaṃ vimokṣas tasya cetasa iti | 
yathā pradyotasya nirvāṇam abhāva, evaṃ bhagavato 'pi cetaso vimokṣa iti | abhidharme 'pi coktam, avastukā dharmāḥ katame | asaṃskṛtā dharmā iti | avastukā aśarīrā asvabhāvā ity uktaṃ bhavati | nāstyāyam arthaḥ | kas tarhi | pañcavidhaṃ (Pradhan: pañcavidha) vastu | svabhāvavastu yathoktaṃ, yad vastu pratilabdhaṃ samanvāgataḥ, sa tena vastuneti | ālambanavastu | yathoktaṃ, sarvadharmajñeyā jñānena yathāvastv iti | saṃyogavastu | yathoktaṃ, yasmin vastuni anunayaḥ saṃyojanena saṃprayuktaḥ pratighagsaṃyojanenāpi tasminn iti | hetuvastu yathoktaṃ, savastukā dharmāḥ katame | saṃskṛtā dharmā iti | parigrahavastu | yathoktaṃ, kṣetravastu gṛhavastv iti | tad atra hetur vastuśabdenoktas tasmād asty evāsaṃskṛtaṃ dravyata iti vaibhāṣikāḥ | tasya tu hetuphale na vidyete iti | gataṃ tāvad etat | 
	athaiṣāṃ phalānāṃ katamat phalaṃ kasya hetoḥ | 
		vipākaphalam antyasya (56a) 
vipākahetur antye 'bhihitatvāt antyaḥ | tasya vipākaphalam | 
		pūrvasyādhipataṃ phalam | (56b) 
kāraṇahetuḥ pūrvam uktatvāt pūrvaḥ | tasyādhipajaṃ phalam | anāvaraṇabhāvamātreṇāvasthitasya kim ādhipatyam | etad eva | aṅgībhāvo 'pi cāsti kāraṇahetos tadyathā, pañcasu vijñānakāyeṣu daśānām āyatanānāṃ bhājanaloke ca karmaṇām | śrotrādīnām apy asti cakṣurvijñānotpattau pāraṃparyeṇādhipatyam | śrutvā draṣṭukāmatotpatter ityevamādi yojyam | 
		sabhāgasarvatragayor niṣyandaḥ (56cd) 
sadṛśaphalatvād anayor niḥṣyandaphalam | [p.95] 
		pauruṣaṃ dvayoḥ ||56d' || 
sahabhūsamprayuktakahetvoḥ puruṣakāraphalam | puruṣabhāvāvyatirekāt puruṣakāraḥ puruṣa eva | tasya phalaṃ pauruṣam | ko 'yaṃ puruṣakāro nāma | yasya dharmasya yat kāritram | puruṣakāra iva hi puruṣakāraḥ | tadyathā kākajaṅghā oṣadhir mattahastī manuṣya iti | kim anyeṣām apy asti puruṣakāraphalam utāho dvayor eva | anyeṣām apy asty anyatra vipākahetoḥ | yasmāt sahotpannaṃ vā samanantarotpannaṃ vā puruṣakāraphalaṃ bhavati | na caivaṃ vipākaḥ | tasyāpy asti viprakṛṣṭapuruṣakāraphalam | yathā karṣakāṇāṃ sasyam ity apare | 
	kiṃ punar idaṃ vipākaphalaṃ nāma, kiṃ yāvad adhipatiphalam | 
		vipāko 'vyākṛto dharmaḥ (57a) 
anivṛtāvyākṛto hi dhamaḥ vipākaḥ | asattvākhyo 'pi syād, ata āha, 
		sattvākhyaḥ (57b) 
aupacayiko 'pi syāt naiḥṣyandiko 'py ata āha, 
		avyākṛtodbhavaḥ | (57b') 
kuśalākuśalaṃ hi vipākaṃ prati vyākaraṇād vyākṛtam | tasmād ya uttarakālaṃ bhavati, na saha nānantaraṃ sa vipākaḥ | etad vipākasya lakṣaṇam | tasmād asattvākhyo 'rthaḥ karmajo na vipākaḥ sādhāraṇatvāt | anyo 'pi hi tat tathaiva paribhoktuṃ samarthaḥ | asādhāraṇas tu vipākaḥ | na hy anyakṛtasya karmaṇo 'nyo vipākaṃ pratisaṃvedayate | adhipatiphalaṃ kasmāt pratisaṃvedayate | sādhāraṇakarmasaṃbhūtatvāt | 
		niḥṣyando hetusadṛśaḥ (57c) 
hetor yaḥ sadṛśo dharmaḥ, sa niṣyandaphalam | tadyathā sabhāgasarvatragahetvoḥ | yadi sarvatragahetor api samānaṃ phalaṃ kasmān na sabhāgahetor eveṣyate | yasmāt bhūmitaḥ klisṭatayā cāsya sādṛśyaṃ na tu prakārataḥ | yasya tu prakārato 'pi sādṛśyaṃ so 'bhyupagamyata eva sabhāgahetuḥ | ata eva yo yasya sabhāgahetuḥ sarvatragahetur api sa tasyeti catuṣkoṭikaḥ kriyate | prathamā koṭir asarvatragaḥ sabhāgahetuḥ | dvitīyānyanaikāyikaḥ sarvatragahetuḥ | tṛtīyaikanaikāyikaḥ sarvatragahetuḥ | caturthy etān ākārān sthāpayitveti | [p.96] 		visaṃyogaḥ kṣayo dhiyā ||57d || 
kṣayo nirodhaḥ | dhīḥ prajñā | tena pratisaṃkhyānirodho visaṃyogaphalam ity uktaṃ bhavati | 
		yadbalāj jāyate yat tat phalaṃ puruṣakārajam | (58ab) 
tadyathā adharabhūmikasya prayogacittasyoparibhūmikaḥ samādhiḥ sāsravasyānāsravo dhyānacittasya nirmāṇacittam ityevamādi | pratisaṃkhyānirodhas tu yadbalāt prāpyata iti vaktavyam | 
		apūrvaḥ sasṃskṛtasyaiva, 
	saṃskṛto 'dhipateḥ phalam ||58cd || 
pūrvotpannād anyaḥ saṃskṛto dharmaḥ saṃskṛtasyaiva sarvasyādhipatiphalam | puruṣādhipatiphalayoḥ kiṃ nānākaraṇam (Pradhan: nānā kāraṇam) | kartuḥ puruṣakāraphalam | akartur apy adhipatiphalam | tadyathā śilpini śilpaṃ puruṣakāraphalam adhipatiphalaṃ ca | anyeṣām adhipatiphalam eva | 
	athaiṣāṃ hetūnāṃ katamo hetuḥ kasmin kāle phalaṃ pratigṛhṇāti, dadāti vā | 
		vartamāṇāḥ phalaṃ pañca, 
		gṛhṇanti (59ab) 
atītāḥ pratigṛhītatvān nāpy anāgatā niṣpuruṣakāratvāt | kāraṇahetur apy evam | sa tu nāvaśyaṃ saphala iti nocyate | 
		dvau prayacchataḥ | (59b') 
sahabhūsaṃprayuktakahetū varttamānau phalaṃ prayacchataḥ | samānakālam eva hy anayoḥ (Pradhan:anayeḥ )phaladānagrahaṇam | 
		varttamānābhyatītau dvau (59c) 
phalaṃ prayacchataḥ sabhāgasarvatragahetū | yuktaṃ tāvad yad atītāv iti | atha kathaṃ vattamānau niṣyandaphalaṃ prayacchataḥ | samanantaranivarttanāt | nivṛtte tu phale tau cābhyatītau bhavataḥ | phalaṃ cāpi dattaṃ na punas tad eva dattaḥ (dattam') | asti kuśalaḥ sabhāgahetuḥ phalaṃ pratigṛhṇāti, na dadātīti catuṣkoṭikaḥ | prathamā koṭiḥ, kuśalamūlāni samucchindan yāḥ prāptīḥ sarvapaścād vijahāti | dvitīyā, kuśalamūlāni pratisaṃdadhāno yāḥ sarvaprathamaṃ pratilabhate | evaṃ tu vaktavyam | syāt tā eva pratisaṃdadhānasya tṛtīyā asamucchinnakuśalamūlasya śeṣāsv avasthāsu | caturty etān ākārān sthāpayitvā | akuśalasya tu prathamā koṭiḥ | kāmavairāgyam anuprāpnuvan yāḥ prāptīḥ sadrvapaścād vijahāti | dvitīyā, kāmavairāgyāt parhīyamāṇo yāḥ sarvaprathamaṃ pratilabhate | evaṃ tu vaktavyam | syāt tā eva parihīyamāṇasya | tṛtīyā, kāmavītarāgasya śeṣāsv avasthāsu | caturthy etān [p.97] ākārān sthāpayitvā | evaṃ nivṛtāvyākṛtasyāpy arhattvaprāptiparihāṇito yathāyogaṃ yojyam | anivṛtāvyākṛtasya paścātpādakaḥ | yas tāvan dadāti, pratigṛhṇāty api saḥ | syāt pratigṛhṇāti na dadāty arhataś caramāḥ skandhāḥ | sālambananiyamena tu kṣaṇaśaḥ | kuśalaḥ sabhāgahetuḥ phalaṃ pratigṛhṇāti na dadātīti | catuṣkoṭikaḥ | prathamā koṭiḥ, kuśalacittānantaraṃ klisṭam avyākṛtaṃ vā cittaṃ saṃmukhīkarotīti | dvitīyā viparyayāt | tṛtīyā, kuśalacittānantaraṃ kuśalam eva | caturthy etān ākārān sthāpayitvā | evam akuśalādayo 'pi yojyāḥ | kathaṃ punaḥ phalaṃ pratigṛhītaṃ bhavati | tasya bījabhāvopagamāt | 
		eko 'tītaḥ prayacchati ||59d || 
vipākahetur atīta eva phalaṃ prayacchati | yasmān na saha vā samanantaro vāsti vipākaḥ | punar anye caturvidhaṃ phalam āhuḥ | pratiṣṭhāphalam | yathā jalamaṇḍalaṃ vāyumaṇdalasya yāvat tṛṇādayaḥ pṛthivyāḥ | prayogaphalam | yathāśubhāyā yāvad anutpādajñānam | sāmagrīphalam | yathā cakṣurādīnāṃ cakṣurvijñānādini | bhāvanāphalam | yathā rūpāvacarasya cittasya nirmāṇam | etat tu puruṣakārādhipatiphalayor antarbhūtam | uktā hetavaḥ phalāni ca || 
	tatra katame dharmāḥ katibhiḥ hetubhir utpadyanta ity āha samāsata ime caturvidhā dharmās tadyathā kliṣṭā dharmā vipākajāḥ prathamānāsravās tebhyaś ca śeṣāḥ | ke punaḥ śeṣāḥ | vipākavarjyāḥ avyākṛtāḥ prathamānāsravakṣaṇavarjyāś ca kuśalā iti | ete caturvidhā dharmāh 
		kliṣṭā vipākajāḥ śeṣāḥ, 
		prathamāryā yathākramam | 
		vipākaṃ sarvagaṃ hitvā, 
		tau sabhāgaṃ ca śeṣajāḥ ||60|| 
kliṣṭā dharmā vipākahetuṃ hitvā śeṣebhyaḥ pañcabhyo jāyante | vipākajāḥ sarvatragahetuṃ hitvā śeṣebhyaḥ pañcabhya eva | śeṣā dharmās tau vipākasarvatragahetū hitvā śeṣebhyaś caturbhyo jāyante | prathamānāsravās tau ca vipākasarvatragahetū sabhāgahetuṃ ca hitvā śeṣebhyaḥ tribhyo jāyante | 
	katame ime dharmāś caturvidhā nirdiṣṭā ity āha 
		cittacaittāḥ (61a) 
atha ye cittaviprayuktā rūpiṇaś ca dharmās te katham ity āha 
		tathānye 'pi 
	saṃprayuktakavarjitāḥ | (61a'b) 
saṃprayuktakahetunaikena varjitāḥ anye 'pi kliṣṭādayo dharmās tathaivotpadyante yathā cittacaittāḥ | [p.98] tatra kliṣṭāś caturbhyo vipākajāś ca | śeṣās tribhyaḥ prathamānāsravā dvābhyām | ekahetusaṃbhūto nāsti dharmaḥ || samāpto hetuvistaraḥ | 
	pratyayāḥ katame | 
		catvāraḥ pratyayā uktāḥ (61c) 
kvoktāḥ | sūtre | catasraḥ pratyayatāḥ | hetupratyayatā, samanantarapratyayatā, ālambanapratyayatā, adhipatipratyayatā ceti | pratyayajātiḥ pratyayatā | tatra 
		hetvākhyaḥ pañca hetavaḥ ||61d || 
kāraṇahetuvarjyāḥ pañca hetavo hetupratyayaḥ | 
		cittacaittā acaramā, 
		utpannāḥ samanantaraḥ | (62ab) 
arhataḥ paścimān apāsyotpannāś cittacaittāḥ samanantarapratyayaḥ | samaś cāyam anantaraś ca pratyaya iti samanantarapratyayaḥ | ata eva rūpaṃ na samanantarapratyayo viṣamotpatteḥ | tathā hi kāmāvacarasya rūpasyānantarṃ kadācit kāmāvacaraṃ rūpāvacaraṃ cāvijñaptirūpam utpadyate, kadācit kāmāvacaraṃ cānāsravaṃ ceti vyākulo rūpasaṃmukhībhāvaḥ | avyākulas tu samanantarapratyayaḥ aniruddha evaikasminn aupacayikarūpasantāne dvitīyotpatter iti bhadantavasumitraḥ | alpabahutarotpatter iti bhadantaḥ | kadācid dhi mahato rūpād alpam utpadyate | tadyathā palālarāśer bhasma | kadācid alpād bahūtpadyate | tadyathā vaḍanikāyāḥ krameṇa yāvad anekaśākhāvaroho nyagrodha iti | nanu cāsti caittānām apy alpabahutarotpattiḥ | kuśalākuśalāvyākṛteṣu citteṣu savitarkasavicārādau ca samādhitraye | asti jātyantaraṃ prati na svajātim | na hi kadācid bahutarā vedanotpadyate saṃjñādayo vā | kiṃ punaḥ svajāter eva samanantarapratyayo bhavati | naitad asti | sakala eva kalāpaḥ sakalasya kalāpāntarasya samanantarapratyayo na tv alpakād vedanādidravyāt prabhūtaṃ vedanādi dravyam utpadyata ity etāvad evātroktam | santānasabhāgikās tu manyante, svajāter eva samanantaraprtyayaḥ | tadyathā cittaṃ cittasyaiva vedanā vedanāyā eveti vistaraḥ | yadā tv akliṣṭāntaraṃ kliṣṭam utpadyate tasya kleśasya pūrvaniruddhaḥ kleśaḥ samanatarapratyayas tadyathā nirodhasamāpatticittaṃ vyutthānacittasyeti | tad etan na vartsyate | prathamānāsravacittanutpattiprasaṅgāt cittaviprayuktā api saṃskārāḥ | ata eva vyākulasaṃmukhibhāvān na samanantarapratyayas traidhātukāpratisaṃyuktānāṃ yugapat saṃmukhībhāvāt | kasmād anāgato neṣyate samanantarapratyayaḥ | vyākulatvād anāgatasyādhvanaḥ [p.99] pūrvottarābhāvāt | kathaṃ tarhi bhagavān jānāti amuṣyānāgatasyānantaram idam anāgataṃ bhavatīti | atītasāṃpratānumānāt | atītaṃ kilādhvānaṃ paśyati bhagavān evaṃjātīyakāt karmaṇaḥ evaṃjātīiyako vipākaḥ utpanno dharmād vā dharmaḥ | idaṃ cāpi saṃpraty evamjātīyakaṃ karma | tasmād ato 'py evaṃjātīyako vipāka utpatsyate dharmād vā dharma iti jānāti | na cānyat jñānam ānumānikaṃ bhavati | yasmād atītasāṃpratānumānena bhagavān vikīrṇāny anāgatāni dravyāṇi pratyakṣam īkṣitvā jānāty anena pudgalenaivaṃvidhaṃ karma kurvatedam anāgataṃ phalaṃ parigṛhītam iti | evaṃ tarhi bhagavān pūrvāntam adṛṣṭvāparāntaṃ na jānīyāt | anye punar āhuḥ | phalacihnabhūtaḥ sattvānāṃ santatau cittaviprayuktaḥ saṃskāraviśeṣo 'sti yaṃ vyavalokya bhagavān anāgataṃ jānāty asaṃmukhīkṛtvāpi dhyānam abhijñāṃ ceti | naimittiko hi nāma bhagavān syād evaṃ sati, na punaḥ sākṣātkārī | tsmāt sarvam icchāmātreṇa bhagavān jānātīti sautrāntikāḥ | acintyo hi buddhānāṃ buddha(Pradhan:buddhi)viṣaya ity uktaṃ bhagavatā | atha asaty anāgatasya kramaniyamāvasthāne kasmād agradharmānantaraṃ duḥkhe dharmajñānakṣāntir evotpadyate, nānyo dharmaḥ | evaṃ yāvad vajropamānantaraṃ kṣayajñānam evotpadyate, nānyo dharma iti | yasya yatpratibaddha utpādaḥ, sa tasyānantaram utpadyate | tadyathā bījādīnām aṅkurādayo vināpi samanantarapratyayeneti | kasmād arhataś caramāś cittacaittā na samananatarapratyayaḥ | anyacittasaṃbandhanāt | nanu caivaṃ samanantaraniruddhaṃ cittaṃ mano bhavatīty anantaravijñānābhāvāt mano 'pi caramaṃ cittaṃ na prāpnoti | āśrayabhāvapratibhāvitaṃ mano na kāritraprabhāvitam ity asty evāśrayabhāvaḥ | kāraṇāntaravaikalyāt tu vijñānāntaraṃ notpadyata iti | kāritraprabhāvitas tu samanantarapratyayas tena yo dharmaḥ phalaṃ pratigṛhītaḥ sa sarvair api dharmaiḥ sarvaprāṇibhir vā na śakyaṃ pratibanddhuṃ yathā notpadyate | ye dharmāś cittasamanantarāś cittanirantarā api te | catuṣkoṭikaḥ | prathamā koṭir, acittakāyāḥ samāpatter vyutthānacittaṃ, dvitīyādayaś ca samāpattikṣaṇāḥ | dvitīyā koṭiḥ, prathamasya samāpattikṣaṇasya sacittakāyāps cāvasthāyā jātyādayaḥ | tṛtīyā koṭiḥ prathamaḥ samāpattikṣaṇaḥ sacittikā cāvasthā | caturthīi koṭir, dvitīyādīnāṃ samāpattikṣaṇānāṃ jātyādayo vyutthānacittasya ca | ye dharmāś cittasamanantarāḥ samāpattinirantarā api te | catuṣkoṭikaḥ | ye tṛtīyācaturthyau te prathamādvitīye ye prathamādvitīye te [p.100] tṛtīyācaturthyau karttavye | katham idājñīṃ dūrāntaravicchinnaṃ vyutthānacittaṃ samāpatticittasya samanantaram ity ucyate | cittāntarāvyavahitatvāt || uktaḥ samanantarapratyayaḥ || 
		ālambanaṃ sarvadharmāḥ (62c) 
yathāyogaṃ cakṣurvijñānasya sasaṃprayogasya rūpam | śrotravijñānasya śabdaḥ | ghrāṇavijñānasya gandhaḥ | jihvāvijñānasya rasaḥ | kāyavijñānasya spraṣṭavyam | mano vijñānasya sarvadharmāḥ | yo dharmo yasya dharmasyālambanaṃ na kadācit sa dharmas taddharmasya nālambanam | anālambyamāno 'pi tathālakṣaṇatvād | yathānidhyamānam apīndhanam ucyate kāṣṭhādikaṃ tathālakṣaṇatvād iti | na ete cittacaittā dharmā āyatanadravyakṣaṇaniyamenālambane yathāsvaṃ niyatāḥ | kim āśrayaniyamenāpi niyatāḥ | om itty āha | utpannās tv āśrayasahitā anutpannā hy atītā āśrayaviśliṣṭāḥ | atītā apy āśrayasahitā ity apare || ukta ālambanapratyayaḥ || 
		kāraṇākhyo 'dhipaḥ smṛtaḥ ||62d || 
ya eva kāraṇahetuḥ sa evādhipatipratyayaḥ | adhiko 'yaṃ pratyaya ity adhipatipratyayaḥ | ālambanapratyayo 'pi sarvagdharmāḥ adhipatipratyayo 'pīti kim asty ādhikyam | na jātu sahabhuvo dharmā ālambanaṃ bhavanti | bhavanti tv adhipatipratyaya ity asyaivādhikyam | adhikasya vā pratyayaḥ | sarvaḥ sarvasya saṃskṛtasya svabhāvavarjyasya | syād dharmo dharmasya caturbhir api pratyayair na pratyayaḥ | syāt svabhāvaḥ svabhāvasya parabhāvo 'pi | syāt saṃskṛtam asaṃskṛtasyāsaṃskṛtaṃ cāsaṃskṛtasya | 
	athasaite pratyayāḥ kāritraṃ kurvantaḥ kimavasthe dharme kurvanti | hetupratyayas tāvat pañcavidha uktaḥ | tatra 
		nirudhyamāne kāritraṃ, 
		dvau hetū kurutaḥ (63ab) 
nirudhyamānaṃ nāma varttamānam | nirodhābhimukhatvāt | tatra sahabhūsaṃprayuktakahetū kāritraṃ kurutaḥ | sahotpanne 'pi phale tayor vyāpāraḥ | 
		trayaḥ 
		jāyamāne (63b'c) 
jāyamānaṃ nāmānāgatam utpādābhimukham | tatra sabhāgasarvatragavipākahetavaḥ kāritraṃ kurvanti | evaṃ tāvad dhetupratyayaḥ | [p.101] 
		tato 'nyau tu, pratyayayau tadviparytayāt ||63c'd || 
yena kāritranyāyena hetupratyayau dvidhā kṛtvoktas tadviparyayāt samanantarapratyayālambana pratyayau veditavyau | samanantarapratyayo jāyamāne kāritraṃ karoty avakāśadānāt | ālambanapratyayo nirudhyamāne | varttamānaiś cittacaittair grahaṇāt | adhipatipratyayas tu sarvasyām avasthāyām anāvaraṇabhāvenāvasthita ity etad evāsya kāritram || uktāḥ sakāritrāḥ pratyayāḥ || 
	atha katamo dharmaḥ katibhiḥ pratyayair utpadyate | 
		 caturbhiś cittacaittā hiA (64a) 
tatra hetupratyay eṣāṃ sarve pañca hetavaḥ | samanantarapratyayaḥ pūrvakaś cittacaittā anyair avyavahitāḥ | ālambanapratyayo yathāyogaṃ pañca viṣayāḥ sarve dharmāś ca | adhipatipratyayaḥ svabhāvavarjyāḥ sarvadharmāḥ | 
		samāpattidvayaṃ tribhiḥ | (64b) 
nirodhāsamjñisamāpattyor ālambanapratyayo nāsti | na hi te ālambike | hetupratyayas tu tayor dvividho hetuḥ | sahabhūhetuś ca jātyādayaḥ sabhāgahetuś ca pūrvotpannāḥ samānabhūmikāḥ kuśalā dharmāḥ | samanantarapratyayaḥ sasaṃprayogaṃ samāpatticittam | adhipatipratyayaḥ pūrvavat | cittābhisaṃskārajatvād ete samāpattī cittasamanantare | cittotpattivibandhakatvāt na	samanantarapratyayaḥ | 
		dvābhyām anye tu jāyante (64c) 
anye tu viprayuktā rūpiṇaś ca dharmā hetvadhipatipratyayābhyāṃ jāyante yathāvihitam eva | hetupratyayebhyo bhāvā upajāyante, na punaḥ sarvasyaiva jagataḥ īśvarapuruṣapradhānādikaṃ kāraṇam iti | ko 'tra hetuḥ | yadi khalu hetukṛtāṃ siddhiṃ manyase | nanu ca ata evāsya vādasya vyudāsaḥ prāpnoty ekaṃ kāraṇam īśvarādikaṃ sarvasyeti | api ca 
		neśvarādeḥ kramādibhiḥ ||64c || 
yadi hy ekam eva kāraṇam īśvaraḥ syād, anyad vā yugapat sarveṇa jagatā bhavitavyaṃ syāt | dṛśyate ca bhāvānāṃ kramasaṃbhavaḥ | sa tarhi cchandavaśād īśvarasya syād ayam idānīm utpadyatām ayaṃ nirudhyatām ayaṃ paścād iti | cchandabhedāt tarhi siddham anekaṃ kāraṇaṃ syāt | sa cāpi cchandabhedo yugapat [p.102] syāt taddhetor īśvarasyābhinnatvāt | kāraṇāntarabhedāpekṣaṇe vā neśvara eva kāraṇaṃ syāt | teṣām api ca kramotpattau kāraṇāntarabhedāpekṣaṇād anavasthāprasaṅgaḥ syād ity anantarabhedāyāḥ kāraṇaparaṃparāyā anāditvābhyupagamād ayam īśvarakāraṇādhimuktaḥ śākyaputrīyam eva nyāyaṃ nātivṛttaḥ syāt | yaugapadye 'pīśvaracchandānāṃ jagato na yaugapadyam | yathācchandam utpādanād iti cet | na | teṣāṃ paścād viśeṣābhāvāt | kaś ca tāvad īśvarasyeyatā sargaprayāsenārthaḥ | yadi prītis tāṃ tarhi nāntareṇopāyaṃ śaktaḥ karttum iti na tasyām īśvaraḥ syāt tathaiva cānyasmin | yadi ceśvaro narakādiṣu prajāṃ bahubhiś cetibhir upasṛṣṭāṃ sṛṣṭvā tena prīyate, namo 'stu tasmai tādṛśāyeśvarāya | sugītaś cāyaṃ tam ārabhya śloko bhavati | 
		yan nirdahati yat tīkṣṇo, yad ugro yat pratāpavān 
		māṃsaśoṇitamajjādo, yat tato rudra ucyata iti | 
ekaṃ khalv api jagataḥ kāraṇaṃ parigṛhṇatānyeṣām arthānāṃ pratyakṣaḥ puruṣakāro nihnutaḥ syāt | sahāpi ca kāraṇaiḥ kārakam īśvaraṃ kalpayatā kevalo bhaktivādaḥ syāt | kāraṇebhyo 'nyasya tadutpattau vyāpārādarśanāt | sahakāriṣu cānyeṣu kāraṇeṣv īśvaro neśvaraḥ syāt | athādisarga īśvarahetukaḥ tasyāpy anyānapekṣatvād īśvaravad anāditvaprasaṅgaḥ | evaṃ pradhāne 'pi yathāyogaṃ vācyam | tasmān na lokasyaikaṃ kāraṇam asti | svāny evaiṣāṃ karmāṇi tasyāṃ tasyāṃ jātau janayanti | akṛtabuddhayas tu varākāḥ svaṃ svaṃ vipākaphalaṃ cānubhavanta īśvaram aparaṃ mithyā parikalpayanti | gatam etad yat tu khalu tad uktaṃ dvābhyām anye tu jāyanta iti | 
	atha kathaṃ bhūtāni bhūtānāṃ hetupratyayaḥ | 
		dvidhā bhūtāni taddhetuḥ (65a) 
bhūtahetur ity arthaḥ | sabhāgasahabhūhetubhyaṃ 
		bhautikasya tu pañcadhā (65b) 
bhautikasya tu bhūtāni pañcaprakāro hetuḥ | katham 
		jananān niḥśrayāt sthānād upastambhopavṛṃhaṇāt 
so 'yaṃ kāraṇahetur eva punaḥ pañcadhā bhinnaḥ | jananahetus tebhya utpatteḥ | niśrayahetur jātasya bhūtānuvidhāyhitvāt puruṣakāraphalād ācāryādiniḥśrayavat | pratiṣṭhāhetur ādhārabhāvāt | citrakṛtyavat | [p.103] upastambhahetur anucchedahetutvāt | evam eṣāṃ janamavikārādhārasthitivṛddhihetutvam ākhyātaṃ bhavati | 
		tridhā bhautikam anyonyaṃ (65c) 
hetusahabhūsabhāgavipākahetubhiḥ kāraṇahetur aviśeṣavarttitvāt na sarvadā gaṇyate | tatra sahabhūhetur anyonyaṃ cittānuparivartti kāyavākkarma | nānyad upādāyarūpam | sabhāgahetuḥ sarvaṃ pūrvotpannaṃ sabhāgasya | vipākahetur yasya kāya'vākkarmaṇaś cakṣurādayo vipākaḥ 
		bhūtānām ekadhaiva tat ||65d || 
bhūtānāṃ tu tad bhautikaṃ rūpaṃ vipākahetur eva, yasya kāyavākkarmaṇo bhūtāni vipākāḥ | 
	abhedena cittacaittāḥ samanantarapratyaya uktāḥ, niyamas tu noktaḥ, kasya cittasyānantaraṃ kasyotpattir iti | sa idānīṃ vaktavyaḥ | tatra tāvat samāsena dvādaśa cittāni | kim artham ity āha 
		kuśalākuśalaṃ kāme, nivṛtānivṛtaṃ manaḥ | (66ab) 
kāmadhātau catvāri cittāni | kuśalam akuśalaṃ nivṛtāvyākṛtam anivṛtāvyākṛtaṃ ca | 
		rūpārūpyeṣv akuśalād, anyatra (66cd) 
rūpadhātāv akuśalaṃ nāsti | trīṇi santi | evam ārūpyadhātau | ity etāni sāsravāṇi daśa cittāni bhavanti | 
		anāsravaṃ dvidhā ||66d' || 
śaikṣam aśaikṣaṃ ca | evam etāni dvādaśa cittāni bhavanti | tatra 
		kāme nava śubhāc cittāc, cittāni (67ab) 
anantaram iti paścād vakṣyate | kāmadhātau yat kuśalaṃ cittaṃ, tasmād anantaraṃ nava cittāny utpadyante | svabhūmikāni catvāri | rūpāvacare dve | samāpattikāle kuśalaṃ pratisandhikāle nivṛtam | ārūpyāvacaraṃ nivṛtam eva pratisandhikāle | ativiprakṛṣṭatvāt na kuśalam | ārūpyā hi kāmadhātoś catasṛbhir dūratābhir dūre | āśrayākārālambanaprtipakṣadūratābhiḥ | śaikṣam aśaikṣaṃ ceti | 
		aṣṭābhya eva tat | (67b') 
tatra punaḥ kāmāvacaraṃ kuśalaṃ cittam aṣṭābhyaḥ samanantaram utpadyate | svabhūmikebhyaś caturbhyo rūpāvacarābhyāṃ dvābhyām | kuśalāc ca vyutthānakāle | nivṛtāc ca kliṣṭasamāpattyutpīpditasyādharakuśalabhūmissaṃśrayaṇāt | śaikṣāśaikṣābhyāṃ ca vyutthānakāle | [p.104] 
		daśabhyo 'kuśalaṃ (67c) 
śaikṣāśaikṣe hitvā kāmadhātau hi pratisandhimukhataḥ sarvebhyo rūpārūpyacittebhyaḥ samanantaram akuśalaṃ cittam utpadyate | 
		tasmāc catvāri (67d) 
akuśalāc cittāt samanantaraṃ cittāny utpadyante svabhūmikāny eva | yathākuśalam uktaṃ kāmadhātau 
		nivṛtaṃ tathā ||67d' || 
daśabhya eva samanantaram | tasmāc ca punaś catvāry eva | 
		pañcabhyo 'nivṛtaṃ (68a) 
kāma iti varttate | anivṛtāvyākṛtyaṃ cittaṃ pañcabhyaḥ samanantaram utpadyate | svabhūmikebhyaś caturbhyo rūpāvacarāc ca kuśalān nirmāṇacittam | 
		tasmāt sapta cittāny anantaram | (68b) 
anivṛtāvyākṛtāt kāmāvacarāt svabhūmikāni catvāri | rūpāvacare dve | kuśalaṃ nirmāṇacittād anantaram | kliṣṭaṃ pratisandhikāle | ārūpyāvacaraṃ ca kliṣṭaṃ pratisandhikāla eva | 
		rūpe daśaikaṃ ca śubhāt (68c) 
rūpe dhātau yat kuśalaṃ cittaṃ tasmād anantaram ekādaśa cittāny utpadyante | ārūpyāvacaram anivṛtāvyākṛtaṃ varjayitvā | 
		navabhyas tadanantaram ||87d || 
rūpāvacaraṃ tu kuśalaṃ cittaṃ navabhyaḥ samanantaram utpadyate | kāmāvacaraṃ kliṣṭadvayam ārūpyāvacaraṃ cānivṛtāvyākṛtaṃ hitvā | 
		aṣṭābhyo nivṛtaṃ (88a) 
nivṛtāvyākṛtaṃ rūpāvacaraṃ cittam aṣṭabhya utpadyate | kāmāvacaraṃ kliṣṭadvayaṃ śaikṣāśaikṣe ca sthāpayitvā | 
		tasmāt ṣaṭ (69a'b) 
rūpāvacarān nivṛtāvyākṛtād anantaraṃ ṣaṭ | svabhūmikāni trīṇi kāmāvacarāṇi cānivṛtāvyākṛtaṃ muktvā | 
		tribhyo 'nivṛtaṃ punaḥ | (69b') 
rūpāvacaram anivṛtāvyākṛtaṃ tribhyaḥ svabhūmikebhya eva | [p.105] 
		tasmāt ṣaṭ (69c) 
svabhūmikāni trīṇi | kāmāvacare ca kliṣṭe | yathā rūpadahātāv anivṛtāvyākṛtam uktam 
		evam ārūpye, tasya nītiḥ (69c'd) 
tatas tad apy anivṛtāvyākṛtaṃ tribhya evotpadyate svabhūmikebhyaḥ | tasmād api ca ṣaḍ evotpadyante | svabhūmikāni trīṇi adharadhātukāni ca kliṣṭāni | 
		śubhāt punaḥ ||69d' || 
		nava cittāni (70a) 
ārūpyāvacarāt kuśalān nava cittāny utpadyante | kāmāvacaraṃ kuśalaṃ kāmarūpāvacare cānivṛtāvyākṛte hitvā | 
		tat ṣaṇṇāṃ (70a') 
ārūpyāvacaraṃ kuśalaṃ svebhyas tribhyo rūpāvacarāt kuśalāc chaikṣāśaikṣābhyaṃ | 
		nivṛtāt sapta (70b) 
ārūpyāvacarān nivṛtāt svabhūmikāni trīṇi rūpāvacaraṃ kuśalaṃ nivṛtaṃ ca kāmāvacraṃ kliṣṭadvayam | 
		tat tathā (70b') 
tad api saptabhya evotpadyate | kāmarūpāvacarāṇ kliṣṭāni śaikṣāśaikṣe ca hitvā | 
		caturbhyaḥ śaikṣam (70c) 
traidhātukebhyaḥ kuśalebhyaḥ śaikṣāc ca | 
		asmāt tu, pañca (70c'd) 
tāny eva catvāry aśaikṣaṃ ca | 
		aśaikṣaṃ tu pañcakāt ||70d' || 
ata evānantaroktāt | 
		tasmāc catvāri cittāni (71a) 
tasmāt punar aśaikṣāc cittāt samanantaraṃ catvāri cittāny utpadyante | traidhātukāni kuśalāny aśaikṣaṃ ca || samāptāni dvādaś cittāni || 
		punaḥ kriyante [p.106] 
		dvādaśaitāni viṃśatiḥ | (71b) 
kathaṃ kṛtvā | 
		prāyogikopapattyāptaṃ, śubhaṃ bhittvā triṣu dvidhā ||71cd || 
triṣu dhātuṣu kuśalaṃ cittaṃ dvidhā bhidyate | prāyogikaṃ copapattilābhikaṃ ca 
		vipākajairyāpatyikaśailpasthānikanairmitam | 
		caturdhāvyākṛtaṃ kāme (72abc) 
bhittveti varttate | kāmāvacaram anivṛtāvyākṛtaṃ caturdhā bhidyate | vipākajam airyāpathikaṃ śailpasthānikaṃ nirmāṇacittaṃ ca | 
		rūpe śilpavivarjitam ||72d || 
rūpadhātau tridhā bhidyate śailpasthānikaṃ varjayitvā | tatra śilpābhāvāt | evam etāni dvādaśa cittāni punar viṃśatir bhavanti | ṣoḍhā kuśalam anivṛtāvyākṛtaṃ ca saptadhā bhidyate | airyāpathikādīni cittānīryāpathādyābhāvād ārūpyadhātau na santi | rūpagandharasaspraṣṭavyāny eṣām ālambanam | śailpasthānikasya tu śabdo 'pi | etāni manovijñānāny eva pañca tu vijñānakāyā airyāpathikaśailpasthānikayoḥ prāyogikāḥ | airyāpathikābhinirhṛtaṃ manoijñānam asti dvādaśāyatanālambanam ity apare | 
	eṣāṃ punar viṃśateś cittānāṃ kasya katamat samanantaram | kāmāvacarāṇāṃ tāvad aṣṭānāṃ prāyogikānantaraṃ daśa cittāny utpadyante | svabhūmikāni saptānyatrābhijñāphalāt | rūpāvacaraṃ prāyogikaṃ śaikṣam aśaikṣaṃ ca | tat punar aṣṭacittānantaram | svebhyaḥ kuśalakliṣṭebhyaḥ rūpāvacarābhyāṃ prāyogikakliṣṭābhyāṃ śaikṣāśaikṣābhyāṃ ca | upapattipratilambhikānantaraṃ nava | svabhūmikāni saptānyatrābhijñāphalād rūpārūpyāvacare ca kliṣṭe | tat punar ekādaśānantaram | svebhyaḥ saptabhyaḥ pūrvavat rūpāvacarābhyāṃ prāyogikakliṣṭābhyāṃ śaikṣāśaikṣābhyaṃ ca | akuśalanivṛtāvyākṛtāanantaraṃ sapta | svāny eva pūrvavat | te punaś caturdaśacittānantaram | svebhyaḥ saptabhyaḥ rūpāvacarebhyaś caturbhyo 'nyatra prāyogikābhijñāphalābhyām | ārūpyāvacarebhyas tribhyo 'nyatra prāyogikāt | airyāpathika vipākajānantaram aṣṭau | svabhūmikāni ṣaḍ anyatra prāyogikābhijñāphalābhyāṃ rūpārūpyāvacare ca kliṣṭe | te punaḥ saptacittānantaraṃ svebhya eva pūrvavat | śailpasthānikānantaraṃ ṣaṭ | svāny evānyatra prāyogikābhijñāphalābhyām | tat punaḥ saptānantaraṃ svebhya evānyatrābhijñāphalāt | [p.107] abhijñāphalānantaraṃ dve | svaṃ cābhijñāphalam eva | rūpāvacaraṃ ca prāyogikam | tad apy asmād eva dvayāt | 
		rūpāvacarāṇām idānīṃ ṣaṇṇāṃ vakṣyāmaḥ (vakṣāmaḥ) | prāyogikānantaraṃ dvādaśa | kāmāvacare kuśale abhijñāphalaṃ (bhalaṃ) ca svāni ṣaṭ ārūpyāvacaraṃ ca prāyogikaṃ śaikṣam aśaikṣaṃ ca | tat punar daśacittānantaram | kāmāvacarābhyāṃ prāyogikābhijñāphalābhyāṃ svebhyaś caturbhyo 'nyatrairyāpathikavipākajābhyām ārūpyāvacarābhyāṃ prāyogikakliṣṭābhyāṃ śaikṣāśaikṣābhyāṃ ca | upapattipratilambhikānantaram aṣṭau | kāmāvacare kliṣṭe svāni pañcānyatrābhijñāphalāt ārūpyāvacaraṃ kliṣṭam | tat punaḥ pañcabhyaḥ svebhyaḥ evānyatrābhijñāphalāt | kliṣṭānantaraṃ nava | kāmāvacarāṇi catvāri kuśalakliṣṭāni svāni pañcānyatrābhijñāphalāt | tat punar ekādaśacittānantaram | kāmāvacarebhya utpattipratilambhikairyāpathikavipākajebhyaḥ svebhyaḥ pañcabhyo 'nyatrābhijñāphalāt ārūpyāvacarebhyas tribhyo 'nyatra prāyogikāt | airyāpathikānantaraṃ sapta | kāmāvacare kliṣṭe svāni catvāry anyatra prāyogikābhijñāphalābhyām ārūpyāvacaraṃ ca kliṣṭam | tat punaḥ pañcānantaraṃ svebhya evānyatrābhijñāphalāt | evaṃ vipākajaṃ vaktavyam | abhijñāphalānantaraṃ dve | sve eva prāyogikābhijñāphale tad apy ābhyām eva | 
		ārūpyāvacarāṇām idānīṃ caturṇāṃ vakṣyāmaḥ | prāyogikānantaraṃ sapta | rūpāvacaraṃ prāyogikaṃ svāni catvāri śaikṣam aśaikṣaṃ ca | tat punaḥ ṣaṭcittānantaram | rūpāvacarāt prāyogikāt svebhyas tribhyo 'nyatra vipākajāt śaikṣāśaikṣābhyāṃ ca | upapattiprātilambhikānantaraṃ sapta | svāni catvāry adharabhūmikāni ca kliṣṭāni | tat punaś caturbhya svebhya eva | kliṣṭānantaram aṣṭau | svāni catvāri rūpāvacare prāyogikakliṣṭe kāmāvacare kliṣṭe | tat punar daśāanantaram | svebhyaś caturbhyaḥ kāmāvacararūpāvacarebhyaś copapattiprātilambhikairyāpathikavipākajebhyaḥ | vipākajānantaraṃ ṣaṭ | svāni trīṇy anyatra prāyogikād adharāṇi trīṇi kliṣṭāni | tat punaś caturbhyaḥ svebhya eva | śaikṣānantaraṃ ṣaṭ | traidhātukāni prāyogikāṇi kāmāvacaram upapattipratilambhikaṃ śaikṣam aśaikṣaṃ ca | tat punaś caturbhyaḥ | prāyogikebhyaḥ tribhyaḥ śaikṣāc ca | aśaikṣānantaraṃ pañca | yathā śaikṣānantaraṃ śaikṣam ekaṃ hitvā | tat punaḥ pañcabhyaḥ | tribhyaḥ prāyogikebhyaḥ śaikṣāśaikṣābhyāṃ ceti | [p.108] 
	kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇaṃ prāyogikacittānantaraṃ vipākajairyāpathikaśailpastshānikāni cittāny utpadyante, na punar ebhyaḥ prāyogikam | īryāpathaśilpābhisaṃskaraṇapravṛttatvāt durlabhānabhisaṃskāravāhitvāc caittāni na prāyogikānukūlāni | niṣkramaṇacittaṃ tv anabhisaṃskāravāhīti yukto 'sya prāyogikacittānantaram utpādaḥ | evaṃ tarhi kliṣṭebhyo 'pi prāyogikaṃ notpadyate | viguṇatvāt | tathāpi kleśasamudācāraparikhinnasya tatparijñānād yuktaḥ prāyogikasaṃmukhībhāvaḥ | kāmāvacaram upapattipratilambhikaṃ paṭutvāt śaikṣssāśaikṣābhyāṃ rūpāvacāraprāyogikāc cānantaram utpadyate | anabhisaṃskāravāhitvāt tasmād etāni notpadyante | rūpāvacarakliṣṭānantaraṃ kāmāvacaram upapattipratilambhikam utpadyate | paṭutvāt | ārūpyāvacarakliṣṭānantaraṃ tu rūpāvacaram upapattipratilambhikaṃ notpadyate 'paṭutvād iti || 
	trayo manaskārāḥ | svalakṣaṇamanaskāraḥ | tadyathā rūpaṇālakṣaṇaṃ rūpam ityevamādi | smāmānyalakṣaṇamanaskāraḥ | ṣoḍaśākārasaṃprayuktaḥ | adhimuktimanaskāraḥ | aśubhāpramāṇārūpyavimokṣābhibhvāyatanakṛtsnāyatanādiṣu | trividhamanaskārānantaram āryamārgaṃ saṃmukhīkaroti tasmād api trividhaṃ manaskāram evaṃ sati yuktam idaṃ bhavati, aśubhasahagataṃ smṛtisaṃbodhyaṅgaṃ bhāvayatīti | sāmānyamanaskārānantaram evāryamārgaṃ saṃmukhīkaroti | tasmāt tu trividham ity apare | aśubhayā tu cittaṃ damayitvā sāmānyamanaskārānantaraṃ mārgaṃ saṃmukhīkaroti | ataḥ pāraṃparyam abhisaṃdhāyoktam, aśubhāsahagataṃ smṛtisaṃbodhyaṅgaṃ bhāvayatīti | āryamārgānantaram api sāmānyamanaskāram evety apare | syāt tāvad anāgamyāditribhūmisaṃniḥśrayeṇa niyāmāvakrāntau tanmārgānantaraṃ kāmāvacaraṃ sāmānyamanaskāraṃ saṃmukhīkuryād | atha dvitīyādidhyānasaṃniḥśrayeṇa niyāmāvakrāntau katham | na hi kāmāvacaraḥ śakyo 'tiviprakṛṣṭatvāt | na ca tadbhūmikaḥ pratilabdho 'nyatra nirvedhabhāgīyāt | na cāryo nirvedhabhāgīyaṃ punaḥ saṃmukhīkaroti | na hi prāptaphalasya tatprayogasaṃmukībhāvo yukta iti anyo 'py asya tajjātīyaḥ sāmānyamanaskāro bhāvanāṃ gacchati | tadyathā, sarvasaṃskārā anityāḥ, sarvadharmā anātmānaḥ śāntaṃ nirvāṇam iti tatsaṃmukhīkariṣyati | tad etan na varṇayanti | anāgamyaṃ niśrityārhattvaṃ prāpnuvataḥ tadbhūmikaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ vā vyutthānaṃ cittam (vyutthānacittam) | āciṃcanyāyatanaṃ niśritya tadbhūmikaṃ bhāvāgrikaṃ vā | śeṣāsu svabhūmikam eva | kāmadhātau trayo manaskārāḥ śritacintāmayopapattipratilambhikāḥ | bhāvanāmayo nāsti | rūpadhātau trayaḥ śrutabhāvanāmayopapattipratilambhikāḥ | cintāmayo nāsti | yadā cintayitum ārabhante tadaiṣāṃ samādhir evopatiṣṭhate | ārūpyadhātau bhāvanāmayopapattiprati[p.109]lambhikau | tatra pañcavidhamanaskārānantaram āryamārgasaṃmukhībhāvo 'nyatropapattipratilambhikebhyaḥ | prayogapratibaddhatvāt | mārgānantaraṃ tūpapattipratilambhikasyāpi kāmāvacarasya saṃmukhībhāvaḥ | paṭutvād iti || 
	yāni dvādaśa cittāni uktāny eṣāṃ katamasmiṃś citte katīnāṃ lābhaḥ | 
		kliṣṭe traidhātuke lābhaḥ, ṣaṇṇāṃ ṣaṇṇāṃ dvayoḥ (73ab) 
kāmāvacare kliṣṭe citte saṃmukhībhūte ṣaṇṇāṃ cittānāṃ lābhaḥ | tair asamanvāgatasya kāmāvacarasya kuśalasya | vicikitsayā kuśalamūlapratisaṃdhānād dhātupratyāgamanāc ca | akuśalanivṛtāvyākṛtayoḥ rūpāvacarasya ca kliṣṭasya dhātupratyāgamanāt parihaṇitaś ca ārūpyāvacarasya kliṣṭasya parihāṇitaḥ śaikṣasya ca | rūpāvacare 'pi kliṣṭe ṣaṇṇāṃ lābhaḥ | rūpāvcarāṇāṃ trayāṇāṃ kāmāvacarasya cānivṛtāvyākṛtasya dhātupratyāgamanāt | ārūpyāvacarasya kliṣṭasya śaikṣasya ca parihāṇitaḥ | ārūpyāvacare tu kliṣṭe dvayor lābhaḥ | parihāṇitas tasyaiva kliṣṭasya śaikṣasya ca | 
		śubhe | (73b') 
		trayāṇāṃ rūpaje (73c) 
rūpāvacare kuśale trayāṇāṃ cittānāṃ lābhas tasyaiva kuśalasya kāmarūpāvacarayoś cānivṛtāvyākṛtayoḥ | 
		śaikṣe, caturṇāṃ (73c'd) 
tasyaiva śaikṣasya kāmarūpāvacarayoś cānivṛtāvyākṛtayor ārūpyāvacarasya ca kuśalasya | āryamārgeṇa kāmarūpadhātuvairāgye | 
		tasya śeṣite ||73d' || 
śeṣaṃ kṛtaṃ śeṣitam | yatra citte lābho na vyākhyātas tatra tasyaiva lābho draṣṭavyo, nānyasya | anye punar abhedenāhuḥ | 
		kliṣṭe citte navānāṃ hi, lābhaḥ ity ucyate budhaiḥ | 
		ṣaṇṇāṃ tu kuśale citte, tasyaivāvyākṛte khalu || 
tatra saptānāṃ kuśale citta iti vaktavyam | kāmāvacarasya kuśalasya samyagdṛṣṭyā kuśalamūlapratisaṃdhānāt kāmarūpāvacarayor anivṛtāvyākṛtayor vairāgyataḥ rūpārūpyāvacarayoḥ [p.110] kuśalayos taqtastyasamādhilābhataḥ śaikṣāśaikṣasya ca niyāmāvakrāntyarhattvayoḥ śeṣamata eva vyākhyānād avadhāryam | saṃgrahaślokaḥ | 
		''upapattisamāpattivairyāgyaparihāṇiṣu | 
		kuśalapratisandhau ca cittalābho hy atadvataḥ'' ||iti | 
				||samāptaḥ pratyayaprasaṅgaḥ || 
			abhidharmakośabhāṣye indriyanirddeśo nāma 
				dvitīyaṃ kośasthānaṃ 
 					samāptam iti | 
					śrīmāvākasya 

 
KAPITEL III

[p.111] 
Abhidharmakośabhāṣye tṛtīyaṃ kośasthānam 
			oṃ namo buddhāya / 
idam idānīṃ vaktavyam / kāmarūpārūpyanaiyamyena cittādīnāṃ kṛto nirdeśaḥ / tatra (2) katame te kāmarūpārūpyadhātava ity ucyate (4) 
		narakapretatiryañco manuṣyāḥ ṣaḍ divaukasaḥ / (5) 
		kāmadhātuḥ (1abc) (6) 
catasro gatayaḥ / ṣaṭ ca devanikāyās tadyathā cāturmāhārājakāyikās trāyastriṃśā yāmās tuṣitā (7) nirmāṇaratayaḥ paranirmitavaśavarttinaś cety eṣa kāmadhātuḥ saha bhājanalokena / sa eṣa kati (8) sthānānīty āha (9) 
		sa narakadvīpabhedena viṃśatiḥ //1c'd // 
sthānānīti vākyaśeṣaḥ saṃbadhyate / aṣṭau mahānarakāḥ / saṃjīvaḥ kālasūtraḥ saṃghāto rauravo (11) mahārauravas tapanaḥ pratāpano 'vīciś ceti / catvāro dvīpāḥ / jambūdvīpaḥ pūrvavideho 'varagodānīyaḥ (12) uttarakuruś ca / ṣaṭ cānantaroktā devanikāyāḥ tiryañcaḥ pretāś ca ity etāni viṃśatiḥ sthānāni / (13) kāmadhātuḥ / paranirmitavaśavarttibhyo yāvad avīciḥ / sabhājanagrahaṇena tu yāvad vāyumaṇḍalam / (14) etasmāc ca kāmadhātoḥ (15) 
		ūrdhvaṃ saptadaśasthāno rūpadhātuḥ (2ab) (16) 
katham ity āha (17) 
		pṛthak pṛthak / (18) 
		dhyānaṃ tribhūmikaṃ tatra (2b'c) (19) 
prathamadvitīyadhyānāni pratyekaṃ tribhūmikāni / (20) 
		caturthaṃ tv aṣṭabhūmikam //2d // (21) 
tatra prathamadhyānaṃ brahmakāyikā brahmapurohitāḥ mahābrahmāṇaḥ /dvitīyaṃ parittābhā (22) apramāṇābhā ābhāsvarāḥ / tṛtīyaṃ parīttaśubhā apramāṇaśubhāḥ śubhakṛtsnāḥ / caturtham anabhrakāḥ (23) puṇyaprasavā bṛhatphalā abṛhā atapāḥ sudṛśāḥ sudarśanā akaniṣṭhā ity etāni saptadaśa sthānāni (24) rūpadhātuḥ / saha tannivāsibhiḥ sattvaiḥ ṣoḍaśeti kāśmīrāḥ / brahmapurohiteṣv eva kila (25) sthānam utkṛṣṭataraṃ mahābrahmaṇaḥ parigaṇa ivābhinirvṛttam ekanāyakaṃ natu bhūmyantaram iti / [p.112] 
		ārūpyadhātur asthāhaḥ (3a) (2) 
na hi arūpiṇāṃ dharmāṇāṃ sthānam asti / atītānāgatāvijñāptyarūpiṇo hi dharmā adeśasthā iti (3) niyamaḥ / sa tu (4) 
		upapattyā caturvidhaḥ / (3b) (4) 
upapattibhedena caturvidha ārūpyadhātuḥ / yad uta ākāśānantyāayatanaṃ vijñānānantyāyatanam ākiṃ(6)canyāyatanaṃ naivasaṃjñāanāsaṃjñāayatanam iti / na tv eṣāṃ deśakṛtam auttarārdharyaṃ bhidyate / yatraiva hi (7) deśe tatsamāpattilābhinaś cyavante tatraivopapadyante iti / punaś ca tasmāc cyavamānānāṃ tatraivāntarā(8)bhavo 'bhinirvarttate / (9) 
	yathā rūpiṇāṃ sattvānāṃ rūpaṃ niśritya pravarttate cittasaṃtatir evam ārūpyeṣu kiṃ niśritya pravarttate / (10) 
		nikāyaṃ jīvitaṃ cātra niśritā cittasantatiḥ //3cd // (11) 
nikāyasabhāgaṃ jivitendriyaṃ ca niśrityety ābhidhārmikāḥ / rūpiṇām api (12) tarhi sattvānāṃ kimarthaṃ na tad eva dvayaṃ niśritya pravarttate cittasantatiḥ / durbalatvāt / tasyāḥ (13) kena b alavattvam / samāpattiviśeṣajatvāt / sā hi samāpattir vibhūtarūpasaṃjñā / tata eva (14) tarhi balavattvāt pravarttiṣyate kiṃ puinar niśrayeṇa / idaṃ cāpi vaktavyam / yathā rūpiṇāṃ sattvānāṃ (15) rūpaṃ niśritya pravarttate nikāyasabhāgo jīvitendriyaṃ ca evam arūpiṇāṃ sattvānāṃ kiṃ niśritya (16) pravarttate / tad eva dvayam anyonyam / rūpiṇām api tarhi kimarthaṃ na tad eva dvayam anyonyam / (17) durbalatvāt tayoḥ / tatredānīṃ kena balavattvam / samāpattiviśeṣajatvāt / tad etac cittasantatau samānaṃ (18) cittacaitteṣu vā / tasmān nāsty arūpiṇāṃ sattvānāṃ cittasantater anyonyaṃ niśraya iti sautrāntikāḥ / (19) api tu yasyāś cittasantater ākṣepahetur avītatṛṣṇo rūpe tasyāḥ saha rūpeṇa saṃbhavād rūpaṃ niśritya (20) pravṛttir yasyās tu hetur vītatrṣṇo rūpe tasyā anapekṣya rūpaṃ pravṛttiḥ / hetos tadvimukyhatvād iti / (21) 
	atha kasmād ete kāmarūpārūpyadhātava ity ucyante / svalakṣaṇadhāraṇād dhātuḥ / kāmapratisaṃyukto dhātuḥ (22) kāmadhātuḥ / rūpapratisaṃyukto dhātū rūpadhātur madhyapadalopād vajrabālakavat (23) maricapānakavac ca / nātra rūpam astīty arūpaḥ / arūpasya bhāva ārūpyam / rūpaṇīyo vā rūpyaḥ / (24) na rūpyo 'rūpyas tadbhāva ārūpyam / tatpratisaṃyukto dhātur ārūpyadhātuḥ / kāmāṇāṃ dhātuḥ kāmadhātuḥ [p.113] kāmān yo dadhāti / evaṃ rūpārūpyadhātū veditavyau / ko 'yaṃ kāmo nāma / samāsataḥ (2) kavaḍīkārāhāramaithunopasaṃhito rāgaḥ / (3) 
	na te kāmā yāni citrāṇi loke, saṃkalparāgaḥ puruṣasya kāmaḥ / (4) 
	tiṣṭhanti citrāṇi tathaiva loke, athātra dhīrā vinayanti kāmam iti // (5) 
gāthābhidhānāt (Pradhan:dhānān) / ājīvaka (Pradhan:ajīvaka) āryaśāriputraṃ pratyāha (6) 
	na te kāmā yāni citrāṇi loke, saṃkalparāgaṃ vadasīha kāmam / (7) 
	bhikṣur bhaviṣyaty api kāmabhogī, saṃkalpayan so 'kuśalān vitarkān // (8) 
āryaśāriputra āha (9) 
	te cet kāmā yāni citrāṇi loke, saṃkalparāgo yadi te na kāmaḥ / (10) 
	śāstāpi te bhavitā kāmabhogī, dṛṣṭvaiva rūpāṇi manoramāni // (11) 
	kiṃ punar ye kecana dharmāḥ kāmarūpārūpyadhātuṣu samudācaranti, sarve te kāmarūpārūpyapratisaṃyuktāḥ / (12) nety āha / kiṃ tarhi / yeṣu kāmarūpārūpyarāgā anuśerate / ke punar amī kāmarūpārūpyarāgaḥ / (13) ye kāmarūpārūpyadhātuṣv anuśerate / idam idānīṃ tad aśvabandhīyam / kasyāyam (14) aśvabandho yasyāyam aśvaḥ / kasyāyam aśvaḥ / yasyāyam aśvab andhaḥ / ity ubhayam api na jñāyate / (15) nedam aśvabandhīyam / kṛtanirdeśāni hi sthānāni kāmadhātau / teṣv avītarāgasya yo rāgaḥ, sa (16) kāmarāgaḥ / yatrānuśete, so 'pi dharmaḥ kāmapratisaṃyuktaḥ / evaṃ rūpārūpyarāgāv adhovītarāgasya (17) yathāyogaṃ veditavyau / asamāhitabhūmiko vā rāgaḥ kāmarāgaḥ / dhyānārūpyeṣv rāgo rūpārūpyarāgaḥ / (18) nirmāṇacitte kathaṃ kāmarāgaḥ / śrutvā parihāya ca tadāsvādanāt / nirmāṇavaśena vā nirmāyaka(19)citto 'pi rāgaḥ / gandharasanirmāṇād vā / tasya kāmāvacaratvam / rūpāvacareṇa tayor anirmāṇāt / kiṃ (20) punar ekam eva traidhātukam / traidhātukānām anto nāsti / yāvad ākāśaṃ tāvanto dhātavaḥ / ata eva ca (21) nāsty apūrvasattvaprādurbhāvaḥ / pratibuddhotpādaṃ cāsaṃkhyeyasattvaparinirvāṇe 'pi nāsti sattvānāṃ parikṣaya (22) ākāśavat / (23) 
	katham avasthānaṃ lokadhātūnām / tiryaksūtra uktaṃ, tadyathā: īṣādhāre deve varṣati nāsti (24) vīcir vā antarikā vā antarīkṣād vāridhārāṇāṃ prapatantīnām / evaṃ pūrvasyāṃ diśi nāsti (25) vīcir vā antarikā vā lokadhātūnāṃ saṃvarttamānānāṃ vivarttamānānāṃ ca / (26) yathā pūrvasyāṃ diśi evaṃ dakṣiṇasyāṃ paścimāyām uttarasyām iti / natūktam ūrdhvam adhaś ceti / [p.114] ūrdhvam apy adho 'pīty apare / nikārāntarapāṭhād / akaniṣṭhād ūrdhvaṃ punaḥ kāmadhātuḥ / kāmadhātoś (2) cādhaḥ punar akaniṣṭhāḥ / yaś caikasmāt kāmadhātor vīitarāgaḥ, sa sarvebhyaḥ / evaṃ rūpārūpyebhyaḥ / yaś ca (3) prathamadhyānasaṃniśrayād dhi utpādayati, sa yatra lokadhātau jāta utpādayati tatratyam eva brahmalokam (4) upagacchati nānyam / ya ete trayo dhātava uktāḥ (5) 
		narakādisvanāmoktā, gatayaḥ pañca teṣu (4ab) (6) 
narakās tiryañcaḥ pretā devā manuṣyā iti / svair eva namābhis teṣu pañca gatayaḥ proktāḥ / kāmadhātau (7) catasro gatayaḥ pañcamyāś ca pradeśaḥ / rūpārūpyadhātvor ekasya devagateḥ pradeśaḥ / kiṃ punar gatinirmuktāḥ (8) santi dhātavo yata etāḥ dhatuṣv ity ucyante / santi ( /) kuśalacliṣṭabhājanāantarābhavasvabhāvā api (9) dhātavaḥ / yās tu pañca gatayaḥ (10) 
		tāḥ (4b') (11) 
		akliṣṭāvyākṛtā eva, sattvākhyā nāntarābhavaḥ //4cd // (12) 
anivṛtāvyākṛtā eva gatayaḥ / anyathā hi gatisaṃbhedaḥ syāt / sattvākyā eva ca na cāntarā(13)bhavasvabhāvāḥ / prajñaptiṣūktaṃ: catasṛbhir yognibhiḥ pañca gatayaḥ saṃgṛhītā (14) na tu pañcabhir gatibhiś catasro yonayaḥ / kim asaṃgṛhītam / antarābhava iti / dharmaskandhe 'pi (15) coktam: cakṣurdhātuḥ katamaḥ / catvāri mahābhūtāny upādāya, yo rūpaprasādaś cakṣur indriyaṃ (16) cakṣurāyatanaṃ cakṣurdhātur nārakas tairyagyonikaḥ paitṛviṣayiko devyo mānuṣyako bhāvanāmayo 'ntarā(17)bhavikaś ceti / sūtre 'pi ca bahiṣkṛto 'ntarābhavo gatibhyaḥ / kasmin sūtre / sapta bhavā (18) narakabhavas tiryagbhavaḥ pretabhavo devabhavo manuṣyabhavaḥ karmabhavo 'ntarābhava iti / atra hi pañca gatayaḥ (20) tābhyo bahiṣkaraṇāt / kāśmīrāś ca sūtraṃ paṭhanti / sthaviraśāriputreṇoktaṃ: nārakāṇām ā(21)yuṣmann āsravāṇāṃ saṃmukhībhāvān narakavedanīyāni karmāṇi karoty upacinoti / teṣām āyuṣman (22) kāyavāṅmanovaṅkānāṃ kāyavāṅmanodoṣakaṣāyāṇāṃ narakeṣu rūpaṃ saṃjñā vedanā saṃskāro vijñānaṃ (23) vipāko vipacyate / nirvṛtte vipāke nāraka iti saṃkhyāṃ gacchati / tatrāyuṣman nārako (24) nopalabhyate 'nyatra tebhyo dharmebhya iti / ato 'py anivṛtāvyākṛtā eva gatayaḥ / prakaraṇagranthas tarhi [p.115] parihāryo gatiṣu sarve 'nuśayā anuśerata iti / pratisaṃdhicittāni hi (2) gatīnāṃ pañcaprakārāṇi santy ataḥ sapraveśagatigrahaṇād adoṣa eva / grāmagrahaṇe grāmopacāragrahaṇavat / (3) kuśalakliṣṭā apīty apare / yat tūktaṃ karmabhavasya tābhyo bahiṣkaraṇād iti / nāvaśyaṃ pṛthagvacanād bahiṣkṛto bhavati / tadyathā pañcasu kaṣāyeṣu kleśadṛṣṭikaṣāyau pṛthag uktau / na ca (5) dṛṣṭayo kleśāḥ / evaṃ karmabhavo 'pi gatiś ca syāt / pṛthak cāsya vacanaṃ syāt gatihetujñāpanārtham / (6) antarābhave 'py eṣa prasaṅgaḥ / nāyoggāt / gacchanti tām iti gatiḥ / na cāntarābhavo gantavyaś (7) cyutideśa evotpādanāt / ārūpyā api gatir na bhaviṣyanti / cyutideśa evotpādāt / (8) evaṃ tarhy entarābhavatvād evāntarābhavo na gatir gatyantarālatvāt / yadi hi gatiḥ syād antarābhava (9) ity eva na syāt / yat tarhi sthaviraśāriputreṇoktaṃ nirvṛtte vipāke nāraka iti saṃkhyāṃ (10) gacchatīti / nirvṛtte vipāka ity uktaṃ na tu vipāka eveti / yat tarhy uktaṃ tatrāyuṣman nārako nopalabhyate (11) 'nyatra tebhyo dharmebhya iti / gatigāminaḥ pudgaladravyasya pratiṣedhaṃ karoti nānyatra skandhebhya upa(12)labhyate nāraka iti na tu skandhāntarapratiṣedham / avyākṛtā eva tu gatayo varṇyante (13) vaibhāṣikaiḥ / tāś ca vipākasvabhāvā evety eke / aupacayikasvabhāvā apīty apare / (14) 
	atraiva pañcagatike dhātutraye yathākramaṃ veditavyāḥ (15) 
		nānātvakāyasaṃjñāś ca, nānākāyaikasaṃjñinaḥ (16)

sūte uktaṃ rūpiṇaḥ santi sattvā nānātvakāyā nānātvasaṃjñinas tadyathā manuṣyās tadekatyāś ca devāḥ / (19) iyaṃ prathamā vijñānasthitiḥ / katame punas te tadekatyā devāḥ / kāmāvacarāḥ prathamadhyānabhūmikāś ca (20) prathamābhinirvṛttavarjyāḥ / nānātvena kāya eṣām iti nānātvakāyāḥ / anekavarṇaliṅgasaṃsthānatvāt / (22) rūpiṇaḥ santi (sattvā) nānātvakāyā ekatvasaṃjñinas tadyathā devā brahmakāyikā ye tatprathamābhinirvṛttāḥ / (23) iyaṃ dvitīyā vijñānasthitiḥ / te hi prathamābhinirvṛttāḥ sarva ekasaṃjñino bhavanti anena vayaṃ (24) brahmaṇā sṛṣṭā iti / brahmaṇo 'py evaṃ bhavati mayaite sṛṭā ity abhinnakāraṇasaṃjñānād ekatvasaṃjñinaḥ / (25) anyathaiva tu mahābrahmaṇa ārohapariṇāha ākṛtivigraho vāgbhāṣā cīvaradhāraṇaṃ ca (26) anyathā tatparṣad iti nānātvakāyāḥ / yad idam uktaṃ sūtre teṣām evaṃ bhavati iyaṃ vayaṃ sattvam adrākṣma [p.116] dīrghāyuṣaṃ dīrgham adhvānaṃ tiṣṭhantaṃ yāvad aho batānye 'pi sattvā ihopapadyeran mama sabhāgatāyām iti / (2) asya ca sattvasyaivaṃ cetasaḥ praṇidhir vayaṃ cehopapannā iti / kathaṃ tam adrākṣuḥ / ābhāsvarasthā (3) ity eke / tato hi te pracyutāḥ katham idānīm alabddhāyāṃ dvitīyadhyānasamāpattau tadbhūmikaṃ (4) pūrvanivāsam asmārṣuḥ labdhāyāṃ vā kathaṃ mahābrahmālambanāṃ śilavrataparāmarśadṛṣṭiṃ niviṣṭāḥ / (5) antarābhavasthā adrākṣur ity apare / tatrāpi na dīrgham adhvānam avasthātuṃ saṃbhavaḥ pratibandhābhāvād iti / (6) kathaṃ teṣām evaṃ bhavati sma imaṃ vayaṃ sattvam adrākṣma dīrghāyuṣaṃ dīrgham adhvānaṃ tiṣṭantam iti / (7) tasmāt tatrasthā eva te tasya pūrvavṛttantaṃ samanusmaranta eva dīrgham adhvānaṃ tiṣṭhantaṃ dṛṣṭavanto dṛṣṭvā ca (8) paścād adrākṣmety eṣāṃ babhūva / rūpiṇaḥ santi sattvā ekatvakāyā nānātvasaṃjñinas tadyathā devā (9) ābhāsvarāḥ / iyaṃ tṛtīyā vijñānasthitiḥ / atra punaḥ paryantagrahaṇāt sakaladvitīyadhyānagrahaṇaṃ (10) veditavyam / anyathā hi parīttābhā apramāṇābhāś ca kasyāṃ ijñānasthitau (11) vyavasthāpyeran / tatrābhinnavarṇaliṅgasaṃsthānatvād ekatvakāyāḥ / sukhasaṃvinitvād aduḥkhāsukhasaṃvinitvāc ca (12) nānātvasaṃjñinaḥ / te kila maulyāṃ bhūmau saumanasyendriyaparikhinnāḥ sāmantakād upekṣendriyaṃ (13) saṃmukhībhavanti / sāmantake copekṣendriyaparikhinnā punar maulyā bhūmeḥ saumanasyendriyaṃ (14) saṃmukhīkurvanti / yathā kāmaratiparikhinnā īśvarā dharmaratiṃ pratyanubhavanti dharmaratiparikhinnāḥ (15) kāmaratim iti / nanu ca śubhakṛtsneṣv apy eṣa prasaṅgaḥ / na teṣāṃ tena sukhenāsti parikhedaḥ / kiṃ (16) kāraṇam / śāntaṃ hi tatsukham aśāntaṃ ca saumanasyaṃ cetasa utplāvakatvād iti sautrāntikā (17) vyācakṣate / sūtra uktaṃ yathā te nānātvasaṃjñinaḥ / tatra ye sattvā ābhāsvare devanikāye 'ciro(18)papannā bhavanti, naiva saṃvarttanīkuśalā na vivarttanīikuśalā asya lokasya te tām arciṣaṃ dṛṣṭvā bhītāḥ (19) santa udvijante saṃvegam āpadyante / sahaivaiṣārciḥ śūnyaṃ brāhmaṃ vināmaṃ dagdhvā 'rvāg āgamiṣyatīti / (20) tatra ye sattvā ābhāsvare devanikāye ciroppapannāḥ saṃvarttanīikuśalā vivarttanīkuśalāś cāsya lokasya (21) te tān sattvān bhītān āśvasayanti / mā bhaiṣṭa mārṣāḥ mā bhaiṣṭa mārṣāḥ / pūrva(22)m apy eṣārciḥ śūnyaṃ brāhmaṃ vimānaṃ dagdhvāatraivāntarhiteti / ato 'rciṣa āgamavyapagamasaṃjñitvāt (23) bhītā bhītasaṃjñitvāc ca te nānātvasaṃjñino na sukhāduḥkhāsukhasaṃjñitvād iti / rūpiṇaḥ santi sattvā (24) ekatvakāyā ekatvasaṃjñinas tadyathā devāḥ śubhakṛtsnāḥ / iyaṃ caturthī vijñānasthitiḥ / tatrābhinna[p.117]varṇasaṃsthānaliṅgatvād ekatvakāyāḥ / ekatvasaṃjñinaḥ sukahsaṃjñitvāt / tatra prathame dhyāne kliṣṭayā (2) saṃjñayā ekatvasaṃjñinaḥ / dvitīye kuśalayā saṃjñayā nānātvasaṃjñinas tṛtīye vipākajā saṃjñayā (3) ekatvasaṃjñinaḥ / ārūpyās trayo yathāsūtram ity etāḥ sapta vijñānasthitayaḥ / kāatra vijñānasthitiḥ / (4) tatpratisaṃyuktāḥ pañcaskandhāś catvāraś ca yathāyogam / śeṣaṃ kasmān na vijñānasthitiḥ / yasmāt (5) 
		śeṣaṃ tatparibhedavat / (6b) (6) 
kiṃ punaḥ śeṣam / durgatayaś caturthaṃ dhyānaṃ bhavāgraṃ ca / atra hi vijñānaparibhedāḥ santy ata eva (7) na vijñānasthitiḥ / kaḥ punaḥ paribhedaḥ yena vijñānaṃ paribhidyate / tatrāpāyeṣu duḥkhā vedanā paribheda (8) upaghātikatvāt / caturthe dhyāne āsaṃjñikam asaṃjñisamāpattiś ca / bhavāgre nirodhasamāpattiś citta(9)sāṃtaticchedāt / punar āha / yatrehasthānāṃ gautukāmatā tatrasthānāṃ cāvyuccalitukāmatāasau (10) vijñānasthitir uktā / apāyeṣu cobhayaṃ nāsti / caturthe dhyāne sattvā (11) uccalitamānasāḥ pṛthagjanā āsaṃjñikaṃ praveṣṭukāmā āryāḥ śuddhāvāsān bhavāgrān apaṭupracāratvān na (12) vijñānasthitir iti / etāś ca sapta vijñānasthitayo yathoktāḥ / (13)

nānyatra bhūmau / kiṃ kāraṇam / pratiṣṭhā hi sthitiḥ / na ca visabhāgabhūmikeṣu skandheṣu (25) vijñānaṃ tṛṣṇāvaśāt pratitiṣṭhatīti / kasmān na vijñānaṃ vijñānasthitir ucyate / yathā saptasu (26) vijñānasthitiṣu pañca skandhā iti / sthātuḥ parihāreṇa sthitividhānāt / na hi sthātaiva [p.118] sthitir ucyate / yathā na rājaiva rājāsanam iti / yāmś ca dharmān abhiruhya vijñānaṃ vāhayati naunāvikanyāyena (2) te dharmā vijñānasthitaya uktāḥ / na tu vijñānaṃ vijñānam evāruhya vāhayaty ato (3) noktam iti vaibhāṣikāḥ / yat tarhi sūtra uktaṃ vijñāne āhāre asti nandī asti rāga iti / (4) yatrāsti nandī asti rāgaḥ pratiṣṭhitaṃ tatra vijñānam adhirūḍham iti / tat katham / sapta ca (5) vijñānasthitayaḥ pañcaskandhasaṃgṛhītā iti tat katham / evaṃ tarhy abhedenopapattyāyatanasaṃgṛhīteṣu (6) skandheṣu sābhirāmāyāṃ vijñānapravṛttau vijñānaṃ vijñānasthitiḥ / pratyekaṃ tu yathā rūpādayo (7) vijñānasya saṃkleśāya bhavanti / tasmāc catasṛṣu vijñānasthitiṣu (8)

aṇḍajā yonir jarāyujā saṃsvedajā upapādukā yoniḥ / yonir nāma jātiḥ / yuvanty asyāṃ (23) sattvā miśrībhavanti prasavasāmyād iti yoniḥ / aṇḍajā yoniḥ katamā / ye sattvā aṇḍebhyo (24) jāyante / tadyathā haṃsakroñcacakravākamayūraśūkaśārikādayaḥ / jarāyujā yoniḥ katamā / ye sattvā (25) jarāyor jāyante / tadyathā hastyaśvagomahiṣakharavarāhādayaḥ / saṃsvedajā yoniḥ katamā / (26) ye sattvā bhūtasaṃsvedāj jāyante / tadyathā kṛmikīṭapataṅgamaśakādayaḥ / upapādukā yoniḥ katamā / [p.119] ye sattvā avikalā ahīnendriyā sarvāṅgapratyaṅgopetāḥ sakṛdupajāyante / ata eva upapādane (2) sādhukāritvād upapādukā ity ucyante / tadyathā devanārakāntarābhavikādayaḥ / (3) 
	atha kasyāṃ gatau kati yonayaḥ saṃvidyanta ity āha (4)

manuṣyāś caturvidhāḥ aṇḍajās tāvad yathā kroñcīniryātau śailopaśailau sthavirau (6) mṛgāramātuś ca dvātriṃśat putrāḥ pañcālarājasya ca pañca putraśatāni / jarāyujā yathedānīm / (7) saṃsvedajās tadyathā māndhātṛcārūpacārukapotamālinyāmrapālyādayaḥ / upapādukāḥ punaḥ prāthamakalpikāḥ / evaṃ tiryañco 'pi caturvidhāḥ / trividhā dṛśyanta eva / upapādukās tu (9) nāgasuparṇiprabhṛtayaḥ / (10)

	katamā yoniḥ sarvasādhvī / upapādukā / atha kimarthaṃ caramabhaviko bodhisattvaḥ prāptopapattivaśitve 'pi jarāyujāṃ yoniṃ bhajate / evaṃ hi kriyamāṇe mahāntam arthaṃ paśyati / (19) jñātisaṃbandhena mahataḥ śākyavaṃśasya dharmo 'vataraṇārthaṃ cakravarttivaṃśyo 'yam iti cānyeṣāṃ bahumānādarāvarjanārthaṃ (20) manuṣyabhūtā api caināṃ siddhiṃ gacchantīti vineyānām utsāhārtham / itarathā (21) hy aprajñāyamānakulagotraḥ ko 'py ayaṃ māyāpuruṣa ity evaṃ parikalpayeyur devaḥ piśāca iti vā (22) yathāanyatīrthyā apabhāṣante kalpaśatasyātyayād evaṃ vidho māyāvī loke (23) prādurbhūya māyayā lokaṃ bhakṣayatīti / apare tv āhuḥ / śarīradhātūnām avasthāpanārthaṃ yeṣu (24) manuṣyā anye ca prajāṃ vidhāya sahasraśaḥ svargaṃ ca prāptā apavargaṃ ceti upapādukānāṃ hi sattvānāṃ (25) bāhyabījabhāvān mṛtānāṃ kāyo nāvatiṣṭhate niśānta iva tailapradyoto 'ntardhīyate / ādhiṣṭhānikīm ṛddhiṃ (26) bhagavata icchatāṃ na yukta eṣa parihāraḥ / praśnāt praśnāntaram upajāyate / yady apy upapādukānāṃ [p.120] kāyanidhanaṃ na prajñāyate katham uktaṃ sūtre upapādukaḥ suparṇī upapādukaṃ nāgam uddharati bhakṣārtham iti / (2) noktaṃ bhakṣayatīty api tūddharati bhakṣārtham ajñatvād ity adoṣaḥ / bhakṣayati vā yāvan na mṛto bhavati na (3) punar mṛtasyāsya tṛpyatīti / (4)

upagato (upapattigato) hy upapanna ity ucyate / pader gatyarthatvāt / na cāyaṃ gamyadeśam upagato 'ntarābhavas tasmān (12) nopapannaḥ / kaḥ punar asau deśo gantavyaḥ / yatrākṣiptasya vipākasyābyhivyaktiḥ samāptiś ca / (13) vicchinna evopapattibhavo maraṇabhavāt saṃbhavatīti nikāyāntarīyāḥ / tad etan neṣyate / kiṃ (14) kāraṇam / yuktitaś ca āgamataś ca / tatra tāvat yuktiṃ niśrityocyate (15)
