santānavarttināṃ hi dharmāṇām avicchedena deśāntareṣu prādurbhāvo dṛṣṭas tadyathā vrīhisantānasya / (17) tasmād asyāapi sattvasantānasyāvicchedena deśāntareṣu prādurbhāvo bhaviṣṇuḥ / vicchinno 'pi dṛṣṭaḥ (18) prādurbhāvas tadyathā ādarśādiṣu bimbāt pratibimbasya / evaṃ maraṇabhavād upapattibhavasya syāt / (19) 
		pratibimbam asiddhatvād asāmyāc cānidarśanam //11cd // (20) 
pratibimbaṃ nāmānyad evotpadyate varṇāntaram ity asiddham etat / siddhāv api ca satyām asāmyād anidarśanaṃ (21) bhavati / kathaṃ tāvad asiddham / (22) 
		sahaikatra dvayābhāvāt (12a) (23) 
tatraiva hi deśe ādarśarūpaṃ dṛśyate pratibimbaṃ ca / na caikatra deśe rūpadvayasyāsti sahabhāva (24) āśrayabhūtabhedāt / tathā digbhedavyavasthiter ekasmin vāpyambudeśe svābhimukhadeśasthānāṃ (25) rūpāṇām anyonyaṃ pratibimbakam upalabhyate na tv ekatra rūpe dvayoḥ paśyatoḥ (26) sahadarśanaṃ na bhavatīti na tatra rūpāntaropapattir yuktā / chāyātapayoś ca dvayoḥ sahaikatrabhāvo na [p.121] dṛṣṭaḥ / upalabhyate ca chāyāstha ādarśe sūryasya pratibimbakam iti na yukto 'sya tatra prādurbhāvaḥ / (2) atha vā sahaikatra dvayābhāvād iti katamasya dvayasya / ādarśatalasyendupratibimbakasya ca / (3) anyatraiva hi deśe ādarśatalaṃ bhavaty anyatraikvāntargataṃ candrapratibimbakaṃ dṛśyate kūpa ivodakam / (4) tac ca tatropapadyamānaṃ nānyatropalabhyate / ato nāsty eva tat kiṃcit / sāmagryās tu sa (5) tasyās tādrśaḥ prabhāvo yat tathā darśanaṃ bhavati / acintyo hi dharmāṇāṃ śaktibhedaḥ / evaṃ (6) tāvad asiddhatvāt / katham asāmyād anidarśanaṃ bhavati / (7) 
		asantānād (12b) (8) 
na hi bimbasya pratibimbakaṃ santānabhūtam ādarśasantānasaṃbaddhatvāt sahabhāvāc ca / yathā maraṇabhavasyopapattibhavaḥ (9) / santānaṃ ca praty avicchedena / deśāntareṣu prādurbhāva udāhṛto nāsantānam ity asāmyaṃ dṛṣṭāntasya (10) pratibimbasya ca (11) 
		dvayodayāt / (12b') (12) 
dvābhyāṃ hi kāraṇābhyāṃ pratibimbasyodayo bhavati / bimbāc cādarśāc ceti / yat (13) pradānaṃ kāraṇaṃ tad āśrityotpadyate / na caivam upapattibhavasyāpi dvābhyāṃ kāraṇābhyāṃ saṃbhavo (14) maraṇabhavāc cānyataś ca pradhānabhūtād ity ato 'py ayam asamāno dṛṣṭāntaḥ / na ca yuktam uktaṃ bāhyam eva (15) cetanaṃ śukraśoṇitaṃ pradhānakāraṇam iti / yatra cāndhakāṇa eva prādurbhāva upapādukānāṃ tatra kiṃ (16) parikalpyate / evaṃ tāvad yuktito neṣyate /na maraṇabhavād vicchinna upapattibhavaprādurbhāvaḥ / (17) tasmād asty evāntarābhavaḥ / (18) 
		kaṇṭhokteś cāsti (12c) (19) 
sūtra uktaṃ sapta bhavāḥ / narakabhavas tiryagbhavaḥ pretabhavo devabhavo manuṣyabhavaḥ karmabhavo 'ntarābhava (20) iti / naitat sūtraṃ tair āmnāyate / itas tarhi (21) 
		gandharvāt (12c') (22) 
trayāṇāṃ sthānānāṃ saṃmukhībhāvāt mātuḥ kukṣau garbhasyāvakrāntir bhavati / mātā kalyāpi bhavati (23) ṛtumatī ca / mātāpitarau raktau bhavataḥ saṃnipatitau ca / gandharvaś ca pratyupasthito bhavatī ti / (24) antarābhavaṃ hitvā ko 'nyo gandharvaḥ / etad api naiva tair āmnāyate / kathaṃ tarhi / skandhabhedaś ca (25) pratyupasthito bhavatīti / yady evam āśvalāyanasūtraṃ kathaṃ nīyate / jānanti bhavanto yo 'sau (26) gandharvaḥ pratyupasthitaḥ / kṣatriyo vāsau brāhmaṇo vā vaiśyo vā śūdro vā / yadi vā pūrvasyā diśi [p.122] āgato yadi vā dakṣiṇasyā paścimāyā uttarasyā iti / na hi skandhabhedasyāgamanaṃ (2) yujyate / athaitad api na paṭhyate / itas tarhi (3) 
		pañcokteḥ (12d) (4) 
pañcānāgāmina ity uktaṃ bhagavatā / antarāparinirvāyī upapadyaparinirvāyī anabhisaṃskāra(5)parinirvāyī sābhisaṃskāraparinirvāyī ūrdhvaṃsrotāś ceti / asaty antarābhave katham antarāparinirvāyī (6) nāma syāt / antarā nāma devāḥ santīty eke / upapadyāadayo 'pi hi nāma devā evaṃ sati (7) prasajyate / tasmān neyaṃ kalpanā sādhvī / itaś ca (8) 
		gatisūtrataḥ //12d' // (9) 
''sapta satpuruṣagataya'' ity atra sūtre / antarāparinirvāyiṇas trayaḥ uktāḥ / kāladeśaprakarṣabhedena / (10) tadyathā parīttaḥ sakalikāgnir abhinivarttamāna eva parinirvāyād evaṃ prathamaḥ / tadyathā 'yaḥprapāṭikā (11) utpatanty eva nirvāyād evaṃ dvitīyaḥ / tadyathā 'yaḥ prapāṭikā utplutya pṛthivyām apatitaiva (12) nirvāyād evaṃ tṛtīyaḥ / na caivam antarā nāma devās triprakārāḥ kāladeśaprakarṣabhinnāḥ santīti (13) kalpanaiveyaṃ kevalā // anye punar āhuḥ / āyuḥpramāṇāntare vā devasamīpāntare vā yaḥ kleśān (14) prajahāti so 'ntarāparinirvāyī / sa punar dhātugato vā parinirvāti (15) samjñāgato vā vitarkagato vā / tena trividho bhavatīti / prathamo vā rūpadhātau nikāyasabhagaparigrahaṃ (16) kṛtvā parinirvāti / dvitīyo devasamṛddhiṃ cānubhūya / tṛtīyo devānāṃ (17) dharmasaṃgītim anupraviśya / upapadyaparinirvāyī punaḥ prakarṣayuktāṃ saṃgītim anupraviśya parinirvāti (18) bhūyasā vā āyur upahatya nopapannamātra eveti ta ete sarve 'pi śakalikādidṛṣṭāntair na saṃbadhyante / (19) deśagativiśeṣābhāvāt / ārūpyeṣv api cāntarāparinirvāyī paṭhyetāyuḥpramāṇāntare parinirvāyāt (vāppāt) / (20) na ca paṭhyate / (21) 
		dhyānaiś catasro daśikā, ārūpyaiḥ saptikātrayam / (22) 
		saṃjñayā ṣaṭṭikāṃ kṛtvā, vargo bhavati samuditaḥ // (23) 
ity etasyām uddānagāthāyām / tasmād etad api sarvaṃ kalpanāmātram / athaitāny api sūtrāṇi (24)tair nāmnāyante / kim idānīṃ kurmo yac chāstā ca parinirvṛtaḥ śāsanaṃ cedam anāyakaṃ bahudhā bhinnaṃ [p.123] bhidyate cādyāapi yathecchaṃ granthataś cārthataś ca / yeṣāṃ tu tāvad ayam āgamaḥ pramāṇaṃ teṣām āgamato 'pi (2) siddho 'ntarābhavaḥ / yat tarhi sūtra uktam atha ca punar dūṣī māraḥ svaśarīreṇāvīcau mahānarake (3) prapatita iti / tat katham iti / sa hi jīvann eva nārakībhir (4) jvālābhir āliṅgitaḥ / kālaṃ kṛtvāntarābhavenāvīciṃ prāpta ity ayaṃ tatrābhiprāyaḥ / atyudīrṇaparipūrṇāni (5) hi karmāṇi kāyasya nikṣepaṃ na pratīkṣante / ato 'sya dṛṣṭadharmavedanīyaṃ karma pūrvaṃ (6) vipakvaṃ paścād upapadyavedanīyam iti / idam idānīṃ kathaṃ nīyate pañcānantaryāṇi karmāṇi yāni (7) kṛtvopacitya samanantaraṃ narakeṣūpapadyata iti / anyāṃ gatim agatvety abhiprāyaḥ / upapadyavedanīyatvaṃ (8) tatra karmaṇo dyotitam / atha yathārutaṃ kalpyate / pañcaiva kṛtvā naikam iti prāpnoti / (9) kriyānantaraṃ ca / na kālāntaraṃ jīvitvā ko vāntarābhavasyopapadyamānatvaṃ necchati / (10) antarābhavenaiva hy asau maraṇāntaraṃ narakeṣūpapadyate / abhimukhatvāt / na ca brūma upapanno (11) bhavatīti / iyaṃ tarhi gāthā kathaṃ nīyate (12) 
		upanītatayā jarāturaḥ saṃprāpto hi manāntikaṃ dvija / (13) 
		vāso 'pi hi nāsti te 'ntarā pātheyaṃ ca na vidyate taveti // (14) 
atrāpi manuṣyeṣva natarāvāso nāstīty abhiprāyaḥ / atha vāntarābhave py asya vāso nāsty upapateti(15)deśasaṃprāptipratigamanādhiṣṭhānād iti / ayam atrābhiprāyo nāyam abhiprāya (16) iti kuta evaitat / tulya eṣa bhavato 'py anuyogaḥ / tasmād ubhayasminn api pakṣe yathoktasūtrā(17)virodhān na bhavaty etad antarābhavasyābhāve jñāpakam / jñāpakaṃ hi nāmāgatikā gatir iti / (18) 
	atha kāṃ gatiṃ gamiṣyataḥ kimākṛtir āntarābhavo 'bhinivarttate / (19) 
		ekākṣepād asāv aiṣyatpūrvakālabhavākṛtiḥ / (13ab) (20) 
yenaiva karmaṇā gatir ākṣipyate tenaivāntarābhavas tatprāptaye / ato yāṃ gatiṃ gantā bhavati, tasyāṃ (21) gatau ya āgamiṣyatpūrvakālabhavas tasyaivāsyākṛtir bhavati / evaṃ tarhi śunīprabhṛtīnām ekasmin (22) kukṣau pāñcagatiko 'ntarābhavo 'bhinirvarttate iti nārako 'ntarābhavaḥ kukṣiṃ nirdahet / (23) pūrvakālabhave 'pi tāvan nārakā na nityaṃ prajvalitā bhavanty utsadeṣu bhramantaḥ kiṃ punar antarāb havikāḥ / (24) astu vā prajvalitḥ / sa tu yathā na draṣṭuṃ śakyate, tathā na spraṣṭum apy acchatvād ātmabhāvasyety acodyam etat / (25) antarābhavānām apy anyonyam kukṣāv asaṃśleṣāt karmapratibandhāc ca na dāhaḥ / pramāṇaṃ tu yathā pañcaṣaḍvarṣasya [p.124] dārakasya ( /) sa tu patvindriyo bhavati bodhisattvasya punar yathā saṃpūrṇayūnaḥ salakṣaṇānuvyañjanaś ca / ata (2) evāntarābhavasthena mātuḥ kukṣiṃ praviśatā koṭīśataś cāturdvīpikānām avabhāsitaḥ / (3) yat tarhi mātā bodhisattvasya svapne gajapotaṃ pānḍaraṃ kukṣiṃ praviśantam adrākṣīt / nimittamātraṃ (4) tat tiryagyoneś ciravyāvṛttatvāt / tadyathā kṛkī rājā daśa svapnān adrākṣīd iti / (5) 
		karikupāsaktṛcandanakalabhārāmās tathā kapere abhiṣekaḥ / (6) 
		aśucikapiḥ paṭakalahāv iti daśa dṛṣṭā nṛpeṇa kṛkiṇā svapnāḥ // (7) 
naiva cāntarābhavikaḥ kukṣiṃ bhittvā praviśaty api tu mātur yonidvāreṇa / ata eva yam amalayor yaḥ (8) paścāt prajāyate, sa jyāyān ucyate, yaḥ pūrvaṃ sa kanīyān iti / dharmasubhūtibhāṣyaṃ kathaṃ nīyate (9) 
		vāraṇa tvam upagamya pānḍaraṃ ṣaḍviṣāṇaruciraṃ catuṣkramam / (10) 
		mātṛgarbhaśayanaṃ viśeṣasaṃprajānan ṛṣir āśramaṃ yatheti // (11) 
naitad avaśyanetavyam / na hy etat sūtraṃ na vinayaḥ nābhidharmaḥ / kāvyam etat / kavīnāṃ ca kāvyaṃ (12) smāyojanatāṃ kecit bhāvāḥ samāropitā bacchanti / atha vā netavyam eva / yathāsya mātā (13) svapne taṃ praviśantam adrākṣīt tathā so 'pi gāthām akārṣīd iti / rūpāvacaro 'py antarābhavaḥ (14) saṃūrṇapramāṇaḥ savastraś ca prādurbhavati / apatrāpyotsadatvāt / bodhisattvasya savastraḥ śuklāyāś ca (15) bhikṣuṇyāḥ praṇidhāṇavaśād yāvantam eva pariveṣṭitā nirdagdhā / anyo nagnaḥ / kāma(16)dhātor anapatrāpyotsadatvāt / (17) 
	atha ko 'yaṃ pūrvakālabhavo nāma / (18) 
		sa punar maraṇāt pūrva upapattikṣaṇāt paraḥ //13cd // (19) 
bhavo hi nāmāviśeṣeṇa pañcopādānaskandhāḥ / sa eva caturdhā bhidyate / antarābhavo yathoktaḥ / (20) upapattibhavo gatiṣu pratisandhikṣaṇaḥ / tasmāt pareṇa maraṇakṣaṇaṃ paryudasyānyaḥ sarvo bhavaḥ / (21) pūrvakālabhavaḥ / caramakṣaṇo maraṇabhavo yata ūrdhvam antarābhavo bhavati, rūpiṣu cet sattveṣūpapadyate / (22) 
	sa cāyam antarābhavaḥ (23) 
		sajātiśuddhadivyākṣidrśyaḥ (14ab) (24) 
samānajātīyair evāntarābhavikair dṛśyate / yeṣām ca divyaṃ cakṣuḥ suviśuddham abhijñāmayaṃ ta enaṃ [p.125] paśyanti / upapatticakṣuṣā tu na dṛśyate / atyartham acchatvāt / devāntarābvhavikaḥ sarvān (2) paśyati / manuṣyapretatiryagnārakāntarābhavikāḥ pūrvaṃ pūrvam apāsyety apare / (3) 
		karmarddhivegavān / (14b') (4) 
ṛddhir ākāśagamanam / karmaṇā ṛddhiḥ karmarddhiḥ / tasyā vegaḥ karmarddhivegaḥ śīghratā / (5) so 'syāstīti karmarddhivegavān / yenāsau na śakyo buddhair api pratibanddhum / karmaṇo 'sya (6) balīyastvāt / (7) 
		sakalākṣaḥ (14c) (8) 
samagrapañcendriyaḥ / (9) 
		apratighavān (14c') (10) 
pratighātaḥ pratighaḥ / so 'syāstīti pratighavān / na pratighāan apratighavān / vajrādibhir (11) apy anivāryatvāt / tathā hi pradīptāyaḥpiṇḍabhede tanmadhyasaṃbhūtaḥ (12) krimir upalabdhaḥ śūryate / yasyāṃ ca gatau sa utpatsyamānas tasyāḥ sarvathā (13) 
		anivartyaḥ (14d) (14) 
na hi kadācin manuṣyāntarābhavo 'ntardhāya devāntarābhavo bhavaty anyo vā / niyatam anena yām eva (15) gatim adhikṛtyābhinirvṛttas tasyām evopapattavyam, nānyasyām iti / (16) 
	kiṃ punar antarābhavo 'pi kāmāvacaraḥ kavaḍīkārāhāraṃ bhuṅkte / om ity āha / na tv audārikam / (17) kiṃ tarhi / (18) 
		sa gandhabhuk //14d' // (19) 
ata eva gandharva ity ucyate / dhātūnām anekārthatvāt / hrasvatvaṃ śakandhukarkandhuvat / alpeśākhyas tu (20) durgandhāhāro maheśākhyaḥ sugandhāhāraḥ / kiyantaṃ kālam avatiṣṭhate / nāsti niyama iti bhadantaḥ / (21) yāvad upapattisāmagrīṃ na labhate na hi tasyāyuṣaḥ pṛthag evākṣepaḥ / ekanikāyasabhāgatvāt / itarathā (22) hi tasyāyuṣaḥ kṣayān maraṇabhavaḥ prasajyete / yady ā sumeroḥ sthalaṃ māṃsasyā syāt tat sarvaṃ varṣāsu [p.126] krimīṇāṃ pūryeta / kim idānīṃ tatpratīkṣā eva teṣām antarābhavā āsan kuto vā tadā tebhyo gatā (2) iti vaktavyam / naitad āgataṃ sūtre śāstre vā / evaṃ tu yujyate / gandharasābhigṛddhānām alpāyuṣāṃ (3) jantūnām anto nāsti / te taṃ gandhaṃ ghrātvā gandharasābhigṛddhāḥ kālaṃ (4) kurvantaḥ krimibhāvasaṃvartanīyaṃ karma prabodhya tayā tṛṣṇayā krimiṣūpapadyanta iti / atha vā (5) nūnaṃ tatpratyaypracura eva kāle tatsaṃvartanīyāni karmāṇi vipākābhinirvṛttau vṛttiṃ labhante nānyatra / (6) tathā hi cakravarttisaṃvarttanīye karmaṇi aśītivarṣasahasrāyuṣi prajāyāṃ bahutarāyuṣi vā cakravarttino (7) jāyante nānyasyām / ata eva coktaṃ bhagavatā: acintyaḥ sattvānāṃ karmavipāka iti / (8) saptāhaṃ tiṣṭhatīti bhadantavasumitraḥ / yadi tāvatā sāmagrīṃ na labhate tatraiva punaś cyutvā jāyante / (9) sapta saptāhānīty apare / alpaṃ kālam iti vaibhāṣikāḥ / sa hi saṃbhavaiṣitvāt saṃdhāvagatvā (10) sandhiṃ badhnātīti yathātvasamagrāḥ pratyayā bhavanti / niyataṃ cānena tasmin deśe tasyāṃ jātau (11) janitavyaṃ bhavati / tadā karmāṇy eva pratyayānāṃ sāmagrīm āvahanti / athāniyataṃ tato 'nyatra (12) deśe tasyāṃ jātau jāyate sadṛśyām ity apare / tadyathā gavām ūṣmasu maithunasya prācuryaṃ śaradi (13) śunāṃ ṛkṣāṇāṃ hemante vasante cāśvānām / gavayaśrgālakharatarakṣāṇāṃ punaḥ kālo nāstīti yenānyatra (14) kāle goṣūpapattavyaṃ sa gavayeṣūpapadyate yena śvasu sa śṛgāleṣu yenāśveṣu (15) sa gardabheṣu yena ṛkṣeṣu sa tarakṣeṣupapadyata iti / na tv asya nikāyasabhāgāntarābhavo nānyatra (16) nikāyasabhāge śakyam utpattum ekakarmākṣepād (kāmākṣepād) iti vaktavyam etat / (17) 
	sa khalv eṣa gatideśasaṃprāptyarthaṃ prādurbhūto 'ntarābhavaḥ (18) 
		viparyastamatir yāti gatideśaṃ riraṃsayā / (15ab) (19) 
sa hi karmaprabhāvasaṃbhūtena cakṣuṣā sudūrastho 'pi svam upapattideśaṃ prekṣate / tatrāsya mātāpitros tāṃ (20) vipratipattiṃ dṛṣṭvā puṃsaḥ sataḥ pauṃsno rāga utpadyate mātari, striyāḥ straiṇo rāga utpadyate pitari / (21) viparyayāt pratighaḥ / evaṃ paṭhyate prajñaptau: gandharvasya tasmin samaye dvayoś cittayor anyatarānyataracittaṃ (22) saṃmukhībhūtaṃ bhavaty anunayasahagataṃ vā pratighasahatagaṃ veti / sa tābhyāṃ viparyasto rautukāmatayā taṃ (23) deśam āśliṣya tām avasthām ātmany adhimucyate / tasmiṃś cāśucau garbhasthānasaṃprāpte jātaharṣo 'bhiniviśate / tato 'sya skandhā ghanībhavanty antarābhavaskandhāś cāntardhīyante ity upapanno bhavati / sa cet pumān bhavati (25) mātur dakṣiṇakukṣim āśritya pṛṣṭhābhimukha utkeṭukaḥ saṃbhavaty atha strī tato vāmakukṣim āśrityodarābhimukhī / [p.127] athedānīṃ napuṃsakaṃ tad yena rāgeṇāśliṣṭaṃ tathā tiṣṭhati / na cāsty antarābhavo (2) vyantaraḥ / sakalendriyatvāt / ataḥ strībhūtaḥ puruṣabhūto vānupraviśya yathāsthānaṃ (3) tiṣṭhati paścāt garbha āpyāyamāno napuṃsakaṃ bhavatīti / idaṃ vicāryate / kim asya śukraśoṇitamahābhūtāny evendriyāśrayabhāvam āpadyante karmavaśād āhosvit bhūtāntarāṇy eva karmabhir jāyante / (5) tāny upaśrityeti tāny evety eke / anindriyaṃ hi śukraśoṇitam antarābhavena sārdhaṃ nirudhyate (6) sendriyaṃ prādurbhavati / bījāṅkuranirodhotpādanyāyena yat tat kalalam ity ākhyāyate / evaṃ ca kṛtvedaṃ (7) sūtrapadaṃ sūtre sunītaṃ bhavati: mātāpitraśucikalalasaṃbhūtasyeti / tathā: dīrgharātraṃ yuṣmābhir (8) bhikṣavaḥ kaṭasiḥ saṃvarddhitā (savarddhitā) rudhirabindur upātta iti / bhūtāntarāṇy evety apare / tadyathā (9) parṇakrimeḥ / aśucisaṃniśrayotpattyabhisaṃbandhivacanāt tu kalalasya sūtrāvirodha iti / (10) evaṃ tāvad aṇḍajāṃ jarāyujāṃ ca yoniṃ pratipadyate / anyatra tu yathāyogaṃ vaktavyam ity āhuḥ / tatra (11) cāyaṃ yogo dṛśyate (12) 
		gandhasthānābhikāmo 'nyaḥ (15c) (13) 
saṃsvedajāṃ yoṇiṃ pratipadyamāna upapattisthānaṃ gandhābhilāṣāt gacchaty amedhyaṃ medhyaṃ vā yathākarma / upapādukāṃ (14) tu yoniṃ pratipadyamānaḥ sthānābhilāṣāt / kathaṃ narakeṣu sthānābhilāṣaḥ / (15) viparyastabuddhitvāt / sa hi śītavātavarṣābhiṣekair ātmānaṃ bādhyamānaṃ paśyati narakeṣu cāgniṃ (16) dīpyamānam / tatra uṣṇābhilāṣād dhāvati / punas taptavātātapāgnisaṃtāpair ātmānaṃ bādhyamānam (17) anupaśyan śaityaṃ ca narakeṣu śītābhilāṣād dhāvati / yadavasthas tadupapattissaṃvarttanīyaṃ karmākārṣīt (18) tadavastham (') ātmānaṃ tāṃś ca sattvān paśyan dhāvatīti pūrvācāryāḥ / tatra punar devāntarābhava ūrdhvaṃ (19) gacchaty āsanād ivotiṣṭhan manuṣyatiryakpretānāṃ manuṣyādivat / (20) 
		ūrdhvapādas tu nārakaḥ //15d // (21) 
	te vai patanti narakād ūrdhvapādā avāṅmukhāḥ / (22) 
	ṛṣīṇām abhivaktāraḥ saṃyatānāṃ tapasvinām iti (23) 
gāthābhidhānāt / 
	yad uktaṃ viparyastamatir yātīti / kim avaśyaṃ sarvo 'ntarābhavas tathā mātuḥ kukṣim (25) avakrāmati / nety āha / kiṃ tarhi / catasro garbhāvakrāntayaḥ sūtra uktāḥ / ktamāś catasraḥ / [p.128] 
		saṃprajānan viśaty ekaḥ (16a) (2) 
tiṣṭhati niṣkrāmati vāsaṃprajānan / (3) 
		tiṣṭhaty apy aparaḥ (16b) (4) 
saṃprajānann iti varttate / praviśaty apīty apiśabdāt / (5) 
		aparaḥ / (16b') (6) 
		niṣkrāmaty api (16c) (7) 
saṃprajānan praviśati tiṣṭhaty api / (8) 
		sarvāṇi mūḍho 'nyaḥ (16c'd) (9) 
kaścit punaḥ sarvāṇy evāsṃprajānan karoti praviśaty asaṃprajānan tiṣṭhati (10) niṣkrāmaty api / etāś catasro garbhāvacrāntayaḥ pratilomaṃ nirdiṣṭāḥ / ślokabandhānuguṇyataḥ / (11) 
		nityam aṇḍajaḥ . //16d' // (12) 
aṇḍajaḥ sattvo nityaṃ mūḍha eva sarvāṇi karoti / katham aṇḍāj jāto garbhaṃ praviśati / yo 'pi (13) janiṣyate so 'py aṇḍajaḥ / atha vā bhāvinyā pi saṃjñayā nirdeśāḥ kriyante / tadyathā saṃskṛtam (14) abhisaṃskarotīti sūtre odanaṃ pacatīti saktuṃ ninaṣṭīti loke / tasmān naiṣa doṣaḥ / (15) 
	kathaṃ punar asaṃprajānan mātuḥ kukṣiṃ praviśati yāvan niṣkrāmati kathaṃ vā saṃprajānan / alpeśākhyasya (16) tāvat sattvasya mātuḥ kukṣiṃ praviśataḥ evaṃ viparītau saṃjñādhimokṣau pravarttete / vāto vāti (17) devo varṣati / śītaṃ durdinaṃ mahato vā janakāyasya kolāhalañm hanta tṛṇagahaṇaṃ vā praviśāmi (18) vanagahanaṃ vā tṛṇakuṭīṃ vā vṛkṣamūlaṃ vā sarpāmi kuḍyamūlaṃ veti / tiṣṭhato 'py eṣu (19) tiṣṭhāmīti niṣkrāmato 'py ebhyo niryāmīti / maheśākhyasya tu sattvasyārāmaṃ vā praviśāmy udyānaṃ (20) vā prāsādaṃ vābhirohāmi kūṭāgāraṃ vā paryaṅkaṃ veti / tathā tiṣṭhāmi niryāmīti / evaṃ (21) tāvad asaṃprajānan praviśati yāvan niṣkrāmati / saṃprajānaṃs tu samyak prajānāti (22) mātuḥ kukṣiṃ praviśāmy atraiva tiṣṭhāmi ata eva niryāmīti / nāsya viparītau saṃjñāadhimokṣau (23) pravarttate / atra punar apadiśyate (24) 
		garbhāvakrāntayas tisraś, cakravarttisvayaṃbhuvām / (17ab) (25) 
cakravarttinaś ca svayaṃbhuvoś ca pratyekabuddhasaṃbuddhayoś ca / yathākramam ity ante vakṣyati / tatra prathamā (26) cakravarttinaḥ / sa hi praviśaty eva saṃprajānan na tiṣṭhati nāpi niṣkrāmati / pratyekabuddhas tiṣṭhaty api / (27) buddho niṣkramaty api / atrāpi bhāvinyā saṃjñayā nirdeśaḥ / [p.129] 
		karmajñānobhayeṣāṃ vā, viśadatvād yathākramam //17cd // 
viśadakarmaṇām udārapuṇyakriyāṇāṃ prathamā / viśadajñānānāṃ bāhuśrutyakṛtapravicayānāṃ dvitīyā / (3) viśadapuṇyakarmajñānānāṃ tṛtīyā / ta eva tv ete cakravarttyādaya evaṃbhūtā yujyante yathākramam / (4) śeṣāṇāṃ caturthīty siddhaṃ bhavati / (5) 
	atredānīṃ bāhyakā ātmavādaṃ parigṛhyotiṣṭhante / yadi sattvo lokāntaraṃ saṃcaratīti (6) pratijñāyate, siddha ātmā bhavatīti / sa eṣa pratiṣidyate (7) 
		nātmāsti (18a) (8) 
kīdṛśa ātmā ( /) ya imān nikṣipaty anyāṃś ca skandhān pratisaṃdadhātīti parikalpyate / sa tādṛśo (9) nāsty antarvyāpārapuruṣaḥ / evaṃ tūktaṃ bhagavatā: asti karmāsti vipākaḥ kārakas tu nopalabhyate (10) ya imāṃś ca skandhān nikṣipati, anyāṃś ca skandhān pratisaṃdadhāty anyatra dharmasaṃketāt / tatrāyaṃ dharma(11)saṃketo yad utāsmin satīdaṃ bhavatīti vistareṇa pratītyasamutpādaḥ / (12) kīdṛśas tarhy ātmā na pratiṣidhyate / (13) 
		skandhamātraṃ tu (18a') (14) 
yadi tu skandhamātram evātmeti upacaryate tasyāpratiṣedhaḥ / evaṃ tarhi skandhā eva lokāntaraṃ (15) saṃcarantīti prāptaṃ ( /) skandhamātraṃ tu nātra saṃcaratīti / (16) 
		kleśakarmābhisaṃskṛtam / (18b) (17) 
		antarābhavasaṃtatyā, kukṣim eti pradīpavat //18cd // (18) 
kṣaṇikā hi skandhās teṣāṃ saṃcarituṃ nāsti śaktiḥ / kleśais tu paribhāvitaṃ karmabhiś ca skandha'mātram (19) antarābhavasaṃjñikayā saṃtatyā mātuḥ kukṣim āyāti / tadyathā pradīpaḥ ( /) kṣaṇiko 'pi saṃtatyā (20) deśāntaram iti nāsty eṣa doṣaḥ / tasmāt siddham etad asaty apy ātmani kleśakarmābhisaṃskṛtaḥ skandhānāṃ (21) saṃtāno mātuḥ kukṣim āpadyata iti / sa punaḥ (22) 
		yathākṣepaṃ kramād vṛddhaḥ, santānaḥ kleśakarmabhiḥ / (23) 
		paralokaṃ punar yāti (19abc) (24) 
na hi sarvasya skandhasantānasyākṣepas tulyo bhavaty āyuṣyasya karmaṇo bhedāt / ato yasya (25) yānān ākṣepas tasya tāvatī vṛddhiḥ krameṇa bhavati / kena krameṇa / [p.130] 
		kalalaṃ prathamaṃ bhavati, kalalāj jāyate 'rbudaḥ / (2) 
		arbudāj jāyate peśī, peśīto jāyate ghanaḥ // (3) 
		ghanāt praśākhā jāyante, keśaromanakhādayaḥ / (4) 
		indriyāṇi ca rūpīṇi, vyañjanāny anupūrvaśaḥ // (5) 
ity āryāḥ / etāḥ pañca garbhāvasthāḥ kalalārbudapeśīghanapraśākhāvasthāḥ / (6) tasya khalu kālāntareṇa paripākaprāptasya garbhaśalyasyābhyantarāt mātuḥ kukṣau karmavipākajā (7) vāyavo vānti, ye taṃ garbhaśalyaṃ saṃparivarttya mātuḥ kāyāvakṣaradvārābhimukham avasthāpayanti / sa (8) drūrapuruṣapiṇḍa ivātimātraṃ sthānāt pracyuto duḥkhaṃ saṃparivarttyate / yadi punaḥ kadācin mātur āhāra(9)vihārakriyāapacāreṇa ca pūrvakarmāparādhena garbha eva vyāpādaṃ prāpnoti tata enaṃ tajjās striyaḥ' (10) kumārabhṛtyakā vā sukhoṣṇena sarpistailena supiṣṭena śālmalīkalkenānyena vā hastam adhyajya tīkṣṇaṃ (11) tanukaṃ cātra śastrakam upanibadhya tasmin varcaskūpa ivogradurgandhāndhakārasamalapalvale subahukrimi(12)kulasahasrāvāse nityasrāviṇi satatapratikriye śukuraśonṇitalasikāmalasaṃklinnaviklinnakvathitapicchile (13) paramabībhatsadarśane chidratanucarmāvacchādite pūrvakarmavipākaje mahati kāmanāḍīvraṇe hastaṃ (14) praveśyāṅgam aṅgaṃ nikṛtyādhyāharanti / sa cāpy aparaparyāyavedanīyena karmaṇā pūrvakeṇa kām api gatiṃ (15) nīyate / atha punaḥ svasti prajāyate, tata enaṃ mātā putrābhilāṣiṇī tatparikā (16) vā striyaḥ sadyojātakaṃ taruṇavraṇāyam ātmānaṃ śastrakṣārāyamāṇasaṃsparśābhyaṃ pāṇibhyāṃ (17) parigṛhya snāpayanti stanyena sarpiṣā vāpyāyanti audārikaṃ cāhāram āhartuṃ krameṇābhyāsayanti / (18) tasya vṛddher anvayād indriyāṇāṃ paripākān punar api kleśāḥ samudācaranti karmāṇi' copacayaṃ gacchanti / (19) sa taiḥ kāyasya bhedād antarābhavasaṃtatyā pūrvavat paralokaṃ punar yāti / (20) 
		ity anādi' bhavacakrakam //19d // (21) 
etena prakāreṇa kleśakarmahetukaṃ janma taddhetukāni punaḥ kleśakaramāṇi tebhyaḥ punar janmety (22) anādi' bhavacakrakaṃ veditavyam / ādau hi parikalpyamāne tasyāhetukatvam eteṣu sajyeta sati (23) cāhetukatve sarvam evedam ahetukaṃ prāduḥ syāt / dṛṣṭañm cāṅkurādiṣu bījādīnāṃ sāmarthyaṃ deśakālaprati(24) niyamād agnyādīnāṃ ca pākajādiṣv iti nāsti nirhetukaḥ prādurbhāvaḥ / nityakāraṇāstitvavādaś ca [p.131] prāg eva pardyudastaḥ / tasmān nāsty eva saṃsārasyādiḥ / antas tu hetukṣayāt yuktaḥ / hetvadhīnatvāj (2) janmano bījakṣayād ivāṅkurasyeti / (3) 
ya evaṃ' skandhasantāno janmatrayāvastha upadiṣṭaḥ (4) 
		sa pratītyasamutpādo, dvādaśāṅgas trikāṇḍakaḥ / (20ab) (5) 
tatra dvādaśāṅgāni avidyā saṃskārā vijñānaṃ nāmarūpaṃ ṣaḍāyatanaṃ (6) sparśo vedanā tṛṣṇā upādānaṃ bhavo jātir jarāmaraṇaṃ ca / trīṇi kāṇḍāni pūrvāparāntamadhyāny atītānāgata(7)pratyutpannāni janmāni / katham eṣu trikāṇḍeṣu dvādaśāṅgāni vyavasthāpyante / (8) 
		pūrvāparāntayor dve dve, madhye 'ṣṭau (20cd) (9) 
avidyā saṃskārāś ca pūrvānte jātirjarāmaraṇaṃ cāparānte / śeṣāṇy aṣṭau madhye / kiṃ punar etāny aṣṭāṅgāni (10) sarvasyāṃ jātau bhavanti / nety āha / kasya tarhi / (11) 
		paripūriṇaḥ //20d' // (12) 
paripūro 'syāstīti paripūrī ya etāni sarvāṇy evāvasthāantarāṇi spṛśati, so 'tra pudgalo 'bhipreto (13) na tu yo 'ntarāla eva mriyate / nāpi rūpārūpyāvacaraḥ / tathā hi mahānidānaparyāye (14) kāmāvacara eva pudgalo nirdiṣṭaḥ / vijñānaṃ ced ānanda mātuḥ kukṣiṃ nāvakrāmed iti vacanāt / (15) yadā tu dvividhaḥ pratītyasamutpāda ucyate paurvāntikaś cāparāntikaś ceti tadā saptāṅgāni paurvāntiko (16) yāavad vedanāntaḥ pañcāparāntikaḥ / saphalahetukayoḥ pūrvāparāntayor grahaṇāt / (17) 
	atha ka ime 'vidyādayaḥ / (18) 
		pūrvakleśa'daśāvidyā (21a) (19) 
yā pūrvake janmani kleśāvasthā sehāvidyety ucyate / sāhacaryāt tadvaśena teṣāṃ (20) samudācārāc ca / rājāgamanavacane tadanuyātrikāagamanasiddhavat / (21) 
		saṃskārāḥ pūrvakarmaṇaḥ / (21b) (22) 
daśeti varttate / pūrvajanmany eva yā puṇyādikarmāvasthā sā' saṃskārā ity ucyante, yasya karmaṇa iha (23) vipākaḥ / (24) 
		saṃdhiskandhās tu vijñānaṃ (21c) (25) 
mātuḥ kukṣau pratisaṃdhikṣaṇe pañca skandhā vijñānam / [p.132] 
		nāmarūpam ataḥ param //21d // (2) 
		prāk ṣaḍāyatanotpādāt (22a) (3) 
saṃdhicittāt pareṇa yāvat ṣaḍāyatanaṃ notpadyate, sāvasthā nāmarūpaṃ tāvat ṣaḍāyatanam ity ucyate / (4) prāk caturāyatanotpādād iti vaktavye ṣaḍāyatanavacanaṃ, tadā tadvyavasthāpanāt / (5) 
		tatpūrvaṃ trikasaṃgamāt / (22b) (6) 
utpanne ṣaḍāyatane sāvasthā tāvat ṣaḍāyatanam ity ucyate, yāvad indriyaviṣayavijñānatrikasaṃnipātaḥ / (7) 
		sparśaḥ prāk sukhaduḥkhādikāraṇajñānaśaktitaḥ //22cd // (8) 
trayāṇāṃ saṃnipātāt sparśo bhavati / sa yāvad vedanātrayakāraṇaparicchede na samartho bhavati /sāvasthā (9) sparśa ity ucyate / paricchedasāmarthye sati (10) 
		vittiḥ prāk maithunāt (23a) (11) 
vedanāvasthā yāvan maithunarāgo na samudācarati / (12) 
		tṛṣṇā bhogamaithunarāgiṇaḥ / (23b) (13) 
kāmaguṇamaithunarāgasamudācārāvasthā tṛṣṇety ucyate, yāvan na tadviṣayaparyeṣṭim āpadyate / (14) 
		upādānaṃ tu bhogānāṃ prāptaye paridhāvataḥ //23cd // (15) 
yasyām avasthāyāṃ viṣayaprāptaye paryeṣṭim āpannaḥ sarvato dhāvaty asāv avasthā upādānam ity ucyate / tathā (16) ca paridhāvan (17) 
		sa bhaviṣyadbhavaphalaṃ kurute karma tat bhavaḥ / (24ab) (18) 
sa viṣayāṇāṃ prāptihetoḥ paridhāvan paunarbhavikaṃ karmopacinoti so 'sya bhavaḥ / tena hi karmaṇā (19) punar itaḥ pracyutasyāyatyāṃ pratisaṃdhir bhavati / yo 'sau (20) 
		pratisaṃdhiḥ punar jātiḥ (24c) (21) 
yad eveha vijñānāṅgaṃ tad evāsyānyatra janmani jātiḥ / tata ūrdhvaṃ (22) 
		jarāmaraṇam ā vidaḥ //24d // (23) 
jāteḥ pareṇa yāvad vedanāvasthā jarāmaraṇam / yāny eveha catvāry aṅgāni nāmarūpaṣaḍāyatanasparśa(24)vedanās tāny evānyatra jarāmaraṇam ity etāni dvādaśāṅgāni / sa caiṣa pratītyasamutpādaś caturvidha ucyate / [p.133] kṣaṇikaḥ prākarṣikaḥ sāmbandhikaḥ āvasthikaś ca / kathaṃ kṣaṇikaḥ / ekasmin khalv api kṣaṇe (2) dvādaśāṅgāni bhavanti / tadyathā lobhavaśena prāṇinaṃ jīvitād vyaparopayet / yo mohaḥ (3) sāvidyā / yā cetanā te saṃskārāḥ / vastuprativijñaptir vijñānam / vijñānasahabhuvaś catvāraḥ (4) skandhā nāmarūpam / nāmarūpe vyavasthāpitāni indriyāṇi ṣaḍāyatanam / ṣaḍāyatanābhinipātaḥ (5) sparśaḥ / sparśānubhavaṃ vedanā / yo lobhaḥ sa tṛṣṭṇā / tatsaṃprayuktāni (6) paryavasthānāni upādānam / tatsamutthitaṃ kāyavākkarma bhavaḥ / teṣāṃ dharmāṇām utsarjanaṃ jātiḥ (7) paripāko jarā bhaṅgo maraṇam iti / punar āhuḥ / kṣaṇikaḥ sāmbandhikaś ca yathā prakaraṇeṣu pratītyasamutpādaḥ katamaḥ / sarve saṃskṛtā dharmā iti / āvasthiko dvādaśa pañcaskandhikā (8) avasthā nirantarajanmatrayasaṃbaddhāḥ / sa eva prākarṣikaḥ / eṣāṃ katamo 'yam abhipreto bhagavataḥ / (9) 
		āvasthikaḥ kileṣṭo 'yaṃ (25a) (10) 
yady aṅgam aṅgaṃ pañca skandhāḥ kiṃ kāraṇam avidyānīn eva dharmān kīrtayati sma / (11) 
		prādhānyāt tv aṅgakīrtanam / (25b) (13) 
avidyāpradhānām avasthām avidyām jagāda, saṃskārapradhānāṃ saṃskārān yāvaj jarāmaraṇam ity adoṣaḥ / kiṃ punaḥ (14) kāraṇaṃ sūtre dvādaśāṅga uktaḥ prakaraṇeṣv anyathā ( /) pratītyasamutpādaḥ katamaḥ / sarve saṃskṛtā dharmā (15) iti / ābhiprāyikaḥ(') sūtre, lākṣaṇiko 'bhidharme (nirdeśaḥ) / tathāavasthikaḥ kṣaṇikaḥ prākarṣikaḥ sāṃbandhikaḥ (16) sattvākhyo 'sattvākhyaś ceti bhedaḥ / kimarthaṃ punaḥ sūtre sattvākhya eva / (17) 
		pūrvāparāntamadhyeṣu saṃmohavinivṛttaye //25cd // (18) 
ata eva ca trikāṇḍaḥ / tatra pūrvāntasaṃmoho yata iyaṃ vicikitsā kiṃ nv aham abhūvam atīte 'dhvani (19) āhosvin nābhūvaṃ ( /) ko nv aham abhūvaṃ, kathaṃ nv aham abhūvam iti / aparāntasaṃmoho (20) yata iyaṃ vicikitsā kiṃ nu bhaviṣyāmy anāgate 'dhvanīti vistaraḥ / madhyasaṃmoho yata iyaṃ (21) vicikitsā kiṃ svid idaṃ, ke santaḥ, ke bhaviṣyāma iti / etasya trividhasya saṃmohasya vyāvartta(22)nārthaṃ sattvākhya eva trikāṇḍaś ca pratītyasamutpāda upadiṣṭaḥ sūtre yathākramam avidyā saṃskārāś ca (23) jātir jaramaraṇaṃ ca vijñānaṃ yāvat bhavaś ca / tathā hi sūtra evoktaṃ yataś ca bhikṣavo bhikṣuṇā [p.134] pratītyasamutpādaś' ca pratītyasamutpannāś ca dharmā evaṃ yathābhūtaṃ samyak prajñayā dṛṣṭā bhavanti / (2) sa na pūrvāntaṃ pratisarati, kiṃ nv aham abhūvam atīte 'dhvanīti vistaraḥ / tṛṣṇopādānabhavā apy aparāntasaṃ(3)mohavyāavartanārtham ity apare / tasyaiva hy ete hetava iti sa punar eṣa dvādaśāṅgaḥ pratītyasamutpādas (4) trisvabhāvo veditavyaḥ / (5) 
	kleśakarmavastūni / tatra (6) 
		kleśās trīṇi (26a) (7) 
trīṇy aṅgāni kleśasvabhāny avidyātṛṣṇopādānāni / (8) 
		dvayaṃ karma (26a') (9) 
aṅgadvayaṃ karmasvabhāvaṃ saṃskārā bhavaś ca / (10) 
		sapta vastu (26b) (11) 
saptāṅgāni vastusvabhāvāni vijñānanāmarūpaṣaḍāyatanasparśavedanājātijarāmaraṇāni / kleśakarmā(12)śrayatvāt / yathā ca vastu saptāṅgāni (13) 
		phalaṃ tathā / (26b') (14) 
saptaivāṅgāni phalabhūtāni / śeṣāṇi pañca hetubhūtāni / karmakleśasvabhāvabhūtatvāt / kiṃ punaḥ (15) kāraṇaṃ madhye phalahetū viśālitau / vastunaḥ pañcadhā bhedāt / kleśasya (16) dvidhā / anāgate 'dhvani phalaṃ saṃkṣiptaṃ dvidhā bhedāt / atīte 'dhvani hetur ekamukhakleśopadeśād iti / (17) 
		phalahetvabhisaṃkṣepo dvayor madhyānumānataḥ //26cd // (18) 
madhyenaiva hi pūrvāntāparāntayor api hetuphalavistaraḥ śakyo 'numātum iti noktaḥ / punar aparātmakaṃ (19) hi yatnaṃ mā kāryam iti / 
	yadi khalu dvādaśāṅgta eva pratītyasamutpāda, evaṃ saty avidyāyā (20) anupadiṣṭahetukatvād ādimān saṃsāraḥ prāpnoti, jarāmaraṇasya cānupadiṣṭaphalatvād antavān / aṅgāntaraṃ (21) vā punar upasaṃkhyātavyaṃ tasyāpy anyad ' ity anavasthāprasaṅgaḥ / nopasaṃkhyātavyam / yasmād upadarśito 'tra (22) bhagavatā (23) 
		kleśāt kleśaḥ kriyā caiva, tato vastu tataḥ punaḥ / (23) 
		vastu kleśāś ca jāyante, bhavāṅgānām ayaṃ nayaḥ //27// (25) 
kleśāt kleśo jāyate tṛṣṇāyā upādānam / kleśāt karma / upādānāt bhavo 'vidyāyāś ca [p.135] saṃskārāḥ / karmaṇo' vastu saṃskārebhyo vijñānaṃ bhavāc ca jātiḥ / vastuno vastu vijñānān nāmarūpaṃ yāvat (2) sparśād vedanā jāteś ca jarāmaraṇam / vastunaḥ kleśo vedanāyās tṛṣṇeti / yasmād eṣa nayo vyavasthito (3) bhavāṅgānāṃ tasmād avidyāapi kleśasvabhāvā vastunaḥ kleśād veti jñāpitaṃ bhavati / (4) vedanāvasānāc' jarāmaraṇavastunaḥ punaḥ kleśo bhāvīti nātra punaḥ kimcid upasaṃkhyeyam / evam asya (5) kevalasya mahato duḥkhaskandhasya samudayo bhavatīti vacanāt / anyathā hi kim asya sāmarthyaṃ (6) syāt / ayoniśomanaskārahetukāvidyoktā sūtrāntare / avidyāhetukaś cāyoniśomanaskāraḥ / (7) sa cehāpy upādānāntarbhūtatvād ukto bhavatīti apare / katham ayoniśomanaskārasyopādāne 'ntarbhāvaḥ / (8) yadi saṃprayogataḥ / tṛṣṇāvidyayor api tasyāntarbhāvaprasaṅgaḥ / saty api cāntarbhāve katham atredaṃ (9) jñāpitaṃ bhavaty ayoniśomanaskārahetukā avidyeti / yadi tarhy antarbhāvenaiva hetuphalabhāvo vijñāyate (10) tṛṣṭṇāvidyayor api tarhi tatrāntarbhāvād aṅgāntaratvaṃ śakyam akarttum / anyaḥ punar āha / ayoniśo(11)manaskāro hetur avidyāyā uktaḥ sūtrāntare / sa cāpi sparśakāle nirdiṣṭaḥ / cakṣuḥ pratītya (12) rūpāṇi cotpadyate āvilo manaskāra mohaja iti / vedanākāle cāvaśyam avidyayā bhavitavyam / (13) avidyāsaṃsparśajaṃ veditaṃ pratītyotpannā tṛṣṇeti sūtrāntarāt / ataḥ (14) sparśakāle bhavann ayoniśomanaskāro vedanāsahavarttinyā avidyāyāḥ pratyayabhāvena siddha iti (15) nāsty ahetukatvam avidyāyā na cāṅgāntaram upasaṃkhyeyam / na cāpy anavasthāprasaṅgaḥ / tasyāpy ayoniśo(16) manaskārasya punar mohajavacanād āvilo manaskāro mohaja iti / tat tarhy etad anyatroktam iha (17) punar vaktavyam / na vaktavyam / katham anucyamānaṃ gamyate / yuktitaḥ / kayā yuktyā / (18) na hi niravadyā vedanā tṛṣṇāyāḥ pratyayībhavaty arhatāṃ na cāviparītaḥ sparśaḥ kliṣṭāyā vedanāyāḥ / (19) na ca punar niravadyasyārhataḥ sparśo viparīta ity anayā yuktyā / atiprasaṅga evaṃ prāpnoti / (20) yāvat yuktyā saṃbhavati, tāvad anuktaṃ gamyata iti / tasmān na bhavaty ayaṃ parihāraḥ / acodyam eva (21) tv etad / avidyājarāmaraṇayoḥ pareṇāṅgāntarānabhidhānāt saṃsārasyādyantvattvaprasaṅgaḥ / na cāparipūrṇo (22) nirdeśa iti / kiṃ kāraṇam / pravṛttisaṃmūḍhebhyo vineyebhyaḥ kathaṃ paralokād ihaloka ihalokāc ca punaḥ (23) paralokaḥ saṃbasdhyata ity etāvato '6rārthasya vivakṣitatvāt / etasya ca pūrvam evoktatvāt / [p.136] 
	purvāparāntamadhyeṣu saṃmohavinivṛttaye / (2) 
uktaṃ bhagavatā: pratītyasamutpādaṃ vo bhikṣavo deśayiṣyāmi pratītyasamutpannāṃś ca dharmān ity atha ka (3) eṣāṃ viśeṣaḥ / śāstratas tāvan na kaścit / ubhayaṃ hi sarve saṃskṛtā dharmā iti / katham idānīm (4) utpannā evānāgatāḥ pratītyasamutpannā ity ucyante / kathaṃ tāvad akṛtā evānāgatāḥ saṃskṛtā (5) ucyante / ābhisāṣkārikayā cetanayā cetitatvāt / anāsravāḥ katham / te 'pi cetitāḥ (6) kuśalayā cetanayā prāptiṃ prati / nirvāṇe 'pi prasaṅgaḥ / tajjātīyatvāt tat tatraivātideśo yathā (7) na ca tāvad rūpyate rūpaṃ cocyate tajjātīyatvād ity adoṣaḥ / sūtrābhiprāyastv ayam ucyate / (8) 
		hetur atra samutpādaḥ samutpannaṃ phalaṃ matam / (28ab) (9) 
hetubhūtam aṅgaṃ pratītyasamutpādaḥ, samutpadyate 'smād iti kṛtvā / phalabhūtam aṅgaṃ pratītyasamutpannam / evaṃ (10) sarvāṇy aṅgāny ubhayathā sidhyanti / hetuphalabhāvāt / na caivaṃ saty avavasthā bhavanty apekṣābhedāt / (11) yad apekṣya pratītyasamutpādo na tad evāpekṣya pratītyasamutpannaṃ hetuphalavat pitṛputravac ca / sthavirapūrṇāśaḥ (12) kilāha syāt pratītyasamutpādo na pratītyasamutpannā dharmā iti / catuṣkoṭikaḥ / (13) prathamā koṭir anāgatā dharmāḥ / dvitīyārhataś caramāḥ / tṛtīyā tadanye 'tītapratyutpannā dharmāḥ / (14) caturthy asaṃskṛtā dharmā iti / atra tu sautrāntikā vijñāpayanti / kiṃ khalv etā iṣṭaya ucyante (15) yā yasyeṣṭir āhosvit sūtrārthaḥ / sūtrārtha ity āha / yadi sūtrārtho naiṣa sūtrārthaḥ ' / kathaṃ kṛtvā / (16) yat tāvad uktam āvasthika eṣa pratītyasamutpādo dvādaśa pañcaskandhikā avasthā dvādaśāṅgānīty etad(17) utsūtram / sūtre 'nyathā nirdeśād / avidyā katamā / yat tat pūrvānte 'jñānam iti (18) vistareṇa / yac ca nītārthaṃ na tat punar neyaṃ bhavatīti naiṣa sūtrārthaḥ / na vai sarvaṃ nirdeśato nītārthaṃ (19) bhavati / yathāpradhānaṃ cāpi nirdeśāḥ kriyante / tadyathā hastipadopame pṛthivīdhātuḥ katamaḥ (20) ity adhikṛtyāhā keśā romāṇīti / santi ca tatrānye 'pi rūpādayaḥ / evam atrāpi (21) yathāpradhānaṃ nirdeśaḥ syāt / anupasaṃhāra eṣaḥ / na hi tatra keśādayaḥ pṛthivīdhātunā nirdiśyante / (22) yata eṣām aparipūrṇo nirdeśaḥ syād api tu keśādibhir eva pṛthivīdhātuḥ tatra nirdiśyate / na ca (23) keśādīn abhyatītyāpy asti pṛthivīdhātur iti saṃpūrṇa evāsya nirdeśaḥ / evam ihāpy avidyādīnāṃ [p.137] paripūrṇa evā nirdeśo na sāvaśeṣaḥ / nanu cābhyatītyāpi keśādīn a(2)śrukeṭaśiṅghāṇakādisṣv asti pṛthivīdhātuḥ / so 'pi nirdiṣṭa eveti / yad vā punar anyad apy asmin (3) kāye khakkaṭaṃ kharagatam iti vacanāt ( /) bhavatu vā tathaivāvidyāvaśeṣo yadi śakyate darśayitum / (4) jātyantarasya tv avidyāyāṃ kiṃkṛtaḥ prakṣepaḥ / yady api ca tāsv avasthāsu pañca skandhā vidyante, yasya (5) tu bhāvābhāvayor yasya bhāvāabhāvaniyamaḥ tad evāṅgaṃ vyavasthāpayitum / saty api ca pañcaskandhake 'rhataḥ (6) saṃskārā na bhavanti pañcaskandhahetukāḥ / kiṃ tarhi / avidyāhetukā eva / tathā puṇyāpuṇyāneñjyopagaṃ (7) ca vijñānaṃ tṛṣṇādayaś ceti / yathānirdeśam ava sūtrārthaḥ / yad apy uktaṃ hetur atra samutpādaḥ samutpannaṃ (8) phalaṃ yāvac catuṣkoṭika iti / etad apy utsūtram / sūtre 'nyathā nirdeśāt / pratītyasamutpādaḥ (9) katamaḥ / yad utāsmin satīdaṃ bhavatīti vistareṇoktvā iti yātra dharmatā dharmasthititā (10) yāvad aviparyastatā ayam ucyate pratītyasamutpāda iti / dharmatā ca nāma dharmajātiḥ dharmāṇāṃ śailiḥ / (11) ato yeyaṃ dharmatā ya eṣa niyamaḥ / avidyāyām eva satyāṃ saṃskārā bhavanti nonyathā / eṣa (12) pratītyasamutpādo na hetur eva / yad apy catuṣkoṭikam uktaṃ tatra yady anāgatāḥ (13) dharmā na pratītyasamutpannāḥ sūtraṃ virudhyate / pratītyasamutpannā dharmāḥ katame / avidyā yāvaj jātiḥ (14) jarāmaraṇam / tayor anāgatādhvavyavasthānaṃ naiṣṭavyam iti trikāṇḍavyavasthā bhidyate / asaṃskṛtaḥ (15) pratītyasamutpāda iti nikāyāntarīyāḥ / utpādād vā tathāgatānām anutpādād vā sthitaiveyaṃ (16) dharmateti vacanāt / tad etad abhiprāyavaśād evaṃ ca na caivam / kathaṃ tāvad evaṃ kathaṃ vā (17) naivam / yady ayam abhiprāya utpādād vā tathāgatānām anutpādād vā nityam avidyādīn pratītya sasṃkārādīnām (18) anutpādo na kadācid apratītyānyad vā pratītyāto nitya iti evam etad iti pratigrāhyam / athāyam abhiprāyaḥ ( /) (19) pratītyasamutpādo nāma kiṃcit bhāvāntaraṃ nityam astīti / naitad evam iti pratiṣeddhavyam / kiṃ (20) kāraṇam / utpādasya saṃskṛtalakṣaṇatvāt / na ca nityaṃ bhāvāntaram anityasya lakṣaṇaṃ yujyate / (21) utpādaś ca nāmotpattur bhavatīti ko 'syāvidyādibhir abhaṃbandho yatas teṣāṃ pratītyasamutāda ity ucyate / (22) padārthaś cāsamartho bhavatīti nityaś ca nāma pratītyasamutpādaś ceti / [p.138] 
	atha pratītyasamutpāda iti kaḥ padārthaḥ / pratiḥ prāptyartha, eti gatyarthaḥ / (2) upasargavaśena dhātvarthapariṇāmāt prāpyeti yo 'rthaḥ so 'rthaḥ pratītyeti / padi sattārthaḥ samutpūrvaḥ (3) prādurbhāvārthaḥ / tena pratyayaṃ prāpya samudbhavaḥ pratītyasamutpādaḥ / na yukta eṣa padārthaḥ / (4) kiṃ kāraṇam / ekasya hi karttur dvayoḥ kriyayoḥ pūrvakālāyāṃ kriyāyāṃ ktvāvidhir bhavati / (5) tadyathā snātvā bhuṅkta iti / na cāsau pūrvam utpādāt paścid asti yaḥ ūrvaṃ pratītyottarakālam utpadyate / (6) na cāpy akartṛkāasti kriyeti / āha cātra (7) 
		''pratyeti pūrvam utpādād yad yady asattvān na yujyate / (8) 
		saha cet ktvā na siddho 'tra pūrvakālavidhānata iti /'' (9) 
naiṣa doṣaḥ / idaṃ tāvad ayaṃ praṣṭavyaḥ śābdikaḥ / kimavastho dharmaḥ utpadyate, varttamāna utāho 'nāgata (10) iti / kiṃ cātaḥ / yadi varttamāna utpadyate / kathaṃ varttamāno yadi notpannaḥ / utpannasya (11) vā punar utpattāv anavasthāprasaṅgaḥ / athānāgata utpadyate katham asataḥ kartṛtvaṃ siddhaty (sidhyati') akkartṛkā vā kriyeti / (12) ato yadavastha utpadyate tadavastha eva pratyeti / kimavasthaś cotpadyate / utpādābhimukho 'nāgataḥ / (13) tadavastha eva pratyayaṃ pratyetīty ucyate / aniṣpannaṃ cedaṃ yad uta śābdikīyaṃ kartṛkriyāvvyavasthānaṃ (14) bhavatīty eṣa karttābhūtir ity eṣā kriyā / na cātra bhavitur arthāt bhūtim anyāṃ (15) kriyāṃ paśyāmaḥ / tasmād acchalaṃ vyavahāreṣu / eṣa tu vākyārthaḥ / asmin saty asya bhāvaḥ (16) asyotpādād idam utpadyata iti yo 'rthaḥ so 'rthaḥ pratītyasamutpāda iti / āha cātra (17) 
		''asann utpadyate yadvat pratyety api tathātha san / (18) 
		utpanna utpadyata ity aniṣṭhā 'san purāpi vā // (19) 
		sahakāle 'pi ca ktvāsti, dīpaṃ prāpya tamo gatam / (20) 
		āsyāṃ vyādāya śete ca, paścāt cet kiṃ na saṃvṛte //'' (21) 
	anye punar asya codyasya parihārārtham anyathā parikalpayanti / pratir vīpsāarthaḥ / iatau (22) sādhava ityā anavasthāyinaḥ / utpūrvaḥ padiḥ prādurbhāvārthaḥ / tāṃ tāṃ kāraṇasāmagrīṃ (23) prati ityānāṃ samavāyenotpādaḥ pratītyasamutpāda iti / eṣā tu kalpanātraiva kalpyate / (24) iha kathaṃ bhaviṣyati cakṣuḥ pratītya rūpāṇi cotpadyate cakṣurvijñānam iti / kim arthaṃ punar bhagavān [p.139] paryāyadvayam āha ( /) asmin satīdaṃ bhavati asyotpādād idam utpadyate iti ' avadhāraṇārtham / (2) yathānyatrāha: avidyāyāṃ satyāṃ saṃskārā bhavanti nānyatrāvidyāyaḥ saṃskārā iti / aṅgaparaṃparāṃ (3) vā darśayitum / asminn aṅge satīdaṃ bhavati, asya punar aṅgasyotpādād idam utpadyata iti / (4) janmaparaṃparāṃ vā / pūrvānte sati madhyānto bhavati, madhyāntasyotpādād aparānta utpadyata iti / (5) sākṣāt pāraṃparyeṇa pratyayabhāvaṃ darśayati / kadācid dhi samanantaram avidyāyāḥ saṃskārā (6) bhavanti kadācit pāraṃparyeṇeti / ahetunityahetuvādapratiṣedhartham ity apare / nāstati hetau (7) bhāvo bhavati, na cānutpattimato nityāt prakṛtipuruṣādikāt kiṃcid utpadyata iti / asyāṃ tu (8) kalpanāyāṃ pūrvapadasya grahaṇam anarthakaṃ prāṇoti / asyotpādād idam utpadyata ity anenāvnobhayavāda(9)pratiṣedhasiddheḥ / santi tarhi kecid ya ātmani saty āśrayabhūte saṃskārādīnāṃ bhāvaṃ parikalpayanti (10) avidyādīnāṃ cotpādāt tadutpattim / atas tāṃ palpanāṃ paryudāsayitum idaṃ nirdhārayāṃ babhūva (11) yasyaivotpādād yad utpadyate, tasminn eva sati tat bhavati nānyasmin / yadutāvidyāpratyayāḥ saṃskārāḥ (12) yāvad evam asya kevalasya mahato duḥkhaskandhasya samudayo bhavatīti / aprahīṇotpattijñāpanārtham ity ācāryāḥ / avidyāyām aprahīṇāyāṃ saṃskārā na prahīyante / tasyā evotpādād utpadyanta (14) iti vistaraḥ / sthityutpattisaṃdarśanārtham ity apare / yāvat kāraṇasrotas (15) tāvat kāryasroto bhavati / kāraṇasyaiva cotpādāt kāryam utpadyata iti / utpāde tv adhikṛte (16) kaḥ prasaṅgaḥ sthitivacanasya ( /) bhinnakramaṃ ca bhagavān kimartham ācakṣīta prāk sthitiṃ paścād utpādam / (17) punar āha asmin satīdaṃ bhavatīti kārye sati kāraṇasya vināśo bhavatīti / syān matam ahetukaṃ (18) tarhi kāryam utpadyata ity ata āha nāhetukam / yasmād asyotpādād idam utpadyata iti / eṣa (19) cet sūtrārtho 'bhaviṣyad asmin satīdaṃ na bhavatīty evācakṣata / pūrvaṃ ca tāvat kāryasyotpādane (20) vācakṣata paścād asmin satīdaṃ na bhavatīti / evaṃ hi sādhu kramo bhavati / itarathā tu (21) pratītyasamutpādaḥ katama ity āder arthe kaḥ prakramo vināśavacanasya / tasmān naiṣa sūtrārthaḥ / (22) kathaṃ punar avidyāpratyayāḥ saṃskārā yāvat jātipratyayaṃ jarāmaraṇam iti / 		prajñopakleśadeśanāt //29d // (18) 
rāgopakliṣṭaṃ cittaṃ na vimucyate' avidyopakliṣṭā prajñā na viśudhyatīty uktaṃ sūtre / na ca saiva (19) prajñā tasyāḥ prajñāyā upakleśo yukta iti / yathā cittasyānyo bhinnajātīya (20) upakleśo rāga evaṃ prajñāyā avidyā / kiṃ punar evaṃ neṣyate / kliṣṭayā prajñāyā kuśalā prajñā (21) vyavakīryamāṇā na viśudhyati / ato 'sau tasyā upakleśa iti / yad vāpi rāgopakliṣṭaṃ cittaṃ (22) na vimucyate / kiṃ tad avaśyaṃ rāgaparyavasthitaṃ bhavati / upahataṃ tu tat tathā rāgeṇa bhavati yan na (23) vimucyate / tāṃ punar bhāvanāṃ vyāvartayato vimuycyate / evam avidyopakliṣṭā prajñā na (24) viśudhyatīty avidyopahatāṃ parikalpayāmaḥ / ko hi parikalpayan vāryate / jātyantaram eva tv avidyāṃ (25) varṇayanti / yo 'pi manyate, sarvakleśā avidyeti tasyāpy ata eva vyudāsaḥ / sarvakleśasvabhāvāpi [p.142] satī saṃyojanādiṣu pṛthag nocyeta dṛṣṭyā ca na saṃprayujyeta / anyena vā kleśena dṛṣṭyādīnāṃ (2) paraṃparāsaṃprayogāt cittam api cāvidyopakliṣṭam evoktaṃ bhavet / atha mataṃ viśeṣaṇārthaṃ tathoktam iti / (3) prajñāyām apy avidyāviśeṣaṇaṃ karttavyaṃ bhavet / bhavatv avidyā dharmāntaraṃ kastu tasyāḥ (4) svabhāvaḥ / satyavan na karmaphalānām asaṃprakhyānam / etac caiva na jñāyate kim idam asaṃprakhyānaṃ (5) nāmeti / yadi yan na saṃprakhyānaṃ saṃprakhyānābhāve 'pi tathaiva doṣo yathā (6) avidyāyām / atha saṃprakhyānavipakṣabhūtaṃ dharmāntaram / tad idaṃ tathaiva na jñāyate kiṃ (7) tad iti / evaṃjātīyako 'pi dharmāṇāṃ nirdeśo bhavati / tadyathā cakṣuḥ katamat / yo (8) rūpaprasādaś caturvijñānasyāśraya iti / asmīti sattvamayatāvidyeti bhadantadharmatrātaḥ / kā (9) punar asmimānād anyā mayatā / yāsau sūtra uktā so 'ham evaṃ jñātvā, evaṃ dṛṣṭvā sarvāsāṃ (10) tṛṣṇānāṃ sarvāsāṃ dṛṣṭīnāṃ sarvāsāṃ mayatānāṃ sarveṣām ahaṃkāramamakārāsmimānābhiniveśānuśayānāṃ (11) prahāṇāt parijñānān niścchāyo nivṛta iti / asty eṣā mayatā / sā tv iyam avidyeti kuta (12) etat / yata eṣā nānyaḥ kleśaḥ śakyate vaktum / nanu cānyo māna eva syāt / atra (13) punar vicāryamāṇe bahu vaktavyaṃ jāyate / 	atha nāmarūpam iti ko 'rthaḥ / rūpaṃ vistareṇa yathoktam / (15)

kiṃ kāraṇam / nāmendriyārthavaśenārtheṣu namatīti nāma / katamasya nāmno vaśena / yad idaṃ (17) loke pratītaṃ teṣāṃ teṣām arthānāṃ pratyāyakaṃ gaur aśvo rūpaṃ rasa ity evamādi / etasya punaḥ kena (18) nāmatvam / teṣu teṣv artheṣu tasya nāmno namanāt / iha nikṣipte kāya (19) upapattyantare namanān nāmārūpiṇaḥ skandhā ity apare // ṣaḍāyatanam uktam / (20)

		sparśāḥ ṣaṭ (30b) (22) 
cakṣuḥsaṃsparśo yāvan manaḥsaṃsparśa iti / te punaḥ [p.143] 
		saṃnipātajāḥ / (30b') (2) 
trayāṇāṃ saṃnipātāj jātā indriyārthavijñānānām / yuktaṃ tāvat pañcānām indriyāṇām arthavijñānābhyaṃ (3) saṃnipātaḥ / sahajatvāt / manaindriyasya punar niruddhasyānāgatavarttamānābhyāṃ dharmamanovijñānābhyāṃ (4) kathaṃ saṃnipātaḥ / ayam eva teṣāṃ saṃnipāto yaḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvaḥ / ekakāryārtho vā saṃnipātārthaḥ / (5) sarve ca te trayo 'pi sparśotpattau praguṇā bhavantīti / atra punar ācāryāṇāṃ bhedaṃ gatā buddhayaḥ / (6) kecid dhi sakṛn nipātam eva sparśaṃ vyācakṣate, sūtraṃ cātra jñāpakam ānayanti, iti ya eṣāṃ trayāṇāṃ (7) dharmāṇāṃ saṃgatiḥ saṃnipātaḥ samavāyaḥ, sa sparśaḥ iti / kecit punaś cittasaṃprayuktaṃ dharmāntaram eva (8) sparśaṃ vyācakṣate, sūtraṃ cātra jñāpakam ānayanti, ṣaṭṣaṭko dharmaparyāyaḥ katamaḥ / ṣaḍ ādhyātmikāny (9) āyatanāni / ṣaṭ bāhyāny āyatanāni / ṣaṭ vijñānakāyāḥ / ṣaṭ sparśakāyaḥ / ṣaṭ (10) vedanākāyāḥ / ṣaṭ tṛṣṇākāyā iti / atra hīndriyārthavijñānebhyaḥ sparśakā(11)yāḥ pṛthag deśitāḥ / tatra ye saṇipātam eva sparśam āhus ta evaṃ parihāram āhuḥ / na vai pṛthagnirdeśāt (12) pṛthagbhāvo bhavati / mā bhūd dharmāyatanād vedanātṛṣṇayoḥ pṛthagbhāva iti / naiṣa doṣas tadvyatiriktasyāpi (13) dharmāyatanasya bhāvāt / na caivaṃ sparśabhūtāt trayād anyat trayam asti yasya śeṣasyatra grahaṇaṃ (14) syāt / yady api hīndriyārthau syātām avijñānakau na tu punar vijñānam anindriyārthakam / tasmāt triṣu (15) nirdiṣṭeṣu punaḥ sparśasya grhaṇam anarthakaṃ prāpnoti / na khalu sarve cakṣūrūpe sarvasya cakṣurvijñānasya (16) kāraṇaṃ nāpi sarvaṃ cakṣurvijñānaṃ sarvayoś cakṣūrūpayoḥ kāryam / ato yeṣāṃ kāryakāraṇabhāvas te (17) sparśabhāve vyavasthāpitā ity eke / ye punaḥ saṃnipātād anyaṃ sparśam āhus ta etat sūtraṃ kathaṃ (18) pariharanti, iti ya eṣāṃ trayāṇāṃ saṃgatiḥ saṃnipātaḥ samavāyaḥ, sa sparśa iti / na vā evaṃ (19) paṭhanti / kiṃ tarhi / saṃgateḥ saṃnipātāt samavāyād iti paṭhanti, kāraṇe vā kāryopacāro 'yam (20) iti bruvanti / atibahuvistaraprasāriṇī tv eṣā kathety alaṃ prasaṅgena / anyam eva sparśaṃ (21) varṇayanty ābhidhārmikāḥ / teṣāṃ punaḥ ṣaṇṇāṃ sparśānāṃ (22) 
		pañca pratighasaṃsparśaḥ, ṣaṣṭho 'dhiavacanāhvayaḥ //30cd // (23) 
cakṣuḥśrotraghrāṇajihvākāyasaṃsparśāḥ pañca pratighasaṃsparśa ity ucyate / sapratighendriyāśrayatvāt / [p.144] manaḥsaṃsparśaḥ ṣaṣṭhaḥ so 'dhivacanasparśa ity ucyate / kiṃ kāraṇaṃ adhivacanam ucyate nāma / (2) tat kilāsyādhikam ālambanam ato 'dhivacanasaṃsparśa iti / yathoktaṃ cakṣur vijñānena nīlaṃ (3) vijānāti no tu nīlaṃ manovijñānena nīlaṃ vijānāti nīlam iti ca vijānātīti / eka (4) āśrayaprabhāvito dvitīya ālambanaprabhāvitaḥ / apare punar āhuḥ / vacanam adhikṛtyārtheṣu (5) manovijñānasya pravṛttir na pañcānāṃ / atas tad evādhivacanam / tena saṃprayuktaḥ sparśo 'dhivacana(6)saṃsparśa ity eka āśrayaprabhāvito dvitīyaḥ saṃprayogaprabhāvitaḥ / punas ta eva ṣaṭ sparśās trayo bhavanti / (7) 
		vidyāvidyetarasparśāḥ (31a) (8) 
vidyāsaṃsparśo 'vidyāsaṃsparśaḥ / tābhyāṃ cānyo naivavidyānāvidyāsaṃsparśa iti / ete punar yathākramaṃ (9) veditavyāḥ (10) 
		amalakliṣṭaśeṣitāḥ / (31b) (11)

sukhavedanīyaḥ sparśo duḥkhavedanīyo 'duḥkhāsukhavedanīyaś ca / sukhavedanādihitatvāt / athavā (19) vedyate tad vedayituṃ vā śakyam iti vedanīyam / kiṃ tat / veditam / sukhaṃ vedanīyam asminn iti (20) sukhavedanīyaḥ sparśo yatra sukhaṃ veditam asti / evaṃ duḥkhāduḥkhāsukhavedanīyāv api yojyau / (21) ta ete ṣoḍaśa sparśo bhavanti / uktaḥ sparśaḥ / (22)

cakṣuḥsaṃsparśajā vedanā śrotraghrāṇajihvākāyamanaḥ saṃsparśajā vedanāḥ / tāsāṃ punaḥ [p.145] 
		pañca kāyikī caitasī parā (32a'b) (2) 
cakṣuḥśrotraghrāṇajihvākāyasaṃsparśajāḥ pañca vedanāḥ kāyikī vedanety ucyate / rūpīndriyāśritatvāt / (3) manaḥsaṃsparśajā punar vedanā caitasikīty ucyate / cittamātrāśritatvāt / 
	atha kiṃ sparśād uttarakālaṃ (4) vedanā bhavaty āhosvit samānakālam / samānakāam iti vaibhāṣikāḥ / anyonyaṃ sahabhūhetutvāt / (5) kathaṃ sahotpannayor janyajanakabhāvaḥ sidhyati / kathaṃ ca na sidhyati / (6) asāmarthyāt / jāte dharme dharmasya nāsti sāmarthyaṃ pratijñāviśiṣṭam / yad eva hīdaṃ (7) sahotpannayor janyajanakabhāvo nāstīti tad evedaṃ jāte dharme dharmasya nāstīti / anyonyajanaka(8)prasaṅgāt tarhi / iṣṭatvād adoṣaḥ / iṣṭam eva hi sahabhūhetor anyonyaphalatvam / iṣṭam idaṃ sūtre (9) tv aniṣṭaṃ sparśavedanayor anyonyaphalatvam / cakṣuḥsaṃsparśaṃ pratītya utpadyate cakṣuḥsaṃsparśajā vedanā (10) na tu cakṣuḥsaṃsparśajāṃ vedanāṃ pratītyotpadyate cakṣuḥsaṃsparśa iti vacanāt / janakadharmātikramāc (11) cāyuktam / yo hi dharmo yasya dharmasya janakaḥ prasiddhaḥ, sa tasmāt bhinnakālaḥ prasiddhaḥ / (12) tadyathā pūrvaṃ bījaṃ paścād aṅkuraḥ, pūrvaṃ kṣīraṃ paścād dadhi, pūrvam abhighātaḥ paścāc chabdaḥ, pūrvaṃ manaḥ paścāt (13) manovijñānam ity evamādi / anyathā paraṃparāpekṣatvāt kāryakāraṇayoḥ sarvaṃ vā yugapad utpadyate, na vā (14) kadācit paścādviśeṣābhāvāt / na hi na siddhaḥ kāryakāraṇayoḥ pūrvāparabhāvaḥ / sahabhāvo 'pi (15) tu siddhaḥ / tadyathā cakṣurvijñānādīnāṃ cakṣūrūpādibhir bhūtabhautikānāṃ ca tatrāpi pūrvam indriyārthau (16) paścād vijñānam / pūrvakāc ca bhūtabhautikasamudācārād uttara utpadyata itīṣyamāṇe (17) kaḥ pratiṣedhaḥ / yathā tarhi cchāyāṅkurayor evaṃ sparśavedanayor iti vaibhāṣikāḥ / sparśād uttarakālaṃ (18) vedanety apare / indriyārtho hi pūrvānto vijñānam / so 'sau trayāṇāṃ saṃnipātaḥ sparśaḥ (19) sparśapratyayāt paścād vedanā tṛtīyakṣaṇa iti / evaṃ tarhi na sarvatra vijñāne vedanā prāpnoti, na ca (20) sarvavedanakāḥ sarvaṃ ca vijñānaṃ sparśa iti / idam ayuktaṃ varttate / kim atrāyuktam / (22) yad uta bhinnālambanayor api sparśayoḥ pūrvasparśahetukotarasya vedaneti / kathaṃ nāmāyajātīyālambana(23)sparśasaṃbhūtā vedanānyālambanā bhaviṣyati, yena vā cittena saṃprayuktā tato bhinnālambaneti / astu [p.146] tarhi tasmin kāle sparśabhūtaṃ vijñānam avedanakam / tasmāc ca yat pūrvaṃ vijñānaṃ savedanakaṃ tan na sparśaḥ / (2) pratyayavaidhuryāad ity evaṃ sati ko doṣaḥ / mahābhūmikaniyamo bhidyate / sarvatra citte daśa mahābhūmikā (3) iti / kva caiṣa niyamaḥ siddhaḥ / śāstre / sūtrapramāṇakā vayaṃ na śāstrapramā(4)nakāḥ / uktaṃ hi bhagavatā sūtrāntapratiśaraṇair bhavitavyam iti / na vā eṣa mahābhūmikārthaḥ (5) sarvatra citte daśa mahābhūmikāḥ saṃbhavantīti / kas tarhi mahābhūmikārthaḥ / tisro bhūmayaḥ / savitarkā (6) savicārā bhūmiḥ avitarkā vicāramātrā avitarkāvicārā bhūmiḥ / punas tisraḥ / kuśalā bhūmiḥ (7) akuśalāvyākṛtā bhūmiḥ / punas tisraḥ / śaikṣī bhūmir aśaikṣī naivaśaikṣīnāśaikṣī bhūmiḥ / tad ya etasyāṃ (8) sarvasyāṃ bhūmau bhavanti, te mahābhūmikāḥ / ye kuśalāyām eva te kuśalamahābhūmikāḥ / ye kliṣṭāyām eva (9) te k leśamahābhūmikāḥ / te punar yathāsaṃbhavaṃ paryāyeṇa na tu sarve yugapad ity apare / akuśalamahā(10)bhūmikās tu pāṭhaprasaṅgenāsaṃjitāḥ / idānīṃ pūrvaṃ na paṭhyate sma / yadi tarhi sparśād uttarakālaṃ (11) vedanā sūtraṃ parihāryaṃ cakṣuḥ pratītya rūpāṇi cotpadyate cakṣurvijñānaṃ, trayāṇāṃ saṃnipātaḥ sparśaḥ (12) sahajātā vedanā saṃjñā cetaneti / sahajātā ity ucyate, na sparśasahajātā iti kim atra parihāryam / (13) samanantare 'pi cāyaṃ sahaśabdo dṛṣṭas tadyathā maitrīsahagataṃ smṛtisaṃbodhyaṅgaṃ bhāvayatīty ajñāpakam (14) etat / yat tarhi sūtra uktaṃ yā ca vecanā yā ca saṃjñā yā ca cetanā (15) yac ca vijñānaṃ saṃsṛṣṭā ime dharmā nāsaṃsṛṣṭā ity ato nāsti vedanābhir asaṃsṛṣṭaṃ vijñānam / (16) saṃpradhāryaṃ tāvad etat, ka eṣa saṃsṛṣṭarthaḥ / tatra hi sūtra evam uktaṃ, yad vedayate, tac cetayate, yac cetayate (17) tat saṃprajānīte, yat saṃprajānīte, tad vijānātīti / tan na vijñāyate kiṃ tāvad ayam eṣām ālambananiyama (18) ukta utāho kṣaṇaniyama iti / āyurūṣmaṇoḥ sāhabhāvye saṃsṛṣṭavacanāt siddhaḥ kṣaṇaniyamaḥ / (19) yac cāpi sūtra uktaṃ, trayāṇāṃ saṃnipātaḥ sparśa iti / tat kathaṃ vijñānaṃ cāsti na ca trayāṇāṃ (20) saṃnipāto na ca sparśa iti / tasmād avaśyaṃ sarvatra vijñāne sparśaḥ sparśasahajā ca vedanaiṣṭavyā / 
	alam atiprasaṅginyā kathayā / prakṛtam evānukramyatām // uktā samāsena caitasikī vedanā // (22) 
		punaś cāṣṭādaśavidhā sā manopavicārataḥ //32cd // (23) 
saiva caitasikī vedanā punar aṣṭādaśamanopavicāravyavasthānād aṣṭādaśadhā bhidyate / punaḥsaṃdhikaraṇaṃ cātra (24) draṣṭavyam / aṣṭādaśa manopavicārāḥ katame / ṣaṭ saumanasyopavicārāḥ, ṣaṭ daurmanasyopavicārāḥ [p.147] ṣaḍ upekṣopavicārāḥ / katham eṣāṃ vyavasthānam / yadi svabhāvataḥ / trayo bhaviṣyanti (2) / saumanasyadaurmanasyopekṣāvicārāḥ / atha saṃprayogataḥ / eko bhaviṣyati / (3) sarveṣāṃ manaḥsaṃprayuktatvāt / athālambanataḥ / ṣaṭ bhaviṣyanti / rūpādiṣaḍviṣayālambanatvāt / (4) tribhir api sthāpanā / teṣāṃ pañcadaśa rūpādyupavicārā asaṃbhinnālambanāḥ pratiniyatarūpādyālambanatvāt / (5) trayo dharmopavicārā ubhayathā / manopavicārā iti ko 'rthaḥ / manaḥ kila (6) pratītyaite saumanasyādayo viṣayān upavicarantīti / viṣayeṣu vā mana upavicārayantīty apare / (7) vedanāvaśena manaso viṣayeṣu punaḥ punar vicāraṇāt / kasmāt kāyikī vedanā na jmanopavicāraḥ / (8) naqiva hy asau mana evāśritā nāpy upavicārikāvikalpakatvād ity ayogaḥ / tṛtīyadhyānasukhasya (9) kasmān manopavicāreṣv agrahaṇam / āditaḥ kila kāmadhātau manobhūmikasukhendriyābhāvāt tatpratidvandvena (10) duḥkhopavicārābhāvāc ceti / yadi manobhūmikā evaite / yat tarhi sūtra uktaṃ, cakṣuṣā rūpāṇi (11) dṛṣṭvā saumanasyasthānīyāni rūpāṇy upavicārantīty evamādiḥ / katham / pañcavijñānakāyābhinirhṛta(12)tvam abhisaṃdhāyaitad uktaṃ, manobhūmikā hy ete / tadyathāaśubhā cakṣurvi(13)jñānābhinirhṛtā ca manobhūmikā ceti / api tu dṛṣṭvā yāvat spṛṣṭveti vacanāt / (14) acodyam evaitat / ye 'py adṛṣṭvā yāvad aspṛṣṭvopavicaranti, te 'pi manopavicārāḥ / itarathā (15) hi kāmadhātau rūpadhātvālambanā rūpādyupavicārā na syuḥ, kāmadhātvālambanāś ca rūpadhātau (16) gandharasaspraṣṭavyopavicārāḥ / yathā tu vyaktataraṃ tathoktaṃ yo 'pi rūpāṇi dṛṣṭvā śabdān upavicarati (17) so 'pi manopavicāraḥ / yathā tv anākulaṃ tathoktam indriyārthasya vyavacchedataḥ / kim asti (18) rūpaṃ yat saumanasyasthānīyam eva syāt, yāvad upekṣāsthānīyam eva / asti saṃtānaṃ niyamayya na (19) tv ālambanam // (20) 
	athaiṣāṃ manopavicārāṇāṃ kati kāmapratisaṃyuktās teṣāṃ ca kati kimālambanā evaṃ yāvad ārūpyapratisaṃyuktā (21) vaktavyāḥ / āha (22) 
		kāme svālambanāḥ sarve (33a) (23) 
kāmadhātau sarve 'ṣṭādaśa santi, sa ca teṣāṃ sarveṣām ālambanam / teṣām eva [p.148] 
		rūpī dvādaśagocaraḥ / (2) 
rūpadhātur dvādaśānām ālambanaṃ ṣaṭ gandharasopavicārān apāsya / tan na tayor abhāvāt / (3) 
		trayāṇām uttaraḥ (33c) (4) 
gocara iti varttate / ārūpyadhātus trayāṇāṃ dharmopavicārāṇām ālambanam / tatra rūpādyābhāvāt // (5) uktāḥ kāmapratisaṃyuktāḥ / (6) 
	rūpapratisaṃyuktā vaktavyāḥ / tatra tāvat (7) 
		dhyānadvaye dvādaśa (33c'd) (8) 
daurmanasyopavicārān apāsya , te ca dvādaśa (9) 
		kāmagāḥ //33d' // (10) 
kāmān gacchantīti kāmagāḥ / kāmadhātum ālambanta ity arthaḥ / dṛṣṭo hi viṣāyāṇāṃ grahaṇārthe (11) gamiprayogaḥ / katham etat gamyate / evam etat gamyata iti / (12) 
		svo 'ṣṭālambanam (34a) (13) 
sa eva rūpadhātus teṣāṃ svaḥ / so 'ṣṭānāṃ manopavicārāṇām ālambanaṃ gandharasopavicārāṃś caturo (14) hitvā / (15) 
		ārūpyo dvayoḥ (34a'b) (16) 
dharmopavicārayor ālambanam / (17) 
		dhyānadvaye tu ṣaṭ / (34b') (18) 
tṛtīyacaturthayor dhyānayoḥ ṣaḍ upekṣopavicārā eva santi nānye / teṣāṃ punar ālambanaṃ (19) 
		kāmāḥ ṣaṇṇāṃ (34c) (20) 
kāmadhātuḥ ṣaṇṇām apy ālambanam / (21) 
		caturṇāṃ svaḥ (34c') (22) 
rūpadhātuś caturṇāṃ, tatra gandharasābhāvāt / (23) 
		ekasyālambanaṃ paraḥ //34d // (24) 
ārūpyadhātur ekasyaiva dharmopavicārasyālambanam / uktā rūpapratisaṃyuktāḥ / [p.149] 
	ārupyapratisaṃyuktā idānīm ucyante / (2) 
		catvāro 'rūppisāmante (35a) (3) 
ākāśānantyāyatanasāmantakam atrārūpisāmantakam / tatra catvāro rūpaśabdaspraṣṭavyadharmopavicārāḥ / (4) te ca (5) 
		rūpuagāḥ (35b) (6) 
caturthaṃ hi dhyānam eṣām ālambanam / yeṣāṃ tat vyavacchinnālambanam asti / yeṣāṃ punaḥ paripiṇḍitāalambanaṃ (7) teṣāṃ tatraika eva saṃbhinnālambano dharmopavicāraḥ / (8) 
		eka ūrdhvagaḥ / (34b') (9) 
eka eva dharmopavicāras tatrārūpyadhātvālambanaḥ / (10) 
		eko maule (35c) (11) 
maule punar ārūpye dharmopavicāra evaiko 'sti, nānyaḥ / sa cāpi (12) 
		svaviṣayaḥ (35c') (13) 
ārūpyadhātvālambana eva / na hi maulānām ārūpyāṇām adharo dhātur ālambanam iti paścāt (14) prativedayiṣāmaḥ / 
	ete ca manopavicārāḥ (15) 
		sarve 'ṣṭādaśa sāsravāḥ //35d // (16) 
nāsti kaścid anāsravaḥ / kaḥ katibhiḥ samanvāgataḥ / kāmadhātūpapanno rūpāvacarasya kuśalasya (17) cittasyālābhī kāmāvacaraiḥ sarvaiḥ prathamadvitīyadhyānabhūmikair aṣṭābhiḥ tṛtīyacaturthadhyānabhūmikaiś (18) caturbhiḥ kliṣṭair gandharasālambanān paryudasya ārūpyāvacareṇaikena kliṣṭenaivālābhī rūpāvacarasya (19) kuśalasya cittasyāvītarāgaḥ sarvaiḥ kāmāvacaraiḥ prathamadhyānabhūmikair daśabhiḥ / caturbhiḥ (20) saumanasyopavicāraiḥ kliṣṭair ghandharasālambanau vyudasya ṣaḍbhir upekṣopavicārair anāgamyabhūmikaiḥ / (21) dvitīyadhyānabhūmikair aṣṭābhiḥ / tṛtīyacaturthadhyānārūpyabyhūmikaiḥ pūrvavat / anayā varttanyā (22) śeṣam anugantavyam / dhyānopapannas tu kāmāvacareṇaikenopekṣādharmopavicāreṇa (23) nirmāṇavdittasaṃprayuktena samanvāgato veditavyaḥ / apara āha / asty evaṃ manopavicārāṇāṃ [p.150] vaibhāṣikīyo 'rthaḥ / sūtrārthas tv anyathā dṛśyate / na hi yo yasmād vīitarāgaḥ, sa tadālambanam upa(2)vicāratīti yuktam / ataḥ sāsravā api na sarve saumanasyādayo manopavicārāḥ / kiṃ tarhi / (3) sāṃkleśikā yair mano viṣayān upavicaratīti / kathaṃ copavicarati / anunīyate ca (4) pratihanyate cāpratisaṃkhyāya copekṣate, yeṣāṃ prtipakṣeṇa ṣaṭ sātatā vihārā bhavanti / cakṣuṣā (5) rūpāṇi dṛṣṭvā naiva sumanā bhavati, na durmanā upekṣako viharati, smṛtimān saṃprajānan evaṃ (6) yāvan manasā dharmān vijñāyeti / na hy arhato laukikaṃ nāsti kuśalaṃ dharmālambanaṃ saumanasyaṃ (7) yat tu tat sāṃkleśikaṃ manasa upavicārabhūtaṃ tasyaiva pratiṣedho lakṣyata iti / punas ta eva (8) saumanasyādayaḥ ṣaṭtriṃśac chāstṛpadāni bhavanti / gardhanaiṣkramyāśritabhedena / tadbhedasya (9) śāstra gamitatvāt / tatra gardhāśritāḥ kliṣṭā naiṣkramyāśritāḥ kuśalāḥ / evam etad vedanākhyaṃ (10) bhavāṅgam anekaprakārabhedaṃ veditavyam / śeṣāṇy aṅgāni punar nocyante / k(11) kiṃ kāraṇam / yasmāt (12) 
		uktaṃ ca vakṣyate cānyat (36a) (13) 
kiṃcid atrāṅgam uktaṃ kiṃcit paścād vakṣyate / tatra vijñānaṃ tāvad uktaṃ (14) 
		''vijñānaṃ prativijñaptir manāayatanaṃ ca tad'' iti / (15) 
ṣaḍāyatanam apy uktaṃ (16) 
		''tadvijñānāśrayā rūpaprassādāś cakṣurādaya'' iti / (17) 
saṃskārā bhavaś ca karmakośasthāne vyākhyāsyante / tṛṣṇopādānāni kleśakośasthāne / 
	sa (18) caiṣa prtītyasamutpādaḥ samāsataḥ kleśakarmavāstūnīti prāk jñāpitam / (19) 
		atra tu kleśa iṣyate / (20) 
		bījavan nāgavan mūlavṛkṣavat tuṣavat tathā //36bcd // (21) 
kim asya ījādibhiḥ sādharmyam / tadyathā bījād aṅkurakāṇḍapatrādīnāṃ prabhavo bhavaty evaṃ kleśāt (22) kleśakarmavastūnām / yathā nāgādhiṣṭhitaṃ saro na śuṣyaty evaṃ kleśanāgādhiṣṭḥitaṃ janmasaraḥ / yathā [p.151] cānapoddhṛtamūlā vanaspatayaś chinnāś chinnāḥ punar api prarohanty evam anapoddhṛtakleśamūlā gatayaḥ / yathā (2) ca vṛkṣāḥ kālena kālaṃ puṣpaphalānāṃ prasotāro bhavanty evaṃ kleśā apy asakṛt kleśakarmavastūnāṃ (3) hetur bhavanti / yathā ca tuṣāvanaddhās taṇḍulāḥ prarohaṇasamarthā bhavanti, na kevalā evaṃ kleśaprāpta'tuṣāvanaddhaṃ (4) karma janmāntaravirohaṇe samarthaṃ bhavati, na kevalam ity evaṃ tāvat kleśo (5) bījādivad veditavyaḥ / (6) 
		tuṣitaṇḍulavat karma, tathaivauṣadhipuṣpavat / (37ab) 
tuṣo 'syāstīti tuṣī / tuṣasthānīyaḥ kleśa uktaḥ / idānīṃ tuṣitaṇḍulasthānīyaṃ karmocyate / (8) santy oṣadhayaḥ phalapākāntā evaṃ karmāṇi vipacya punar vipākān abhinirvarttayante / yathā puṣpaṃ (9) phalotpattāv āsannaṃ kāraṇam evaṃ karmāṇi vipākotpattau veditavyāni / (10) 
		siddhānnapānavad vastu (37c) (11) 
yathā siddham annaṃ pānaṃ ca kevalaṃ paribhogāya kalpyate, na punar virohāya evaṃ vipākākhyaṃ vastu / (12) na hi punar vipākād vipākāntaraṃ janmāntareṣu pravardhate / yadi hi pravardheta mokṣo na syāt / 
	na khalv eṣa janmasaṃtāna evaṃ pratītyasamutpadyamāno bhavacatuṣṭayaṃ nātikrāmati / yad utāntarābhavam upapattibhavaṃ (14) pūrvakālabhavaṃ maraṇabhavaṃ ca / te ca vyākhyātāḥ / (15) 
		tasmin bhavacatuṣṭaye //37d // (16) 
		upapattibhavaḥ kliṣṭaḥ (38a) (17) 
ekāntena / katamena kleśena / (18) 
		sarvakleśaiḥ svabhūmikaiḥ / (38b) (19) 
yadbhūmika' upapattibhavas tadbhūmikair eva sarvakleśaiḥ / na hi sa kleśo 'sti yena pratisaṃdhibandhañh (20) pratividyata ity ābhidhārmikāḥ / kleśair eva tu na paryavasthānaiḥ svatantraiḥ / (21) yady api sāvasthā mandikā yas tu yatrābhikṣṇaṃ carita āsannaś ca tasya tadānīṃ sa eva kleśa (22) upatiṣṭhate / pūrvāvedhāt / antarābhavapratisaṃdhir apy evam avaśyaṃ kliṣṭo veditavyaḥ / (23) 
		tridhānye (38c) (24) 
anye trayo bhavās triprakārāḥ antarābhavādayaḥ kuśalakliṣṭāvyākṛtāḥ / [p.152] 
	athaiṣāṃ bhavānāṃ katamaḥ kiṃpratisaṃyuktaḥ / (2) 
		traya ārūpye (38c') (3) 
antarābhavaṃ varjayitvā / na hy ārūpyadhātuḥ sthānāntaraparicchinno yasya prāptyartham antarābhavo 'bhinirvarteta (4) / kāmarūpadhātvor aparisaṃkhyānāt sarva eva catvāro bhavāḥ santīty anujñāpitaṃ bhavati / (5) ukto yathā sattvānāṃ pratītyasamutpādo vistareṇa / (6) 
	atha kathaṃ sattvānāṃ sthitir bhavatīty āha (7) 
		āhārasthitikaṃ jagat //38d // (8) 
eko dharmo bhagavatā svayam abhijñāyābhisaṃbadhyākhyāto yad uta sarvasattvā āhārasthitikā iti (9) sūtrapadam / ke punar āhārāḥ / catvāra āhārāḥ / kavaḍīkārāhāra prathamaḥ / audārikaḥ (10) sūkṣmaś ca / sūkṣmo 'ntarābhavikānāṃ gandhāhāratvāt / devānāṃ prāthamakalpikānāṃ ca niḥṣyandābhāvāt / (11) tailasyeva sikatāsv aṅgeṣv anupraveśāt / sūkṣmāṇāṃ vā sūkṣmo bālakasaṃsvedajantukādīnām / (12) sparśo dvitīyaḥ / manaḥsaṃcetanā tṛtīyaḥ / vijñānam āhāraś caturthaḥ / (13) tatra punaḥ (14) 
		kavaḍīkāra āhāraḥ kāme (39ab) (15) 
na rūpārūpyadhātvos tadvītarāgasya tatropapatteḥ / sa ca (16) 
		tryāyatnātmakaḥ / (39b') (17) 
kāmāvacarāṇi gandharasaspraṣṭavyāyatanāni sarvāṇy va kavaḍīkāra āhāraḥ / kavaḍīkṛtyābhyavaharaṇāt / (18) mukhanāsikāgrāsavyavacchedataḥ / cchāyātapajvālāprabhāsu teṣāṃ katham āhāratvam / bāhulyena kilaiṣa (19) nirdeśaḥ / yāny api tu nābhyavahriyante, sthitiṃ cāharanti, tāny api sūkṣma āhāraḥ / snānābhyaṅgavad (20) iti / kasmān na rūpāyataqnam āhāraḥ / tad api kavaḍīkṛtyābhyavahriyate / (21) 
		na rūpāyatanaṃ tena svākṣamuktānanugrahāt //39cd // (22) 
āhāro hi nāma ya indriyamahābhūtānām anugrahāya saṃvartate / rūpāyatanaṃ cābhyavaharaṇakāle (23) svam indriyaṃ tanmahābhūtāni vā nānugṛhṇāti' / kuta evānyānyaviṣatvāt / yadāpi ca [p.153] dṛśyamānaṃ' sukhasaumanasye ādadhāti, tadāpi tadālambanaḥ sukhavedanīyaḥ sparśa āhāro bhavati (2) na rūpam / muktānām anāgāmyarhatāṃ sumanojñam apy āhāraṃ paśyatām anugrahābhāvāt / (3) 
		sparśasaṃcetanāvijñā āhārāḥ sāsravās triṣu / (40ab) (4) 
sparśas trikasaṃnipātajaḥ / cetanā manaskarma vijñānaṃ ca sāsravāṇy evāhārāḥ (5) triṣv api dhātuṣu saṃvidyante / kim arthaṃ nānāsravāṇi / yasmāt bhavapoṣaṇārtha' āhārārthaḥ / (6) tāni ca bhavakṣayāyotthitānīti vaibhāṣikāḥ / api tu sūtra uktaṃ catvāra ime āhārā (7) bhūtānāṃ sattvānāṃ sthitaye yāpanāyai saṃbhavaiṣiṇāṃ cānugrahāyeti / na caivam anāsravā dharmā iti (8) nāhārāḥ / (9) 
	bhūtā hi tāvat sattvā upapannā iti jñāyante ' / atha saṃbhavaiṣiṇaḥ katame / (10) 
		manomayaḥ saṃbhavaiṣī, gandharvaś cāntarābhavaḥ //40cd // (11) 
		nivṛttiś ca (41a) (12) 
antarābhavo hy ebhir abhidhānair ukto bhagavatā / sa eva manonirjātaqtvāt manomaya uktaḥ / (13) śukraśoṇitādikaṃ kiṃcid bāhyam anupādāya bhāvāt / saṃbhavaiṣaṇaśīlatvāt saṃbhavaiṣī / gandharvaṇāt (14) gandarvaḥ / upapattyabhimukhatvād abhinirvṛttiḥ / savyābādham' ātmabhāvam abhinirvartya savyābādhe (15) loke upapadyata iti sūtrapadāt / tathāsti pudgalo yasyābhirvṛttisaṃyojanaṃ prahīṇaṃ (16) nopapattisaṃyojanam iti sūtre catuḥkoṣikāt / prthamā koṭir dvidhātuvītarāgasyordhva[ṃ']srotaso (17) 'nāgāminaḥ / dvitīyāntarāparinirvāyiṇaḥ / tṛtīyārthatām / caturthy etān ākārān (18) sthāpayitvā / bbhūtā vārhantaḥ saṃbhavaiṣiṇaḥ satṛṣnāḥ / (19) 
	atha katy āhārāḥ sattvānāṃ sthitaye, kati saṃbhavaiṣiṇām anugrahāya / sarve 'py ubhayatheti vaibhāṣikāḥ / (20) kavaḍīkārāahāro 'pi hi tadrāgiṇāṃ punarbhavāya saṃvartate / uktaṃ hi bhagavatā catvāra (21) ime āhārā rogasya mūlaṃ gaṇḍasya śalyasya jarāmaraṇasya pratyaya iti / manaḥsaṃcetanāpi ceha (22) sthitaye dṛśyate / evaṃ hi varṇayanti / durbhikṣābhyāhatena kila pitrā putrakau saktava iti (23) bhasmanā bhastrāṃ paripūrya kīlake āsajyāśvāsitau / tau ca tāṃ parikalpyantau ciram apy āsitau (24) kenāpi codghāṭitāyāṃ nairāśyam āpannau vyāpannāv iti / punaś ca mahāsamudre [p.154] bhagnayānapātrāḥ puruṣā sthalam iti mahānte phenajpiṇḍaṃ pradrutāḥ āmṛśya cainaṃ nirāśā uparemur iti / (2) saṃgītaparyāye coktaṃ mahāsamudrād audārikāḥ prāṇino jalāt sthalam abhiruhya sikatāsthale 'ṇḍāni (3) sthāpayitvā sikatābhir avaṣṭabhya punar api mahāsamudre 'vataranti / tatra yāsāṃ mātṛrṇām aṇḍāny ārabhya (4) smṛtir na muṣyate tāny aṇḍāni na pūtībhavanti, yāsāṃ tu muṣyate tāni pūtībhavanti / tad etan na (5) varṇayatni sautrāntikāḥ / mā bhūt parakīyeṇāhāraṇāhāra iti / evaṃ tu varṇayanti / (6) yeṣām aṇḍānāṃ mātaram ārabhya smṛtir na muṣyate, tāni na pūtībhavanti / yeṣāṃ tu muṣyate tāni (7) pūtībhavanti / tasyāḥ sparśāvasthāyāḥ smarantīti / (8) 
	atha kasmāc catvāra evāhārāḥ / nanu ca sarva eva sāsravā dharmā bhavānāṃ poṣakāḥ / (9) yady apy etad evaṃ, tathāpi prādhānyenoktam (10) 
		iha puṣṭyartham āśrayāśritayor dvayam / (11) 
		dvayam anyabhavākṣepanivṛttyarthaṃ yathākramam //41a'bcd // (12) 
āśrayo yi sendriyaḥ kāyaḥ / tasya puṣṭaye kavaḍīkārāhārāḥ / āśritāś cittacaittās teṣāṃ puṣṭaye (13) sparśaḥ / anayos tāvad ihotpannasya bhavasya poṣaṇe (15) prādhānyam / ataś catvāra uktāḥ / 
	pūrvakau hi dvau dhātrīsthānīyau jātasya poṣakatvāt / (16) uttarau mātṛsthānīyau janakatvād iti / yaḥ kaścit kavaḍīkāraḥ sarvaḥ sa āhāraḥ / syāt (17) kavaḍīkāro nāhāra iti catuṣkoṭikam / prathamā koṭiryaṃ kavaḍīkāraṃ pratītyendriyāṇām apacayo (18) bhavati mahābhūtānāṃ ca paribhedaḥ / dvitīyā koṭis traya āhārāḥ / tṛtīyā yaṃ kavaḍīkāraṃ (19) pratītyendriyāṇām upacayo bhavati mahābhūtānāṃ ca vṛddhiḥ / caturthy etān ākārān sthāpayitvā / (20) evaṃ sparśādibhir api yathāyogaṃ catuḥkoṭikāni karttavyāni / syāt (21) sparśādīn pratītyendriyāṇām upacayo mahābhūtānāṃ ca vṛddhir na ca ta āhārāḥ / syād anyabhūmikān (22) anāsravāṃś ca / yo 'pi hi paribhukto bhoktur bādhām ādadhāti so 'py āhāra eva āpāte 'nugrahāt (23) / dvayor hi kālayor āhāra āhārakṛtyaṃ karoti paribhujyamāno jīryaṃś ceti vaibhāṣikāḥ / (24) atha kasyāṃ gatau katy āhārāḥ / sarvāsu sarve / evaṃ yoniṣv api kathaṃ narakeṣu kavaḍīkāra (25) āhāraḥ / pradīptāyaspiṇḍāḥ kvathitaṃ ca tāmram / yady upaghātako 'py āhāro bhavati catuḥkoṭikaṃ [p.155] bādhyate prakaraṇagranthaś ca kavaḍīkāra āhāraḥ katamaḥ / yaṃ kavaḍīkāraṃ pratītyendriyāṇām upacayo (2) bhavati mahābhūtānāṃ ca vṛddhir yāpanāanuyāpaneti vistareṇa yāvad vijñānam / upacayāhārābhisaṃdhi(3)vacanād avirodhaḥ / apacayāhāras tu narakeṣu manuṣyavat kavaḍīkārāhārasadbhāvād yuktaṃ pāñcagatikatvam (5) / uktaṃ bhagavatā yaś ca bāhyakānām ṛṣīṇāṃ kāmebhyo vītarāgāṇāṃ śataṃ bhojayed yaś caikaṃ (6) jambūṣaṇḍagataṃ pṛthagjanam / ato dānād idaṃ dānaṃ mahāphalataram iti / (7) ko 'yaṃ jambūṣaṇḍagato nāma pṛthagjanaḥ / jambūdvīpanivāsinaḥ kukṣimanta ity eke / tad etan na (8) yuktam ekam iti vacanāt / kaś cātra viśeṣaḥ / syād yadi bhūyasaḥ pṛthagjanān bhojayitvā bhūyaḥ (9) puṇyaṃ syāt nālpīyaso vītarāgān iti / saṃnikṛṣṭo bodhisattva ity apare / tad etan na varṇayanti (10) bahutaraṃ hi tasmai dattvā puṇyaṃ nārhatkoṭibhya iti /ato nirvedhabhāgīyalābhi pṛthagjana eṣo 'bhipretaḥ (11) iti vaibhāṣikāḥ / na tv ayam anvarthā saṃjñā nāpi kvacit paribhāṣitā sūtre śāstre vā / (12) jambūṣaṇḍagato nirvedhabhāgīyalābhīti parikalpa evāyaṃ kevalaḥ / bodhisattva eva tv eṣu jambīdvīpa(13)ṣaṇḍeṣu niṣaṇṇo yujyate / sa hi pṛthagjana kāmavairāgyasaṃbandhena tadānīṃ tebhyo viśiṣyamāṇa (14) ukta iti / anantebhyo 'pi hi sa tebhyo viśiṣyamāṇaḥ / śatagrahaṇaṃ tu pūrvādhikārāt / (15) itthaṃ caitad evaṃ yad evaṃ paryudasya bāhyakebhya eva srotāapattiphalapratipannakaṃ viśeṣayāṃbabhūva / (16) anyathā hi jambūṣaṇḍagatād eva vyaśeṣayiṣyat / uito yathā sattvānāṃ pratītyasamutpādo yathā(17)vasthitiś cyutir apy uktā yathāyuḥkṣayādibhiḥ / (18) 
	idaṃ idānīṃ vaktavyam / katamasmin vijñāne varttamāne cyutyupapattī bhavata ity āha (19) 
		chedasaṃdhānavairāgyahānicyutyupapattayaḥ / (20) 
		manovijñāna eveṣṭāḥ (42abc) (21) 
kuśalamūlasamucchedaḥ kuśalamūlapratisaṃdhānaṃ dhātubhūmivairāgyaṃ parihāṇiś cyutir upapattiś ca, ete (22) ṣaṭ dharmāḥ manovijñāna eveṣyante nānyatra / upapattivacanād antarābhavapratisaṃdhir apy uktārūpo (23) veditavyaḥ / vedanāyāṃ tu [p.156] 
		upekṣāyāṃ cyutodbhavau //42d // (2) 
cyutir eva cyutam upapattir udbhavaḥ / etāv aduḥkhāsukhāyāṃ vedanāyāṃ bhavatas, tasyā apaṭutvāt / (3) itare hi vedane paṭvyau(') / na ca paṭuvijñāne cyutyupapattī yujyete / tatrāpi ca manovijñāne 'pi (4) 
	naikāgrācittayor etau (43a) (5) 
cyutodbhavāv iti vartate / na hi samāhitacittasyāsti cyutir upapattir vā / visabhāgabhūmikatvād abhisaṃskārikatvād (6) anugrāhakatvāc ca / nāpy acittasya (acittakasya Y) sā na hy acittaka upakramituṃ śakyeta (śakyate') / yadā (7) cāsyāśrayo vipariṇantum ārabhate, tadāśrayapratibaddhaṃ cittaṃ saṃmukhībhūya paścāt pracyaveta (8) nānyathā / upapattau ca cittacchedahetvabhāvād v inā ca kleśenānupapatter ayuktam acittakatvam / (9) 
	maraṇabhavas triprakāra ity uktam / arhaṃs tu (10) 
		nirvātyavyākṛtadvaye / (43b) (11) 
airyāpathike vipākacitte vā / asti cet kāmadhātau vipāka upekṣā / nāsti ced airyāpathika (12) eva / kim artham avyākṛta eva nānyasmin / tad dhi cittacchedānukūlaṃ durbalatvāt / (13) 
	atha miryamāṇasya kasmin śarīrapradeśe vijñānaṃ nirudhyate / sakṛnmaraṇe samanaskaṃ kāyendriyaṃ (14) sahasāntardhīyate / yadi tu krameṇa cyavate tadā (tataḥ!) 
		kramacyutau pādanābhihṛdayeṣu manaścyutiḥ //43cd // (16) 
		adhonṛsuragājānāṃ (44a) (17) 
adho gacchatīty adhogā apāyagāminaḥ / nṛn' gacchantīti nṛgā manuṣyagāminaḥ / surān (18) gacchantīti suragā devagāminaḥ / teṣāṃ yathāsaṃkhyaṃ pādayor nābhyāṃ hṛdaye ca vijñānaṃ saṃnirudhyate / (19) na punar jāyanta ity ajā arhantaḥ / teṣām api hṛdaye vijñānaṃ nirudhyate / mūrdhnīty apare / kāyendriyasya (20) teṣu nirdhāt kāyendriyaṃ hi mriyamāṇasya tapta ivopale jalaṃ niṣṭhyūtaṃ saṃkocam āpadyamānaṃ pādādiṣv antardhīyata iti / evaṃ ca punaḥ krameṇa maraṇam, prāyeṇa marmacchedavedanābhyāṃ' (22) hatānāṃ jāyate (23) 
		marmacchedas tv abādibhiḥ / (44b) (24) 
śarīrapradeśāḥ kecid upahanyamānā maraṇam ānayanti / te hy etad ucyante [p.157] marmāṇīti / tāni cāptejovāyudhātūnām anyatamenātiprāyaṃ gatena niśitaśastrasaṃpātasyārdhināḍī(2)tīvrābhir vedanābhiḥ chidyanta iva, na ca punas tāni kāṣṭhādivac chidyante, chinnavad vā punar na ceṣṭanta (3) iti cchinnāny ucyante / kasmān na pṛthivīdhātunā / caturthadoṣābhāvāt / vātapittaśeṣmāṇo (4) hi trayo doṣāḥ / te cāptejovāyudhātupradhānā yathāyogam iti / bhājanalokasaṃvartanīsādharmyeṇety (5) apare / deveṣu nāsti marmacchedaḥ / kiṃ tu cyavanadharmaṇo devaputrasya pañcopanimittāni (6) prādurbhavanti / vastrāṇām ābharaṇānāṃ ca manojñaḥ śabdo niścarati, parīraprabhā mandībhavati (7) snātasyodabindavaḥ kāye saṃtiṣṭhante capalātmanāapy ekatra viṣaye buddhir avatiṣṭhate unmeṣanimeṣau cākṣaṇoḥ (8) saṃbhavataḥ / etāni tu vyabhicārīṇi / pañca punar nimittāni maraṇaṃ nātivarttante / vāsāṃsi (9) kliśyanti, mālā mlāyanti, kakṣābhyāṃ svedo mucyate, daurgandhyaṃ kāye 'vakrāmati, sve cāsane devaputro (10) nābhiramate / 
	so 'yaṃ sattvaloka evam utpadyamānas tiṣṭḥan cyavamānaś ca triṣu rāśiṣu vyava'sthāpito (11) bhagavatā / trayo rāśayaḥ / samyaktvaniyato rāśir, mithyātvaniyato rāśir aniyato (12) rāśir iti / tatra punaḥ (13) 
		samyaṅgmithyātvaniyatā āryānantaryakāriṇaḥ //44cd // (14) 
samyaktvaṃ katamat / yat tat paryādāya rāgaprahāṇaṃ paryādāya dveṣaprahāṇaṃ paryādāya mohaprahāṇaṃ paryādāya (15) sarvakleśaprahāṇam idam ucyate samyaktvam iti sūtram / āryāḥ katame / yeṣām anāsravo mārga (16) utpannaḥ / ārād yātāḥ pāpakebhyo dharmebhya ity āryāḥ / ātyantikavisaṃyogaprāptilābhāt / ete hi (17) kleśakṣaye niyatatvāt samyaktvaniyatā mokṣabhāgīyalābhino 'py avaśyaṃ parinirvāṇadharmāṇa iti / (18) kasmān na samyaktve niyatāḥ / te hi mithyātve 'pi niyatā bhaveyuḥ / na ca te (19) kālaniyamena samyaktve niyatā yathā saptakṛtvaḥparamādayaḥ / mithyātvam katamat / (20) narakāḥ pretās tiryañca idam ucyate mithyātvam / tatrānantaryakāriṇo narake niyatatvān mithyātva(21)niyatāḥ / niyatebhyo 'nye 'niyatā iti siddham / pratyayāpekṣaṃ hi teṣām ubhayabhāktvam anubhayabhāktvaṃ (22) ca / 
	uktaḥ sattvaloko bhajanoka idānīṃ vaktavyaḥ / (23) 
		tatra bhājanalokasya saṃniveśam uśanty adhaḥ / (24) 
		lakṣaṣoḍaśakodvedham asaṃkhyaṃ vāyumaṇḍalam //45// [p.158] 
trisāhasramahāsāsralokadhātor evaṃ saṃniveśam icchanti / yadutākāśapratiṣṭham adhastād vāyumaṇḍalam abhivṛttaṃ (2) sarvasattvānām [ /] karmādhipatyena / tasya yojanalakṣāṇāṃ (3) ṣoḍaśakam udvedhaḥ, pariṇāhenāsaṃkhyaṃ, tathā ca dṛḍhaṃ yan mahālagno 'pi vajreṇa bhettum aśaktaḥ / (4) tasyopariṣṭāt (5) 
		apām ekādaśaodvedhaṃ sahasrāṇi ca viṃśatiḥ (46ab) (6) 
maṇḍalam iti varttate / tasmin vāyumaṇḍale sattvānāṃ karmbhir meghāḥ saṃbhūyākṣamātrābhir dhārābhir abhivarṣanti (7) / tat bhavaty apāṃ maṇḍalam / tasya yojanānām ekādaśalakṣāṇy ūrdhve 'dho viṃśatiś ca (8) sahasrāṇi / kathaṃ tā āpo na tiryag visravanti / sattvānāṃ karmādhipatyena / yathā hi (9) bhuktaṃ pītam annaṃ pānaṃ ca nāpakvaṃ pakvāśayam āpatatīty eke / kusūlanyāyena vāyunā saṃdhāryanta (10) iti nikāyāntarīyāḥ / tāś ca punar āpaḥ sattvānāṃ karmaprabhāvasaṃbhūtair vāyubhir āvarttyamānā upariṣṭāt kāñcanībhavanti (11) pakvakṣīra'śarībhāvayogena / tat bhavaty apāṃ maṇḍalam / (12) 
		aṣṭalakṣocchrayaṃ paścāc cheṣaṃ bhavati kāñcanam //46cd // (13) 
kiṃ ca śeṣam / trayo lakṣāḥ sahasrāṇi ca viṃśatiḥ / sā kāñcanamayī mahī bhavaty apām upariṣṭāt / (14) ukto jalakāñcanamaṇḍaloccrāyaḥ / (15) 
		tiryak trīṇi sahasrāṇi sārdhaṃ śatacatuṣṭayam / (16) 
		lakṣadvādaśakaṃ caiva jalakāñcanamaṇḍalam //47// (17) 
samānaṃ hy etad ubhayaṃ vistārataḥ / (18) 
		samantatas tu triguṇaṃ (48a) (19) 
smantataḥ parikṣepeṇa tu parigaṇyamānaṃ triguṇaṃ jāyate ṣaṭtriṃśallakṣā daśasahasrāṇi sārdhāni ca (20) trīṇi śatāni yojanānām / yac ca tat kāñcanamayaṃ mahīmaṇḍalam apām upariṣṭāt saṃniviṣṭaṃ (21) 
		tatra merur yugandharaḥ / (22) 
		īśādhāraḥ khadirakaḥ sudarśanagiris tathā //48bcd // [p.159] 
		aśvakarṇo vinitako nimindharagiriḥ (49ab) (2) 
itīme kāñcanamaṇḍalapratiṣṭhā aṣṭau mahāparvatāh / madhye sumeruḥ / śeṣāḥ sumeruparivārāvasthitāḥ' / (3) tasyānyasaptaparvataprākāraparikṣiptasya yo bāhyaḥ parvate nimindaragiriḥ / (4) 
		tataḥ / (5)

		sapta haimāḥ sa āyasaḥ //49d // (10) 
yugandharādayaḥ sapta parvatāḥ sauvarṇāś cakravāḍaḥ śastrakaḥ / (11) 
		catūratnamayo meruḥ (50a) (12) 
suvarṇamayo rūpyamayo vaidūryamayaḥ sphaṭikamayaś ca yathāsaṃkhyaṃ caturṣu pārśveṣu / yac ca yanmayaṃ pārśvaṃ (13) sumeros tasyānubhāvena tadvarṇaṃ tasyāṃ diśi nabho dṛśyate / jāmbūdvīpakam asya pārśvaṃ vaidūryamayaṃ (14) varṇayanti / tasyeha prabhānurāgeṇa vaidūryamayaṃ nabho dṛśyata iti / (15) 
	atha kathaṃ teṣāṃ saṃb havaḥ / kāñcanamayyāṃ pṛthivyāṃ punar vāridhārā (16) atipatanti / tā hy āpo nānāvidhabījagarbhā bahuvidhaprabhāvabhinnair vāyubhir mathyamānās tāṃ tāṃ (17) jātiṃ pariṇamayanti / evaṃ ca punaḥ pariṇamayanti yadbhinnajātīyasya kāryaviśeṣotpattāv asamavasdhānena (18) pratyayībhavanti / na tu khalu yathā saṃkhyānāṃ pariṇāmaḥ / kathaṃ ca sāṃkhyānāṃ (19) pariṇāmaḥ / avasthitasya dravyasya dharmāntaranivṛttau dharmāntaraprādurbhāva iti / kaś cātra doṣaḥ / sa eva hi dharmī na saṃvidyate yasyāvasthitasya dharmāṇāṃ pariṇāmaḥ kalpyeta / kaś caivam āha (21) dharmebhyo 'nyo dharmīti / tasyaiva tu dravyasyānyathībhāvamātraṃ pariṇāmaḥ / evam apy ayuktam / (22) kim atrāyuktam / tad eva cedaṃ na cedaṃ tatheti apūrvaiṣā vāyo' yuktiḥ / evaṃ ca punaḥ saṃbhūtāḥ suvarṇādayaḥ (23) karmaprabhāvāt preritair vāyubhiḥ samāhṛtya rāśīkriyante / sa ete parvatāś ca bhavanti dvīpāś ca / te punaḥ (24) sumervādayaś cakravāḍaparyantāḥ parvatāḥ [p.160] 
		jale 'śitisahasrake / (2) 
		magnāḥ (50bc) (3) 
kāñcanamayyāḥ pṛthivyā upary aśītiyojanasahasrāṇy udakaṃ tatra te magnāḥ / (4) 
		ūrdhvaṃ jalāt merur bhūyo 'śītisahasrakaḥ //50c'd // (5) 
iti hi meroḥ ṣoḍaśa(Pradhan:ṣaṣṭi)yojanaśatasahasraṃ samucchrayaḥ / (6) 
		ardhārdhahānir aṣṭāsu (51a) (7) 
jalād ūrdhvaṃ yāvan sumerus tato 'rdhena yugandharaś catvāriṃśad yojanasahasrāṇi / tato 'rdhena īśādhara (8) ity evamanyeṣv apy ardhārdhahānir veditavyā yāvan nimindharārdhenārdhatrayodaśottarāṇi trīṇi yojanaśatāni (9) cakravāḍaḥ / (10) 
		samocchrāyaghanāś ca te / (51b) (11) 
yāvān eva caiṣāṃ jalād ūrdhvaṃ samuccrāyas (Pradhan:samucchāyas) tāvān eva ghano vistāra ity arthaḥ / (12) 
		śītāḥ saptāntarāṇy eṣāṃ (51c) (13) 
eṣāṃ ca nimindharāntānāṃ parvatānāṃ saptāntarāṇi sapta śītā ucyante pūrṇā aṣṭāṅgopetasya (14) pānīyasya / tad dhi pānīyaṃ śītalaṃ ca svādu ca laghu ca mṛdu cācchaṃ ca niṣpratikaṃ (15) ca pibataś ca kaṇṭhaṃ na kṣiṇoti pītaṃ ca kukṣiṃ na vyābādhate / tāsāṃ ca punaḥ (16) 
		ādyāśītisahasrikā //51d // (17) 
sumeruyugandharāntaraṃ prathamā śītā / aśitir yojanasahasrāṇi vaipulyena / (18) 
		ābhyantaraḥ samudro 'sau (52a) (19) 
dvau hi samudrāv ābhyantaro bhāhyaś ca / tadāsau śītābhyantaraḥ samudraḥ (20) 
		triguṇaḥ sa tu pārśvataḥ / (52b) (21) 
aśītir yojanasahasrāṇy asya vaipulyam uktam / pārśvatas triguṇo bhavati yugandharatīreṇa gaṇyamānaḥ (22) catvāriṃśat sahasrāṇi lakṣadvayaṃ ca / [p.161] 
		ardhārdhenāparāḥ śītāḥ (52c) (2) 
yugandharasyeṣādharasya cāntaraṃ dvitīyā śītā ardhena prathamāyāś catvāriṃśat (3) yojanasahasrāṇi / tato 'rdhena punas tṛtīyety evam ardhārdhenāparāḥ śītā bhavanti / yāvad ardhatrayodaśa(4) śatāni saptamī śītāḥ / dairghyaṃ tu tāsāṃ na parisaṃkhyātam atibahuprakarṣavisarpaṇāt / (5) 
		śeṣaṃ bāhyo mahodadhiḥ (Pradhan:dadheḥ) //52d // (6) 
kiṃ śeṣam / nimindharacakravāḍayor antaram / tad dhi bāhyo mahāsamudro lavaṇaḥ pūrṇaḥ (7) kṣārodakasya / sa khalu vistāreṇa yojanānāṃ (8) 
		lakṣatrayaṃ sahasrāṇi viṃśatir dve ca (53ab) (9)

		tatra tu / (11)

dvisāhasrāṇi trīṇi pārśvānyasya śakaṭasyevākṛtiḥ / tasya ca madhye kāñcanamayyāṃ pṛthivyāṃ (13) vajrāsanam abhirvṛttaṃ yasminn niṣadya sarve bodhisattvā vajropamaṃ samādhim utpādayanti / na hi tam anya (14) āśrayaḥ pradeśo vā soḍhuṃ samarthaḥ / (15) 
		sārdhatriyojanaṃ tv ekaṃ (54a) (16) 
caturtham asya pārśvaṃ sārdhāni trīṇi yojanāni / ata eva hy asau śakaṭākṛtiḥ / (17) 
		prāgvideho 'rdhacandravat / (54b) (18) 
itaḥ pūrveṇa sumerupārśve pūrvavideho (Pradhan:pūrvaṃ videho) dvīpaḥ / so 'rdhacandra ivābhinirvṛttaḥ / parimāṇatas tu (19) 
		pārśvatrayaṃ tathāsya (54c) (20) 
yathā jambūdvīpasya dve dve yojanasahasre / (21) 
		ekaṃ sārdhaṃ triśatayojanam //54c'd // (22)

itaḥ paścimena sumerupārśve 'paragodānīyo dvīpaḥ sārdhāni sapta yojanasahasrāṇi sākalyena / (2) maṇḍalaś cāsau pūrṇacandravat / (3)

		aṣṭau caturasraḥ kuruḥ samaḥ //55c'd // (6) 
iha uttareṇa sumerupārśve uttarakurudvīpaḥ / so 'ṣṭau yojanasahasrāṇi sākalyena caturasraḥ / (7) kṛtyāpīṭhikāvat' / sarveṣu ca pārśveṣu samo yathaikaṃ pārśvaṃ dviyojanasahasre / tathānyāni (8) stokam api nādhikam / yaś ca dvīpo yadākṛtis tadākṛtīny eva tatra manuṣyāṇāṃ mukhāni / (9)

tatra dehavidehau pūrvavidehaparivārau (Pradhan:bau) / kurukauravau uttarakuroḥ / śāṭhottaramantirṇāv aparagodānīyasya (13) / cāmarāvarau jambūdvīpasya / sarve manuṣyair āvāsitāḥ / eko rakṣasair ity apare / (14) 
		ihottareṇa kīṭādrinavakād dhimavān / (57ab) (15) 
ihaiva jambūdvīpe uttareṇāsya jambūdvīpasya kṛṣṇaparvatās trayas tān atikramyāpare trayaḥ (14) punaś ca traya iti navabhyaḥ kīṭaparvatebhyaḥ pareṇa himavān parvataḥ / (17) 
		tataḥ / (18)

tasmād dhimavataḥ pareṇānavataptaṃ nāma saro gandhamādanādarvāk yataś catasro nadyaḥ sravanti gaṅgā (20) sindhuḥ śītā vakṣuś ca / tasya pañcāśadyojanāni vistāraḥ / pañcāśadāyāmaḥ / pūrṇam aṣṭāṅgasyā(21)ṅgopetasyāmbhasaḥ / durgamaṃ ca manuṣyāṇām anṛddhimatām / tasyaiva cāntike jāmbūr abhinirvṛttā (22) madhurasvādūni yasyāḥ phalāni / tasyā adhikāreṇāyaṃ jambūdvīpa iti khyātaḥ / tatphalādhikāreṇa (23) vā jambūdvīpa iti / (24) 
	narakāḥ kasmin avakāśe, kiyatpramāṇaś ca / [p.163] 
		adhaḥ sahasrair viṃśatyā tanmātro 'vīcir asya hi / (58ab) (2) 
asyaiva jambūdvīpasyādho viṃśatyā yojanasahasrair avīcir mahānarako viṃśatisahasrapramāṇa (3) evodvedhavistārābhyām evam asyādharatalam itaś catvāriṃśatā yojanasahasrair bhavati / duḥkhanirantaratvād avīciḥ / (4) anyeṣu sāntaraṃ duḥkham / tadyathā saṃjīve cchinnāvabhinnasaṃpiṣṭaśarīrāṇāṃ śītalāvāyavo vānti, (5) punar api tān sattvān saṃjīvayanti / ata eva saṃjīvaḥ / nāsmin sukhavīcir astīty avīcir ity apare / (6) anyeṣv api sukhā vedanā vipāko nāsti / naiḥṣyandikī na vāryate / (7) 
		tadūrdhvaṃ sapta narakāḥ (58c) (8) 
tasmād avīcer ūrdhvaṃ sapta narakāḥ upary upari saṃniviṣṭāḥ / pratāpanas, tāpano, mahārauravo, rauravaḥ, (9) saṃghātaḥ, kālasūtraḥ, saṃjīvaś ca / avīcipārśveṣv ity apare / te punaḥ (10)

uktaṃ hi bhagavatā (12)
