ṣoḍaśotsadāḥ katame / (19) 
		kukūlaṃ kuṇapaṃ cātha kṣuramārgādikaṃ nadī / (20) 
		teṣāṃ caturdiśaṃ (59abc) (21) 
dvāre dvāre teṣāṃcatvāra utsadāḥ / kukūlaṃ jānumātram [ /] yatra teṣāṃ sattvānāṃ nikṣipte saṃśīryate (22) tvaṅmāṃsaśoṇitam utkṣipte pāde punar api saṃjāyate tvaṅmāṃsaśoṇitam / kuṇapaṃ gūthamṛttikā (23) yatra nyaṅkuṭā nāma prāṇinaḥ prativasanti sarvaśvetāḥ kṛṣṇaśirasaḥ sūcimukhās teṣāṃ sattvānāṃ (24) yāvad asthīni bhindanti / kṣuradhārācito mahāpathaḥ yatra teṣāṃ sattvānām [p.164] anvākrāmatāṃ nikṣipte pāde saṃchidyate tvaṅmāṃsaśoṇitam iti pūrvavat / asipatravanaṃ yatra teṣāṃ (2) sattvānāṃ tīkṣṇā asayaḥ saṃnipatanti aṅgapratyaṅgāny avakṛntanti, śyāmaśabalāś ca śvāno (3) bhakṣayanti / ayaḥśālmalīvanaṃ tīkṣṇaṣoḍaśāṅgulakaṇṭakam / teṣāṃ sattvānām abhirohatāṃ kaṇṭakā (4) avāṅmukhībhavantaḥ kāyaṃ bhindanti avataratāṃ cordhvībhavantaḥ / ayastuṇḍāś ca vāyasā akṣīṇy utpāṭyotpāṭya (5) bhakṣayanti / tad etat kṣuramārgādikaṃ trayaṃ śastrābhinipātasāmānyād ekīkriyante (Pradhan: pātaṃ sāmānyād) / (6) caturtha utsado nadī vaitaraṇī pūrṇā taptasya kṣārodakasya tasyāṃ te sattvā asiśaktiprāsahastaiḥ (7) puruṣair ubhābhyāṃ tīrābhyāṃ prativāryamāṇā ūrdhvam api gacchantaḥ svidyante pacyante adhas tiryag api (8) gacchantaḥ svidyante pacyante / tadyathā bahūdakāyāṃ sthālyām agnāv adhiśritāyāṃ tilataṇḍulādayaḥ / (9) sā hi mahānarakasya parikhevotpannā / ta ete catvāra utsadā digbhedena ṣoḍaśocyante / (10) adhikayātanāsthānitvād utsadāḥ ity ucyante / narakeṣu hi patitā eteṣu punar yāty ante / (11) narakāvarodhād ūrdhvam eteṣu sīdanty utsadā ity apare / (12) 
	praśnāt praśnāntaram upajāyate / kiṃ te narakapālāḥ sattvasamkhyātā (13) utāho neti / nety eke / katham idānīṃ ceṭṭante / sattvānāṃ karmabhir vivarttanīvāyubījavat / (14) yat tarhi bhadantadharmasubhītinoktaṃ (15) 
		krodhanāḥ kruruakarmāṇaḥ, pāpābhirucayaś ca ye / (16) 
		duḥkhiteṣu ca nandanti, jāyante yamarākṣasā iti // (17) 
ye te yamenānuśiṣṭāḥ sattvān narakeṣu prakṣipanti, ta ete yamarākṣasā uktā, na tu ye kāraṇāḥ (18) kārayantīti / sattvasaṃkhyātā ity eke / tasyedānīṃ karmaṇaḥ kava vipākaḥ / teṣv eta narakeṣu hi (19) ānantaryakāriṇāṃ vipākāvakāśas tatra teṣāṃ ko vipratibandhaḥ / katham agninā na dahyante / (20) agner nūnaṃ karmabhiḥ kṛtāvadhitvāt / bhūtaviśeṣanivṛtter vā / (21) 
	ime tāvad uṣṇā aṣṭau mahānarakā ucyante / (22) 
		śītā anye 'ṣṭāv arbudādayaḥ //59c'd // (23) 
anye śītanarakā aṣṭau / tadyathā arbudo nirarbudaḥ aṭaṭo hahavaḥ huhuvaḥ utpalaḥ padmo mahāpadmaś ca / [p.165] 
teṣāṃ sattvānāṃ tīvraśītābhihatānāṃ kāyaśabdavikārānurūpāṇy etāni nāmāni / te 'py asyaiva jambū(2)dvīpasyādhastāt mahānarakānāṃ tiryak / kuta iyato jambūdvīpasyādhastād avīcyādīnām avakāśaḥ / (3) dhānyarāśivad adho viśālā hi dvīpāḥ / ata eva mahāsamudro 'nupūrvanimnaḥ / (4) itīme ṣoḍaśa narakāḥ sarve sattvadarmādhipatyanivṛttāḥ / pratyekaṃ narakās tu svaiḥ svaiḥ karmabhir abhirvṛttāḥ / (5) bahūnāṃ sattvānāṃ dvayor ekasya vā / teṣām anekaprakalpo bhedaḥ sthānaṃ cāniyataṃ nadīparvatamarupradeśeṣv (6) anyeṣu vādhaś ca bhāvāt / eṣa tāvan narakabhājanānāṃ saṃniveśaḥ / tiryañcaḥ sthalajalākāśagocarāḥ / (7) teṣāṃ kila mūlaṃ sthānaṃ mahāsamudras tato 'jyatra visṛtā iti / pratānāṃ yamo rājā / tasya (8) jambūdvīpasyādhastāt pañcayojanaśatāntaraparicchinnā rājadhānī teṣāṃ mūlasthānaṃ tato 'nyatra (9) visṛtāḥ / kecit pretā maharddhikā daivīm iva śriyam udvahanti / śeṣā yathā pretāvadāne / (10) 
	athemau candrārkau kasmin pratiṣṭhitau / vāyau / vāyavo 'ntarīkṣe sarvasattvasādhāraṇakarmā(11)dhipatyanivṛttā āvartavat sumeruṃ parivartante / candrārkatārāṇāṃ vordhvacāraḥ /

yugandharagirer mūrdhnā samaṃ vahataḥ / kiṃpramāṇau (Pradhan: kiṃ pramāṇau) / yathākramaṃ (15) 
		pañcāśat saikayojanau / (60b) (16) 
pañcāśat yojanāni candramaṇḍalasya pramāṇam / saikāni pañcāśat sūryamanḍḍalasya pramāṇam / (17) ekapañcāśad ity arthaḥ / tārakavimānānāṃ yasyālpapramāṇaṃ pramāṇaṃ tasya (18) krośaḥ / sūryavimānasyādhastāt bahiḥ sphaṭikamaṇḍalaṃ taijasam abhinirvṛttaṃ tāpanaṃ prakāśanaṃ ca / (19) candravimānasyādhastād āpyaṃ śītalaṃ bhāsvaraṃ ca / prāṇināṃ karmabhir dṛṣṭiśarīraphalapuṣpasasyoṣadhī(20) nām anugrahārtham upaghātakārthaṃ yathāsaṃbhavam / caturdvīpake ekaś candramāḥ kṛtyaṃ karoty ekaḥ sūryaḥ / (21) kiṃ punaś caturdvīpeṣu sūryo yugapat kṛtyaṃ karoti / nety āha / kiṃ tarhi / teṣu (22)

		prāvṃnmāse dvitīye 'ntyanavamyāṃ vardhate niśā / (61ab) (2)

yadā rātrir vardhate, tadā divaso hīyate / yadā rātrir hīyate, tadā divaso vardhate / kiyatyā (8) mātrayā vardhate / (9)

jambūdvīpasya dakṣiṇaṃ pārśvaṃ gacchati bhāskare rātrivṛddhiḥ uttaraṃ gacchaty aharvṛddhiḥ / (13)

yadā hi sauryasya' vimānasyāsanne cāndramasaṃ vimānaṃ vahati, tadā kila sauryo bhāsas tasmin (16) vimāne patanti / tato 'parapārśve chāyā patantī vikalaṃ maṇḍalaṃ darśayatīti prājñaptiko nirdeśaḥ / (17) vāhayogaḥ sa tādṛśo bhavati, yat kadācid vimānasyārdhaṃ dṛśyata iti pūrvācāryāḥ / 
	athaitāni (18) sūryādivimānāni katame sattvā adhyāvasanti / devāś cāturmahārājakāyikāḥ / kim etāny eva (19) teṣāṃ sthānāni / vimānavāsinām etāni / bhūminivāsināṃ punaḥ sumerupariṣaṇḍādīni / (20) kati cāsya pariṣaṇḍāḥ kiyanto vā / (21)

		ṣoḍaśāṣṭau sahasrāṇi catvāri dve ca nirgatāḥ //63cd // (2)

prathamāyāṃ pariṣaṇḍāyāṃ karoṭapāṇayo nāma yakṣāḥ prativasanti / dvitīyāyāṃ mālādhārās (7) tṛtīyāyāṃ sadāmadāḥ sadāmattāḥ / sarva ete caturmahārājakāyikāḥ / katurthyāṃ tu (8) catvāro mahārājāḥ svayaṃ prativasanti tatparicārāś ca / atas tasyāṃ mahārājakāyikā devā ity uktam / (9) yathā pariṣaṇḍāsu caturmahārājakāyikā devā, evaṃ (10)

ekaikapārśvam aśītisahasrāṇi yathaivādhastāt / anye punar āhuḥ / sa viṃśatisahasradik / catvāri (16) pārśvāny asya catasro diśaḥ / ekaikaṃ pārśvaṃ viṃśatir yojanasahasrāṇi samantāt parikṣepeṇāśītir (17) iti / tasya ca (18)

sumerumūrdhno vidiśaḥ koṇāḥ / tāsu pañcayojanaśatapramāṇāś catvāraḥ kūṭā abhyudgatā yeṣu (20) vajrapāṇayo nāma yakṣāḥ prativasanti / tasya ca merumūrdhnaḥ (21) 
		madhye sārdhadvisāhasrapārśvam adhyardhayojanam / (22)

sumerutalasya madhye sudarśanaṃ nāma nagaraṃ dairghyena' sārdhatṛtīyayojanasahasre' ekaikaṃ pārśvam ucchrāyeṇādhyardha(24)yojanam / prākāraḥ 
sauvarṇa ekotareṇa dhātuśatenāsya bhūmiś citritā / tac ca bhūmitalaṃ (25) tūlapicuvat mṛdusaṃsparśaṃ pādakṣepotkṣepābhyāṃ natotnnataṃ śakrasya devānām indrasya rājadhānī / [p.168] 
		sārdhadviśatapārśvo 'tra vaijayantaḥ (67ab) (2) 
śakrasya devānām indrasya vaijayanto nāma prāsādo nagarasya madhye nānāratnasthānavidhānasaṃpadā sarvānya(3)bhavanaśrīmahimnā hrepaṇo dairghyeṇārdhatṛtīye yojanaśate pārśvaṃ pārśvam / iyaṃ tāvan nagarasyābhirāmatā / (4) 
		bahiḥ punaḥ / (5) 
		tac caitrarathapāruṣyamiśranandanabhūṣitam //67b'cd'' (6) 
tasya hi nagarasiha bahiś caturṣu pārśveṣu catvāry udyānādīni (Pradhan: ādīni) devānāṃ krīḍābhūmayaḥ / caitrarathm udyānaṃ (7) pāruṣyakaṃ miśrakāvaṇaṃ nandanavanaṃ ca / tais tannagaraṃ bahir alaṃkṛtam / (8)

eṣāṃ codyānānāṃ caturdiśaṃ catvāri subhūmīni viṃśatiyojanāntaritāni krīḍāsthānāny eva (10) devānāṃ parasparaspardhayaiva śobhāṃ vitanvanti / bahir eva nagarasya pārśve (11) 
		pūrvottare pārijātaḥ sudharmā dakṣiṇāvare //68cd // (12) 
pārijāto nāma kovidāras trāyastriṃśānāṃ devānāṃ kāmaratiprakarṣāśrayaḥ / tasya pañca yojanāni (13) mūlābhiniveśo yojanaśatam uccrāyaḥ / pañcāśat yojanāni śākhāpatrapalāśaṃ skaritvā tiṣṭhati / (14) tasya khalu sarvapariphullasya yojanaśatam anuvātaṃ gandho vāti, pañcāśad yojanayojanāni ' prativātam / (15) yuktaṃ tāvad anuvātam / prativātaṃ tu katham / vṛkṣānatikramaṃ saṃdhāyoktam ity eke / na hi nāma (16) prativātaṃ (Pradhan: saprativātaṃ!) vāto vāti / tasyaiva tu sā gandhasya tādṛśī prabhāvasapmpadeṣṭavyā yat pratibādhyamāno (17) 'pi divyair mṛdumārutair gandhāntaraṃ saṃtanoti / mandataratamasmārambhāt tu saṃtāna āśv eva (18) samuchidyate yato na tathā viprakṛṣṭam adhvānaṃ sprasarpati / kiṃ punaḥ svabhūtāśrita evam apuṣpagandhasaṃtāno (19) vartate, utāho vāyur adhivāsito jāyate / nātra niyamaḥ ubhayathāpi hy ācāryeṣṭiḥ / yat tarhi bhagavatoktaṃ (21) 
		''na puṣpagandhaḥ prativātam eti, na maulikas tāgaraś cāndano vā / (22) 
		satāṃ tu gandhaḥ prativātam eti sarvā diśaḥ satpuruṣaḥ pravātīti /'' [p.169] 
mānuṣyakaṃ puṣpagandhaṃ sandhāyoktam / tad dhi pratītaṃ loke / na ca (2) tasya tādṛśī śaktiḥ / mahīśāsakās (Pradhan: sāsakās) tu paṭhanti, yojanaśatam anuvātaṃ gandho vāti, pañcāśad yojanāni (3) pativātam iti / sudharmā nāma devasabhā dakṣiṇapaścime digbhāge yasyāṃ niṣyadya devāḥ (4) kṛtyākṛtyaṃ samarthayanti / eṣa tāvat tridaśānāṃ bhājanasaṃniveśaḥ / (5) 
		tata ūrdhvaṃ vimāneṣu devāḥ (69ab) (6) 
tridaśebhya ūrdhvaṃ devā vimāneṣu pratiṣṭhitāḥ / te punar yāmās tuṣitā nirmāṇaratayaḥ (7) paranirmitavaśavartinaś ca brahmakāyikādayaś ca pūrvoktāḥ ṣoḍaśasthānāntaragatāḥ / ity ete dvāviṃśati(8)devanikāyāḥ samāsena yeṣāṃ bhājanaṃ prajñāyate / (9) 
		kāmabhujas tu ṣaṭ / (69b') (10) 
teṣāṃ tu ṣaṭ kāmāvacarā devanikāyāḥ kāmān paribhuñjate, na śeṣāḥ / tadyathā caturmahārājakāyikā (11) yāvat paranirmitavaśavartinaḥ / te punaḥ (12) 
		dvaṃdvāliṅganapāṇyāptihasitekṣitamaithunāḥ //69cd // (13) 
dvaṃdvena maithunaṃ bhūmisaṃbaddhavāsinām / cāturmahārājakāyikānāṃ trāyastriṃśānāṃ yathā manuṣyāṇām / (14) teṣāṃ tu vāyunirmokṣāt dāhavigamaḥ śukrābhāvāt / āliṅganena maithunaṃ yāmānām āliṅganamātreṇa (15) dāhavigamāt / pāṇisaṃprāptyā tuṣitānāṃ nirmāṇaratīnāṃ (16) prekṣitena paranirmitavaśavartinām / svavartināṃ sarveṣāṃ dvaṃdvasamāpattyā / kālaparimāṇaṃ tu (17) prajñaptāv uktam iti vaibhāṣikāḥ 
 / yāvad yāvad viṣayāṇāṃ tīvratarā tāvat tāvad rāgo 'pi tīvrataraḥ / (18) yasya devasya devyā vā utsaṅge devamumāro devakanyā vā jāyate, sa tayoḥ putro bhavati, sā ca (19) duhitā / kiyatpramāṇo jāyte / (20)

rūpāvacarā devāḥ saṃpūrṇakāyāḥ vastreṇa saṃvītā upapadyante / sarve devā āryabhāṣābhāṣiṇaḥ / (2) tatra (Pradhan: tadatra) kāmadhātau veditavyāḥ (3) 
		kāmopapattayas tisraḥ kāmadevāḥ samānuṣāḥ / (71ab) (4) 
kathaṃ kṛtvā / santi sattvāḥ pratyupasthitakāmāḥ pratyupasthiteṣu kāmeṣv aiśvaryaṃ vaśe vartayanti / (5) tadyathā manuṣyās tadekatyāś ca devāḥ / te punaś catvāro devanikāyāḥ / santi sattvā nirmitakāmā (6) nirmāya kāmān aiśvaryaṃ vaśe vartayanti / tadyathā devā nirmāṇaratayaḥ / santi sattvāḥ (7) paranirmitakāmāḥ paranirmiteṣu kāmeṣv aiśvaryaṃ vaśe varttayanti / yadyathā devāḥ paranirmitavaśavarttinaḥ / (8) tā etā yathotpannaparibhogitvāt yathecchātmanirmitaparibhogitvād yathe(9)cchātmaparanirmitaparibhogitvāc ca tisraḥ kāmopapattaya ity ucyante / rūpadhātau tu (10) 
		sukhopapattayas tisro navatridhyānabhūmayaḥ //71cd // (11) 
triṣu dhyāneṣu yā nava bhūmayaḥ tās tisraḥ sukhopapattayaḥ / te hi devā vivekajena samādhijena (12) ca prītisukhena ca niṣprītikena ca sukhena sukhaṃ viharanto dīrgham adhvānaṃ tiṣṭhanti / ata etā (13) nirduḥkhadīrghasukhatvāt sukhā upapattayaḥ sukhopapattayaḥ / dhyānāntarotpattau tu prītisukhābhāvāt sukho(14)papattitvaṃ vicāryam / (15) 
	yāny etāni devānāṃ dvāviṃśatisthānāny uktāni, teṣām adharād uttaraṃ kiyadviprakṛṣṭam / naitat (16) sarvaṃ yojanaparisaṃkhyayā sukaraṃ parisaṃkhyātum / api tu (17) 
		sthānāt sthānād adho yāvat tāvad ūrdhvaṃ tatas tataḥ / (72ab) (18) 
jambūdvīpāt prabhṛti yad uttaraṃ sthānaṃ tasmād yāvad adho jambūdvīpas tāvat punas tasmād ūrdhvaṃ sthānāntaram / (19) yadyathā caturthī pariṣaṇḍā caturṇāṃ mahārājānāṃ mūlasthānam itaś catvāriṃśad yojanasahasrāṇi / (20) tasmād yāvad adho jambūdvīpas tāvad ūrdhvaṃ tridaśānāṃ sthānam / tasmād api yāvad adho jambūdvīpas tāvad ūrdhvaṃ (21) yāmānāṃ sthānam / tato 'pi yāvad adho jambūdvīpas tāvad ūrdhvaṃ tuṣiṭānāṃ (22) sthānam iti / evaṃ vistareṇa sarvam anukramya sudarśanebhyo yāvad adho jambūdvīpas tāvad ūrdhvam akaniṣṭhānāṃ (23) sthānam / tasmād ūrdhvaṃ na punaḥ sthānam asti / ata eva jyeṣṭhabhūtvād akaniṣṭhā ucyante / (24) aghaniṣṭhā ity apare / aghaṃ kila cittasthaṃ rūpaṃ tanmātraniṣṭheti / (25) 
	kiṃ punar adharasthānopapannā ūrdhvāni vimānāni gatvā paśyanti / [p.171]

ṛddhyā vā trāyastriṃśā yāmān gaccheyuḥ parāśrayeṇa vā / yady ṛddhimatā nīiyeran devena vā tatratyena / (3) evaṃ śeṣāḥ / āgataṃ tūrdhvopapannaṃ paśyen na tūrdhvadhātukaṃ nordhvabhūmikam / yathā spraṣṭavyaṃ na spṛśed aviṣayatvāt (4) / ata eva tenāsvena kāyenāgacchanti / kiṃ tarhi nirmitenādharabhūmikena / (5) tadicchayā paśyed ihatyam iveti nikāyāntarīyāḥ / (6) 
	athaiṣāṃ yāmādivijānānāṃ kiyat pramāṇam / caturṇaṃ tāvad yāvat sumerumūrdhnaḥ ity eke / (7) dviguṇotaram ity apare / prathamaṃ tu dhyānaṃ yāvāṃś cāturdvīpakaḥ / dvitīyaṃ yāvān sāhasraś cūḍiko (8) lokadhātuḥ / tṛtīyaṃ yāvān dvisāhasraḥ / caturthaṃ yāvāṃs trisāhasra ity eke / (9) prathamādīn sāhasrādiparimāṇāni / caturthaṃ tv aparimāṇam ity apare / (10) 
	atha ko 'yaṃ sāhasraś cūḍiko lokadhātuḥ, ko dvisāhasras trisāhasro vā / (11)

sahasraṃ jambūdvīpānāṃ pūrvavidehānām aparagodānīyānām uttarakurūṇāṃ sahasraṃ sūryāṇāṃ candrāṇāṃ sumerūṇāṃ (14) sahasraṃ cāturmahārājakāyikānāṃ devānāṃ yāvat paranirmitavaśavartināṃ sahasraṃ brahmalokānām ayam ucyate (15) sāhasraś cūḍiko lokadhātuḥ / (16)

pādaḥ krośasya caturtho bhāgaḥ / tanmātraṃ śarīraṃ cāturmahārājakāyikānāṃ dvau pādau trāyastriṃśānāṃ (6) trayo yāmānāṃ catvāras tuṣitānāṃ pañca nirmāṇaratīnāṃ adhyardhaḥ krośaḥ paranirmitavaśavartinām / (7)

apramāṇābhānāṃ catvāri yojanāni / ābhāsvarāṇām aṣṭau / evaṃ dviguṇavṛddhyāṃ yāvac chubhakṛtsnānāṃ (15) catuḥṣaṣṭiḥ / anabharakānāṃ tato yojanatrayeṇa hīnaṃ dviguṇaṃ pañcaviṃśatiyojanaśatam / tasmāt (16) pareṇa punaḥ puṇyaprasavānāṃ dviguṇaṃ dviguṇaṃ yāvad akaniṣṭhānāṃ ṣoḍaśayojana(17)sahasrāṇi śarīram / (18)

	uktaṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ devānāṃ vaktavyam / tac cāhorātraṃ vvyavasthāpy śakyaṃ vaktum iti sa eva (9) caiṣāṃ vyavasthāpyate / (10)

yāni manuṣyāṇāñ pañcāśad varśāṇi tāni kāmadhātāv adharāṇāṃ devānāṃ cāturmahārājakāyikānām ekaṃ (13) rātriṃ divam / (14)

tena tatas tenāhorātreṇa teṣāṃ triṃśadrātrakeṇa māsena dvādaśamāsakena saṃvatsareṇa divyāni pañcavarṣa(16)śatāny āyuḥpramāṇam / (17) 
		dviguṇottaram ūrdhvānām ubhayaṃ (80ab) (18) 
ūrdhvānāṃ devānām ubhayaṃ dviguṇottaram ahorātraś cāyuś ca / kathaṃ kṛtvā / yan manuṣyāṇāṃ varṣaśataṃ, (19) tat trāyastriṃśānāṃ devānām ekaṃ rātriṃdivam / tena rātriṃdivena divyaṃ varṣasahasream āyuḥ / evaṃ (20) yāmādīnāṃ yathākramam / mānuṣyakāni dve catvāry aṣṭau ṣoḍaśa varṣaśatāny ekaṃ rātriṃdivam / tena (21) rātriṃdivena dve catvāry aṣṭau ṣoḍaśa divyāni varṣasahasrāṇy āyuṣaḥ pramāṇam / yugandharād ūrdhvaṃ (22) sūryacandramasor (Pradhan: sūryācandramasor) abhāvāt / kathaṃ devānām ahorātravyavasthānam ālokakṛtyaṃ vā / puṣpāṇāṃ saṃkocavikāsāt (23) kumudabandhuvat śaunīnāṃ kūjanākūjanāt middhāpagamāc ca ālokakṛtyaṃ svayaṃprabhatvāt / (24) uktam āyuḥ kāminām / [p.174] 
		rūpiṇāṃ punaḥ / (2) 
		nāsty ahorātram āyus tu kalpaiḥ svāśrayasaṃmitaiḥ //80b'cd // (3) 
yeṣāṃ rūpiṇām ardhayojanam āśrayaḥ teṣām ardhakalpam āyuḥ / yeṣāṃ yojanaṃ teṣāṃ kalpam / evaṃ (4) yasya yāvad yojanam āśrayas tasya tāvat kalapam āyur yāvad akaniṣṭhānāṃ ṣoḍaśakalpasahasrāṇy āyuḥpramāṇam / (5) 
		ārūpye viṃśatiḥ kalpasahasrāṇy adhikādhikam / (6) 
ākāśānantyāyatane viṃśatikalpasahasrāṇy āyuṣaḥ pramāṇam / (7) vijñānānantyāyatane tasmād adhikaṃ viṃśatiḥ sahasrāṇi / ākiñcanyāyatane tasmād adhikaṃ viṃśatiḥ / (8) bhavāgre tasmād adhikaṃ viṃśatiḥ / evaṃ teṣāṃ yathākramaṃ viṃśatiś catvāriṃśat ṣaṣṭir aśītiḥ (9) kalpasahasrāṇy āyuḥpramāṇam / (10) 
	katamo 'tra kalpo veditavyaḥ kim antarakalpo 'tha saṃvartakalpo 'tha vivarttakalpo 'tha mahākalpaḥ / (11) 
		mahākalpaḥ parīttābhāt prabhṛty ardham adhas tataḥ //81cd // (12) 
parīttābhād eva nikāyāt prabhṛti mahākalpenāyuḥ veditavyam / tasmād adho mahākalpasyārdhaṃ (13) kalpīkṛtya mahābrahmādīnām āyur vyavasthāpitam / kathaṃ kṛtvā / yac ca loko viṃśatim antarakalpān (14) vivartate yac ca loko viṃśatim antarakalpān vivarta āste yac ca viṃśatim antarakalpān saṃvartate ime (15) ṣaṣṭir antarakalpā mahābrahmaṇo 'dhyardhaḥ kalpa uktaḥ / evaṃ ca kṛtvā mahākalpasyārdhaṃ catvāriṃśad antara(16)kalpān kalpīkṛtya teṣām āyuḥpramāṇam uktam / uktaṃ sugatāv āyuḥpramāṇam / (17) 
	durgatāv idānīṃ vaktavyam / tatra tāvat (18) 
		kāmadevāyuṣā tulyā ahorātrā yathākramam / (19) 
		saṃjīvādiṣu ṣaṭsu (82abc) (20) 
yāvat ṣaṇṇāṃ kāmāvacarāṇāṃ devanikāyānām āyur uktaṃ, tena tulyā ahorātraḥ ṣaṭsu (Pradhan: ṣaṭṣu) narakeṣu yathākramaṃ (21) veditavyāḥ / saṃjīve kālasūte saṃghāte raurave mahāraurave tāpane ca / (22) 
		āyus tais teṣāṃ kāmadevavat //82c'd // (23) 
tair idānīṃ svair ahorātrais teṣāṃ yathā ṣaṇṇāṃ kāmāvacarāṇāṃ devānām āyus tathaiva yathākramaṃ veditavyam / (24) kathaṃ kṛtvā / yad dhi cāturmahārājakāyikānām āyuḥpramāṇaṃ tat saṃjīvane mahānarake ekaṃ rātriṃdivam / [p.175] tena yāvat dvādaśamāsakena saṃvatsareṇa tatratyāni pañcavcarṣaśatāny āyuḥ / yat trāyastriṃśānām āyuḥ(2)pramāṇaṃ tat kālasūtre mahānarake ekaṃ rātriṃdivam / tena rātriṃdivena tasmin varṣasahasrāṇy āyuḥpramāṇam / (3) evam anyeṣv api yathā yogaṃ yojyaṃ yāvat paranirmitavaśavartinām āyuḥpramāṇaṃ tattulyenāhorātreṇa tāpane (4) ṣoḍaśa varṣasahasrāṇy āyuḥpramāṇam / (5)

upamānamātreṇa teṣv āyur ākhyātaṃ bhagavatā, tadyathā bhikṣavaḥ iha syād viṃśatikhārīko māgadhakas (19) tilavāhaḥ pūrṇas tilānāṃ cūḍikābaddhaḥ / tataḥ kaścid eva varṣaśatasyātyayād ekaṃ tilam apanayate (20) kṣiprataraṃ bhikṣavaḥ saviṃśatikhārīko māgadhas tilavāho 'nenopakrameṇa parikṣayaṃ paryādānaṃ (21) gacchet / na tv evāham arbudopapannānām āyuṣaḥ paryantaṃ vadāmi / yathā khalu bhikṣavo viṃśatir arbudā (22) evam eko nirarbudo vistareṇa yathā khalu bhikṣavo viṃśatiḥ padmā evam eko mahāpadma iti / (23)

uttarakuruṣu niyatāayuṣaḥ sattvā avaśyaṃ kṛtsnam āyur jīvanti / anyeṣu nāvaśyam / pudgalānāṃ (3) tu bahūnāṃ nāsty antareṇa kālakriyayā / tuṣitasthasyaikajātibaddhasya bodhisattvasya caramabhavikasattvasya (4) jinādiṣṭasya jinabhūtasya śraddhadharmānusāriṇo bodhisattvacakravartti(5)mātroś ca tadgarbhayor ity evamādīnām / (6)

catvāro māsā hemantānāṃ catvāro grīṣmānāṃ catvāro varṣāṇām ity ete dvādaśa māsā saṃvatsaraḥ (20) sārdham ūnarātraiḥ / saṃvatsareṇa hi ṣaḍūnarātrā nipātyante / kathaṃ kṛtvā / (21)

antarakalpo saṃvarttakalpo vivarttakalpo mahākalpaś ceti / tatra tāvat (4) 
		saṃvarttakalpo narakāsaṃbhavāt bhājanakṣayaḥ / (90ab) (5) 
narakeṣu hi sattvāsaṃbhavāt prabhṛti yāvat bhājanasaṃkṣayaḥ / dve hi saṃvarttanyau / gatisaṃvartanī (6) dhātusaṃvartanī ca / punar dve saṃvartanyau / sattvasaṃvartanī bhajanasaṃvartanī ca / bhavati sa kālo (7) yan narakeṣu sattvāś cyavante nopapadyante / sa ārambhaḥ saṃvartakalpasya / yad ayaṃ loko viṃśatyantara(8)kalpān vinṛtto 'sthāt tan niryātaṃ vaktavyam / tad viṃśatim antarakalpān saṃvartiṣyate, tat (9) pratipannaṃ vaktavyam / yadā narakeṣv ekasattvo nāvaśiṣṭo bhavati, iyatāyaṃ lokaḥ saṃvṛtto bhavati / (10) yad uta narakasaṃvarttanyā yasya tadānīṃ niyataṃ narakavedanīyaṃ karma dhriyate, sa lokadhātvantaranarakeṣu (11) kṣipyate / evaṃ tiryaksaṃvarttanī pretasaṃvarttanī ca vaktavyā / mahāsamudragatās tiryañcaḥ pūrvaṃ saṃvartante // (12) manuṣyasahacariṣṇavas tu tair eva sārdhaṃ bhavanti / sa loko yan manuṣyeṣv anyatamaḥ sattvaḥ svayam anācāryakaṃ (13) dharmatāpratilambhikaṃ prathamaṃ dhyānaṃ samāpadyate, sa tasmāt vyutthāya vācaṃ bhāṣate sukhaṃ bata vivekajaṃ (14) prītisukhaṃ śāntaṃ bata vivekajaṃ prītisukham iti / taṃ ca śabdaṃ śrutvā anye 'pi sattvāḥ (15) samāpadyante / kālaṃ kṛtvā brahmaloka upapadyante / yadā jambūdvīpa (16) ekasattvo 'pi nāvaśiṣṭo bhavati iyatāyaṃ lokaḥ saṃvṛtto bhavati yad uta jambūdvīpasaṃvartanyā / (17) evaṃ pūrvavidehagodānīyottarakurusaṃvartanyo vaktavyāḥ (Pradhan: vaktavyā) / yadā manuṣyeṣv ekasattvo 'pi nāvaśiṣṭo bhavati, (18) iyatāyaṃ lokaḥ saṃvṛtto bhavati yad uta manuṣyagatisaṃvartanyā / auttarakauravās tu kālaṃ kṛtvā (19) kāmavacareṣu deveṣūpapadyante / tatra vairāgyābhāvāt / evaṃ cāturmahārājakāyikeṣv api deveṣu prathamaṃ (20) dhyānaṃ samāpadya brahmaloka upapadyante / yadā tatraikasattvo 'pi nāvaśiṣṭo bhavati, iyatāyaṃ lokaḥ (21) saṃvṛtto bhavati yad uta cāturmahārājakāyikasaṃvarttanyā / evaṃ yāvat paranirmitavaśavartisaṃvartinyo (22) vaktavyāḥ / yad ekasattvo 'pi kāmāvacareṣu deveṣu nāvaśiṣṭo bhavati, iyatāyaṃ lokaḥ saṃvṛtto (23) bhavati yad uta kāmadhātusaṃvartanyā / brahmaloke 'py anyatamaḥ sattvo dharmatāprātilambhikaṃ dvitīyaṃ (24) dhyānaṃ samāpadyotthāya vācaṃ bhāṣate sukhaṃ bata samādhijaṃ prītisukhaṃ śāntaṃ bata samādhijaṃ prītisukham (25) iti / taṃ śabdaṃ śrutvānye 'pi sattvāḥ samāpadyante kālaṃ ca kṛtvā ābhāsvareṣu [p.179] deveṣūpapadyante / yadā brahmaloka ekasattvo 'pi nāvaśiṣṭo bhavati, (2) iyatāyaṃ lokaḥ saṃvṛtto bhavati yad uta sattvasaṃvarttanyā / tataḥ śūnye bhājane ita eva sāmantakāt (3) sattvānāṃ tadākṣepake karmaṇi parikṣīṇe sapta sūryāḥ prādurbhūya krameṇa yāvat pṛthivīṃ sumeruṃ ca (4) niḥśeṣaṃ dahanti / tasmād evaṃ prajvalitād arcir vāyunā kṣiptaṃ śūnyaṃ brāhmaṃ vimānaṃ nirdahat (5) paraiti / tac ca tadbhūmikam evārcir veditavyam / na hi visabhāgā apakṣālāḥ kramante / (6) tatsaṃbaddhasaṃbhūtatvāt tasmāt tad ity uktam / kāmāvacaro hy agnī rūpāvacaram agniṃ saṃbadhnātīti / (7) evam anyasyām api saṃvartanyāṃ yathāyogaṃ veditavyam iti / narakeṣu sattvānāṃ cyutyanutpādāt (8) prabhṛti yāvat bhājanānāṃ saṃkṣaya eṣa kālaḥ / (9) 
		vivartakalpaḥ prāgvāyor yāvan narakasaṃbhavaḥ //90cd // (10) 
prathamād vāyoḥ prabhṛti yāvan narakeṣu sattvasaṃbhavaḥ eṣa kālo vivartakalpa ity ucyate / tathā saṃvṛtte (11) hi loka ākāśamātrāvaśeṣaś ciraṃ kālaṃ tiṣṭhati yāvat punar api sattvānāṃ karmādhipatyena (12) bhājanānāṃ pūrvanimittabhūtā ākāśe mandamandā vāyavaḥ syandante / tadā yad ayaṃ loko viṃśatim (13) antarakalpān saṃvṛtto 'sthāt tani niryātaṃ vaktavyam / yad viṃśatim antarakalpān (14) vivarttiṣyate, tad upayātaṃ vaktavyam / tatas te vāyavo vardhamānā yathoktaṃ vāyumaṇḍalaṃ jāyate / (15) tataḥ śanair yathoktakramavidhānaṃ sarvaṃ jāyate apmaṇḍalaṃ kāñcanamayī mahāpṛthivī dvīpāḥ (16) sumervādayaś ca / prathamaṃ tu brāhmavimānam utpadyate / tato yāvat yāmīyaṃ tato vāyumaṇḍalādīni / (17) iyatāyaṃ loko nivṛtto bhavati yad uta bhājanavivartanyā / athānyataraḥ sattva ābhāsvarebhyaś cyutvā (18) śūnye brāhme vimāna utpadyate / anye 'pi sattvās tataś cyutvā brahmapurohiteṣūpapadyante / tato (19) brahmakāyikeṣu paranirmitavaśavartiṣu / krameṇa yāvad uttarakurau godānīye pūrvavidehe jambūdvīpe preteṣu (20) tiryakṣu narakeṣūpapadyante / dharmatā hy eṣā yat paścāt saṃvarttate tat pūrvaṃ vivarttate / yadā narakeṣv ekaḥ (21) sattvo 'pi prrādurbhūto bhavati, tadā yad ayaṃ loko viṃśatim antarakalpān vivartate, tan niryātaṃ bhavati / (22) yad viṃśatim antarakalpān vivarttaḥ sthāsyati tad upayātaṃ bhavati / (23) 
		antaḥkalpo 'mitāt yāvad daśavarṣāyuṣaḥ (91ab) (24) 
vivartamāne loke ekānaviṃśatir antarakalpā aparimitāyuṣāṃ [p.180] manuṣyāṇām atikrāmanti / aparimitāyuṣām eva hrasatāṃ (Pradhan: kramatā) yāvad daśavarṣāyuṣo bhavanti / so 'sau (2) vivṛttānāṃ tiṣṭhatāṃ prathamo 'ntarakalpaḥ / (3) 
		tataḥ / (4)

tasmād apare 'ṣṭādaśotkarṣā apakarṣāś cāṣṭādaśāntarakalpā bhavanti / kathaṃ kṛtvā / tebhyo hi (6) daśavarṣāyuṣkebhya utkarṣaṃ gacchantaḥ krameṇāśītivarṣasahasrāyuṣo bhavanti / punaś cāpakarṣaṃ gacchanto (7) daśavarṣāyuṣo bhavanti / evaṃ dvitīyo 'ntarakalpaḥ / evaṃ yāvad aṣṭādaśa / (8) 
		utkarṣa ekaḥ (92a) (9) 
ekāntarakalpo viṃśatitama utkarṣa eva nāpakarṣaḥ (Pradhan: evam apakarṣaḥ) / daśavarṣāyuṣkebhyo yāvad aśītivarṣāyuṣāṃ (10) manuṣyāṇām iti varttate / athaite utkarṣāḥ kiyantaṃ prakarṣam gacchanti / (11) 
		te 'śītisahasrād yāvad āyuṣaḥ / (92a'b) (12) 
nātaḥ pareṇa vardhante yāvān eva cānyeṣām antarakalpānām utkarṣāpakarṣakālas tāvān eva prathamasyāpakarṣakālaḥ (13) paścimasya cotkarṣakāla iti samānakālāḥ sarve bhavanti / (14)

		vivartate 'tha saṃvṛtta āste saṃvarttate samam / (93ab) (18) 
viṃśatim evāntarakalpān vivartate viṃśatiṃ saṃvartate viṃśatiṃ saṃvṛtta āste / yady api (19) tadānīm utkarṣā apakarṣāś ca na pravartante kālas tu samānaḥ parisaṃkhyāyate / tatraienāntarakalpena (20) bhājanāny abhinivartante ekānaviṃśatyāntarakalpair āvāsyante ekenāntarakalpena bhājanāni vidhvasyante (21) ekānaviṃśatyā śūnyībhavanti / tā etā antarakalpānāṃ catasro viṃśatyo 'śītir bhavanti / (22)

etan mahākalpasya pramāṇam / kalpaṃ kiṃsvabhāvaḥ / pañcaskandhasvabhāvaḥ / yad ucyate [p.181] tribhir asaṃkhyeyaiḥ kalpānāṃ buddhatvaṃ prāpyata iti / tat katameṣāṃ kalpānām / ya eva mahākalpo (2) nirdiṣṭaḥ (3) 
		tadasaṃkhyatrayodbhavam //93d // (4) 
		buddhatvam (94a) (5) 
asaṃkhyeyānāṃ kalpānāṃ teayeṇa / katham asaṃkhyeyasyāsati saṃkhyeyāvasāne punas tritvam ucyate / (6) naitad evaṃ veditavyam / kiṃ tarhi / ṣaṣṭiḥ sthānāntarāṇy asaṃkhyeyam iti muktakasūtraṃ paṭhyate / (7) katamāni ṣaṣṭiḥ / eko hy advitīyaḥ prathamaṃ sthānāntaram / ekakānāṃ daśako dvitīyam / (8) daśa daśakāni śataṃ tṛtīyam / daśa śatāni sahasram / daśa sahasrāṇi prabhedaḥ (9) / daśa prabhedā lakṣam / daśa lakṣā atilakṣaḥ / daśātilakṣāḥ kauṭiḥ / (10) daśa kauṭyo madhyaḥ / daśa madhyā ayutam / daśāyutā mahāyutam / daśamahāyutā niyutam / (11) daśa niyutā mahāniyutam / daśa mahāniyutāḥ prayutam / daśa prayutā mahāprayutaḥ / daśa (12) mahāprayutāḥ kaṅkaraḥ / daśa kaṅkarā mahākaṅkaraḥ / daśa mahākaṅkarā visvaraḥ / daśa visvarā (13) mahāvisvaraḥ / daśa mahāvisvarā akṣobhyaḥ / daśākṣobhyā mahākṣobhyaḥ / daśa mahākṣobhyā (14) vivāhaḥ / daśa vivāhā mahāvivāhaḥ / daśa mahāvivāhā utsaṅgaḥ / daśotsaṅgā mahotsaṅgaḥ / (15) daśa mahotsaṅgā vāhanaḥ / daśa vāhanāni mahāvāhanam / daśa mahāvāhanāni tiṭibhaḥ / (16) daśa tiṭibhā mahātiṭibhaḥ / daśa mahātiṭibhā hetuḥ / daśa htavo mahāhetuḥ / daśa (17) mahāhetavaḥ karabhaḥ / daśa karabhā mahākarabhaḥ / daśa mahākarabhā indraḥ / daśendrā mahendraḥ / (18) daśa mahendrāḥ samāptam / daśa samāptāni mahāsamāptam / daśa mahāsamāptāni gatiḥ / daśa gatayo (19) mahāgatiḥ / daśa mahāgatayo nimbarajaḥ / daśa nimbarajāṃsi mahānimbarajaḥ / daśa mahā(20)nimbarajāṃsi mudrā / daśa mudrā mahāmudrā / daśa mahāmudrāḥ balam / (21) daśa balāni mahābalam / daśa mahābalāni saṃjñā / daśa saṃjñā mahāsaṃjñā / daśa mahāsaṃjñā (22) vibhūtaḥ / daśa vibhūtā mahāvibhūtaḥ / daśa mahāvibhūtā balākṣam / daśa balākṣā (23) mahābalākṣam / daśa mahābalākṣāṇy asaṃkhyam / aṣṭakaṃ madhyād vistṛtam / ity eteṣāṃ [p.182] ṣaṣṭhisthānāntaragatāṃ saṃkhyām anuprāptāḥ kalpā asaṃkhyeyānīty ucyante / tato vyāvṛtya punar gaṇyante / (2) evaṃ trīṇi asaṃkhyeyānīty ucyante / na tu naiva parisaṃkhyātuṃ śakyanta iti / (3) 
	kin nu khalu kālaprakarṣeṇaiva kṛtapraṇidhānā bodhisattvā bhodhim abhisaṃbudhyante / kim etad eva (4) bhaviṣyati / mahatāṃ hi puṇyajñānasaṃbhāreṇa ṣaḍbhiḥ pāramitābhiḥ bahubhir duṣkaraśatasahasrais tribhiḥ (5) kalpāsaṃkhyeyair anuttarāṃ samyaksaṃbodhim abhisaṃbudhyante bodhisattvāḥ / yady apy anyathāpy asti mokṣāvakāśaḥ (6) kim arthaṃ ta iyantaṃ yatnam ārabhante / parārthaṃ ta iyantaṃ yatnam ārabhante, kathaṃ parān api mahato duḥkhaughāt (7) paritrātuṃ śaknuyām iti / ka eṣāṃ parārthena svārthaḥ / eṣa eva teṣāṃ svārtho yaḥ parārthas tasyābhimatatvāt (8) / idānīm etac chraddhāsyate / satyaṃ duḥśraddhānam etad ātmaṃbharibhir niṣkaruṇaiḥ / kāruṇikais tu (9) śraddhitam evaitat, yathā ceha kecid abhyastanairghṛṇyā asaty api svārthe (10) paravyasanābhiratā upalabhyante / tathā unar abhyastakāruṇyā asty api svārthe parahitakriyābhirāmāḥ (11) santīti sdaṃbhāvyam / tathaiva cābhyāsavaśād anātmabhūteṣu saṃskāreṣu saṃskṛtalakṣaṇānabhijñā ātmasnehaṃ (12) niveśya taddhetor duḥkhāny udvahanti evaṃ punar abhyāsavaśād ātmasnehaṃ tebhyo nirvartya pareṣv apekṣāṃ vardhayitvā (13) taddhetor duḥkhāny udvahantīti saṃbhāvyam / gotrāntaram eva hi tat tathājātīyaṃ nirvartate yat pareṣāṃ (14) duḥkhena duḥkhāyate sukhena sukhāyate nātmana iti / na te punaḥ svārtham anyaṃ paśyanti / (15) āha cātra (16) 
		''hīnaḥ prārthayate svasaṃtatigataṃ yais tair upāyaiḥ sukhaṃ (17) 
		madhyo duḥkhanivṛttim eva na sukhaṃ duḥkhāspadaṃ yad yataḥ / (18) 
		śreṣṭhaḥ prārthayate svasaṃtatigatair duḥkhaiḥ pareṣāṃ sukhaṃ (19) 
		duḥkhātyantanivṛttim eva ca yatas tadduḥkhaduḥkhy eva saḥ //'' (20) 
	kiṃ punar utkarṣā buddhā utpadyante, āhosvid apakarṣāḥ / (21) 
		apakarṣo hi śatād yāvat tadudbhavaḥ / (94a'b) (22) 
aśītivarṣaśatāyuṣi prajāyām apakarṣe ārabdhe yāvad varṣaśatāyuṣo manuṣyā bhavanty etasminn antare (23) buddha utpadyante / kasmān notkarṣakāle / tadā hi duḥsamudvejāḥ' sattvā bhavanti / [p.183] kasmān na śatāt / tadā ca pañca kaṣāyā abhyutsadā bhavanti / (2) tadyathāyuṣkaṣāyaḥ kalpakaṣāyaḥ kleśakaṣāyaḥ dṛṣṭikaṣāyaḥ sattvakaṣāyaś ca / apakarṣasyādhastāt (3) pratyavarā āyurādayaḥ kiṭabhūtatvāt kaṣāyā ucyante / dvābhyāṃ hi jīvitopakaraṇavipattī (4) yathākramam / dvābhyāṃ jkuśalapakṣavipattiḥ / kāmasukhallikātmaklamathānuyogādhikārāt gṛhipravrajita(5)pakṣayor vā / ekenātmabhāvavipattiḥ / pramāṇarūpārogyabalavṛddhismṛtivīryadhairyabhraṃśāt / (6) 
	atha pratyekabuddhānāṃ kasmin kāle utpādaḥ / (7) 
		dvayoḥ pratyekabuddhānāṃ (94c) (8) 
utkarṣe 'py apakarṣe 'pi / dvividhā hi pratyekabuddhā vargacāriṇaḥ khaḍgaviṣāṇakalpāś ca / tatra (9) vargacāriṇaḥ śrāvakapūrviṇaḥ pratyekajinā ucyante / pṛthagjanapūrviṇo 'pi santīty apare / (10) ye 'nyatrotpāditanirvedhabhāgīyā iha svayaṃ mārgam abhisaṃbudhyante / tathā hi pūrvayogaṃ paṭhanti parvate (11) kila pañcaśatāni tāpasānāṃ kaṣṭāni tapāṃsi tapyante sma, yāvat pratyekabuddhasahoṣitena (12) markaṭenāgamya tadīryāpathasaṃdarśanāt pratyekabodhim abhisaṃbuddhā iti / na cāryāḥ santaḥ kaṣṭāni (13) tapāṃsi tapyeran / khaḍgaviṣāṇakalpāḥ punar ekavihāriṇaḥ / teṣāṃ pratyekabuddhānāṃ (14) 
		khaḍgaḥ kalpaśatānvayaḥ //94d // (15) 
mahākalpānāṃ śataṃ bodhisaṃbhāreṣu caritaḥ khaḍgaviṣāṇakalpo bhavati / (16) vinopadeśenātmānam ekaṃ pratibuddhā iti pratyekabuddhāḥ / te hy ekam ātmānaṃ damayanti nānyat / (17) kiṃ punar atra kāraṇam / na hi tāvad aśaktā dharmaṃ deśayituṃ pratisaṃvitprāptatvāt / śakyaṃ ca taiḥ (18) pūrvabuddhānāṃ śāsanam anusmṛtyāpi dharmaṃ deśayitum / nāpi niṣkaruṇāḥ / sattvānu(19)grahārtham ṛddher āviṣkaraṇāt / nāpi sattvānām abhavyatvāt / tathā hi laukikamārgavītarāgāḥ (20) saṃvidyante / kiṃ tarhi / pūrvābhyāsavaśenālpotsukatādhimuktatvāt motsahante gambhīradharmagrahaṇāya (21) pareṣāṃ vyāpartum / anusrotogāminīnāṃ hi prajānāṃ duṣkaraṃ pratisrotonayanam / gaṇaparikarṣaṇaprasaṅgaparihārārthaṃ ca vyākṣepasaṃsargabhīrutvāt [p.184] 
	atha cakravatttinaḥ kadotpadyante / (2) 
		cakravartisamutpattir nādho 'śītisahasrakāt / (95ab) (3) 
amite cāyuṣi manuṣyāṇāṃ yāvad aśītisahasrake cotpatiś cakravarttināṃ nādhaḥ / tasyāḥ sasyasaṃpadas tad(4)ūnāyuṣām abhājanatvāt / rājyaṃ cakreṇa vartayituṃ śīlam eṣām iti cakravarttinaḥ / te punaś caturvidhāḥ / (5) 
		suvarṇarūpyatāmrāyaścakriṇaḥ (95c) (6) 
suvarṇarūpyatāmrāyaścakrāṇi yeṣāṃ santi / prathama eṣām uttamo dvitīya (7) upottamaḥ tṛtīyo madhyamaś caturtho 'dhamaḥ / (8) 
		te 'dharakramāt //95c'd // (9) 
		ekadvitricaturdvīpāḥ (96a) (10) 
yasyāyasāṃ cakraṃ, sa ekadvīpādhipatiḥ / yasya tāmaramayaṃ, sa dvayoḥ / yasya rūpyamayaṃ sa trayāṇām / (11) yasya suvarṇamayaṃ sa caturdvīpādhipatiḥ / eṣa prājñaptiko nirdeśaḥ / sūtre tu pradhānagrahaṇād ekam eva (12) sauvarṇaṃ cakram / yasya rājñaḥ kṣatriyasya mūrdhābhiṣiktasya tad eva poṣadhe pañcadaśyāṃ śiraḥsnāta(13)syopoṣadho poṣitasyopariprāsādatalagatasyāmātyagaṇaparivṛtasya pūrvasyāṃ diśi cakraratnaṃ prādurbhavati (14) sahasrāraṃs sanābhikaṃ sanemikaṃ sarvākāraparipūrṇaṃ śubhakarmārakṛtaṃ divyaṃ sarvasauvarṇaṃ sa rājā (15) bhavati cakravartīti / evaṃ caite cakravartina utpadyante / (16) 
		na ca dvau saha buddhavat / (96b ) (17) 
sūtra uktam asthānam anavakāśo yad apūrvācaramau dvau tathāgatāv arhantau samyaksaṃbuddho loka utpadyeyātāṃ (18) nedaṃ sthānaṃ vidyate, sthānam etad vidyate yad ekas (Pradhan:aikas) tathāgataḥ / yathā tathāgata evaṃ cakravartināv iti / (19) idam atra saṃpradhāryam / kim atra trisāhasramahāsāhasro lokadhātur loka iṣṭa utāho sarvalokadhātava iti / (20) nānyatra buddhā utpadyante ity eke / kiṃ kāraṇam / mā bhūt bhagavataḥ (21) śaktivyāghātaḥ iti / eka eva hi bhagavān sarvatra śaktaḥ / yatra buddha eko na śaktaḥ (22) syād vineyān vinetuṃ tatrānyo 'pi na śakta iti / uktaṃ ca sūtre sacet (Pradhan: sa cet) tvāṃ śāriputra (23) kaścid upasaṃkrāmyaivaṃ pṛcchet, asti kaścid etarhi śramaṇo vā brāhmaṇo vā samasamaḥ śramaṇena (24) gautamena yadutābhisaṃbodhāya / evaṃ ca pṛṣṭaḥ kiṃ vyākuryāḥ / sacen (Pradhan: sa cen) māṃ bhadanta kaścid upasaṃkra(25) myāivaṃ pṛcchet tasyāhaṃ pṛṣṭa evaṃ vyākuryāṃ, nāsti kaścid etarhi śramaṇo vā brāhmaṇo vā samasamo [p.185] bhagavatā yad utābhisaṃbodhāya / tat kasya hetoḥ / saṃmukhaṃ me bhagavato 'ntkāc chrutaṃ saṃmukham udgṛhītaṃ (2) asthānam anavakāśo pad apūrvācaramau tathāgatau loka utpadyeyātāṃ nedaṃ sthānaṃ vidyata iti / (3) yat tarhi bhagavatoktaṃ brahmasūtre yāvat trisāhasramahāsāhasrako loko vaśe me 'tra vartata iti / (4) ābhiprāyika eṣa nirdeśaḥ / ko 'trābhiprāyaḥ / tāvato 'nabhisaṃskāreṇa vavalokanāt / (5) abhisaṃskāreṇa tv ananto buddhānāṃ cakṣurviṣayaḥ / santy evānyalokashātuṣu buddhā iti nikāyāntarīyāḥ / (6) kiṃ kāraṇam / bahavo hi samaṃ saṃbhāreṣu pravartamānā dṛśyante / na caikatra bahūnāṃ buddhānāṃ yugapat (7) yoga utpattuṃ na cāsti tadutpattau kaścit pratibandhaḥ iti niyataṃ (8) lokadhātvantareṣūtpadyante / antarā lokadhātava iti na śakyaṃ bhagavatā kalpam apy āyurvibhramatā (9) yatheha tathānyesv api ananteṣu lokadhātuṣu vyāpartuṃ kiṃ punaḥ puruṣāyuṣam / kathaṃ ceha buddho (10) vyāpriyate / asya pudgalasyedam indriyam iyatā kālenāmuṣmin deśe amuṃ pudgalam āgamyāsya (11) doṣasya parihārād asyāṅgasyopasaṃhārād anena prayogeṇānutpannaṃ votpatsyate aparipūrṇaṃ vā paripūrayi(12)ṣyatīti / yat tv idaṃ sūtram atropanītam ''asthānam anavakāśo yad apūrvacaramau dvau tathāgatāv ekatra (13) loka utpadyeyātām'' iti tad evedaṃ saṃpradhāryate kim idam ekaṃ lokadhātum adhikṛtyoktam āhosvit (14) sarvān iti / cakravartino 'pi cāsya lokadhātau na syād utpādaḥ / sahotpattipratiṣedhāt (15) buddhavat / athaitat kṣamyate idaṃ tu kasmān na kṣamyate puṇyas tu buddhānāṃ loka utpāda iti / (16) yadi bahūnāṃ bahuṣu syān na doṣaḥ syāt / bhūyasāṃ lokānām abhyudayena yogaḥ syān niḥśreyasena ca / (17) athaikasminn api kasmāt dvau tathāgatau na sahotpadyete / prayojanābhāvāt praṇidhānavaśāc ca / (18) evaṃ hi bodhisattvāḥ praṇidhānaṃ kurvanti aho vatāham andhe loke 'pariṇāyake (19) buddho loka utpadyeyam anāthānāṃ nātha iti / ādarābhisvārārthaṃ ca / ekasmin hi buddhe (20) sutarām ādriyante durlabha īdṛśo 'nya iti manyamānāḥ sutarāṃ cābhisvarante śāsanapratipattau māsmin (21) gate parinirvṛte vānātā bhūmeti / (22) 
	athaite cakravartinaḥ suvarṇādimayaiś cakraiḥ pṛthivīṃ jayantaḥ kathaṃ jayanti / yathākramaṃ (23) 
		pratyudyānasvasaṃtānakalahāstrajitaḥ (96cd) (24) 
yasya sauvarṇaṃ cakraṃ bhavati, taṃ koṣarājānaḥ svayaṃ pratyudgacchanti / ime devasya janapadāḥ ṛddhāś ca [p.186] sphītāś ca kṣemāś ca subhikṣāś cākīrṇabahujanamanuṣyāś ca / tān devaḥ samanuśāstu / vayaṃ (2) devasyānuyātrikā bhaviṣyāma iti / yasya rūpyamayaṃ, sa svayam eva teṣām antikaṃ yāti (3) paścād asya prahvībhavanti / yasya tāmramayaṃ, sa teṣām antikaṃ gatvā kalahāyate paścāt prahvībhavanti / (4) yasya śastramayaṃ, sa teṣām antikaṃ gacchati anyonyaṃ śastrāṇy āvahanti paścān namanti / sarve tu (5) cakravartinaḥ (6) 
		avadhāḥ //96d' // (7) 
śastreṇāpi jayatāṃ vadho na pravartate, nirjitya ca sattvān daśasu kuśaleṣu parmapatheṣu pratiṣthāpayanti / (8) ata eva te niyataṃ deveṣūtpadyante / sūtra uktaṃ rājñaś cakravartino loke prādurbhāvāt saptānāṃ (9) ratnānāṃ loke prādurbhāvo bhavati / tadyathā cakraratnasya hstiratnasyā(10)śvaratnasya maṇiratnasya strīratnasya gṛhapatiratnasya pariṇāyakaratnasyeti / kathaṃ sattvasaṃkhyātā (11) hastyādayaḥ parakīyeṇakarmotpadyante / na vai kaścit parakīyeṇotpadyate / yena tu sattvena (12) tatsaṃbandhasaṃvartanīyaṃ karmopacitaṃ tasminn utpannaṃ svāny evainaṃ karmāṇy utpādayanti / kim eṣa (13) evānyarājyabhyaś cakravartinā viśeṣaḥ / anyo 'pi viśeṣo 'sti / tadyathā dvātriṃśanmahāpuruṣa(14)lakṣaṇāny eṣāṃ bhavanti, tadyathā buddhānām / tatra tu (15) 
		deśasthottaptapūrṇatvair lakṣaṇātiśayo muneḥ / (97ab) (16) 
deśasthatarāṇi buddhānāṃ lakṣaṇāni / uttaptatarāṇi saṃpūrṇatarāṇi cety eṣa teṣāṃ viśeṣaḥ / (17) 
	kiṃ khalu prāthamakalpikā api manuṣyāḥ sarājakā āsan / nety āha / kiṃ tarhi / (18) 
		prāg āsan rūpivat sattvāḥ (97c) (19) 
prāthamakalpikā manuṣyā rūpāvacarā ivāsan / sūtra uktaṃ te bhavanti rūpiṇo manomayāḥ (20) sarvāṅgapratyaṅgopetā avikalā ahīnendriyāḥ śubhā varṇasthāyinaḥ svayaṃprabhā vihāyasaṃgamāḥ prītibhakṣāḥ (21) prītyāhārā dīrghāyuṣo dīrgham adhvānaṃ tiṣṭhantīti / (22) 
		rasarāgāt tataḥ śanaiḥ //97d // (23) 
		ālasyāt saṃnidhiṃ kṛtvā sāgrahaiḥ kṣetrapo bhṛtaḥ / (98ab) (24) 
teṣāṃ tathābhūtānāṃ bhūmirasaḥ prādurbhūto madhusvādurasaḥ / tasyānyatamo [p.187] lolupajātīyaḥ sattvo gandhaṃ ghrātvā rasaṃ svāditavān bhakṣitavāṃś ca / tathānye 'pi sattvās (2) tathaivākārṣuḥ / sa ārambhaḥ kavaḍīkārāhārasya / teṣāṃ tadāhārābhyāsāt kharatvaṃ gurutvaṃ ca (3) kāye 'vakrāntaṃ prabhāvā antarhitāḥ / tato 'ndhakāra utpanne sūryacandramasau (Pradhan:sūryācandramasau) prādurbhūtau / (4) so 'py eṣāṃ bhūmirasa āsvādagṛddhānāṃ krameṇāntarhito bhūmiparpaṭakaṃ prādurbhūtam / tatrāpi (5) gṛddhās tad apy antarhitam / vanalatā prādurbhūtā / tatrāpi gṛddhāḥ sāpy antarhitā / akṛṣtoptaḥ (6) śālir utpannas taṃ prabhuktāḥ / tasyedānīm audārikatvān niḥṣyandanirvāhārthaṃ sattvānāṃ mūtrapurīṣamārgau (7) saha strīpuruṣendriyābhyāṃ prādurbhūtau saṃsthānaṃ ca bhinnam / teṣām anyonyaṃ aśyatāṃ (8) pūrvābhyāsavaśād ayoniśomanaskāragrāhagrāsatāṃ gatānāṃ kāmarāga udīrṇo yato vipratipannāḥ / (9) eṣa ārambhaḥ kāmināṃ kāmabhūtagrahāveśasya / te ca khalu taṃ śāliṃ sāyaṃ ca sāyamāśārthaṃ (10) prātaś ca prātarāśārthaṃ praveśayanti sma / athānyatamaḥ sattvo 'lasajātīyaḥ saṃnidhikāram akārṣīt / (11) anye 'pi ca sattvāḥ saṃnidhikāram akārṣuḥ / teṣāṃ tatra mamakāra utpanne sa śālir lūno (12) lūno na punar jāyate sma / tataḥ kṣetrāṇi pravibhajya sveṣv āgrahaṃ kṛtvā (13) parakīyaṃ hartum ārabdhāḥ / prathama ārambhaś cauryasya / tais teṣām aṅkuśārthaṃ sametyānyatamaḥ puruṣaviśeṣaḥ (14) kṣetrāṇi pālayituṃ ṣaṣṭhabāgena bhṛtaḥ / tasya kṣetrāṇām adhipatiḥ kṣatriyaḥ kṣatriya iti saṃjñotpannā / (15) mahājanakāyasya saṃataḥ prajāṃ ca rañjayatīti mahāsaṃmato rājeti saṃjñotpannā / eṣa ārambho (16) rājaparaṃparāyāḥ / tatra ye gṛhebhyo bahir manasaḥ saṃvṛttās teṣāṃ brāhmaṇā iti saṃjñotpannā / athānyatamasya (17) rājño lobhāt saṃvibhāgam akurvantaḥ sattvānāṃ tāskaryaṃ prācuryam āpannaṃ, sa tān śastreṇopasaṃkramate (18) sma / tato 'nye naivaṃkārakāḥ sma iti mṛṣā vācaṃ vaktum ārabdhāḥ iti / (19) 
		tataḥ karmapathādhikyād apahrāse daśāyuṣaḥ //98cd // (20) 
tata evaṃ karmapathānāṃ vṛddhau satyāṃ krameṇa hrasatāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ daśavarṣāyuṣo manuṣyāḥ saṃbhavanti / (21) ato 'sya kṛtsnasyānarthaughasya dvau dharmau mūlayoniḥ rasarāga ālasyaṃ ca / (22) 
	daśavarṣāyuṣāṃ manuṣyāṇām antarakalpasya niryāṇaṃ bhavati / kathaṃ bhavatīty āha / (23) 
		kalpasya śastrarogābhyāṃ durbhikṣeṇa ca nirgamaḥ / (99ab) (24) 
tribh antarakalpasya niryāṇaṃ bhavati / śastreṇa rogeṇa durbhikṣeṇa ca / antarakalpasya [p.188] niryāṇakāle daśavarṣāyuṣo manuṣyāḥ adharmarāgaraktā bhavanti viṣamalobhābhibhūtā (2) mithyādharmaparītāḥ / teṣāṃ vyāpāda utkarṣaṃ gato 'nyonyaṃ sattvāṃ dṛṣṭvā tīvram āghātacittaṃ (3) vadhakacittaṃ ca pratyupasthitaṃ bhavati / tadyathedānīṃ mṛgalubdhakasyāraṇyakaṃ mṛgaṃ dṛṣṭvā te yad yad eva (4) gṛhṇanti kāṣṭhaṃ vā loṣṭaṃ vā tat teṣāṃ tīkṣṇaṃ śastraṃ prādurbhavati / te 'nyonyaṃ sattvaṃ jīvitād vyaparopayanti / (5) punaḥ kalpasya iryāṇakāle daśavarṣāyuṣāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ tair eva doṣair amanuṣyā ītim utsṛjanti / (6) teṣām asādhyā vyādhyādayaḥ prādurbhavanti yato miryante / punar daśavarṣāyuṣāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ tair eva (7) doṣair devā varṣaṃ notsṛjanti yato durbhikṣaṃ jāyate cañcaḥ śvetāsthi śalākāvṛttiḥ / (8) kathaṃ ca cañcaḥ / dvābhyāṃ kāraṇābhyām / ya idānīṃ samavāyaḥ, sa tadānīṃ cañca ity ucyate / (9) samugo' 'pi cañcaḥ / te ca manuṣyā jighatsādaurbalyaparītāḥ sametya kālaṃ kurvanti, samudgeṣu (10) cānāgatajanatānugrahārthaṃ bījāny avasthāpayanti / atas taddurbhikṣaṃ cañcam ity ucyate / kathaṃ (11) śvetāsthi / dvābhyāṃ kāraṇābhyām / teṣāṃ hi śuṣkarukṣakāyānāṃ kālaṃ kurvatām āśvetāsthīni (12) śvetāni bhavanti, bubhukṣāhatāś ca śvetāny asthīni saṃhṛtya kvāthayitvā pibanti / kathaṃ śalākāvṛttiḥ / (13) dvābhyāṃ kāraṇābhyām / te hi sattvāḥ śalākoddeśikayā gṛheṣv āmiṣaṃ (14) saṃvibhajante adya gṛhasvāmī bhokṣyate, śvo gṛhasvāminīity evamādi / dhānyasthānavivarebhyaś ca śalākayā (15) dhānyaphalāni niṣkṛṣya bahulodakena kvāthayitvā pibanti / evaṃ varṇayanti / yenaikāham api (16) prāṇātipātaviratiḥ saṃrakṣitā bhavati, ekaharītakī vā saṃghāyaikapiṇḍpāto vā satkṛtyānupradatto (17) bhavati, sa teṣu śastrarogadurbhikṣāntarakalpeṣu notpadyata iti / (18) 
	atha kiyantaṃ kālam etāni śastrāghātarogadurbhikṣāṇi teṣāṃ sattvānāṃ bhavanti (19) 
		divasān sapta māsāṃś ca varṣāṇi ca yathākramam //99cd // (20) 
śastrakaḥ prāṇātipātaḥ sapta divasān bhavati / rogaḥ sapta māsāṃś ca divasāṃś ca / durbhikṣaṃ (21) sapta varṣāṇi ca māsāṃś ca divasāṃś ceti samuccayārthaś cakāraḥ / tadā ca dvayor dvīpayos teṣāṃ (22) pratirūpakāṇi bhavanti / vyāpāda udrekaprāpto bhavati, vaivastryadaurbalye jighatsāpipāse ca / (23) 
	yad uktam evam anyasyām api saṃvartanyāṃ veditavyaṃ yathāyogam iti / (24) atha katīmāḥ saṃvatanyaḥ / [p.189] 
		saṃvartanyaḥ punas tisro (Pradhan:tistro) bhavanty agnyambuvāyubhiḥ / (100ab) (2) 
ekatra dhyāne sattvāḥ samaṃ samvartante etasyām iti saṃvartanī / saptabhiḥ sūryais tejaḥsaṃvartanī (3) bhavati, varṣodakenāpsaṃvartanī, vāyuprakopād vāyusaṃvartanī / tābhiś ca (4) bhājanānāṃ sūkṣmo 'py avayavo nāvaśiṣyate / atra tu kecit tīrthaṃkarā icchanti / paramāṇavo (5) nityās te tadānīṃ śiṣyanta iti / kasmāt ta evam icchanti / mā bhūd abījakaḥ sthūlānāṃ (6) prādurbhāva iti / nanu ca sattvānāṃ karmajaḥ prabhāvaviśiṣṭo vāyur bījam uktam / saṃvartanīśīrṣavāyur vā (7) tasya nimittaṃ bhaviṣyati / vāyunā lokāntarebhyo bījāny āhriyanta iti mahīśāsakā sūtre (8) paṭhanti / evam api na te bījādibhyo 'ṅkurādīnām utpattim icchanti / kiṃ tarhi / svebhya (9) evāvayavebhyas teṣām api svebhyaḥ / evaṃ yāvat paramāṇubhyaḥ / kim idaṃ bījādīnām aṅkurādiṣu (10) sāmarthyam / na kiṃcid anyatra tatparamāṇūupasarpaṇāt / kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇaṃ ta evam icchanti / (11) na hi vijātīyāt saṃbhavo yukta iti / kasmān na yuktaḥ / aniyamo hi syāt / śaktiniyamān naivaṃ (12) bhaviṣyati / śabdapākajotpattivat / citro hi guṇadharmo dravyaṃ tu naivam / samānajātīyebhyaḥ (13) eva hi dravyebhyaḥ samānajātīyānāṃ dṛṣṭa utpādas tadyathā vīraṇebhyaḥ kaṭasya tantubhyaḥ paṭasyeti / (14) idam ayuktaṃ vartate / kim atrāyuktam / yad asiddhaṃ sādhanāyodāhriyate / (15) kim atrāsiddham / anyo vīraṇebhyaḥ kaṭo 'nyaś ca tantubhyaḥ paṭa iti / ta eva hi te yathāsaṃ(16)niviṣṭās tāṃ tāṃ saṃjñāṃ labhante / pipīlikāpaṅktivat / kathaṃ gamyeta / ekatantusaṃyoge (17) paṭasyānupalambhāt / ko hi tadā sataḥ paṭasyopalabdhau pratibandhaḥ / akṛtsnavṛttau paṭabhāgo 'tra (18) syān na paṭaḥ / samūhamātraṃ ca paṭaḥ syāt / kaś ca tantubhyo 'nyaḥ paṭabhāgaḥ / anekāśrayasaṃyogāpekṣaṇe (19) daśāmātrasaṃyoge paṭotpalabdhiḥ syān na vā' kadācit / madhyaparabhāgānām indriyeṇāsaṃnikarṣāt (20) / kramasaṃnikarṣe cāvayavānaṃ cakṣuḥsparśanābhyām avayavavijñānaṃ na syāt / tasmāt krameṇa (21) saṃnikarṣād avayavivyavasāyād avayaveṣv eva tadbuddhir alātacakravat / bhinnarūpajātikriyeṣu tantuṣu (22) paṭasya rūpādyasaṃbhavāt / citrarūpāditve vijātīyārambho 'pi syāt acitre ca pārśvāntare (23) paṭasyādarśanaṃ citradarśanaṃ vā / kriyāapi citrety aticitram / tāpaprakāśabhede vāgni(24)prabhāyā ādimadhyānte tadrūpasparśayor anupapattiḥ / paramānvatīndriyatve pi samastānāṃ pratyakṣatvaṃ [p.190] yathā teṣāṃ kāryārambhakatvaṃ cakṣurādīnāṃ ca taimirikānāṃ ca (2) vikīrṇakeśopalabdhiḥ / teṣāṃ paramāṇuvad ekaḥ keśo 'tīndriyaḥ / rūpādiṣv eva ca paramāṇusaṃjñā(3)niveśāt tadvināśe siddhaḥ paramāṇuvināśaḥ / dravyaṃ hi paramāṇur anyac ca rūpādibhyo dravyam iti na (4) teṣāṃ vināśe tadvināśaḥ sidhyati / ayuktam asyānyatvaṃ yāvatā na nirdhāryate kecit imāni (5) pṛthivyaptejāṃsi ima eṣāṃ rūpādaya iti / cakṣuḥsparśanagrāhyāṇi ca prajñāyante dagdheṣu (6) corṇākarpāsakusumbhakuṅkumādiṣu(!karṣā!') tadbuddhyabhāvād rūpādibhedeṣv eva tadbuddhiḥ / pākajotpattau ghaṭaparijñānaṃ (7) saṃsthānasāmānyāt paṅktivat / cihnam apaśyataḥ parijñānābhāvāt / ko vā bālapralāpeṣv ādaraḥ (8) iti tiṣṭhatu tāvad evāpratiṣedhaḥ / (9) 
	atha kasyāḥ saṃvartanyāḥ katamac chīrṣaṃ bhavati / (10) 
		dhyānatrayaṃ dvitīyādi śīrṣaṃ tāsāṃ yathākramam //100cd // (11) 
trīṇi saṃvartanīśīrṣāṇi / tejaḥsaṃvartanyā dvitīyaṃ dhyānaṃ śīrṣaṃ bhavaty adho dahyate / apsaṃvartanyās tṛtīyaṃ (12) dhyānaṃ śīrṣaṃ bhavaty adhaḥ klidyate / vāyusaṃvarttanyāś caturthaṃ dhyānaṃ śīrṣaṃ bhavaty adho vikīryate / yad dhi (13) saṃvartanyā upariṣṭāt tac chīrṣam ity ucyate / kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇaṃ prathamadvitīyatṛtīya(14)dhyānāni tejojalavāyubhir dhvasyante / (15) 
		tadapakṣālasādharmyāt (101a) (16) 
prathame hi dhyāne vitarkavicārā apakṣāñāḥ / te ca manasaḥ paridāhakatvād agnikalpāḥ / dvītīye (17) prītir apakṣālaḥ, sā ca prasrabdhiyogenāśrayamṛdūkaraṇād apkalpāḥ / ata eva tasmin kṛtsnakāya(18)krauryāpagamāt duḥkhendriyasya nirodha uktaḥ sūtre / tṛtīye dhyāne āśvāsapraśvāsāḥ / (19) te ca vāyava eva / iti yasyāṃ dhyānasamāpattau yathābhūta ādhyātmiko 'pakṣālas tasyāṃ dhyānopapattau (20) tathābhūto bāhya iti / kasmāt pṛthivīsaṃvartanī na bhavati / pṛthivy eva hi bhājanākhyā tasyāṃ (21) tejojalavāyubhir virodho na pṛthivyeti / atha caturthadhyāne kathaṃ na saṃvartanī / (22) 
		na caturthe 'sty aniñjanāt / (101b) (23) 
caturthadhyānam ādhyātmikāpakṣālarahitatvād āneñjam uktaṃ bhagavatā / ato 'tra bāhyo 'pakṣālo (24) na pravartata iti nāsty atra saṃvartanī / śuddhāvāsaprabhāvād ity apare / na hi taiḥ śakyam ārūpyān [p.191] praveṣṭuṃ nāpy anyatra gantum iti / nityaṃ tarhi caturthadhyānabhājanaṃ prāpnoti / (2) 
		na nityaṃ saha sattvena tadvimānodayavyayāt //101cd // (3) 
na hi caturthaṃ dhyānam ekabhūmisaṃbaddham / kiṃ tarhi / vicchinnasthānāntaraṃ tārakāvat (4) / tatrapi yaḥ ' sattva upapadyeta cyaveta vā, sa sārdhaṃ vimāneneti nāsty asya nityatvam / (5) kena punaḥ krameṇaitāḥ saṃvartanyo bhavanti / nirantaraṃ tāvat (6) 
		saptāgninā (102a) (7) 
sapta saṃvartanyas tejasā bhavanti / tataḥ (8) 
		adbhir ekā (102a') (9) 
saptānāṃ tejaḥsaṃvartanīnām anantaram adbhiḥ saṃvartanī bhavati / (10) 
		evaṃ gate 'dbhiḥ saptake punaḥ / (102a''b) (11) 
		tejasā saptakaḥ (102c) (12) 
etena krameṇāpsaṃvartanīnāṃ gate saptake punaḥ tejaḥsaṃvartanīnāṃ saptako bhavati / (13) 
		paścād vāyusaṃvartanī tataḥ //102c'd // (14) 
paścād ekā vāyusaṃvartanī bhavati / kiṃ kāraṇam / yathaiva hi teṣāṃ sattvānāṃ samāpattiviśeṣād (15) ātmabhāvānāṃ sthitiviśeṣas tathā bhājanānām apām iti tā etā bhavanti ṣaṭpañcāśat tejaḥsaṃvartanyaḥ (16) saptāpsaṃvarttanyaḥ ekā vāyusaṃvarttanī / evaṃ ca prajñaptibhāṣyaṃ sunītaṃ bhavati, catuḥṣaṣtikalpāḥ (17) śubhakṛtsnānāṃ devānām āyuḥpramāṇam iti / (18) 
		ye dharmā hetuprabhavā hetuṃ teṣāṃ tathāgato hy avadat / (19) 
		teṣāṃ ca yo nirodha evaṃvādī mahāśramaṇaḥ // iti / (20)

KAPITEL IV

[p.192] 
Abhidharmakośabhāṣye Caturthaṃ Kośasthānam, 
			(1) oṃ namo buddhāya || (2) 
	atha yad etat sattvabhājanalokasya bahudhā vaicitryam uktaṃ, tat kena kṛtam | na khalu kenacid buddhipūrvakaṃ (3) kṛtam | kiṃ tarhi | sattvānāṃ, (4) 
		karmajaṃ lokavaicitryaṃ (1a) (5) 
	yadi karmajaṃ kasmāt sattvānāṃ karmabhiḥ kuṅkumacandanādayo ramyatarā jāyante, na teṣāṃ śarīrāṇi | (6) karmāṇy eva tāny evaṃjātīyani vyāmiśrakāriṇāṃ sattvānāṃ yad āśrayāś ca vraṇabhūtā jāyante, bhogāś ca (7) ramyās tatpratīkārabhūtāḥ | avyāmiśrakāriṇāṃ tu devānām ubhaye 'pi ramyāḥ | 
	kiṃ punas tat karmetyāha, (8) 
		cetanā tatkṛtaṃ ca tat | (1b) (9) 
	sūtra uktaṃ "dve karmaṇī, cetanā karma cetayitvā ce"ti | yat tac cetayitvā cetanākṛtaṃ ca tat | (10) te ete dve karmaṇī trīṇi bhavanti | kāyavāṅmanaskarmāṇi | katham eṣāṃ karamaṇāṃ vyavasthānam | (11) kim āśrayataḥ āhosvit svabhāvataḥ samutthānato vā | āśrayataś ced ekaṃ kāyakarma prāpnoti | sarveṣāṃ (12) kāyāśritatvāt | svabhāvataś ced vākkarmaikaṃ prāpnoti | vacasaḥ karmasvabhāvatvāt | samutthānataś cen manaskarmaikaṃ (13) prāpnoti | sarveṣāṃ manaḥsamutthitatvāt | yathākramaṃ tribhiḥ kāraṇais trayāṇām iti vaibhāṣikāḥ | 
	tatra punaḥ, (14) 
		cetanā mānasaṃ karma (1c) (15) 
	cetanā manaskarmeti veditavyam | (16) 
		tajjaṃ vākkāyakarmaṇī ||1d || (17) 
	yat tac cetanājanitaṃ cetayitvā karmety uktaṃ kāyavākkarmaṇī te veditavye | (18) 
		te tu vijñaptyavijñaptī (2a) (19) 
	te tu kāyavākkarmaṇī pratyekaṃ vijñaptyavijñaptisvabhāve veditavye | 
	tatra tu, (20) 
		kāyavijñaptir iṣyate | (21) 
		saṃsthānaṃ (2bc) (22) 
	cittavaśena kāyasya tathā tathā saṃsthānaṃ kāyavijñaptiḥ | gatir ity apare | prasyandamānasya hi (23) kāyakarma no 'prasyandamānasyeti | 
	ta ucyante [p.193] 
		na gatir yasmāt saṃskṛtaṃ kṣaṇikaṃ (2bc) (2) 
	ko 'yaṃ kṣaṇo nāma | ātmalābho 'nantaravināśī | so 'syāstīti kṣaṇikam | daṇḍikavat | (3) sarvaṃ hi saṃskṛtam ātmalābād ūrdhvaṃ na bhavatīti yatraiva jātaṃ tatraiva dhvasyate | tasyāyuktā deśāntara(4)saṃkrāntiḥ | tasmān na gatiḥ kāyakarma | syād etad eva yadi sarvasya kṣaṇikatvaṃ sidhyet | siddham evaitat (5) viddhi | kutaḥ | saṃskṛtasyāvaśyaṃ, (7) 
		vyayāt ||2d || (8) 
	ākasmiko hi bhāvānāṃ vināśaḥ | kiṃ kāraṇam | kāryasya hi kāraṇaṃ bhavati | vināśaś cābhāvaḥ | (9) yaś cābhāvas tasya kiṃ kartavyam | so 'sāv ākasmiko vināśo yadi bhāvasyotpannamātrasya na syāt paścād api na syād bhāvasya tulyatvāt | athānyathībhūtaḥ | na yuktaṃ tasyaivānyathātvam | na hi sa eva (10) tasmād vilakṣaṇo yujyate | dṛṣṭo vai kāṣṭhādīnām agnyādisaṃyogād vināśaḥ | na ca (11) dṛṣṭād gariṣṭhaṃ pramāṇam astīti | na ca sarvasyākasmiko vināśaḥ | kathaṃ tāvat bhavān kāṣṭhādīnām agnyādi(12)saṃyogād vināśaṃ paśyāmīti manyate | teṣāṃ punar adarśanāt | saṃpradhāryaṃ tāvad etat | kim agnisaṃyogāt (13) kāṣṭhādayo vinaṣṭā ato na dṛśyante, utāho svayaṃ vinaṣṭā anye ca punar notpannā ato na dṛśyante | (14) yathā vāyusaṃyogāt pradīpaḥ pāṇisaṃyogād ghaṇṭāśabda iti | tasmād anumānasādhyo 'yam arthaḥ | kiṃ (15) punar atrānumānam | uktaṃ tāvat akāryatvād abhāvasyeti | punaḥ, (16) 
		na kasyacid ahetoḥ syāt (3a) (17) 
	yadi vināśo hetusāmānyān na kasyacid ahetukaḥ syād utpādavat | kṣaṇikānāṃ ca buddhiśabdārciṣāṃ (18) dṛṣṭa ākasmiko vināśa iti nāyaṃ hetum apekṣate | yas tu manyate buddhantarād buddher vināśaḥ (19) śabdāntarāc chabdasyeti | tad ayuktam | buddhyor asamavadhānāt | na hi saṃśayaniścayajñānayor yuktaṃ (20) samavadhānaṃ sukhaduḥkhayo rāgadveṣayor vā | yadā ca paṭubuddhiśabdānantaram apaṭubuddhiśabdāv utpadyete, (21) tadā katham apaṭuḥ samānajātīyo dharmaḥ paṭīyāṃsaṃ hiṃsyāt | antyayoś ca katham | yo 'py arciṣām avasthānahetvabhāvād (22) dharmādharmavaśād vā vināśaṃ manyate tad apy ayuktam | na hy abhāvaḥ (23) kāraṇaṃ bhavitum arhati | nacāpy utpādavināśahetvor dharmādharmayoḥ kṣaṇe kṣaṇe vṛttilābhapratibandhau (24) bhavitum arhataḥ | śakyaś caiṣa kāraṇa' parikalpaḥ sarvatra saṃskṛte kartum ity alaṃ vivādena | [p.194] 
	yadi ca kāṣṭhādīnām agnyādi saṃyogahetuko vināśaḥ syād evaṃ sati pākajānāṃ guṇānāṃ pakvatara(2)tamotpātau, (3) 
		hetuḥ syāc ca vināśakaḥ | (3b) (4) 
	hetur eva ca vināśakaḥ syāt | kathaṃ kṛtvā | yato 'gnisaṃbandhād guṇā pākajā utpannās tata (5) eva tādṛśād vā punaḥ pakvataratamotpattau teṣāṃ vināśa iti hetur eva teṣāṃ vināśakaḥ syād dhetvaviśiṣṭo (6) vā | na ca yuktaṃ yata eva tādṛśād vā teṣāṃ bhāvas tata eva tādṛśāc ca teṣāṃ punar abhāva iti | (7) jvālāntareṣu tāvad dhetubhedakalpanāṃ parikalpayeyuḥ | kṣārahimaśūktasūryodakabhūmisaṃbandhāt tu (8) pākajaviśeṣotpattau kāṃ kalpanāṃ kalpayeyuḥ | yat tarhy āpaḥ kvāthyamānāḥ kṣīyante kiṃ tatrāgnisaṃyogāḥ (9) kurvanti | tejodhātuṃ prabhāvato vardhayanti, yasya prabhāvād apāṃ saṃghātaḥ kṣāmakṣāmo jāyate (10) yāvad atikṣāmatāṃ gato 'nte na punaḥ saṃtānaṃ saṃtanoti | idam atrāgnisaṃyogāḥ kurvanti | (11) tasmān nāsti bhāvānaṃ vināśahetuḥ | svayam eva tu bhaṅguratvād vinaśyanta (12) utpannamātrā vinaśyantīti siddha eṣāṃ kṣaṇabhaṅgaḥ kṣaṇabhaṅgāc ca gatyabhāvaḥ | atyabhimānas tu (13) deśāntareṣu nirantarotpattau tṛṇajvālāvat | gatyabhāve ca saṃsthānaṃ kāyavijñaptir iti siddham | (14) 
	nāsti saṃsthānaṃ dravyata iti sautrāntikāḥ | ekadiṅmukhe hi bhūyasi varṇa utpanne dīrghaṃ (15) rūpam iti prajñapyate | tam evāpekṣyālpīyasi hrasvam iti | caturdiśaṃ bhūyasi caturasram iti | (16) sarvatra same vṛttam iti | evaṃ sarvam | tadyathālāatam ekasyāṃ diśi deśāntareṣv anantareṣu nirantaram āśu (17) dṛśyamānaṃ dīrgham iti pratīyate sarvato dṛśyamānaṃ maṇḍalam iti | na tu khalu dravyāntaram asti (18) saṃsthānam | 
	yadi hi syāt, (19) 
		dvigrāhyaṃ syāt (3c) (20) 
	cakṣuṣā hi dṛṣṭvā dīrgham ity avasīyate kāyendriyeṇāpi spṛṣṭveti dvābhām asya grahaṇaṃ prāpnuyāt | na ca (21) rūpāyatanasya dvābhyāṃ grahaṇam asti | yathā vā spraṣṭavye dīrghādigrahaṇaṃ tathā varṇe saṃbhāvyatām | (22) smṛtimātraṃ tatra spraṣṭavyasāhacaryāt bhavati | sa tu sāhacaryāt bhavati na tu sākṣāt grahaṇam | (23) yathāgnirūpaṃ dṛṣṭvā tasyoṣṇatāyāṃ smṛtir bhavati puṣpagandhaṃ ca ghrātvā tadvarṇa iti | yuktam atrāvyabhicāratvād (24) anyenānyasya smaraṇaṃ na tu kiṃcit spraṣṭavyaṃ kvacit saṃsthāne [p.195] niyataṃ yatas tatra smaraṇaṃ niyamena syāt | athāsaty api sāhacaryaniyame saṃsthānasmaraṇaṃ niyamena (2) syāt | varṇe 'pi syāt varṇavad vā saṃsthāne 'py aniyamena syāt | na caivaṃ bhavati | ayuktam asya (3) spraṣṭavyāt smaraṇam | citrāstaraṇe vānekavarṇasaṃsthāne darśanād bahūnām aikadeśyaṃ prāpnuyāt | (4) tac cāyuktaṃ varṇavat | tasmān nāsti dravyataḥ saṃsthānam | (5) 
	yac cāpi kiṃcit sapratighaṃ rūpam asti tad avaśyaṃ paramāṇau vidyate | (6) 
		na cāṇau tat (3c) (7) 
	na ca saṃthānaṃ paramāṇau vidyate dīrghādi | tasmād bahuṣv eva tathā saṃniviṣṭeṣu dīrghādiprajñaptiḥ | (8) atha mataṃ saṃsthānaparamāṇava eva tathā saṃniviṣṭā dīrghādisaṃjñāṃ labhanta iti | so 'yaṃ kevalaḥ (9) pakṣapātas teṣām asiddhatvāt | siddhasvalakṣaṇānāṃ hi teṣāṃ saṃcayo yujyate | na ca sasṃsthānāvayavānāṃ (10) varṇādivat svabhāvaḥ siddha iti kuta eṣāṃ saṃcayaḥ | yat tarhi varṇaś cābhinno bhavati saṃsthānaṃ ca (11) bhinnaṃ dṛśyate mṛdbhājanānām | nanu coktaṃ yathā kṛtvā varṇe dīrghādisaṃjñā prajñapyate, yathā ca (12) pipīlikādīnām abhede paṅkticakrādīnāṃ bhedaḥ prajñāyate tathā saṃsthānasyāpi | yat tarhi tam api (13) dūrād vā varṇam apaśyantaḥ sthāṇvādīnāṃ darighyādīni paśyanti varṇam eva te (14) tatrāvyaktaṃ dṛṣtvā dīrghādiparikalpaṃ kurvanti | paṅktisenāparikalpavat | itthaṃ caitad evam | (15) yat kadācid anirdhāryamāṇaparicchedaṃ saṃghātamātram avyaktaṃ dṛśyate kim apy etad iti | athedānīṃ kāyasya (16) gatiṃ nirākṛtya saṃsthānaṃ ca tatra bhavanti sautrāntikāḥ kāṃ kāyavijñāptiṃ prajñapayanti | saṃsthānam eva (17) hi te kāyavijñaptiṃ prajñapayanti | nanu punar dravyataḥ | tāṃ ca prajñapayantaḥ kathaṃ kāyakarma prajñapayanti | (18) kāyādhiṣṭhānaṃ karma kāyakarma yā cetanā kāyasya tatra tatra praṇetrī | evaṃ vāmanaskarmaṇī api (19) yathāyogaṃ veditavye | yat tarhi "cetanā karma cetayitvā ce"ty uktaṃ saṃkalpacetanā pūrvaṃ bhavaty evaṃ (20) caivaṃ ca kariṣyāmīti | tathā cetayitvā paścāt kriyā cetanotpadyate | yayā kāyaḥ preryate (21) sāsau cetayitvā karmety ucyate | evaṃ tarhi vijñaptyabhāvād avijñaptir api kāmāvacarī nāstīti (22) mahānto doṣā anuṣajyante | anuṣaṅgānāṃ punaḥ pratyanuṣaṅgā bhaviṣyanti | yadi tasmād eva (23) kāyakarmasaṃśabditāc cetanāviśeṣād avijñaptiḥ syāt | cittānuparivartinī syāt samāhitavijñaptivat (24) naivaṃ bhaviṣyati | cetanāviśeṣeṇa tadākṣepaviśeṣāt | sāpi ca [p.196] vijñaptiḥ satī tadākṣepe cetanāyā valaṃ nibhālayate | jaḍatvāt | 
	dravyam eva tu saṃsthānaṃ (2) vaibhāṣikā varṇayanti saṃsthānātmikāṃ tu kāyavijñaptim | (3) 
		vāgvijñaptis tu vāgdhvaniḥ ||3d || (4) 
	vāksvabhāvo yaḥ śabdaḥ saiva vāgvijñaptiḥ | avijñaptiḥ pūrvam evoktā | sāpi dravyato (5) nāstīti sautrāntikāḥ | abhyupetyākaraṇamātratvāt | atītāny api mahābhūtāny upādāya (6) prajñaptes teṣāṃ cāvidyamānasvabhāvatvād rūpalakṣaṇābhāvāc ca | astīti vaibhāṣikāḥ | kathaṃ jñāyate | (7) 
		trividhāmalarūpoktivṛddhyakurvatpathādibhiḥ | (4ab) (8) 
	trividhaṃ rūpam uktaṃ sūtre | "tribhiḥ sthānai rūpasya rūpasaṃgraho bhavati | asti rūpaṃ sanidarśanaṃ (9) sapratigham | asti rūpam anidarśanaṃ sapratigham | asti rūpam anidarśanam apratigham" iti | (10) 
	anāsravaṃ ca bhagavatā rūpam uktam | "anāsravāḥ dharmāḥ katame | yasmin rūpe 'tītānāgata(11)pratyutpanno notpadyate, anunayo vā pratigho vā, yāvad yasmin vijñāne | ima ucyante 'nāsravā (12) dharmā" iti | na cāvijñaptiṃ virahayyāsti rūpam anidarśanam apratighaṃ, nāpy anāsravam | 
	vṛdddhir api (13) coktā | "ebhiḥ saptabhir aupadhikaiḥ puṇyakriyāvastubhiḥ samanvāgatasya (14) śrāddhasya kulaputrasya kuladuhitur vā carato vā svapato vā tiṣṭhato vā jāgrato vā satatasamitam abhivardhata (15) eva puṇyam upajāyata eva puṇyam | evaṃ niraupadhikair" iti | na cāvijñaptim antareṇānyamanaso 'pi (16) puṇyasyābhivṛddhir yujyate | 
	akurvataś ca svayaṃ paraiḥ kārayataḥ karmapathā na sidhyeyur asatyām avijñaptau | (17) na hy ājñāpanavijñaptiḥ maulaḥ karmapatho yujyate | tasya karmaṇo 'kṛtatvāt | kṛte 'pi ca (18) tasyāḥ svabhāvāviśeṣād iti | 
	uktaṃ ca bhagavatā "dharmā bhikṣavo bāhyam āyatanam ekādaśabhir (19) āyatanair asaṃgṛhītam anidarśanam apratigham" iti | na tv arūpīty uktam | tatra kiṃ prayojanaṃ syād yadi (20) dharmāyatanāntargatam avijñaptirūpaṃ naśyeta | 
	aṣṭāṅgaś ca mārgo na syād avijñaptim antareṇa | samāpannasya (21) samyagvākkarmāntājīvānām ayogāt | yat tarhīdam uktaṃ "tasyaivaṃ jānata evaṃ paśyataḥ samyagdṛṣṭir (22) bhāvanāparipūriṃ gacchati, samyaksaṃkalpaḥ samyagvyāyāmaḥ samyaksmṛtiḥ samyaksamādhiḥ | (23) pūrvam eva cāsya samyagvākkarmāntājīvāḥ pariśuddhā bhavanti paryavadātā" iti | laukikamārga(24)vairāgyaṃ pūrvakṛtam abhisaṃdhāyāaitad uktam | prātimokṣasaṃvaraś cāpi na [p.197] syād asatyām avijñaptau | na hi samādānād ūrdhvaṃ tad asti yenānyamanasko 'py ayaṃ bhikṣuḥ syāt (2) bhikṣuṇī veti | 
	setuś ca sūtre viratir uktā | dauḥśīlyavibandhatvāt | na vā bhavantī (3) setur bhavitum arhatīty asty evāvijñaptiḥ | 
	atra sautrāntikā āhuḥ bahv apy etac citram apy etat | naivaṃ (4) tv etat | kiṃ kāraṇam | 
	yat tāvad uktaṃ "trividharūpokter" iti | tatra yogācārā (5) upadiśanti | dhyāyināṃ samādhiviṣayo rūpaṃ samādhiprabhāvād utpadyate | cakṣurindriyāviṣayatvāt (6) anidarśanam | deśānāvaraṇatvād apratigham iti | atha matam katham idānīṃ tat rūpam iti | (7) etad avijñaptau samānam | 
	yad apy uktam "anāsravarūpokter" iti, tad eva samādhiprabhāvasaṃbhūtaṃ rūpam anāsrave (8) samādhāv anāsravaṃ varṇayanti yogācārāḥ | arhato yad rūpaṃ bāhyaṃ cety apare | āsravāṇām aniśrayatvāt | (9) yat tarhi sūtra uktaṃ "sāsravā dharmāḥ katame | yāvad eva cakṣur yāvad eva rūpāṇī"ti vistaraḥ | (10) tatra punar āsravāṇām aprātipakṣatvāt sāsravam uktam | paryāyeṇa tarhi tad eva sāsravaṃ cānāsravaṃ ca syāt | (11) kiṃ syāt | lakṣaṇasaṃkaraḥ syāt | yathā tat sāsravaṃ tathā na kadācid anāsravam iti [ |] (12) ko 'tra saṃkaraḥ | yadi ca rūpāyatanādīni ekāntena sāsravāṇi syur, iha (13) sūtre kim arthaṃ viśeṣitāni syur "yāni rūpāṇi sāsravāṇi sopādānīyāni cetaḥkhilamrakṣavastv" iti (14) vistaraḥ | 
	yad apy uktaṃ "puṇyābhivṛddhivacanād" iti, tatrāpi pūrvācāryā nirdiśanti "dharmatā (15) hy eṣā yathā dātṛrṇāṃ dāyāḥ paribhujyante, tathā tathā bhokṭṛrṇāṃ guṇaviśeṣād anugrahaviśeṣāc cānyamanasām api (16) dātṛrṇāṃ tadālambanadānacetanābhāvitāḥ saṃtatayaḥ sūkṣmaṃ pariṇāmaviśeṣaṃ prāpnuvanti, yenāyatyāṃ (17) bahutaraphalābhiṣpattaye samarthā bhavanti" | idam abhisaṃdhāyoktaṃ bhaved "abhivardhata eva puṇyam upajāyata (18) eva puṇyam" iti | 
	atha mataṃ, katham idānīṃ saṃtānāntaraviśeṣād anyamanaso 'pi saṃtānāntarasya (19) pariṇāmaḥ setsyatīti | etad avijñaptau samānam | katham idānīṃ saṃtānāntaraviśeṣāt saṃtānāntare (20) dharmāntaram avijñaptiḥ setsyatīti, nirupadhikeṣv idānīṃ puṇyakriyāvastuṣu kathaṃ bhaviṣyatīti | (21) abhīkṣṇaṃ tadālambanacetanābhyāsāt svapneṣv api tā anuṣaṅginyo bhavanti | avijñaptivādinas tu (22) niraupadhike yatra vijñaptir nāsti tatra katham avijñaptiḥ syāt | aupadhike(23) 'py abhīkṣṇaṃ tadālambane cetanābhyāsād ity apare | 
	yat tarhi sūtra uktaṃ "yasyograbhikṣuḥ (24) śīlavān kalyāṇadharmā piṇḍakaṃ paribhujyāpramāṇaṃ cetaḥsamādhiṃ kāyena sākṣātkṛtvopasaṃpadya (25) viharaty apramāṇas tannidānaṃ dāyakasya dānapateḥ puṇyābhiṣyandaḥ kuśalābhiṣyandaḥ sukhāsvādadhāraḥ [p.198] pratikāṃkṣitavya" ity atra tadānīṃ dātuḥ kaś cetanāviśeṣaḥ | tasmāt saṃtatipariṇāmaviśeṣa eva (2) nyāyyaḥ | 
	yad apy uktaṃ "kārayataḥ kathaṃ karmapathāḥ setsyantī"ti, tatrāpy evaṃ varṇayanti | (3) tatprayogeṇa pareṣām upaghātaviśeṣāt prayoktuḥ sūkṣmaḥ saṃtatipariṇāmaviśeṣo jāyate, yata āyatyāṃ (4) samante 'pi bahutaraphalābhinirvarttanasamarthā bhavatīti svayam api ca kurvataḥ kriyāphalaparisamāptāv eṣa (5) eva nyāyo veditavyaḥ | so 'sau saṃtatipariṇāmaviśeṣaḥ karmaphatha ity ākhyāyate | kārye kāraṇopacārāt (6) | kāyikavācikatvaṃ tu tatkriyāphalatvād yathāvijñapaptivādinām avijñpter iti | (7) 
	upātteṣu skandheṣu trikālayā cetanayā prāṇātipātāvadyena spṛśyata iti bhadantaḥ | haniṣyāmi (8) hanmi hatam iti cāsya yadā bhavatīti | 
	na tv iyatā karmapathaḥ parisamāpyate | mā bhūd ahate 'pi (9) mātrādau hatābhimāninām ānantaryaṃ karmeti | svayaṃ tu ghnata etāvāṃś (10) cetanāsamudācāra ity ayam atrābhiprāyo yuktarūpaḥ syāt | 
	ka idānīm eṣa pradveṣo yad avijñaptiḥ (11) prakṣipyate saṃtatipariṇāmaviśeṣaś cābhyupagamyate tathaivāprajñāyamānaḥ | na khalu kaścit pradveṣaḥ | (12) kiṃ tu cittānvayakāyaprayogeṇa kriyāparisamāptau tābhyāṃ pṛthagbhūtaṃ dharmāntaraṃ prayojayitur utpadyata (13) iti notpadyate paritoṣaḥ | yat kṛtaprayogasaṃbhūtā tu kriyāparisamāptis tasyaiva tannimittaḥ (14) saṃtatipariṇāmo bhavatīti bhavati paritoṣaḥ | cittacaittasaṃtānāc cāyatyāṃ phalotpatteḥ | uktaṃ cātra | kim uktam | "vijñaptyabhāvād" ity evamādi | tadabhāvād avijñapter abhāvaḥ | 
	yad apy uktaṃ (16) "dharmāyatanasyārūpitvaṃ yasmān noktam" iti | tad yad evātra rūpam anidarśanam apratighaṃ coktaṃ tad evāstu (17) dharmāyatanaparyāpannam | yad apy uktam "aṣṭāṅga āryamārgo na syād" iti | aṅgaṃ tāvad ācakṣva | (18) kathaṃ mārgasamāpannasya samyagvākkarmāntājīvā bhavantīti | kim asau vācaṃ bhāṣyate kriyāṃ vā (19) karoti cīvarādīn vā paryeṣate | nety āha | kiṃ tarhi | tadrūpām anāsravām avijñaptiṃ (20) pratilabhate, yasyāḥ pratilambhād vyutthito 'pi na punar mithyāvāgādiṣu pravartate, samyagvāgādiṣu ca (21) pravartate | ato nimitte naimittikopacārād avijñaptau tadākhyā (22) kriyate | yady evam ihāpy evaṃ kiṃ na gṛhyate mārgasamāpanno vināpy avijñaptyā tadrūpam āśayaṃ ca (23) āśrayaṃ ca pratilabhate, yasya pratilambhāt vyutthito 'pi na punar mithyāvāgādiṣu (24) pravartate, samyagvāgādiṣu ca pravartate | ato nimitte naimittikopacāraṃ kṛtvā aṣṭau mārgāṅgāni vyavasthāpyanta [p.199] iti | 
	aparas tv āha | tadakriyāmātram atrāṅgam uktaṃ syāt | yad asāv ārgyamārgasāmarthyād akriyā(2)niyamaṃ pratilabhate tac cānāsravamārgasaṃniśrayalābhād anāsravaṃ syāt | na hi sarvatra dravyasanto dharmāḥ (3) parisaṃkhyāyante | tadyathāṣṭau lokadharmāḥ lābho 'lābhaḥ yaśo 'yaśaḥ nindā praśaṃsā sukhaṃ (4) duḥkham iti | na cātra cīvarādīnām alābho nāmāsti dravyāntaram | prātimokṣasaṃvaro 'pi (5) syāt, yayā cetanayā vidhipūrvaṃ kṛtvābhyupagamaḥ pratiṣiddhāt karmaṇaḥ kāyavācau saṃvṛṇoti | (6) anyacitto na saṃvṛtaḥ syād iti cet | na | tadbhāvanayā kriyākāle smarataḥ tatpratyupasthānāt (7) setubhāvo 'pi syād akriyāṃ pratijñāṃ saṃskṛtya saṃskṛtya lajjito dauḥśīlyākaraṇāt (8) ity artham eva ca tasyāḥ samādānam | yadi punar avijñapter eva dauḥśīlyaṃ pratibadhnīyāt (9) na kaścit muṣitasmṛtiḥ śikṣāṃ bhindyāt | alaṃ vistareṇa | asty eva dravyāntaram avijñaptirūpam iti (10) vaibhāṣikāḥ | (11) 
		yady asti tac ca "mahābhūtāny upādāye"ty uktam | tat kiṃ vijñaptimahābhūtāny evopādāyāvijñaptir utpadyate, (12) athānyāni | anyāny eva sā mahābhūtāny upādāyotpadyate | na hi saiva sāmagrī sūkṣmaphalā (13) caudārikaphalā ca yujyate | kiṃ khalu yadātanī vijñaptis tadātanāny eva sā mahābhūtāny upādāya (14) varttate | sarvam upādāyarūpaṃ prāyeṇaivaṃ kiṃcit tu vartamānam anāgataṃ cātītāni mahābhūtāny upādāya | (15) kiṃ punas tad iti, (16) 
		kṣaṇād ūrdhvam avijñaptiḥ kāmāptātītabhūtajā ||4cd || (17) 
	prathamāt kṣaṇād ūrdhvam avijñaptiḥ kāmāvacarī atītāni mahābhūtāny upādāyotpadyate | tāny asyā (18) āśrayārthena bhavanti | pratyutpannāni śarīramahābhūtāni saṃniśrayārthena | pravṛttyanuvṛttikāraṇatvād (19) yathākramam | cakrasyeva bhūmau saṃparivartamānasya pāṇyāvedhabhūmipradeśau | (20) 
		atha kutastyāni mahābhūtāny upādāya kutasytyaṃ kāyavākkarma | (21) 
		svāni bhūtāny upādāya kāyavākkarma sāsravam | (5ab) (22) 
	kāmāvacaraṃ kāyavākkarma kāmāvcarāṇy eva mahābhūtāny upādāya [ |] evaṃ (23) yāvac caturthadhyānabhūmikaṃ tadbhūmikāny evopādāya | (24) 
		anāsravaṃ yatra jātaḥ (5c) (25) 
	anāsravaṃ tu kāyavākkarma yasyāṃ bhūmau jātas tad utpādayati tadbhūmikāny upādāya tad veditavyam | [p.200] dhātvapatitatvād anāsravāṇāṃ ca bhūtānām abhāvāt tadbalena cotpatteḥ | 
	tatra vijñaptyavijñpaptyākhya(2)karmaṇī veditavye | (3) 
		avijñaptir anupāttikā ||5d || (4) 
		naiḥṣyandikī ca sattvākhyā (6a) (5) 
	kiṃ ca, (6)

	dhyānānāsravasaṃvarā vijñaptiḥ samādhijā | sā samādhisaṃbhūtāny aupacayikāny upāttāni ca (11) mahābhūtāny upādāyotpadyate abhinnāni ca [ |] yāny eva ca bhūtāny upādāya prāṇātipātād viratir utpadyate (12) tāny eva yāvat saṃbhinnapralāpāt | kiṃ kāraṇam | cittavat bhūtabhedāt | prātimokṣasaṃvare (13) tv anyānyāni mahābhūtāny upādāya saptāvijñaptayo bhavanti | vijñaptis tu naiḥṣyandikī | upāttā tu (14) kāyikī | kiṃ punar iyaṃ vijñaptir utpadyamānā pūrvakasya saṃsthānasya saṃtānaṃ bādhitvotpadyate, (15) utāho na | kiṃ cātaḥ [ |] yadi bādhitvotpadyate | na | vipākarūpasyocchinnasya (16) punaḥ prabandhād avaibhāṣikīyaṃ prāpnoti | athābādhitvā | katham ekasmin bhūtasaṃghāte saṃsthānadvayaṃ (17) sidhyati | anyāny eva tāni naiḥṣyandikāni tadānīm upajāyante yāny upādāya vijñaptir bhavati | (18) evaṃ tarhi yady evāṅgaṃ niśrityotpadyate vijñaptis tena tenāṅgena mahīyasā bhavitavyam | tanmahābhūtair (19) abhivyāpanāt | anabhivyāpane ca punaḥ kathaṃ kṛtsnāṅgena vijñapayet | śuṣiratvāt kāyasyāsti (20) teṣām avakāśaḥ | (21)

 nivṛtā tu vijñaptiḥ kāmadhātāv api nāsti | brahmaloka evāsti | mahābrahmaṇo hi (18) śāṭhyasamutthitaṃ kāyakarma śrūyate | sa hi svaparṣanmadhye āyuṣmato 'śvajitaḥ kṣepārtham ātmānaṃ (19) kṣiptavān | tata ūrdhvam asatyāṃ vāgvijñaptau kathaṃ tatra śabdāyatanaṃ bāhyamahābhūtahetukam | (20)

		samutthānam asad yataḥ | (8b) (2)

dve asaṃskṛte niṣparyayeṇānivṛtāvyākṛte | ākāśam apratisaṃkhyānirodhaś ca | idaṃ vicāryate | (6) yadi samutthānavaśāt kuśalākuśalatvaṃ kāyavākkarmaṇaḥ kiṃ na mahābhūtānām | (7) karmāṇi hi karttur abhiprāyo na mahābhūteṣu | samāhitasyāvijñaptau nāsty abhiprāyo na cāsamāhitaṃ (8) cittaṃ tasyāḥ samutthāpakaṃ visabhāgabhūmikatvād iti kathaṃ tasyāḥ kuśalatvam | divyayor api vā (9) cakṣuḥśrotrayoḥ kuśalatvaprasaṅgaḥ | kartavyo 'tra yatnaḥ |

hetusamutthānaṃ pravartakam ākṣepakatvāt | tatkṣaṇasamutthānam anuvartakaṃ kriyākālānuvartanāt | (16) kim idānīṃ tasya tasyāṃ kriyāyāṃ sāmarthyam | tena hi vināsau mṛtasyeva na syād ākṣiptāpi satī | (17) acittakasya tarhi saṃvarotpattau kathaṃ bhavati | sphuṭatarā tarhi sacittakasya bhavatīty etatsāmarthyam' | (18) tatra ca (19)

		nobhayaṃ tu vipākajam ||12d || (2) 
vipākajaṃ tu cittaṃ naiva pravartakaṃ nānuvartakaṃ nirabhisaṃskāravāhitvāt | kim idānīṃ yathā pravartakaṃ (3) tathā vijñaptir āhosvid yathānuvartakam | kiṃ cātaḥ | yathā pravartakaṃ cet | ihāpi (4) nivṛtāvyākṛtā vijñaptiḥ prāpnoti | satkāyāntagrāhadṛṣṭipravartitatvāt (5) na vā sarvaṃ darśanaprahātavyaṃ pravartakam iti viśeṣaṇaṃ vaktavyam | yathānuvartakaṃ cet, akuśalāvyākṛta(6)cittasya prātimokṣavijñaptiḥ kuśalā na prāpnoti | yathā pravartakaṃ tathā vijñaptir na tu yathā (7) darśanaprahātavyam | bhāvanāheyāntaritatvāt | yadi nānuvartakavaśād vijñapteḥ kuśalāditvaṃ na (8) tarhīdaṃ vaktavyam | hetusamutthānaṃ saṃdhāyoktaṃ sūtre, na tatkṣaṇasamutthānam | ato nāstīha (9) nivṛtāvyākṛtā vijñaptir iti | evaṃ vaktavyam | anyavyavahitaṃ hetusamutthānaṃ saṃdhāyoktam iti | (10) avasitaḥ prasaṅgaḥ | (11)

bhikṣusaṃvaro bhikṣuṇīsaṃvaraḥ śikṣamāṇāsaṃvaraḥ śrāmaṇerasaṃvaraḥ śrāmaṇerīsaṃvaraḥ upāsakasaṃvara (20) upāsikāsaṃvara upavāsasaṃvaraś ca | eṣo 'ṣṭavidhasaṃvaraḥ prātimokṣasaṃvara (21) ity ākhyāyate | nāmata eṣo 'ṣṭavidhaḥ | (22)

		liṅgato nāmasaṃcārāt (14c) (2) 
liṅgam iti vyañjanasyākhyā, yena strīpuruṣo liṅgyete | liṅgato hi bhikṣubhikṣuṇyādīnāṃ (3) nāmasaṃvāro bhavati | kathaṃ kṛtvā | parivṛtte hi vyañjane bhikṣur bhikṣuṇīty ucyate, bhikṣunī ca (4) punar bhikṣuḥ | śrāmaṇeraḥ śrāmaṇerīty ucyate, śrāmaṇerī ca punaḥ śikṣamāṇā ca śrāmaṇeraḥ | upāsaka (5) upāsikety ucyate, upāsikā ca punar upāsaka iti | na ca vyañjanaparivṛttau pūrvasaṃvaratyāge (6) kāraṇam asti nāpy apūrvasaṃvarapratilambhe | tasmād abhinna eṣāṃ caturṇāṃ saṃvarāṇāṃ tribhyaḥ svabhāvaḥ | (7) 
		ya upāsakasaṃvarāc chrāmaṇerasaṃvaraṃ samādatte, tasmāc ca punar bhikṣusaṃvaraṃ, kiṃ te saṃvarā virativṛddhi(8)yogād anyonya ucyante pañcadaśa viṃśativat dīnāraśateravac ca ' (9) āhosvit pṛthag eva te sakalā jāyante | āha | (10)

		te cāvirodhinaḥ ||14d' || (19)

yathāsaṃkhyam anudeśo veditavyaḥ | pañcabhyo varjanīyebhyo dharmebhyo viratisamādānād upāsakasaṃvarastho [p.207] bhavati | prāṇātipātād adattādānāt kāmamithyāacārāt mṛṣāvādāt (2) surāmaireyamadyapānāc ca | aṣṭabhyo viratisamādānād upavāsasthaḥ | prāṇātipātādattādānābrahmacarya(3)mṛṣāvādamadyapānebhyo gandhamālyavilepananṛtyagītavāditrād uccaśayanamahāśayanād akālabhojanāc ca | (4) daśabhyo viratisamādānāc chrāmaṇero bhavati | ebhya eva jātarūparajatapratigrahāc ca | nṛtyagīta(5)vāditragandhamālyavilepanaṃ cātra dvayīkṛtya daśa bhavanti | sarvebhya eva varjanīyebhyaḥ kāyavākkarmabhyaḥ (6) viratisamādānāt bhikṣur ity ucyate | sa eva' prātimokṣasaṃvaraḥ (7)

		sukhaṃ śīlasamādānaṃ, 
		kāyo na paridahyata iti (10) 
gāthāvacanāt | vidvatpraśastatvāt sucaritam | kriyāsvabhāvatvāt karma | nanu cāvijñaptir a(11)kriyety ucyate | sā kathaṃ kriyā bhavati | na kurvanti tayā samāttayā lajjinaḥ pāpam ity akriyety ucyate | (12) sāpi tu vijñapticittābhyāṃ kriyata iti kriyā bhavati | kriyāhetutvāt kriyāphalatvāc cety apare | (13) saṃvara iti kāyavācoḥ saṃvaraṇāt | evaṃ tāvad aviśeṣeṇa prātimokṣasaṃvaraḥ saṃ(14)śabdyate | (15) 
		punaḥ | (16) 
		ādye vijñaptyavijñaptī prātimokṣakriyāpathaḥ ||16b'cd || (17) 
saṃvarasamādānasya prathame vijñaptyavijñaptī prātomokṣa ity ucyate | pāpasya tena pratimokṣaṇād' utsarjanād (18) ity arthaḥ | svārthe vṛddhividhānād vaikṛtavaiśasavat | prātimokṣasaṃvara ity api kāyavāksaṃvaraṇāt (19) karmapatha ity ucyate | maulasaṃgṛhītatvāt | dvtīyādiṣu kṣaṇeṣu prātimokṣasaṃvara eva na (20) prātimokṣaḥ | pṛṣṭhaṃ ca na maulaḥ karmapathaḥ | (21) 
	athaiṣāṃ saṃvarāṇāṃ kena kaḥ samanvāgataḥ | (22) 
		prātimokṣānvitā aṣṭau (17a) (23) 
prātimokṣasaṃvareṇāṣṭau nikāyāḥ samanvāgatā bhikṣur bhikṣuṇī yāvad upavāsastho 'ṣṭamaḥ | kiṃ khalu [p.208] bāhyakānāṃ samādānaśīlaṃ nāsti | asti na tu prātimokṣasaṃvaraḥ | kiṃ kāraṇam | na hi (2) tad atyantaṃ pāpasya pratimokṣaṇāya saṃvartate | bhavasaṃniśritatvāt | (3) 
		dhyānajena tadanvitaḥ | (17b) (4) 
dhyānād dhyānena' vā jāto dhyānajaḥ | yo dhyānena samanvāgataḥ, so 'vaśyaṃ dhyānasaṃvareṇa | (5) sāmantakam apy atra dhyānaṃ kṛtvocyate | yathā grāmasāmantakam api grāma ity ucyate | asty asmin (6) grāme śāleyaṃ kṣetram asti braiheyam iti | (7) 
		anāsraveṇāryasattvāḥ (17c) (8) 
āryapudgalā anāsraveṇa saṃvareṇa samanvāgatāḥ | te punaḥ śaikṣāśaikṣāḥ | yad uktaṃ sahabhūhetāv (9) ucyamāne dvau saṃvarau cittānuvartināv iti | katamau tau | (10) eṣām eva trayāṇām (11) 
		antyau cittānuvartinau ||17d || (12) 
dhyānasaṃvaro 'nāsravasaṃvaraś ca | na prātimokṣasaṃvaraḥ | kiṃ kāraṇam | anyacittācittakasyāpy anuvṛtteḥ | (13) punas tāv eva dhyānānāsravasaṃvarau prahāṇasaṃvarākhyāṃ labhete | kasyām avasthāyām ity āha (14) 
		anāgamye prahāṇākhyau tāv ānantaryamārgajau |(18ab) (15) 
anāgamye tau dhyānānāsravasaṃvarau navasv ānantaryamārgeṣu prahāṇasaṃvarāv ity ucyete | tābhyāṃ dauḥśīlyasya (16) tatsamutthāpakānāṃ ca kleśānāṃ prahāṇāt | ata eva syād dhyānasaṃvaro na prahāṇasaṃvara iti (17) catuṣkoṭikaṃ kriyate | prathamā koṭir anāgamyānantaryamārgavarjyaḥ sāsravo dhyānasaṃvaraḥ | (18) dvitīyā anāgamyānantaryamārgeṣv anāsravaḥ | tṛtīyā anāgamyānantaryamārgeṣu sāsravaḥ | caturthī (19) anāgamyānantaryamārgavarjyo 'nāsravasaṃvaraḥ | evaṃ syād anāsravasaṃvaro na prahāṇasaṃvara iti catuṣkoṭikaṃ (20) yathāyogaṃ veditavyam | yat tarhi bhagavatoktaṃ (21) 
		kāyena saṃvaraḥ sādhu sādhu vācātha saṃvaraḥ | (22) 
		manasā saṃvaraḥ sādhu sādhu sarvatra saṃvaraḥ || iti | (23) 
yac coktaqṃ cakṣurindriyeṇa saṃvarasaṃvṛto viharatīti | etau manaindriyasaṃvarau kiṃsvabhāvau | (24) vaitāv avijñaptiśīlasvabhāvau | kiṃ tarhi | [p.209] 
		saṃprajānasmṛtī dve tu manaindriyasaṃvarau ||18cd || (2) 
pratyekaṃ dvisvabhāvajñāpanārthaṃ punar dvigrahaṇaṃ mā yathāsaṃkhyaṃ vijñāyīti | manaḥsaṃvaro 'pi smṛtisaṃprajñāna(3)svabhāva iti | indriyasaṃvaro 'pi | (4) 
	idaṃ vicāryate | kaḥ katamayāvijñaptyā (Pradhan:+vā) kiyantaṃ kālaṃ samanvāgata iti | (5) tatra (6) 
		prātimokṣasthito nityam atyāgād vartamānayā | (7) 
		avijñaptyānvitaḥ (19abc) (8) 
yaḥ prātimokṣasaṃvarasthaḥ pudgala uktaḥ, sa yāvat tām avijñaptiṃ na tyajati, tāvat tayā vartamānayā nityaṃ (9) samanvāgataḥ | (10) 
		pūrvāt kṣaṇād ūrdhvam atītayā ||19c'd || (11) 
prathamāt kṣaṇād ūrdhvam atītayāpi samanvāgataḥ | atyāgād iti sarvatrādhikṛtaṃ veditavyam | yathā (12) prātimokṣasaṃvarastha uktaḥ (13) 
		tathaivāsaṃvarastho 'pi (20a) (14) 
asaṃvarastho 'pi yāvad asaṃvraṃ na tyajati, tāvan nityam avijñaptyā vartamānayā samanvāgataḥ | (15) kṣaṇād ūrdhvam atītayāpi | (16) 
		dhyānasaṃvaravān sadā | (17) 
		atītājātayā (20bc) (18) 
dhyānasaṃvarasya lābhī nityam atītānāgatābhyām avijñaptibhyāṃ samanvāgataḥ ātyāgāt | prathame 'pi (Pradhan:hi) (19) kṣaṇe sa janmāntaratyaktaṃ dhyānasaṃvaram atītaṃ labhate | (20) 
		āryas tu, prathame nābhyatītayā ||20c'd || (21) 
āryas tu pudgalo 'py evam anāsravayā | ayaṃ tu viśeṣaḥ | sa prathame kṣaṇe nātītayā (22) samanvāgato mārgasya pūrvam anutpāditatvāt | [p.210] 
		samāhitāryamārgasthau tau yuktau vartamānayā | (21ab) (2) 
tau dhyānānāsravasaṃvarānvitau samāhitāryamārgasamāpannau vartamānayā (3) avijñaptyā samanvāgatau yathākramaṃ na tu vyutthitau | saṃvarāsaṃvarasthānāṃ tāvad eṣa vṛttāntaḥ | (4) 
	athedānīṃ madhyasthasya | (5) 
		madhyasthasyāsti ced ādau, madhyayā (21cd) (6) 
yo naivasaṃvaranāsaṃvare sthitaḥ, sa madhyasthaḥ | tasya nāvaśyam avijñaptir asti | yasya (7) tv asti dauḥśīlyaśīlāṅgādisaṃgṛhitā, sa ādau madhyayā samanvāgataḥ | vartamānā (8) hy avijñaptir atītānāgatayor madhyād (9) 
		ūrdhvaṃ dvikālayā ||21d' || (10) 
prathamāt kṣaṇād ūrdhvam atītayā vartamānayā ca | ātyāgād iti vartate | kim asaṃvarasthaḥ (11) kadācit kuśalayā vijñaptyā samanvāgato bhavati, saṃvarastho vā punar akuśalayā bhavati | bhavan (12) kadā kiyantaṃ vā kālam ity āha (13) 
		asaṃvarasthaḥ śubhayāśubhayā saṃvare sthitaḥ | (14) 
		avijñaptyānvito yāvat prasādakleśavegavān ||22|| (15) 
yena prasādavegenāsaṃvarasthasya kuśalāvijñaptir utpadyate stūpa(Pradhan:stava)vandanādikriyāṃ kurvataḥ, yena ca kleśavegena (16) saṃvarasthasyākuśalāvijñaptir utpadyate vadhabandhanatāḍanādikriyāṃ kurvataḥ tau yāvad anuvartete, t āvat te (17) apy avijñaptī | sa ādye kṣaṇe vartamānayaivāvijñaptyā samanvāgato bhavaty anyeṣv atītayāpi | (18) avijñaptyadhikāraḥ samāptaḥ | (19) 
		vijñaptyā tu punaḥ sarve, kurvanto madhyānvitāḥ | (23ab) (20) 
sarve saṃvrāsaṃvaramadhyasthā yāvad vijñaptiṃ kurvanti, tāvat tayā varttamānayā samanvāgatāḥ | (21) 
		atītayā kṣaṇād ūrdhvam ātyāgāt (23cd) (22)
