		nāsty ajātayā ||23d' || [p.211] 
anāgatayā tu vijñaptyā na kaścit samanvāgataḥ | (2) 
		nivṛtānivṛtābhyāṃ ca nātītābhyāṃ samanvitaḥ | (24ab) (3) 
atītābhyām api nivṛtānivṛtāvyākṛtābhyāṃ vijñaptibhyāṃ na kaścit samanvāgataḥ | durbalasya hi (4) dharmasya prāptir api durbalā nānubandhībhavati | kiṃkṛtaṃ tasyā daurbalyam | cittakṛtam | (5) cittasyāpi tarhi nivṛtāvyākṛtasya mā bhūt | naitad evam | jaḍā hi vijñaptiḥ paratantrā ca | (6) na caivaṃ cittam | sā hi vijñaptir durbalenothāpitā durbalatarā bhavati | (7) 
	asaṃvarastha ity uktam | ko 'yam asaṃvaro nāma | (8) 
		asaṃvaro duścaritaṃ dauḥśīlyaṃ karma tatpathaḥ ||24cd || (9) 
ḥasaṃvarasyeme paryāyaśabdāḥ | tatra kāyavācor asaṃvaraṇād asaṃvaraḥ | sadbhiḥ kutsitatvād aniṣṭaphalatvād (10) duścaritam | śīlavipakṣād dauḥśīlyam | kāyavākkarmatvāt karma | maulasaṃgṛhītatvāt karmappathaḥ | (11) syād vijñaptyā samanvāgato nāvijñaptyeti catuṣkoṭikam | tatra tāvat (12) 
		vijñaptyaivānvitaḥ kurvan madhyastho mṛducetanaḥ | (25ab) (13) 
mṛdvyā cetanayā kuśalam akuśalaṃ vā kurvan naivasaṃvaranāsaṃvarasthito vijñaptyaiva (14) samanvāgato bhavati, nāvijñaptyā prāg evāvyākṛte anyatraupadhikapuṇyakriyāvastukarmapathebhyaḥ | (15) 
		tyaktānutpannavijñaptir avijñaptyāryapudgalaḥ ||25cd || (16) 
avijñaptyaiva samavnāgato na vijñapatyā yenāryapudgalena janmāntaraparivṛttau na tāvad vijñaptaṃ vā (17) punar vihīnam | uktaṃ saṃvarāsaṃvaramadhyasthānāṃ vijñaptyavijñaptisamanvāgamanavyavasthānam | (18) 
	athaite saṃvarāḥ kathaṃ labhyante | (19) 
		dhyānajo dhyānabhūmyaiva, labhyate (26ab) (20) 
yadā dhyānabhūmikaṃ cittaṃ pratilabhyate maulīyaṃ sāmantakīyaṃ vā sāsravaṃ tadā dhyānasaṃvaro 'pi (21) sahabhūtatvāt | (22) 
		anāsravas tayā | (26b') (23) 
		āryayā (26c) (24) 
tayaiva dhyānabhūmyānāsravayā labhyamānayānāsravaḥ (Pradhan:stravaḥ) saṃvaro labhyate | tatra ṣaṭ dhyānabhūmayo 'nāsravā (25) bhavanti catvāri dhyānāni anāgamyasyānantaraṃ ceti paścāt pravedayiṣyāmaḥ | [p.212] 
		prātimokṣākhyaḥ, paravijñapanādibhiḥ ||26c'd || (2) 
prātimokṣasaṃvaras tu paravijñaptito labhyate | yady enaṃ paro vijñapayati, asau ca param | sa punaḥ (3) saṃghād vā pudgalād vā | saṃghād bhikṣuṇīśikṣamāṇāsaṃvarāḥ, pudgalād anye | daśavidhā upasaṃpad iti (4) vinayavibhāṣikāḥ | tasya upasaṃgrahaṇārtham ādiśabdaḥ | svayaṃbhūtvena buddhānāṃ pratyekabuddhānāṃ ca (5) | niyāmāvakrāntyā pañcakānām | ehibhukṣukayā yaśaḥprabhṛtīnām | śāstur abhyupagamān mahākāśyapasya | praśnārādhanena sodāyinaḥ | gurudharmābhyupagamena mahāprajāpatyāḥ | dūtena dharmadinnāyāḥ | vinayadharapañcamena pratyantimeṣu janapadeṣu daśavargeṇa madhyeṣu janapadeṣu śaraṇagamanaṃ traivācikena ṣaṣṭibhadra vargapūgopasaṃpāditānām iti teṣāṃ nāvaśyaṃ vijñaptyadhīnaḥ prātimokṣasaṃvaraḥ | 
	sa punar eṣa prātimokṣasaṃvraḥ samādīyamānaḥ kiyantaṃ kālaṃ samādātavyaḥ | (10) 
		yāvajjīvaṃ samādānam ahorātraṃ ca saṃvṛteḥ | (27ab) (11) 
saptanaikāyikasya prātimokṣasaṃvarasya yāvajjīvaṃ samādānam upavāsasaṃvarasyāhorātram ity eṣa (12) niyamaḥ | kiṃ kāraṇam | dvau hi kālaparyantau | ahorātraparyanto jīvitaparyantaś ca | (13) ahorātrāṇāṃ (P.ahārotrānāṃ) paunaruktyena pakṣādayaḥ | kālo nāma ka eṣa dharmaḥ | saṃskāraparidīpanādhi(14)vacanam etat | ālokāvasthā hi dvīpeṣu divasa ity ucyate | tamo'vasthā rātriḥ | yuktaṃ (15) tāvaj jīvitād ūrdhvaṃ saty api samādāne saṃvarasyānutpattiḥ | visabhāgatvād āśrayasya tena ca (16) tatrāprayogād asmaraṇāc ca | athāhorātrād ūrdhvaṃ pañcarātraṃ daśarātraṃ vā upavāsasamādānasya kaḥ pratibandho (17) bahūnām upavāsasaṃvarāṇām utpattau | ittham asti pratibandho yad bhagavān aho(18)rātrikam evopavāsaṃ sūtre śāsti sma | idam idānīṃ saṃpradhāryam | kiṃ tāvad ahorātrād ūrdhvaṃ (19) saṃvarasyānutpattiṃ paśyatā tathāgatenāhorātrika upavāso deśita utāho durbalendriyāṇām ahorātrake 'pi (20) saṃvarotpattau yuktyavirodhāt | tad etat kasyacid apy ahorātrād ūrdhvam adeśanāṃ necchanti vaibhāṣikāḥ | (22) asaṃvarasyedānīṃ kaḥ kālaniyamaḥ | (23) 
		nāsaṃvaro 'sty ahorātraṃ (27c) (24) 
yāvajjīvaṃ pāpadharmābhyupagamād (P. karmābhy) asaṃvara upajāyate nāhorātraṃ yathopavāsaḥ | kiṃ kāraṇam | [p.213] 
		na kilaivaṃ sa (P.pra) gṛhyate (27d) (2) 
na kila kaścid evam asaṃvaraṃ samādatte yathopavāsaṃ kaccid aham ahorātram asaṃvṛtaḥ syām iti | (3) kutsitatvāt karmaṇaḥ | evaṃ caiva na kaścid ādatte kaccid ahaṃ yāvajjīvam asaṃvṛtaḥ syām iti | (4) yāvajjīvam apy asya lābho na syāt | yady api naivam ādatte tathāpy atyantavipannenāśayena tāṃ kriyāṃ (5) prakurvann asaṃvaraṃ pratilabhate na kālāntaravipannena | upavāsasaṃvaras tu samādānabalādhānād anātyantike (6) 'py āśaye labhyata eva | saṃvarārthitvāt | yadi punaḥ kaścid asaṃvareṇāpy arthī (7) kālāntaram asaṃvaraṃ samādadīta, so 'vaśyaṃ labheta | na tv etad dṛṣṭam iti naivaṃ vyavasthāpyate | (8) avijñaptivad asaṃvaro 'pi nāsti dravyata iti sautrāntikāḥ | sa eva tu pāpakriyābhisaṃdhir asaṃvaraḥ | (9) sānubandho yataḥ kuśalacitto 'pi tadvān ucyate | tasyānirākṛtatvāt | (10) 
	athāhorātraṃ gṛhyamāṇa upavāsaḥ kathaṃ grahītavyaḥ | (11) 
		kālyaṃ grāhyo 'nyato nīcaiḥ sthitenoktānuvādinā (P. ditā) | (12) 
		upavāsaḥ samagrāṅgo nirbhūṣaṇenā niśākṣayāt ||28|| (13) 
kālyaṃ tāvat sūryodayakāle ahorātrikatvāt saṃvarasya | yas tu pūrvakṛtasamādāno nityam aṣṭabhyām upace(14)ṣyāmīti sa bhuktvāpi gṛhṇīyāt | anyataś ca grahītavyo, na svayam evāparāpekṣayā satsv api (15) pratyayeṣv anatikramārtham | nīcaiḥ sthitenotkuṭṭakena vā jānupātena vā kapotakam añjaliṃ kṛtvānyatra (16) sthānāt | agauravasya hi saṃvaro notpadyate | dātuś ca vacanam anubruvīta | na pūrvaṃ na (17) yugapat | evaṃ hy asau prasmāt gṛhīto bhavati | anyathā hi dānagrahaṇaṃ na sidhyet | (18) samagrāṅgaś cāṣṭāṅga eva grahītavyo na vikalāṅgaḥ | nirbhūṣaṇena ca | ājasriṃkam abhyalaṃkāraṃ (19) muktvā | ājasriko hy alaṃkāro nātyarthaṃ madam ādadhāti | ā rātriparikṣayāc ca (20) grahītavyo yāvat punaḥ sūryodayāt | ato 'nyathā gṛhṇataḥ sucaritamātraṃ syān na tūpavāsasaṃvaraḥ | (21) evaṃ ca kṛtvā aurabhrikapāradārikayo rātridivasopavāsakayoḥ sāphalyaṃ yujyate (P.prayujyate) | arhatāṃ samīpe (22) vasanty anenety upavāsas teṣām anuśikṣaṇāt | yāvajjīvikasaṃvarasamīpe vasanty anenety apare | (23) alpakuśalamūlānāṃ kuśalamūlapoṣaṇāt poṣadha iti vā | [p.214] 
		poṣaṃ dadhāti manasaḥ kuśalasya yasmād (2) 
		uktas tato bhagavatā kila poṣadho 'yam iti | (3) 
	kimarthaṃ punar aṣṭāṅgāny upādīyante | yasmāt (4) 
		śīlāṅgāny apramādāṅgaṃ vratāṅgāni yathākramam | (5) 
		catvāry ekaṃ tathā trīṇi (27abc) (6) 
catvāri tāvac chīlāṅgāni yāvan mṛṣāvādaviratiḥ | prakṛtisāvadyaviratitvāt | ekam apamādāṅgaṃ (7) madyapānād viratiḥ | samāttaśīlo 'pi madyapaḥ pramādyeta | trīṇi vratāṅgāni yāvad akālabhojanād vi(8)ratiḥ | saṃvegānuguṇatvāt | 
	kiṃ punar ebhir apramādāṅgavratāṅgair anupāttaiḥ syāt | (9) 
		smṛtināśo madaś ca taiḥ ||29d || (10) 
madyaṃ hi pibataḥ kāryākāryasmṛtir eva naśyet | uccaśayanamahāśayananṛttagītādikaṃ pratisevamānasya (11) madaḥ saṃbhavet | mattasya ca dauḥśīlyam adūraṃ bhavet | kāle (12) punar bhūñjānasyocitabhaktakālaparihārād upavāsasmṛtiḥ saṃvegaś copatiṣṭhet | tadabhāvād ubhayaṃ na (13) syād iti | kecit tu khalv akālabhojanāt prativiratim evopavāsaṃ manyante | tasya (14) śeṣāṇy aṣṭāṅgānīti | nṛtyagītavāditraṃ gandhamālyavilepanaṃ ca dvayaṃ kṛtvā | evaṃ tu sati (15) sūtrapāṭho na yujyeta | akālabhojanād viratim uktvā anenāham aṣṭamāṅgena teṣām āryāṇām arhatāṃ (16) śikṣāyām anuśikṣe anuvidhīya iti | kas tarhi so 'nya upavāso yasyemāny aṅgāni | (17) samudāyasyāvayavā aṅgāni | yathā rathasyāṅgāni caturaṅgo balakāyaḥ pañcāṅgaṃ tūryaṃ tathāṣṭāṅga (18) upavāso draṣṭavyaḥ | akālabhojanāt prativiratir upavāsa upavāsāṅgaṃ ca yathā samyagdṛṣṭir mārgo (19) mārgāṅgaṃ ca | dharmapravicayasaṃbodhyaṅgaṃ bodhir bodhyaṅgaṃ samādhir dhyānaṃ dhyānāṅgaṃ ceti vaibhāṣikāḥ | (20) na tu teṣām eva samyagdṛṣṭyādīnāṃ ta evāṅgatvāya kalpanta iti | pūrvakāḥ samyagdṛṣṭyādaya uttareṣām aṅgaṃ (21) yadi syuḥ prathamakṣaṇotpanna āryamārgo nāṣṭāṅgaḥ syāt | (22) 
	kiṃ khalv ayam upāsaka(P. :upavāsaka)syaivopavāsa āhosvid anyasyāpi | (23) 
		anyasyāpy upavāso 'sti śaraṇaṃ tv agatasya na | (30ab) (24) 
anupāsake 'pi yas tam ahorātraṃ buddhadharmasaṅghān śaraṇaṃ gatvopavāsaṃ [p.215] grhṇāti tasyotpadyate upavāsasaṃvaro nānyathā | anyatrājñānāt | sūtra uktaṃ: yataś ca mahānāman (2) gṛhī avadātavasanaḥ puruṣaḥ puruṣendriyeṇa samanvāgato buddhaṃ śaraṇaṃ gacchati, dharmaṃ saṃghaṃ śaraṇaṃ (3) gacchati, vācaṃ ca bhāṣate upāsakaṃ (P. upavāsakaṃ) ca māṃ dhāraya | iyatā upāsako (P. upavāsako) bhavatīti | tat (4) kiṃ śaraṇagamaṇād evopāsako (P. upavāsako) bhavati | bhavatīti bahirdeśakāḥ | yasmād asyotpadyate tata eva (6) 
		upāsakatvopagamāt saṃvṛt (30cd) (7) 
upāsakatvābhyupagamād evāsyopāsakasaṃvaro jāyate | yad evābhyupagcchaty upāsakaṃ māṃ dhārayety adyāgreṇa' (8) yāvajjīvaṃ prāṇāpetam iti | prāṇātipātādyapetam ity artho madhyapadalopāt | labdhasaṃvarasyāpi (9) vyutpādanārthaṃ śikṣāpadānām (10) 
		uktis tu bhikṣuvat ||30d' || (11) 
yathaiva ' bhikṣur labdhasaṃvaro 'pi punaḥ śikṣāpadāni grāhyate, śrāmaṇeraś ca vyutpādanārtham itaś cāmutaś ca te (12) saṃvara iti tathopāsako 'pi, na tu vinā saṃvareṇopāsako 'sti | (13) 
		tatpālanāt kila proktāḥ (31a) (14) 
yo hi yac chikṣāpadaṃ pālayati, sa tatkārīty uktaḥ | sarve tu samaṃ saṃvarasthāḥ | idam utsūtraṃ vartate | (18) kim atrotsūtram | upāsakatvābhyupagamād eva saṃvaralābho yasmāt prāṇātipātam ity āheti | na (19) hy evaṃ sūtrapāṭhaḥ ukto yathā mahānāmasūtre pāṭhaḥ | tatraiva copāsakalakṣaṇopadeśo nanyatra | (20) yatra tv eṣa pāṭho: yāvajjīvaṃ prāṇāpetaṃ śaraṇagatam abhiprasannam iti | tatra te dṛṣṭasatyā (21) avetyaprasādānvayaṃ prāṇair api saddharmopagamanaṃ darśayanti sma | jīvitahetor apy abhavyā vayam enaṃ (22) dharmaṃ parityaktum iti | na tv eṣa lakṣaṇopadeśaḥ saṃvarasya | prāṇāpetaṃ tu na kvacit paṭhyate | [p.216] kaś caitad aparisphuṭārthaṃ paṭhet | ekadeśakāryādīṃs tu khaṇḍitaśikṣān adhikṛtya raśna eva na yujyate | (2) kuto visarjanam āveṇikadharmāṇām | ko hy upāsakasaṃvaraṃ jānan etan na jñāsyate yo hi yac chikṣāpadaṃ (3) na khaṇḍayati, sa tatkārī bhavatīti | upāsakasaṃvarasya tu parimāṇānabhijñāṃs tanmātraśikṣākṣamān (4) praty eṣa praśno yujyate | kiyatā bhadantopāsaka ekadeśakārī bhavati, yāvat paripūrṇakārī bhavati | (5) yadi tarhi vinā saṃvareṇopāsakaḥ syād vikalena vā bhikṣuśrāmaṇerāv api (6) syātām | kathaṃ tāvad eṣām upāsakasaṃvarādīnām aṅgapratiniyamo bhavati | śāstṛprajñaptivaśāt | (7) upāsakatvādipratiniyamo 'pi śāstṛprajñaptivaśād iṣyatām | vināpi hi saṃvareṇopāsakaḥ prajñaptito (8) na tu bhikṣuśrāmaṇerāv iti te tv etan necchanti kāśmīrāḥ | sarveṣāṃ tu saṃvarāṇām (9) 
		mṛdvāditvaṃ yathā manaḥ ||31d || (10) 
mṛdumadhyādhimātratvaṃ samādānacittavaśāt ' | evaṃ ca kṛtvārhato 'pi mṛduḥ prātimokṣasaṃvaraḥ syāt (11) pṛthagjanasyādhimātraḥ | kiṃ punaḥ saṃvaragrahaṇād evopāsakaḥ syād vinā śaraṇagamanaiḥ | na syād anyatrājñānāt | (12) 
	yo buddhadharmasaṃghāñ charaṇaṃ gacchati, kim asau śaraṇaṃ gacchati | (13) 
		buddhasaṃghakarān dharmān aśaikṣān ubhayāṃś ca saḥ | (14) 
		nirvāṇaṃ ceti śaraṇaṃ yo yāti śaraṇatrayam ||32|| (15) 
yo buddhaṃ śaraṇaṃ gacchati aśaikṣān asau buddhakarakān dharmāñ charaṇaṃ gacchati, yeṣāṃ prādhānyena sa ātmabhāvo (16) buddha ity ucyate, yeṣāṃ vā lābhena sarvāvabodhasāmarthyād buddho bhavati | ke punas te | kṣayajñānādayaḥ (17) saparivārāḥ | rūpakāyasya pūrvaṃ paścāc cāviśeṣāt | kiṃ sarvabuddhān athaikam | lakṣaṇataḥ sarvabuddhān | (18) mārgasyāvilakṣaṇatvāt | yaḥ saṃghaṃ śaraṇaṃ gacchati śaikṣāśaikṣān asau (19) saṃghakarakān dharmān gacchati, yeṣāṃ lābhenāṣṭau pudgalāḥ saṃghībhavanti | abhedatvāt | kiṃ (20) sarvasaṃghān athaikam | lakṣaṇataḥ sarvasaṃghān mārgasyāvilakṣaṇatvāt | yat tu sūtra uktaṃ: yo 'py asau (21) bhaviṣyaty anāgate 'dhvani saṃgho nāma tam api śaraṇaṃ gacchatam iti | tat pratyakṣabhāvinaḥ (22) saṃvaratvasyodbhāvanārtham | yo dharmaṃ śaraṇaṃ gacchati asau nirvāṇaṃ śaraṇaṃ gacchati pratisaṃkhyā(23)nirodham | svaparasaṃtānakleśānāṃ duḥkhasya ca śāntyekalakṣaṇatvāt | yady aśaikṣā dharmā eva buddhaḥ (24) kathaṃ tathāgatasyāntike duṣṭacittarudhirotpādanād ānantaryaṃ bhavati | āśrayavipādanāt te 'pi vipāditā [p.217] bhavantīti vaibhāṣikāḥ | śāstraṃ tu naivaṃvācakam' aśaikṣā dharmā eva buddha iti | kiṃ tarhi | (2) buddhakarakā iti | ata āśrayasya buddhatvāpratiṣedhād acodyam evaitat | anyathā hi laukikacittastho (3) na buddhaḥ syān na saṃghaḥ śīlam eva ca bhikṣukarakaṃ bhikṣuḥ syāt | yathā tu yo bhikṣūn pūjayati (4) bhikṣukarakam asau śīlaṃ pūjayati | evaṃ yo buddhaṃ śaraṇaṃ gacchaty aśaikṣān asau buddha karakān dharmān (5) śaraṇaṃ gacchati | yo buddhaṃ śaraṇaṃ gacchati, so 'ṣṭādaśāveṇikān bu(6)ddhadharmān' ity apare | kiṃsvabhāvāni śaraṇagamanāni | vāgvijñaptisvabhāvāni | kaḥ punaḥ (7) śaraṇārthaḥ | trāṇārthaḥ śaraṇārthaḥ | tadāśrayeṇa sarvaduḥkhātyantanirmokṣāt | uktaṃ hi (8) bhagavatā (9) 
		bahavaḥ śaraṇaṃ yānti, parvatāṃś ca vanāni ca (10) 
		ārāmān vṛkṣāṃś caityāṃś ca, manuṣyā bhayatarjitāḥ' || (11) 
		na tv etac charaṇaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ, naitac charaṇam uttamam | (12) 
		naitac charaṇam āgamya, sarvaduḥkhāt pramucyate || (13) 
		yas tu buddhaṃ ca dharmaṃ ca, saṃghaṃ ca śaraṇaṃ gataḥ | (14) 
		catvāri cāryasatyāni, paśyati prajñayā yadā || (15) 
		duḥkhaṃ duḥkhasamutpādaṃ', duḥkhasya samatikramam | (16) 
		āryaṃ cāṣṭāṅgikaṃ mārgaṃ, kṣemaṃ nirvāṇagāminam || (17) 
		etad dhi śaraṇaṃ śreṣṭham etac charaṇam uttamam | (18) 
		etac charaṇam āgamya, sarvaduḥkhāt pramucyate || iti | (19) 
ata eva śaraṇagamanāni sarvasaṃvarasamādāneṣu dvārabhūtāni | (20) 
	kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇam anyeṣu saṃvareṣv abrahmacaryād viratiḥ śikṣāpadaṃ vyavasthāpitam, upāsakasya tu (21) kāmamithyācārāt | (22) 
		mithyācārātigarhyatvāt saukaryād akriyāptitaḥ | (33ab) (23) 
kāmamithyācāro hi loke 'tyantaṃ garhitaḥ | pareṣāṃ dāropaghātād āpayikatvāc ca | na tathābrahmacaryam | (24) sukarā ca kāmamithyācārād viratir gṛhān adhyāvasatāṃ, duṣkarā tv abrahmacaryād iti duṣkaraṃ darttuṃ (25) notsaheran | āryāś cākaraṇasaṃvaraṃ kāmamithyācārāt pratilabhante | (26) janmāntareṣv apy anadhyācaraṇān na tv abrahmacaryād ity upāsakasyāpi tasmād eva' viratiḥ śikṣāpadaṃ vyvasthāpitaṃ [p.218] mā bhūt parivṛttajanmāntaraḥ śaikṣa upāsakasaṃvarāṅgeṣv asaṃvṛta iti | akriyāniyamo hy akaraṇasaṃvaraḥ | (2) ya upāsakāḥ santo bhāryāḥ pariṇayanti, kiṃ tais tābhyo 'pi saṃvaraḥ pratilabdho 'tha na | pratilabdho, (3) mā bhūt prādeśikasaṃvaralābha iti | kathaṃ saṃvarakṣobho na bhavati | yasmāt (4) 
		yathābhyupagamaṃ lābhaḥ saṃvarasya na saṃtateḥ ||33cd || (5) 
yathā hy eṣām abhyupagamas tathā saṃvaralābhaḥ | kathaṃ caiṣām abhyupagamaḥ | kāmamithyācārād viramāmīti | (6) na tv atra saṃtāne mayā brahmacaryaṃ na kartavyam ity ata evaiṣāṃ tadadhiṣṭhāt kāmamithyācārāṅgād eva saṃvaralābho (7) nābrahmacaryād iti nāsti bhāryībhūtāyāṃ saṃvarakṣobhaḥ | (8) 
	atha kasmāt mṛṣāvādād viratir evopāsakasaṃvaraśikṣāpadaṃ na paiśunyādiviratiḥ | ebhir eva (9) ca tribhiḥ kāraṇaiḥ | (10) 
	mṛṣāvādātigarhyatvāt saukaryād akriyāptitaḥ | (11) 
		mṛṣāvādaprasaṅgāc ca sarvaśikṣāvyatikrame | (34ab) (12) 
sarvatra hi śikṣātikrame samanuyujyamānasyopasthitam idaṃ bhavati, nāham' evam akā(13)rṣam iti | mṛṣāvādasya prasaṅgo bhavaty, ato mṛṣāvādād viratir vidhīyate, kathaṃ kṛtātikramo 'py ātmānam' (14) āviṣkuryād iti | 
	kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇaṃ pratikṣepaṇasāvadyāc chikṣāpadam asya' na vyavasthāpitam | vyavasthāpitam' (15) 
		pratikṣepaṇasāvadyān madhyād eva (34cd) (16) 
kiṃ kāraṇaṃ madyād eva nānyasmāt | (17) 
		anyaguptaye ||34d' || (18) 
madyaṃ pibato 'nyāny aṅgāny aguptāni syuḥ | kathaṃ punar madyapānaṃ pratikṣepaṇasāvadyaṃ gamyate | (19) prakṛtisāvadyalakṣaṇābhāvāt | prakṛtisāvadyaṃ hi kliṣṭenaiva cittenādhyācaryate | śakyaṃ tu madyaṃ pratīkāra(20)buddhyaiva pātuṃ yāvan na madayet | kliṣṭam eva tac cittaṃ yan madanīyaṃ jñātvā pibati | na tat kliṣṭaṃ (21) yad amadanīyamātrāṃ viditvā pibati | prakṛtisāvadyaṃ madyam iti vinayadharāḥ | kathaṃ bhadanta glāna (22) upasthātavyaḥ | prakṛtisāvadyam upālin sthāpayitvā ity uktaṃ bhagavatā | śākyeṣu ca (23) glāneṣu madyapānaṃ nābhyanujñātam | idaṃ coktaṃ mā bhikṣavaḥ śāstāram uddiśadbhiḥ kuśāgreṇāpi (24) madyaṃ na pātavyam ity ataḥ prakṛtisāvadyam iti jñāyate | āryaiś ca janmāntaragatair apy anadhyā(25)cārāt | prāṇivadhādivat | kāyaduścaritavacanād durgatigamanāc ceti | nety ābhidhārmikāḥ | [p.219] utsargavihatasyāpi glāneṣu prajñaptisāvadyasya punar madhyasyāpavādaḥ prasaṅgapari(2)hārārthaṃ madanīyamātraniyamanāt | ata eva kuśāgrapānapratiṣedhaḥ | āryair anadhyācaraṇaṃ (3) hrīmattvāt tena ca smṛtināśāt | alpakasyāpy apānam aniyamād viṣavat | duścaritavacanaṃ (4) pramādasthānatvāt | ata evātra pramādasthānagrahaṇaṃ nānyeṣu teṣāṃ prakṛtisāvadyatvāt | (5) atyāsevitena durgatigamanābhidhānam | tatprasaṅgenābhīkṣṇam akuśalasaṃtatipravṛtter āpāyikasya karmaṇa (6) ākṣepād vṛttilābhād vā | surāmaireyamadyapramādasthānam iti ko 'rthaḥ | surā annāsavaḥ | (7) maireyaṃ dravāsavaḥ | te ca kadācid aprāptacyutamadyabhāve bhavataḥ ity ato madyagrahaṇam | (8) pūgaphalakodravādayo 'pi madayantīti surāmaireyagrahaṇam | prajñaptisāvadyasyāpy ādareṇa praheyatve (9) kāraṇajñāpanārthaṃ pramādasthānavacanam | sarvapramādāspadatvād iti | (10) 
	ya ime trayaḥ prātimokṣadhyānānāsravasaṃvarāḥ kim eṣāṃ yata eko labhyate tataḥ śeṣau | nety āha | (11) kiṃ tarhi | (12) 
		sarvobhayebhyaḥ kāmāpto vartamānebhya āpyate | (35ab) (13) 
vartamānebhya eva skandhāyatanashātubhyaḥ kāmāpta iti prātimokṣasaṃvaraḥ | sarvebhya iti (14) maulaprayogapṛṣṭhebhyaḥ | ubhayebhya iti sattvāsattvākhyebhyaḥ prakṛtiprati(15) kṣepaṇasāvadyebhyaś ca vartamānebhya eva skandhāyatanadhātubhyo labhyate, sattvādhiṣṭhānapravṛttatvāt (16) nātītānāgatebhyaḥ, teṣām asattvasaṃkhyātatvāt | (17) 
		maulebhyaḥ sarvakālebhyo dhyānānāsravasaṃvarau ||35cd || (18) 
maulebhya eva karmapathebhyo dhyānānāsravasaṃvarau labhyete, na prayogapṛṣṭhebhyaḥ, kuta eva prajñaptisāvadyebhyaḥ | (19) sarvakālebhyaś ca skandhāyatanadhātubhyo labhyete, atītānāgatebhyo 'pi | ata eva catuṣkoṭikaṃ (20) kriyate | santi tāni skandhadhātvāyatanāni yebhyaḥ prātimokṣasaṃvaraḥ eva labhyate na (21) dhyānānāsravasaṃvarāv iti vistaraḥ | prathamā koṭiḥ, pratyutpannebhyaḥ sāmantakapṛṣṭhebhyaḥ pratikṣepaṇasāvadyāc ca | (22) dvitīyātītānāgatebhyo maulebhyaḥ karmapathebhyaḥ | tṛtīyā pratyutpannebhyo maulebhyaḥ karmapathebhyaḥ | [p.220] caturthy atītānāgatebhyaḥ sāmantakapṛṣṭhebhyaḥ iti | na tu saṃvarakāle vartamānāḥ karmapathāḥ santīti (2) vartamānādhiṣṭhānebhyaḥ iti vaktavyam | anāgatānām eva saṃvaraṇaṃ yujyate, nātītavatamānānām | (3) 
	atha kiṃ saṃvarāsaṃvarau sarvasattvebhya eva labhyete, sarvāṅgebhyaḥ sarvakāraṇaiś ca, āhosvid asti (4) bhedaḥ | niyataṃ tāvat labhyate (5) 
		saṃvaraḥ sarvasattvebhyo, vibhāṣā tv aṅgakāraṇaiḥ | (36ab) (6) 
sarvasattvbebhya eva saṃvaro labhyate, kebhyaścit aṅgebhyas tu vibhāṣā | kaścit sarvebhyo labhyate | (7) bhikṣusaṃvaraḥ | kaścic caturbhyaḥ | tato 'nyaḥ | karmapathā hi saṃvarasyāṅgāni | kāraṇair api kenacit paryāyeṇa (8) sarvaiḥ kenacid ekena | kena tāvat sarvaiḥ | yady alobhādveṣāmohāḥ kāraṇānīṣyante | kenaikena | yadi (9) mṛdumadhyādhimātrāṇi cittāni kāraṇānīṣyante | paścimaṃ paryāyaṃ niyamayyocyate, asti saṃvarasthāyī (10) sarvasattveṣu saṃvṛto na sarvāṅgaiḥ, na sarvakāraṇaiḥ | yo mṛdunā cittena madhyenādhimātreṇa vā upāsakopavāsa (11) śrāmaṇerasaṃvaraṃ samādatte | asti sarvasattveṣu saṃvṛtaḥ sarvāṅgaiś ca, na tu sarvakāraṇaiḥ | yo mṛdunā (12) cittena madhyenādhimātreṇa vā bhikṣusaṃvaraṃ samādatte | asti sarvasattveṣu sarvāṅgaiḥ sarvakāraṇaiś ca | (13) yas trividhena cittena trīn saṃvarān samādatte | asti sarvsattveṣu sarvakāraṇaiś ca na tu sarvāṅgaiḥ | (14) ya upāsakopavāsaśrāmaṇerasaṃvarān mṛdumadhyādhimātraiḥ samādatte | yas tu na sarvasattveṣu syād īdṛśo (15) nāsti | yasmāt sarvasattvānugate kalyāṇaśate sthitaḥ saṃvaraṃ pratilabhate, nānyathā pāpāśayastyānuparata(16)tvāt | pañca niyamān kurvan prātimokṣasaṃvaraṃ pratilabhate | (17) sattvāṅgadeśakālasamayaniyamāt | amusṃāt sattvād viramāmīti sattvaniyamaḥ | amuṣmād aṅgād ity aṅga(18)niyamaḥ | amuṣmin deśa iti deśaniyamaḥ | māsād yāvad iti kālaniyamaḥ | anyatra (19) yuddhād iti samayaniyamaḥ | sucaritamātraṃ tu syāt tathā gṛhṇataḥ | katham aśakyebhyaḥ (20) saṃvaralābhaḥ | sarvasattvajīvitānupaghātādhyāśayenābhyupagamāt | yadi punaḥ śakyebhya eva (21) saṃvaro labhyate cayāpacayayuktaḥ syāt | śakyāśakyānām itaretarasaṃcārāt | evaṃ ca sati (22) vināpi lābhatyāgakāraṇābhyāṃ saṃvarasya lābhatyāgau syātām iti vaibhāṣiāḥ | naivaṃ bhaviṣyati | (23) yathā hy apūrvatṛṇādyutpattau śeṣe vā saṃvarasya vṛddhhrāsau na bhavatas, tathā śakyāśakyasaṃcāre 'pi na [p.221] syātām | na sattvānāṃ pūrvaṃ paścāc ca bhāvāt tṛṇādīnāṃ tv abhāvāt | ko nv atra viśeṣo na vā (2) bhaved asatsu tṛṇādiṣu saṃvaras tadvad aśakyo vā bhavet | yadā ca parinirvṛttā na bhavanty eva tadā (3) kathaṃ saṃvarahrāso na syād iti naiṣa yuktaḥ parihāraḥ | tasmāt pūrvaka eva parihāraḥ sādhuḥ | (4) evaṃ tarhi pūrvabuddhaparinirvṛtebhyaḥ uttareṣāṃ buddhānāṃ prātimokṣasaṃvarasyālābhāt kathaṃ śīlanyūnatā na (5) prasajyeta | sarveṣāṃ sattvebhyo lābhāt | yadi hi te 'py abhaviṣyaṃs (6) tebhyo 'pi te 'lapsyanta | uktaṃ yataḥ saṃvaro labhyate | (7) 
		asāmvaras tu sarvebhyāḥ sarvāṅgebhyo na kāraṇaiḥ ||36cd || (8) 
asaṃvaras tu sarvasattvebhyo labhyate sarvakarmapathebhyaś ca | nāsti hi vikalenāsaṃvareṇāsaṃvarikaḥ | (9) na tu sarvakāraṇair yugapat mṛdvādicittāsaṃbhavāt | yo mṛdunā cittenāsaṃvaraṃ pratilabhate, (10) so 'dhimātreṇāpi cittena prāṇinaṃ jīvitād vyaparopayan mṛdunaivāsaṃvareṇa samanvāgato (11) bhavaty adhimātrayā tu prāṇātipātavijñaptyā | evaṃ madhyādhimātreṇa yojyam | tatreme āsaṃvarikās (12) tadyathā aurabhiriāḥ kaukkuṭikāḥ saukarikāḥ śākunikā mātsikā mṛgalubdhakāś (13) caurā vadhyaghātakā bandhanapālakā nāgabandhakāḥ śvapākā vāgurikāś ca | rājāno daṇḍanetāro (14) vyāvahārikāś cārthata āsaṃvarikāḥ | asaṃvare bhavatvāt tatrasthatayā asaṃvara eṣām astīti (15) āsaṃvarikā vā | urabhrān ghnantīti aurabhirikāḥ | evam anye 'pi yojyāḥ | yuktas tāvat (16) saṃvarasya sarvasattvebhyo lābhaḥ | sarvasattvahitādhyāśayena grahaṇāt | aurabhirikādīnāṃ (17) tu mātāpitṛputradārādisv avipannāśayānāṃ jīvitahetor apy ahantukāmānāṃ katham asaṃvaraḥ sarva(18)sattvebhyo yujyate | mātrādīn api hi ta urabhirībhūtān hanyuḥ | na hi (19) tāvat te ta eva iti vidvāṃso hanyuḥ | āryībhūtānāṃ ca punaḥ paśu bhavituṃ nāsty avakāśa iti tebhyaḥ kathaṃ syāt | yadi cānāgatātmabhāvāpekṣayā varttamānād asaṃvṛtaḥ syād urabhrādīn api (21) te putrībhūtāt sarvathā na hanyur iti na syāt tebhyo 'saṃvaraḥ | kathaṃ hi nāma jighāṃsatām eva tebhyo [p.222] na syād asaṃvaraḥ | etanmātrādiṣu samānam | kathaṃ hi nāmājighāṃsatām eva tebhyaḥ syād asaṃvaraḥ (2) iti | yaś corabhriko janmanāpy ādatte svadāraparituṣṭo mūkaś ca | katham asya pūrvāṅgebhyo 'saṃvaraḥ (3) syāt | āśayasyāvipannatvāt | mūko 'pi ca vākprāpaṇīyam arthaṃ kāyena prāpayituṃ śakta iti | (4) yas tarhi dve trīṇi vā śikṣāpadāni samādatte | sarvathā nāsti vikalaḥ prādeśikaś cāsaṃvarika (5) iti vaibhāṣikāḥ | yathābhyupagamaṃ viale 'pi syāt prādeśiko 'py asaṃvaraḥ saṃvaraś cānyatrāṣṭa(6)vidhād iti sautrāntikāḥ | tanmātraśīladauḥśīlyapratibandhāt | (7) 
	uktam idam asaṃvarasya yebhyo lābhaḥ | kathaṃ tu lābha iti noktam | tata idam ucyate (8) 
		śeṣāvijñaptilābhas tu kṣetrādānādarehaṇāt ||37cd || (9) 
kṣetraṃ vā tadrūpaṃ bhavati, yatrārāmādipradānamātreṇāvijñaptir utpadyate | yathaupadhikeṣu puṇyakriyāvastuṣu | (13) atha vā samādānam ādatte buddham avanditvā na bhokṣye tithimāsārdhamāsabhaktāni vā nityaṃ (14) kariṣyāmīty ādi | ādareṇa tadrūpeṇa kriyām īhate kuśalām akuśalāṃ vā yato 'syāvijñaptir utpadyate | (15) uktam etad yathā saṃvarāsaṃvaretarāṇāṃ pratilambhaḥ | (16) 
	tyāga idānīṃ vaktavyaḥ | tatra tāvat (17) 
		prātimokṣadamatyāgaḥ śikṣānikṣepaṇāc cyuteḥ | (18) 
		ubhayavyañjanotpatter mūlacchedān niśātyayāt ||38|| (19) 
dāmyanty aneneti damaḥ saṃvaro 'bhipretas tenendriyadamanāt | caturbhiḥ kāraṇaiḥ prātimokṣasaṃvarasya (20) tyāgaḥ | sthāpayitvopavāsam | śikṣāpadānāṃ vijñapuruṣasyāntike pratyākhyānād āśayataḥ (21) nikāyasabhāgatyāgāt yugapad ubhayavyañjanaprādurbhāvāt kuślamūlasamucchedāc ca | upavāsasaṃvarasya (22) tv ebhiś caturbhī rātrikṣayāc ca' | tāny etāny abhisamasya pañca tyāgakāraṇāni bhavanti | kiṃ punaḥ (23) kāraṇam ebhiḥ kāraṇais tyāgo bhavati | samādānaviruddhavijñaptyutpādādāśraya(24)tyāgād āśrayavikopanān nidānacchedāt tāvad evākṣepāc ca | [p.223] 
		patanīyena cety eke (39a) (2) 
anye punar āhuś caturṇāṃ patanīyānām anyatamena bhikṣuśrāmaṇerasaṃvaratyāga iti | (3) 
		saddharmāntardhito 'pare | (39b) (4) 
saddharmasyāntardhānād ity apare | yasmād antarhite saddharme sarvaśikṣāsīmākarmāntāḥ pratiprasrabhyanta (5) iti | (6) 
		dhanarṇavat tu kāśmīrair āpannasyeṣyate dvayam ||39cd || (7) 
kāśmīrās tu khalu vaibhāṣikāḥ evam icchanti | na maulīyam apy' āpattim āpannasyāsti bhikṣusaṃvara(8)tyāgaḥ | kiṃ kāraṇam | na hy ekadeśakṣobhāt kṛtsnasaṃvaratyāgo yukta iti | naiva (9) cānyām apy āpattim āpannasyāsti śīlacchedaḥ | kiṃ tarhi | dvayam asya bhavati śīlaṃ dauḥśīlyaṃ (10) ca | yathā kasyacid dhanaṃ syād ṛṇaṃ ca | āviṣkṛtāyāṃ tu tasyām āpattau śīlavān bhavati, na duḥśīlo yathā ṛṇaṃ śodhayitvā dhanavān bhavati na tv ṛṇavān iti | yat tarhi bhagavatoktam (12) bhikṣur bhavaty aśramaṇo 'śākyaputrīyo dhvasyate bhikṣubhāvāt | hatam asya' bhavati śāmaṇyaṃ dhvastaṃ patitaṃ (13) parājitam iti | paramārthabhikṣutvaṃ saṃdhāyaitad uktam | idam abhisāhasaṃvartate | kim atrābhisāhasam | (14) yat bhagavatā nītārthaṃ punar anyathā nīyate | dauḥśīlyāya ca bahukleśebhyaḥ pratyayā dīyante | (15) katham etan nītārtham | eṣa hi vinaye nirdeśaḥ | caturvidho (16) bhikṣuḥ | saṃjñābhikṣuḥ pratijñābhikṣur bhikṣata iti bhṣur bhinnakleśatvāt bhikṣuḥ | asmiṃs tv arthe (17) jñapticaturthakarmopasaṃpanno bhikṣur iti | na cāsau pūrvaṃ paramārthabhikṣur āsidyataḥ paścād a(18)bhikṣur bhavet | yac coktam ekadeśakṣobād iti atra śāstraiva datto 'nuyogas tadyathā tālo (19) mastakāc chinno 'bhavyo 'ṅkuritatvāya abhavyo virūḍhiṃ vṛddhiṃ vipulatām āptum ity upamāṃ kurvatā | (20) kaḥ punar upamārthaḥ |evam ekadeśasyāpi mūlabhūtasya cchedād abhavyaḥ saṃvaraśeṣo viroḍhum iti | (21) sa ca gurvīṃ bhikṣubhāvamaryādābhedinīṃ maulīm āpattim āpadyamānas tīvrānapatrāpyayogāt saṃvarasya mūlaṃ (22) cchinattīti yuktaḥ kṛtsnasaṃvaratyāgaḥ | yasya caikagrāsaparibhogo 'py ekapādapārṣṇipradeśaparibhogo [p.224] 'pi ca nābhyanujñāyate, sāṃghikayor āhāravihārayoḥ sarvabhikṣusaṃbhogabahiṣkṛtaś ca śāstrā, yaṃ (2) cādhikṛtyoktaṃ (3) 
		''nāśayata kāraṇḍavakaṃ kaśambakam apakarṣata | (4) 
		athotplāvinaṃ vāhayata abhikṣuṃ bhikṣuvādinam'' iti (5) 
tasya kīdṛśo bhikṣubhāvaḥ | yādṛśas tādṛśo 'stu | asti tu bhikṣubhāvaḥ | tathā hi (6) 
		catvārtaḥ śramaṇā na pañcamo 'sti cundeti (7) 
bhagavān avocat (8) 
	mārgajino mārgadeśiko mārge jīvati yaś ca mārgadūṣī (9) 
asty etad uktam | satveṣa' ākṛtimātrāvaśeṣatvāc chramaṇa ukto dagdhakāṣṭhaśuṣkahradaśukanāśā(10)pūtibījālātacakramṛtasattvavat | yadi hi dauḥśīlyād abhikṣuḥ syāt śikṣādattako na (11) syāt | na vayaṃ brūmaḥ sahādhyāpattyā sarvaḥ pārājika iti | yas tu pārājikaḥ so 'vaśyam abhikṣuḥ | (12) kaścit tu saṃtānaviśeṣān na pārājika ekacittenāpy apraticchādanād iti vyavasthāpitaṃ dharmasvāminā | (13) yadi tarhi pārājiko na bhikṣuḥ kiṃ punar na pravrājyate | tīvrānapatrāpyavipāditatvāt sasṃtateḥ (14) saṃvarābhavyatvān na tu khalu bhikṣubhāvāpekṣayā | tathā hy asau nikṣiptaśikṣo 'pi na pravrājyate | (15) kaś cāyam anarthe nirbandho yady asau tathābhūto 'pi bhikṣur namo 'stu tasmai tādṛśāya bhikṣutvāya | (16) saddharmāntardhāne tu vinayakarmābhāvād apūrvasaṃvaralābho nāsti | labdhasya tu nāsti tyāgaḥ | (17) 
	atha dhyānānāsravasaṃvarayoḥ kathaṃ tyāgaḥ | (18) 
		bhūmisaṃcārahānibhyāṃ dhyānāptaṃ tyajyate śubham | (40ab) (19) 
sarvam eva dhyānāptaṃ kuśalaṃ dvābhyāṃ kāraṇābhyāṃ parityajyate | upapattito vā bhūmisaṃcārād ūrdhvaṃ (20) cāvaśyaṃ' parihāṇito vā samāpatter nikāyasabhāgatvāc ca kiṃcit | yathā ca rūpāptaṃ kuśalaṃ (21) bhūmisaṃcārahānibhyāṃ tyajyate (22) 
		tathārūpyāptam āryaṃ tu phalāptyuttaptihānibhiḥ ||40cd || (23) 
anāsravaṃ tu kuśalaṃ tribhiḥ kāraṇaiḥ parityakṣyate | phalaprāptitaḥ pūrvako mārgaḥ parityakṣyate | [p.225] 
indriyottāpanena mṛdvindriyamārgaḥ | parihāṇita uttaro mārgaḥ | phalaṃ phalaviśiṣṭo vā | (2) evaṃ tāvat saṃvarās tyajyante | (3) 
		asaṃvaraḥ saṃvarāptimṛtyudvivyañjanodayaiḥ | (41ab) (4) 
tribhiḥ kāraṇair asaṃvaracchedaḥ | saṃvaraprāptitaḥ | yadi saṃvaraṃ samādatte dhyānasaṃvaraṃ vā labhate | (5) hetupratyayabalena samādhilābhāt | tenāsaṃvaraś chidyate | pratidvandvabalīyastvāt | maraṇenā(6)śrayatyāgāt | dvivyañjanotpādanenāśrayavikopanād iti | śastrajālatyāge 'py akaraṇāśayataḥ | (7) saṃvaram antareṇāsaṃvaracchedo nāsti | nidānaparivarjane 'py auṣadham antareṇa pravṛddharogāvinivṛttivat | (8) 
	ya āsaṃvarika upavāsaṃ gṛhṇāti kim asau tasmāt saṃvarāt punar asaṃvaraṃ gacchaty āhosvin naivasaṃvaraṃ (9) nāsaṃvaram | asaṃvaram ity eke | tyāgāśayasyānātyantikatvāt | pradīpta ivāyaḥpiṇḍaḥ punaḥ (10) śyāmatāṃ nāprayujyamāno gacchantīty apare | tallābhasya vijñaptyadhīnatvāt | (11) 
	atha saṃvarāsaṃvaraninirmuktā katham avijñaptis tyajyate | (12) 
		vegādānakriyārthāyurmūlacchedais tu madhyamā ||41cd || (13) 
yena hy asau prasādakleśavegenāvijñaptir ākṣiptā bhavati, tasya cchedāt sāpi (14) cchidyate | kumbhakāracakreṣugativat | ādānatyāgād api cchidyate | yadi samādānaṃ (15) tyajaty alaṃ samādāneneti | kriyāviddhedād api vicchidyate yathāsamāttam akurvataḥ | (16) arthavicchedād api vicchidyate | katamasyārthasya | caityārāmavihāraśayanāsanayantrajālādino (17) vastunaḥ | āyuṣo 'pi kuśalamūlānām api cchedād vicchidyate | yadā kuśalamūlāni samucchetum ārabhata (18) ity ebhiḥ ṣaḍbhiḥ kāraṇair avijñaptir madhyamā tyajyate | (19) 
		kāmāptaṃ kuśalārūpaṃ mūlacchedordhvajanmataḥ | (42ab) (20) 
kāmāvacaraṃ kuśalam arūpasvabhāvaṃ dvābhyāṃ kāraṇābhyāṃ parityajyate | kuśalamūlasamucchedād rūpārūpyadhātū(21)papattito vā | (22) 
		pratipakṣodayāt kliṣṭam arūpaṃ tu vihīyate ||42cd || [p.226] 
kliṣṭaṃ tv arūpasvabhāvaṃ sarvam eva pratipakṣodayād vihīyate | yasyopakleśaprakārasya yaḥ prahāṇamārga(2)s tenāsau saparivāraḥ parityajyate nānyathā | (3) 
	atha keṣāṃ sattvānām asaṃvaro bhavati, keṣāṃ saṃvaraḥ | (4) 
		nṛṇām asaṃvaro hitvā śaṇḍhapaṇḍadvidhākṛtīn | (5) 
		kurūṃś ca (43abc) (6) 
manuṣyagatāv evāsaṃvaro nānyatra | tatrāpi śaṇḍhapaṇḍakobhayavyañjanān uttarakau(7)ravāṃś ca hitvā | (8) 
		saṃvaro 'py evaṃ devānāṃ ca (43cd) (9) 
saṃvaro hi manuṣyānām eva yathoktaṃ hitvā devānāṃ ceti gatidvaye saṃvaraḥ | śaṇḍhādīnāṃ saṃvaro (10) nāstīti kathaṃ gamyate | sūtrād vinayāc ca | sūtra uktaṃ yataś ca mahānāman' gṛhī avadātavasanaḥ (11) puruṣaḥ puruṣendriyeṇa samanvāgato buddhaṃ śaraṇaṃ gacchati yāvad vācaṃ bhāṣate upāsakaṃ māṃ dhāraya (12) iyatā copāsako bhavatīti | vinaye 'pi tadrūpo nāśayitavya uktaḥ | kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇam eṣāñm (13) saṃvaro nāsti | ubhayāśrayakleśādhimātratayā pratisaṃkhyānākṣamatvāt tīvrahrīvyapatrāpyābhāvāc ca | (14) asaṃvaras tarhi kasmān nāsti | pāpe 'py asthirāśayatvāt | yatraiva ca saṃvaras tatrāsaṃvaro 'pi (15) pratidvaṃdvabhāvāt | uttarakauravāṇāṃ samādānasamādhyabhāvāt pāapakriyāśayābhāvāc ca saṃvārāsaṃvarā(16)bhāvaḥ | āpāyikānām api tīvraṃ hrīvyapatrāpyaṃ nāsti tadyogād yadvipādanāc ca saṃvarāsaṃvarau (17) syātām | api khalv āśraya eva, sa teṣāṃ tādṛśa ūṣarakṣetrabhūtaḥ śaṇḍhapaṇḍakobhayavyañja(18)nottarakauravāpāyikānāṃ yatrāśraye saṃvaro 'pi na virohaty asaṃvaro 'py ūṣara iva kṣetre sasyam apy atimātraṃ (19) kakvam apīti | yat tarhi sūtra uktam: aṇḍajo bhikṣavo nāgo 'ṣṭamyāṃ pakṣasya bhāvanād abhyudgamyā(20)ṣṭāṅgasamanvāgatam upavāsam upavasatīti | sucaritamātraṃ tat teṣāṃ, na saṃvaraḥ | (21) tasmād eva manuṣyāṇām eva saṃvaraḥ | tatra punaḥ (22) 
		nṛṇāṃ trayaḥ ||43d || (23) 
manuṣyāṇāṃ sarve trayaḥ prātimokṣādisaṃvarāḥ saṃvidyante | (24) 
		kāmarūpajadevānāṃ dhyānajaḥ (44ab) (25) 
dhyānasaṃvaraḥ kāmarūpadhātūpapannānāṃ devānām ūrdhvaṃ nāsti | (26) 
		anāsravaḥ punaḥ (44b') [p.227] 
		dhyānānantarāsaṃjñisattvavarjyānām apy arūpiṇām ||44cd || (2) 
anāsravasaṃvaras tu kāmarūpadhātūpapannānām apy asti dhyānāntarikāsaṃjñisattvopapannān varjayitvā | (3) ārūpyopapannānām api teṣāṃ tu samanvāgamato 'sti na saṃmukhībhāvataḥ | (4) 
	ataḥ param idānīṃ karmanirdeśādhikārāt sūtrodiṣṭānāṃ karmaṇāṃ nirdeśa ārapsyate | trīṇi (5) karmāṇi | kuśalaṃ karmākuśalam avyākṛtaṃ karmeti | tatra (6) 
		kṣemākṣemetarat karma kuśalākuśaletarat | (45ab) (7) 
idaṃ kuśalādīnāṃ lakṣaṇam | kṣemaṃ karma kuśalaṃ yad iṣṭavipākaṃ nirvāpnaprāpakaṃ ca duḥkhaparitrāṇāt | (8) tatkālam atyantaṃ ca akṣemam akuśalaṃ kṣemapratidvaṃdvabhāvena yasyyaniṣṭo vipākaḥ | tābhyām itarat karma (9) naiva kṣemaṃ nākṣemaṃ yat tat kuśalākuśalābhyām itarat veditavyam | avyākṛtam ity arthaḥ | punaḥ (10) 
		puṇyāpuṇyam aniñjaṃ ca sukhavedyādi ca trayam ||45cd || (11) 
trīṇi karmāṇi puṇyam apuṇyam āneñjaṃ ca | punaḥ trīṇi | sukhavedanīyaṃ karma duḥkhavedāniyam (12) aduḥkhāsukhavedanīyaṃ ca | tatra tāvat (13) 
		kāmadhātau śubhaṃ karma puṇyam āneñjam ūrdhvajam | (14) 
śubham iti vartate | rūpārūpyāvacaraṃ kuśalaṃ karmāneñjam | nanu ca trīṇi dhyānāni señjitāny uktāni (15) bhagavatā | yad atra vitarkitaṃ vicāritam idam atrāryā iñjitam ity āhur ity evamādi | samādhau (16) sāpakṣālatāṃ teṣām adhikṛtyaivam uktam | āniñjāny api tu tāny uktāny āniñjasūtre | (17) āniñjasṃpreyagāminīṃ pratipadam ārabhya | kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇaṃ señjitam evānyatrāniñjam uktam | (18) 
		tadbhūmiṣu yataḥ karma vipākaṃ prati neñjati ||46cd || (19) 
kāmāvacaraṃ hi karma vipākaṃ prati kampate | kathaṃ kampate | avyavasthānāt | anyagatikamk api (20) hy anyasyāṃ gatau vipacyate | anyadevanaikāyikaṃ cānyadevanikāye | yad eva hi pramāṇabala(21)varṇakarmāny abhūmikasukhabhogādisaṃvartanīyaṃ' karma deveṣu vipacyate, tad eva kadācid anyapratyaya(22)vaśān manuṣyatiryakpreteṣu vipacyate | rūpārūpyāvacaraṃ tu karmāny abhūmikam anyasyāṃ bhūmau vipaktuṃ [p.228] na jātūtsahate | tasmād vyavasthitavipākatvād āneñjam ity ucyate | apuṇyaṃ tu karmākuśalam iti (2) prasiddhaṃ loke | yaś ca lokato 'rthaḥ prasiddhaḥ kiṃ tatra yatnena | kṛtaḥ (3) puṇyādīnāṃ karmaṇāṃ nirdeśaḥ | sukhavedanīyādīnāṃ kartavyaḥ | sa eṣa kriyate | (4) 
		sukhavedyaṃ śubhaṃ dhyānād ātṛtīyāt (47ab) (5) 
kuśalaṃ karma sukhavedanīyaṃ yāvat tṛtīyād dhyānāt | eṣā hi bhūmiḥ sukhāyā vedanāyā (6) yad uta kāmadhātus trīṇi ca dhyānāni | (7) 
		ataḥ param | (8) 
		aduḥkhāsukhavedyaṃ tu (47b'c) (9) 
śubham iti vartate | tṛtīyadhyānāt pareṇa kuśalaṃ karmāduḥkhāsukhavedanīyaṃ sukhaduḥkhavedanābhāvāt | (10) 
		duḥkhavedyam ihāśubham ||47d || (11) 
akuśalaṃ karma duḥkhavedanīyam | ihagrahaṇam kāmadhātāv eva tadbhagajñāpanārtham | na caiṣāṃ vedanaiva (12) phalam | kiṃ tarhi | sasaṃbhārā | (13) 
		adho 'pi madhyam asty eke (48a) (14) 
anye punar āhuḥ | yad'(Tib.) etan madhyam aduḥkhāsukhavedanīyaṃ karmoktam, etac caturthadhyānād adho 'py asti | kiṃ (15) kāraṇam | (16) 
		dhyānāntaravipākataḥ | (48b) (17) 
itarathā hi dhyānāntarakarmaṇo vipāko na syād dhyānāntaraṃ vā kasyacit karmaṇaḥ | tatra sukhaduḥkhayor (18) abhāvāt | dhyānāntarakarmaṇo dhyāna eva sukhendriyaṃ vipāka ity eke | naiva tasya vedanā (19) vipāka ity apare | tad etad ucchāstram | śāstre hi paṭhitaṃ: syāt karmaṇaś caitasiky eva vedanā (20) vipāko vipacyeta | syāt kuśalasyāvitarkasya karmaṇa iti | (21) 
		apūrvācaramaḥ pākas trayāṇāṃ ceṣyate yataḥ ||48cd || (22) 
yataś coktaṃ śāstre': syāt trayāṇāṃ karmaṇām apūrvācaramo vipāko vipacyeta | syāt sukhavedanīyasya (23) rūpaṃ duḥkhavedanīyasya cittacaittā dharmāḥ | aduḥkhāsukhavedanīyasya cittaviprayuktā ity ato 'py asty (24) aduḥkhāsukhavedanīyaṃ karmādhastāt | na hi kāmadhātor anyatrāsti yugapat karmatrayasya vipāke [p.229] saṃyogaḥ | kim idānīṃ tat kuśalam āhosvid akśalam | durbalaṃ tu tat | evaṃ tarhi (2) sukhavedyaṃ śubhaṃ dhyānād ātṛtīyād, iṣṭavipākaṃ ca kuśalam ity asya virodhaḥ | bāhulika eṣa (3) nirdeśo draṣṭavyaḥ | kathaṃ punar avedanāsvabhāvaṃ karma dukhādivedanīyam ity ucyate | sukhavedanāyai (4) hitaṃ sukhavedanīyaṃ, sukho 'sya vedanīya iti vā | kaś ca vedanīyaḥ | yo vipākaḥ | sa hy asau (5) vidyate sukhasya vā vedanīyaṃ, yena sukhaṃ vedayate | snānīyakaṣāyavat | evaṃ duḥkhavedanīyam (6) aduḥkhāsukhavedanīyaṃ ca draṣṭavyam | api ca (7) 
		svabhāvasaṃprayogābhyām ālambanavipākataḥ | (8) 
		saṃmukhībhāvataś ceti pañcadhā vedanīyatā ||49|| (9) 
svabhāvavedanīyatā vedanānām | svabhāvenaiva vedanīyatvāt | saṃprayogavedanīyatā sparśasya | (10) sukhavedanīyaṃ sparśaḥ iti | ālambanavedanīyatā viṣayāṇām | yathoktaṃ (11) cakṣuṣā rūpāṇi dṛṣṭvā rūpapratisaṃvedī bhavati, no tu rūparāgaprati(12)saṃvedīty evamādi | vedanayā hi tām ālambamānaḥ pratisaṃvedayate | vipākavedanīyatā karmaṇaḥ | (13) dṛṣṭadharmavedanīyaṃ karmeti vistaraḥ | saṃmukhībhāvavedanīyatā | yathoktaṃ: yasmin samaye (14) sukhāvedanā vartate, tasmin punar anyā vedanāsti, sa yāṃ tāṃ vedayate | saṃmukhīkurvāṃs tu tāṃ vedayata (16) ity ucyate | ato vipākasya vedanīyatvāt karmāpy ucyate sukhavedanīyam ity evamādi | (17) 
		niyatāniyataṃ tac ca (50a) (18) 
tac caitat sukhavedanīyādi trividhaṃ karma niyataṃ cāniyataṃ ca veditavyam | nāvaśyavedanīyam aniyatam | (19) 
		niyataṃ trividhaṃ punaḥ | (20) 
		dṛṣṭadharmādivedyatvāt (50c) (21) 
dṛṣṭadharmavedanīyam upapadyavedanīyam aparaparyāyavedanīyaṃ cety etat trividhaṃ karma niyatam ity etac caturvidhaṃ karma (22) bhavati sahāniyatavedanīyena | (23) 
		pañcadhā karma kecana ||50d || (24) 
apare punaḥ pañcavidhaṃ karmecchanti | aniyatavedanīyaṃ dvidhā kṛtvā | vipākena niyatam aniyataṃ [p.230] ceti | tatra dṛṣṭadharmavedanīyaṃ yatra janmani kṛtaṃ, tatraiva vipacyate | upapadyavedanīyaṃ dvitīye (2) janmani | aparaparyāyavedanīyaṃ tasmāt pareṇa | jahmāntare 'py asti (3) dṛṣṭadharmavedanīyasya karmaṇo vipāka ārambhavaśāt tannāmavyavasthānam ity apare | mā bhūd evam iṣṭakarma (4) tasyālpiṣṭho vipāka iti | tad evaṃ necchanti vaibhāṣikāḥ | asti hi karma saṃnikṛṣṭaphalaṃ (5) na viprakṛṣṭaphalam asti viparyayād bāhyabījavat | yathā tripakṣā suvarcalā tribhiḥ pakṣaiḥ phalaṃ dadhāti (6) yavagodhūmādayaḥ ṣaḍbhir māsair iti | (7) 
		catuṣkoṭikam ity anye (51a) (8) 
dārṣṭāntikās tu catuṣkoṭikaṃ kurvanti | asti karmāvasthāniyataṃ na vipāke niyatam | (9) yat karma dṛṣṭadharmavedanīyaṃ vipāke 'niyatam | asti vipāke niyataṃ nāvasthāyām | (10) yat karmāniyatavedanīyaṃ vipāke niyatam | asty ubhayaniyataṃ yad dṛṣṭadharmādivedanīyaṃ vipāke (11) niyatam | asti nobhayaniyataṃ yat karmāniyatavedanīyaṃ vipāke aniyatam | teṣāṃ tat karmaṣṭavidhaṃ (12) dṛṣṭadharmavedanīyaṃ niyatam aniyataṃ ca | evaṃ yāvad aniyatavedanīyam | niyatam eva tu dṛṣṭadharmādi(13)vedanīyam aniyataṃ caturtham iti varṇayanti | syād ekasmin kṣaṇe caturvidhaṃ karmākṣipet | syāt triṣu (14) paraṃ prayojya kāmamithyācāre svayaṃ prayuktas teṣāṃ yugapatsamāptau | eṣāṃ ca punaś caturṇāṃ (15) karmaṇāṃ (16) 
		nikāyākṣepaṇaṃ tribhiḥ | (51b) (17) 
na hi dṛṣṭadharmavedanīyena karmaṇā nikāyasabhāga ākṣipyate | (18) 
	katamasmin dhātau katividhaṃ karmākṣipyate, kasyāṃ vā gatau | (19) 
		sarvatra caturākṣepaḥ (51c) (20) 
sarveṣu triṣu dhātuṣu sarvāsu ca gatiṣu caturṇāṃ karmaṇām ākṣepaḥ kuśalānām akuśalānāṃ ca yathāsaṃbhavam | (21) asyotsargasyāyam apavādaḥ | (22) 
		śubhasya narake tridhā ||51d || (23) 
narakeṣu kuśalasya karmaṇas trividhasyākṣepo na dṛṣṭadharmavedanīyasya | tatreṣṭavipākābhāvāt | (24) 
		yadviraktaḥ sthiro bālas tatra notpadyavedyakṛt | (52ab) (25) 
		yato bhūmeḥ vītarāgaḥ' pṛthagjano bhavaty, asau ca sthiro bhavaty aparihāṇadharmā, sa tatropapadyavedanīyaṃ karma (26) na karoti | kim anyat karoti | [p.231] 
		nānyavedyakṛd apy āryaḥ (52c) (2) 
sthira iti vartate | āryapudgalas tu yato vītarāgo na ca parihāṇadharmā sa tatropapadyavedanīyaṃ (3) cāparaparyāyavedanīyaṃ ca karma na karoti | na hy asau bhavyaḥ punar ādhastanīṃ bhūmim āyātum aniyataṃ (4) kuryād dṛṣṭadharmavedanīyaṃ cātropapannaḥ | (5) 
		kāme 'gre vāsthiro 'pi na ||52d || (6) 
parihāṇadharmāpi tv āryapudgalaḥ kāmadhātor bhavāgrād vītarāgaḥ tayor upapadyāparaparyāyavedanīyaṃ karmābhavyaḥ (7) karttum | kiṃ kāraṇam | phalād dhi sa parihīṇo bhavati | na cāsti phalaparihīṇasya (8) kālakriyeti paścāt pravedayiṣyāmaḥ | (9) 
	kim antarābhave 'py asti karmaṇa ākṣepaḥ | astīty āha | (10) 
		dvāviṃśatividhaṃ kāmeṣv ākṣipaty antarābhavaḥ | (53ab) (11) 
kāmāvacaro hy antarābhavo dvāviṃśatividhaṃ karmākṣipati | kathaṃ kṛtvā | pañca hi garbhāvasthāḥ | (12) kalalārbudapeśīghanapraśākhāvasthāḥ | pañca jātāvasthāḥ | bālakumārayuvamadhyavṛddhāvasthāḥ | (13) tatrāntarābhavaḥ kalalavedanīyaṃ karmākṣipaty aniyataṃ niyataṃ ca | evaṃ yāvad vṛddhavedanīyam antarābhava(14)vedanīyaṃ ca | (15) 
		dṛṣṭadharmaphalaṃ tac ca (53c) (16) 
tac caitad antarābhavikaṃ karma yan niyatam ekādaśavidham uktaṃ dṛṣṭadharmavedanīyaṃ tad veditavyam | kiṃ kāraṇam | (17) 
		nikāyo hy eka eva saḥ ||53d || (18) 
eka eva hy asau nikāyasabhāga ekakarmākṣipto yaś cāntarābhavo yāś ca tadanvayā daśāvasthāḥ | (19) ata evānyad antarābhavavedanīyaṃ karma noktam | upapadyavedanīyena ca' tasyākṣepāt | (20) 
	kīdṛśaṃ punaḥ karma niyataṃ veditavyam | (21) 
		tīvrakleśaprasādena sātatyena ca yat kṛtam | (22) 
		guṇakṣetre ca niyataṃ tat pitror ghātakaṃ ca yat ||54|| (23) 
yad dhi karma tīvreṇa kleśena kṛtaṃ, yac ca tīvreṇa prasādena, yac ca sātatyena guṇakṣetre ca tan niyataṃ [p.232] veditavyam | tatra guṇakṣetraṃ trīṇi ratnāni | pudgalaviśeṣo vā kaścit phalasamāpattiviśeṣaprāptaḥ (2) | tatra hy antareṇāpi tīvrakleśaprāsādaṃ' sātatyaṃ ca kuśalam akuśalaṃ (3) ca karma niyataṃ sampadyate | pitarau punar mātā ca pitā ca | tayoś cāpi yad' ghātikaṃ (4) karma yathā tathā vā tan niyataṃ saṃpadyate nānyat | (5) 
	atha dṛṣṭadharmavedanīyaṃ karma kīdṛśaṃ veditavyam | (6) 
		dṛṣṭadharmaphalaṃ karmakṣetrāśayaviśeṣataḥ | (55ab) (7) 
dṛṣṭadharmavedanīyaṃ karma kṣetraviśeṣād vā bhavati | yathā saṃghastrīvādasamudācārād vyañjanaparivṛttiḥ (8) śrūyate | āśayaviśeṣād vā | yathā śaṇḍhasya gavām apuṃstvapratimokṣaṇāt pumbhāvaḥ | atha vā (9) 
		tadbhūmyatyantavairāgyāt (55c) (10) 
tasya vā karmaṇo yā bhūmiḥ kuśalasyākuśalasya vā tato 'tyantavairāgyāt tat karma dṛṣtadharmavedanīyaṃ karma (11) saṃpadyate | tasyāṃ bhūmau jātasya kīdṛśaṃ karmety āha (12) 
		vipāke niyataṃ hi yat ||55d || (13) 
yad dhi karma niyataṃ vipāke na tv avasthāyāṃ nāpy aniyataṃ tasyaiṣa niyamaḥ | yat punar avasthāntaraniyataṃ (14) tasya tatraiva niyato vipākas tadvato 'tyantavairāgyāsaṃbhavāt | yac cāniyataṃ tasyāvipāka (15) evātyantavairāgyāt | (16) 
	kīdṛśaṃ punaḥ kṣetraṃ yatrāvaśyaṃ dṛṣṭadharmavedanīyaṃ karma saṃpadyate | āha | buddhapramukho (17) bhikṣusaṃghaḥ pudgalās tu pañca | 
		ye nirodhāraṇāmaitrīdarśanārhatphalothitāḥ | (19) 
		teṣu kārāpakārasya phalaṃ sadyo 'nubhūyate ||56|| (20) 
nirodhasamāpattivyutthitaḥ parāṃ cittaśāntiṃ labhate | nirvāṇasadṛśatvāt samāpatteḥ | sa hi (21) gatapratyāgata iva nirvāṇād bhavati | araṇāvyutthitasyāpramāṇasattvāraṇāhitādhyāśayānugatā(22)tyudagrāpramāṇapuṇyaparibhāvanānugatā ca saṃtatir vartate | maitrīvyutthitasyāpramāṇasattvasukhādhyā(23)śayānugatātyudagrāpramāṇapuṇyaparibhāvanānugatā ca saṃtatir vartate | darśanamārgavyutthitasyāśeṣadarśana(24)prahātavyaprahāṇāt pratyagrāśrayaparivṛttinirmalā saṃtatir vartate | arhatphalavyutthitasyāśeṣabhāvanā(25)prahātavyaprahāṇāt pratyagrāśrayaparivṛttiśuddhā saṃtatir vartate | ata eteṣu kṛtānāṃ kārāpakārāṇāṃ [p.233] phalaṃ dṛṣṭa eva dharme niyataṃ prāpyate | śeṣasya tu bhāvanāmārgasyāparipūrṇasvabhāvaphalatvāc ca (2) tadvyutthitānāṃ na tathā pratyagrāśryaparivṛttiśuddhāśayasaṃtatir vartata iti na te tathā puṇyakṣetraṃ (3) bhavati | (4) 
	vipākaḥ punar vedanāpradhānaḥ | tad idānīṃ vicāryate | syāt karmaṇaś caitasiky eva vedanā (5) vipāko vipacyeta, na kāyikī | syāt kāyiky eva na caitasikī, syād ity āha (6) 
		kuśalasyanitarkasya karmaṇo vedanā matā | (7) 
		vipākaś caitasiky eva (57abc) (8) 
avitarkaṃ karma dhyānāntarāt prabhṛti yāvat bhavāgre | tasyāvitarkasya kuśalasya karmaṇaś caitasiky (9) eva vedanā vipākaḥ | kasmān na kāyikī | tasyā avaśyaṃ savitarkavicāratvāt | (10) 
		kāyiky evāśubhasya tu ||57d || (11) 
akuśalasya karmaṇaḥ kāyiky eva vedanā vipākaḥ | kasmān na caitasikī | tasya hi duḥkhā (12) vedanā vipākaś caitsikī ca duḥkhā vedanā daurmanasyaṃ na ca daurmanasyaṃ viāka iti vyākhyātam etat | (13) 
	yat tarhi sattvānāṃ cittakṣepo bhavati | katamasminn asau citte bhavati kena vā kāraṇena | (14) 
		cittakṣepo manaścitte (58a) (15) 
manovijñāna ity arthaḥ | na hi pañcasu vijñānakāyeṣu cittaṃ kṣipyate | avikalpakatvāt | (16) 
		sa ca karmavipākajaḥ | (58b) (17) 
sa caiṣa cittakṣepaḥ sattvānāṃ karmajaḥ | ye hi dravyamantraprayogeṇa vā pareṣāṃ cittaṃ kṣepayanti (18) akāmakaṃ vā viṣaṃ madyaṃ vā pāpayanti, trāsayanti vā mṛgayādiṣv agniṃ vā dāveṣūtsṛjanti prapātād vā (19) pātayanty anyena vā kenacit pareṣāṃ smṛtiṃ bhraṃśayanti, teṣāṃ tasya karmaṇo vipākenāyatyāṃ cittaṃ (20) kṣipyate | (21) 
		bhayopaghātavaiṣamyaśokaiś ca (58cd) (22) 
kathaṃ tāvad bhayena | amanuṣyādayo manuṣyān virūpeṇa rūpeṇopasaṃkrāmanti | (23) tebhyo bhītānāṃ cittaṃ kṣipyate | katham upaghātena | amanuṣyā eva kupitā manuṣyāṇāṃ [p.234] marmasu praharanti | kathaṃ mahābhūtavaiṣamyeṇa | vātapittaśleṣmāṇaḥ prakopam āpadyante | kathaṃ śokena | (2) yathā vāsiṣṭhīprabhṛtīnām | yadi mano vikṣipyate karmavipākajaś ca cittakṣepaḥ kathaṃ na caitasikī (3) vedanā vipākaḥ prāpnoti | na hi brūmas tad eva cittaṃ' vipāka ity api tu yo mahābhūtānāṃ prakopaḥ (4) sa vipākaḥ | tasmāj jātam ato vipākajam | karmajena hi dhātūnāṃ' vaiṣamyeṇa vyākulam avaśaṃ (5) bhraṣṭasmṛtikaṃ icttaṃ vartata iti kṣiptam ity ucyate | evaṃ cedaṃ catuṣkoṭikaṃ yujyate | asti cittaṃ (6) kṣiptaṃ na vikṣitam iti vistaraḥ | kṣiptaṃ tāvan na kṣiptaṃ vikṣiptacittasya | klsiṣṭaṃ cittaṃ vikṣiptaṃ citt (7) na kṣiptaṃ svasthacittasya kliṣṭam | ubhayaṃ kṣiptacittasya kliṣṭam | nobhayaṃ svasthacittasyākliṣṭam | (8) 
	katameṣāṃ punaḥ sattvānāṃ cittaṃ kṣipyate | (9) 
		akurukāminām ||58d' || (10) 
akurūṇāṃ kāminām | deveṣv api hi devā unmattāḥ santi prāg eva manuṣyatiryakpreteṣu | (11) nārakās tu nityaṃ kṣiptacittā eva | te hi vividhayātanāsahasravyatibhnamarmāṇas tīivrābhir vedanābhir (12) abhitunnā ātmānam api tāvan nābhicetayanti, kiṃ punaḥ kāryam akāryaṃ (13) vā | hācittaparidevakaś cātra nāraka udāhāryaḥ | āryāṇām api cittaṃ kṣipyate bhūtavaiṣamyeṇānyatra (14) buddhāt | na karmaṇā niyatasya pūrvaṃ vipākād aniyatasyāvipākāt | na bhayena | pañcabhayasama(15)tikramāt | nopaghātena | manuṣyaprakopakāraṇasyāprāsādikasyākaraṇāt | na śokena | (16) dharmatābhijñatvāt | (17) 
	punas trayo vaṅkā uktāḥ sūtre | kāyavaṅko vāgvaṅko manovaṅkaḥ | trayo doṣāḥ | kāyadoṣo (18) vāgdoṣo manodoṣaḥ | trayaḥ kaṣāyāḥ | kāyakaṣāyo vākkaṣāyo manaḥkaṣāyaḥ iti | tatra (19) yathākramaṃ veditavyāḥ | (20) 
		vaṅkadoṣakaṣāyoktiḥ śāṭhyadveṣajarāgaje | (59ab) (21) 
śāṭhyajaṃ kāyakarma kāyanaṅka ity uktam | kuṭilānvayatvāt | evaṃ vāṅmanovaṅkau | dveṣajaṃ (22) kāyakarma kāyadoṣa ity uktam | cittapradoṣānvayatvāt | evaṃ vāṅmanodoṣau | rāgajaṃ kāyakarma (23) kāyakaṣāya ity uktam | rañjanānvayatvāt | evaṃ vāṅmanaḥkaṣāyau | (24) 
		kṛṣṇaśuklādibhedena punaḥ karma caturvidham ||59cd || [p.235] 
asti karma kṛṣṇaṃ kṛṣṇavipākam | asti karma śuklaṃ śuklavipākam | asti karma kṛṣṇaśuklaṃ (2) kṛṣṇaśuklavipākam | asti karmākṛṣṇam aśuklam avipākam | yat tat karma (3) karmakṣayāya saṃvartata iti | tatra (4) 
		aśubhaṃ rūpakāmāptaṃ śubhaṃ caiva yathākramam | (5) 
		kṛṣṇaśuklobhayaṃ karma tatkṣayāya nirāsravam ||60|| (6) 
aśubhaṃ karma ekāntena kṛṣṇaṃ kliṣṭatvāt kṛṣṇavipāakaṃ cāmanojñavipākatvāt | rūpāptaṃ (7) śubham ekāntena śuklam akuśalenāvyatibhedāt' | śuklavipākaṃ ca manojñavipākatvāt | ārūpyāptaṃ (8) kasmān nocyate | yatra kila dvividho 'sti vipāka āntarābhavika aupapattibhavikaś ca | (9) trividhasya kāyavāṅmanaskarmaṇo yatraiva saṃbhavas tatraivoktam iti | tad api tūktaṃ sūtrāntare | kāmāptaṃ (10) śubhaṃ' kṛṣṇaśuklam akuśalavyavakīrṇatvāt kṛṣṇaśuklavipākaṃ vyavakīrṇavipākatvāt | saṃtānata (11) etad vyavasthāpitaṃ, na svabhāvataḥ | na hy evaṃjātīyakam ekaṃ karmāsti vipāko vā, yat kṛṣṇaṃ ca syāt śuklaṃ (12) cānyonyavirodhāt | nanu caivam akuśalasyāpi karmaṇaḥ kuśalavyavakīrṇatvāt kṛṣṇaśuklatvaṃ prāpnoti | (13) nāvaśyam akuśalaṃ kuśalena vyavakīryate | kāmadhātau tasya balavattvāt | kuśalaṃ tu (14) vyavakīryate | durb alatvād iti | anāsravaṃ karmaiṣāṃ trayāṇāṃ karmaṇāṃ kṣayāya prahāṇāya saṃvartate | (15) tad dhy akṛṣṇam akliṣṭatvād aśuklaṃ vipākaśuklatābhāvāt | ābhiprāyiko hy eṣo 'śukla(16)śabdaḥ | uktaṃ tu bhagavatā mahatyāṃ śūnyatāyām aśaikṣān (17) dharmān ārabhya: ime te ānanda dharmā ekāntakuśalā ekāntānavadyā iti | śāstre ca: (18) śuklā dharmāḥ katame | akuśalā dharmā anivṛtāvyākṛtāś ceti | avipākaṃ (19) dhātvapatitatvāt pravṛttinirodhataḥ' | (20) 
		kiṃ punaḥ sardvam anāsravaṃ karma sarvasyāsya trividhasya karmaṇaḥ kṣayāya saṃvartate | (21) nety ucyate | kiṃ tarhi | [p.236] 
		dharmakṣāntiṣu vairāgye, cānantaryapathāṣṭake | (2) 
		yā cetanā dvādaśadhā, karma kṛṣṇakṣayāya tat ||61|| (3) 
darśanamārge catasṛṣu dharmajñānakṣāntiṣu kāmavairāgye cāṣṭāsv ānantaryamārgeṣu yā cetanā seyaṃ (4) dvādaśavidhā cetanā kṛṣṇasya karmaṇaḥ prahāṇāya karma | (5) 
		navame cetanā yā sā, kṛṣṇaśuklakṣayāya ca | (62ab) (6) 
navame kāmavairāgyānantaryamārge yā cetanā, sā kṛṣṇaśuklasya kuśalasya karmaṇaḥ kṛṣṇasya (7) cākuśalasya navamasya prakārasya prahāṇāya | (8) 
		śuklasya dhyānavairāgyeṣv antyānantaryamārgajā ||62cd || (9) 
dhyānād dhyānād vairāgyaṃ kurvato yo 'ntyo navama ānantaryamārgas tatra yā cetanā, iyaṃ caturvidhā (10) cetanā śuklasya karmaṇaḥ prahāṇāya | kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇam antyenaivānantaryamārgeṇa kuśalasya karmaṇaḥ (11) prahāṇaṃ, nānyena | na hi tasya svabhāvaprahāṇaṃ | prahīṇasyāpi saṃmukhībhāvāt | kiṃ tarhi | (12) tadālambanakleśaprahāṇāt | ato yāvad eko 'pi tadālambanakleśaprakāra (13) āste tāvad asya prahāṇaṃ nopapadyate | (14) 
		anye narakavedyānyakāmavedyaṃ dvayaṃ viduḥ | (63ab) (15) 
anye punar ācāryāḥ paśyanti narakavedanīyaṃ narakāc cānyakāmadhātuvedanīyaṃ karma yathākramaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ (16) kṛṣṇaśuklaṃ ceti | narake hy akuśalasyaiva karmaṇo vipākas, tena tadvedanīyaṃ kṛṣṇam ity ucyate | (17) ato 'nyakāmadhātau kuśalauśalasya karmaṇo vipākas tena tadvedanīyaṃ kṛṣṇaśuklam ity uktam | (18) 
		dṛggheyaṃ kṛṣṇam anye (63c) (19) 
anye punar āhur darśanaprahātavyaṃ karma kṛṣṇaṃ' kuśalenāmiśratvāt | (20) 
		anyat kṛṣṇaśuklaṃ tu kāmajam ||63c'd || (21) 
anyat tu kāmāvacaraṃ karma kṛṣṇaśuklam | kiṃ tadanyat | bhāvanāprahātavyam | tad dhi (22) kuśalaṃ cākuśalaṃ ceti | 
	sūtra uktaṃ trīṇi mauneyāni | kāyamauneyaṃ vāṅmauneyaṃ (23) manomauneyaṃ ca | tatra [p.237] 
		aśaikṣaṃ kāyavākkarma manaś caiva yathākramam | (2) 
		maunatrayam (64abc) (3) 
aśaikṣe kāyavākkarmaṇī kāyavāṅmauneye | aśaikṣaṃ tu mana eva manomauneyaṃ na manaskarmeti | (4) kiṃ kāraṇam | cittaṃ hi paramārthamuniḥ | tat kila kāyavākkarmabhyām anumīyata iti | api (5) khalu kāyavākkarmaṇī viratisvabhāvena na manaskarma | cittāvijñaptyabhāvāt | viramārthena ca (6) maunam | ato mana eva virataṃ maunam ity ucyate | kasmād aśaikṣam eva nānyat | (7) arhataḥ paramārthamunitvāt | sarvakleśajalpoparateḥ | 
	trīṇi śauceyāni (8) uktāni sūtre | kāyaśauceyaṃ vākśauceyaṃ manaḥśauceyaṃ ca | tad etat (9) 
		tridhā śaucaṃ sarvaṃ sucaritatrayam ||64c'd || (10) 
sarvaṃ kāyasucaritaṃ kāyaśaucam | sarvaṃ vāṅmanaḥsucaritaṃ vāṅmanaḥśaucam | duścaritamalāpakarṣaṇāt (11) tāvatkālam atyantaṃ vā | eṣā ca deśanā mithyāmaunaśaucādhimuktikānāṃ vivecanārtham | 
	trīṇi (12) duścaritāny uktāni | tatra (13) 
		aśubhaṃ kāyakarmādi, mataṃ duścaritatrayam | (65ab) (14) 
akuśalaṃ kāyavāṅmanaskarma yathākramaṃ kāyavāṅmanoduścaritam | (15) 
		akarmāpi tv abhidhyādi manoduścaritaṃ tridhā ||65cd || (16) 
akarmasvabhāvam apy asti trividhaṃ manoduścaritaṃ cetanārthāntarabhūtam abhidhyā vyāpādo mithyādṛṣṭiś ca | (17) abhidhyādaya eva manaskarmeti dārṣṭāntikāḥ | saṃcetanīyasūtre vacanāt | evaṃ tu sati (18) karmakleśayor aikyaṃ syāt | kiṃ syād yadi kaścit kleśo 'pi karma syāt | naitad asti | sūtraṃ (19) viruddhaṃ syāt | sūte tu cetanāyās tanmukhena pravṛttes tais tāṃ darśayatīti vaibhāṣikāḥ | aniṣṭaphala(20)tvāt kutsitaḥ kāyavāṅmanaścāro duścaritam | 
		viparyayāt sucaritam (66a) (22) 
iṣṭaṃ kuśalaṃ kāyavāṅmanaskarmānabhidhyāvyāpādasamyagdṛṣṭayaś ca | prānugrahopaghātābhisaṃdhyabhāve [p.238] kathaṃ samyagdṛṣṭimithyādṛṣṭyoḥ kuśalākuśalatvam | tanmūlatvāt | (2) 
	yāni caitāni duścaritasucaritāny uktāni (3) 
		tadaudārikasaṃgrahāt | (4) 
		daśa karmapathā uktā yathāyogaṃ śubhāśubhāḥ ||66bcd || (5) 
teṣām eva sucaritaduścaritānāṃ caudārikasaṃgraheṇa daśa karmapathāḥ sūtra uktāḥ yathāyogaṃ (6) kuśalāḥ sucaritebhyo 'kuśalā duścaritebhyaḥ | kim atra duścaritam asaṃgṛhītaṃ kiṃ vā (7) sucaritam | akuśaleṣu tāvat' karmapatheṣu kāyaduścaritasya pradeśo na saṃgṛhītaḥ prayogapṛṣṭhabhūtaḥ (8) kliṣṭaś cānyo 'pi bandhanādih | tasya nātyaudārikatvāt | yad dhi kāyaduścaritaṃ pareṣāṃ (9) jīvitabhogadārebhyaś cyāvakaṃ tat karmapatha uktaṃ tato vivecanārtham | vāgduścaritam api yad eva (10) praśādaṃ tat karmapatha uktaṃ tadartham eva | manoduścaritasya ca pradeśaś cetanā | kuśaleṣv api (11) kāyasucaritasya pradeśaḥ prayogapṛṣṭhamadyādiviratidānejyādiko vāksucaritasya priyavacanādiko (12) manaḥsucaritasya cetanā | (13) 
	eṣāṃ ca karmapathānām (14) 
		aśubhāḥ ṣaḍ avijñaptiḥ (67a) (15) 
akuśalāḥ ṣaṭ karmapathā avaśyam avijñaptisvabhāvāḥ prāṇātipātādattādānamṛṣāvādapaiśunyapāruṣya(16)saṃbhinnapralāpāḥ | pareṇa kārayato maulavijñaptyabhāvāt | (17) 
		dvidhaikaḥ (67b) (18) 
kāmamithyācāro nityaṃ vijñaptyavijñaptisvabhāvas tasya svayam eva' niṣṭhapanāt | (19) na hi taṃ pareṇa kārayatas tādṛśī prītir bhavatīti | (20) 
		te 'pi kurvataḥ | (67b') (21) 
te 'pi ṣaṭ karmapathāḥ svayaṃ kurvato dvividhā bhavanti vijñaptir avijñaptiś ca tatkālamaraṇe, paścāt(22)maraṇe tv avijñaptir eva | (23) 
	kuśalānāṃ punaḥ karmapathānāṃ (24) 
		dvividhāḥ sapta kuśalāḥ (67c) (25) 
kuśalāḥ sapta rūpiṇaḥ karmapathāḥ avaśyaṃ dviprakārā vijñaptir avijñaptiś ca | vijñaptyadhīnatvāt sa(26)mādānaśīlasya | [p.239] 
		avijñaptiḥ samādhijāḥ ||67d || (2) 
dhyānānāsravasaṃvarasaṃgṛhītāh samādhijāḥ ucyante | te hy avijñaptir eva cittamātrādhīinatvāt | (3) 
		sāmantakās tu vijñaptiḥ (68a) (4) 
karmapathasāmantakās tu vijñaptisvabhāvā avaśyam | (5) 
		avijñaptir bhaven na vā | (68b) (6) 
yadi tīvreṇa paryavasthānena prasādena vā ghanarasena prayogam ārabheta, syād avijñaptir, anyathā na (7) syāt | (8) 
		viparyayeṇa pṛṣṭhāni (68c) (9) 
sāmantakebhyo viparyayeṇa karmapathānāṃ pṛṣṭhāni veditavyāni' | tāni avaśyam avijñaptisvabhāvāni | (10) vijñaptis tu bhaven na vā | yadi karmapathaṃ kṛtvā punas tasyānudharmaṃ ceṣṭeta tasya' syāt vijñaptir, anyathā (11) na syāt | 
	atha kuto yāvad eṣāṃ prayogamaulapṛṣṭhānāṃ vyavasthānam | yadā tāvad iha kaścit (12) paśuṃ hantukāmo mañcakād uttiṣṭhati mūlyaṃ gṛhṇāti, gacchaty āmṛśati paśuṃ (13) krīṇāty ānayati puṣṇāti praveśayati hihantuṃ śastram ādatte prahāram ekaṃ dadāti dvau vā yāvan na (14) jīvitād vyaparopayati, tāvat prayogaḥ | yena tu prahāreṇa jīvitād vyaparopayati, tatra (15) yā vijñaptis tatkṣaṇikā vāvijñaptir ayaṃ maulaḥ karmapathaḥ | dvābhyāṃ hi kāraṇābhyāṃ prāṇātipātāvadyena (16) spṛśyate prayogataḥ mṛte sati phalaparipūritaś ca | tata ūrdhvam avijñaptirlkṣaṇāḥ pṛṣṭhaṃ bhavanti | (17) yāvac ca taṃ paśuṃ kuṣṇāti śodhayati vikrīṇīte pacati khādayaty anukīrtayati vā, (18) tāvad asya vijñaptikṣaṇā api pṛṣṭhaṃ bhavanti | evam anyeṣv api yathāsaṃbhavaṃ yojyam | abhidhyādīnāṃ (19) nāsti prayogo na pṛṣṭhaṃ saṃmukhībhāvamātrāt karmapathaḥ | (20) 
	idam atra vaktavyam | kiṃ tāvat maraṇabhavasthe tasmin prāṇini ye vijñaptyavijñaptī, te (21) karmapatha āhosvin mṛte | kiṃ cātaḥ | yadi tāvan maraṇabhavasthe sahamṛtasyāpi hantur ghātayitur vā [p.240] prāṇātipātāvadyena yogaḥ prāpnoti | na caiṣa siddhāntaḥ | atha mṛto yad uktaṃ yena tu prahāreṇa (2) jīvitād vyaparopayati, tatra yā vijñaptis tatkṣaṇikā vāvijñaptir ayaṃ maulaḥ karmapatha iti (3) tan na vaktavyam | yac cāpīdaṃ śāstra uktaṃ: syāt prāṇī hataḥ prāṇātipātaś cāniruddhaḥ | (4) syād yathā hi tatprāṇī jīvitād vyaparopito bhavati prayogaś cāpratiprasrabdha (5) ity atra pṛṣṭhaṃ prayogaśabvdenoktam iti vaibhāṣikīyo 'rthavirodho maulasyaiva tadānīm aniruddhatvāt | (6) yathā na doṣas tathāstu | kathaṃ ca na doṣaḥ | maula evātra prayogaśabdenokta iti | (7) vijñaptis tarhi tadā kathaṃ maulaḥ karmapatho bhavati | kathaṃ ca na bhavitavyam | asāmarthyāt | (8) avijñaptir idānīṃ kathaṃ bhavati | tasmāt prayogaphalaparipūrikāle tadubhayaṃ karmapathaḥ syāt (9) karmapatho 'py anyasya prayogaḥ pṛṣṭhaṃ ca bhavati | prāṇātipātasya daśāpi karmapathaḥ prayogaḥ | yathā (10) ca śatror vadhārthaṃ kṛtyaṃ samupasthāpayan paśunā baliṃ kuryāt parakīyaṃ kṛtvā dāreṣu cāsya (11) vipratipadyeta tair eva tadghātanārtham anṛtapiśunaparuṣasāntvaiś cāsya mitrabhedaṃ kuryād yāny asya paritrāṇāya (12) kalperan abhidhyāṃ ca tatsve' kuryāt tatraiva ca vyāpādaṃ tadvadhārthaṃ ca mithyādṛṣṭiṃ bṛṃhayed iti | (13) evam anyeṣv api yathāyogaṃ yojyam | na tv abhidhyādayaḥ prayogā yujyante | na hi cittotpādamātreṇa (14) prayukto bhavati | kriyānārambhāt | (15) 
	sūtra uktaṃ: prāṇātipāto bhikṣavas trividho lobhajo dveṣajo mohajo yāvan mithyādṛṣṭir (16) iti | tatra kīdṛśaḥ prāṇātipāto lobhaja ity evamādi vaktavyam | nātra sarveṣāṃ karma(17)pathānāṃ lobhādibhir niṣṭhā | (18) 
		prayogas tu trimūlajaḥ ||68d || (19) 
prayogas teṣām akuśalamūlatrayāj jāta ity ādisamutthānavacanād evam uktaḥ | tatra loghajaprāṇātipāto (20) yathā taccharīrāvayavārtham arthārthaṃ krīḍārthaṃ' ca prāṇina jīvitād vyaparopayati ātmasuhṛtparitrāṇāya | (21) dveṣajo yathā vairanipīḍanārtham | mohajo yathā yājinikānāṃ dharmabuddhyā rājñāṃ ca dharmapāṭhaka(22)prāmaṇyād dhiṃsatām | duṣṭānāṃ kila nigrahaṃ kurvanto rājānaḥ puṇyabhājo bhavantīti | (23) pārasīkānāṃ ca | te hy evam āhuḥ | mātāpitarau jīrṇau vā glānau vā (24) hantavyāv iti | ye cānye 'py evam āhur ahivṛścikatryambukādayo manuṣyāṇām upaghātāpakā' hantavyāḥ [p.241] mṛgapaśupakṣimahiṣādayaś copabhogārthatvād iti | yaś ca mithyādṛṣṭipravartitaḥ prāṇātipātaḥ | lobhajam ada(2)ttādānaṃ yo yenārthī sa tad dharati anyalābhasatkārayaśorthaṃ vā ātmasuhṛt'paritrāṇāya vā | dveṣajaṃ (3) yad vairanipīḍanārtham | mohajaṃ yathā rājñāṃ dharmapāṭhakarāmāṇyāt | duṣṭanigrahārthaṃ yathā brāhmaṇā (4) āhuḥ: sarvam idaṃ brahmaṇā brāhmaṇebhyo dattaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ daurbalyād vṛṣalāḥ paribhuñjate | tasmād apaharan (5) brāhmaṇaḥ svam ādatte svam eva brāhmaṇo bhuṅkte svaṃ vaste svaṃ dadāti ceti | (6) na caiṣām aparasvasamjñā bhavati | yac ca imthyādṛṣṭipravarttitam | lobhajaḥ kāmamithyācāraḥ (7) paradārādiṣu tatsaṃrāgād abrahmacaryaṃ lābhasatkārārtham ātmasuhṛtparitrāṇāya | dveṣajo vairanipīḍanārtham | (8) mohajo yathā pārasīkānāṃ mātrādigamanaṃ gosave ca yajñe | yathoktaṃ: brāhmaṇo gosaveneṣṭyā (9) saṃvatsaragovratī bhavati | upahā udakaṃ cūṣati tṛṇāni cchinatti upaiti mātaram upa svasāram upa (10) sagotrām iti | ye cāhur: udūkhalapuṇyaphalapakvānnatīrthamārgaprakhyo mātṛgrāma iti | (11) mṛṣāvādādayo lobhajā dveṣajāś ca pūrvavat | mohajo mṛṣāvādo yathāha: (12) 
		na narmayuktam anṛtaṃ hi nāsti (13) 
		na strīṣu rājan na vivāhakāle | (14) 
		prāṇātyaye sarvadhanāpahāre (15) 
		pañcānṛtāny āhur apātakānīti | (16) 
yaś ca mithyādṛṣṭipravartito mṛṣāvādaḥ | paiśunyādayas tu mithyādṛṣṭipravartitā mohajāḥ | yaś ca (17) vedādyasacchāstrapralāpaḥ | (18) 
	abhidhyādayaḥ kathaṃ lobhajāḥ | (19) 
		tadanantarasaṃbhūter abhidhyādyās trimūlajāḥ | (69ab) (20) 
	lobhād anantaram utpannā lobhajāḥ dveṣād anantaraṃ dveṣajāḥ mohād anantaraṃ mohajāḥ | uktā akuśalāḥ (21) karmapathāḥ | (22) 
	kuśalāḥ katham ity āha (23) 
		kuśalāḥ saprayogāntālobhadveṣamohajāḥ ||69cd || (24) 
saprayogapṛṣṭhāḥ kuśalāḥ karmapathā alobhadveṣamohajāḥ | kuśalacitta(25)samutthitatvāt | tasya ca avaśyam alobhādibhiḥ saṃprayogāt | tatrākuśalakarmapathaprayogād viratiḥ [p.242] kuśalakarmapathaprayogaḥ | maulād viratiḥ maulaḥ | pṛṣṭhād viratiḥ pṛṣṭhaḥ | tadyathā śrāmaṇera (2) upasaṃpadyamāno nānāvāsaṃ praviśati, bhikṣūn vandate, upādhyāyaṃ yācate, yāvad ekaṃ karmavācanaṃ kriyate (3) dvitīyaṃ ca | ayaṃ prayogaḥ | tṛtīyakarmavācane yā vijñaptis tatkṣaṇikā cāvijñaptir ayaṃ (4) maulaḥ karmapathaḥ | tata ūrdhvaṃ yāvan niśrayā ārocyante tadadhiṣṭhānaṃ ca vijñapayati avijñaptiś ca (5) yāvad anuvartate idaṃ pṛṣṭham | (6) 
	yad uktaṃ na sarveṣāṃ karmapathānāṃ lobhādibhir niṣṭhety atha kasya kena niṣṭhā | (7) 
		vadhavyāpādapāruṣyaniṣṭhā dveṣeṇa (70ab) (8) 
eṣāṃ prāṇātipātavyāpādapāruṣyāṇāṃ dveṣeṇa samāptiḥ | parityāgaparuṣacittasaṃmukhībhāvāt | (9) 
		lobhataḥ | (10) 
		prastrīgamanābhidhyādattādānasamāpanam ||70b'cd || (11) 
lobhena kāmamithyācārābhidhyāadattādānānāṃ samāptiḥ | (12) 
		mithyādṛṣṭes tu mohena (71a) (13) 
adhimātrabhūto hi tāṃ niṣṭhāpayati | (14) 
		śeṣāṇāṃ tribhir iṣyate | (71b) (15) 
ke punaḥ śeṣāḥ | mṛṣāvādapaiśunyasaṃbhinnapralāpāḥ | eṣāṃ tribhir api niṣṭhā lobhena dveṣeṇa (16) mohena vā (17) 
	ya ete karmapathāś caturbhiḥ kāṇḍair uktās trayas traya ekas trayaś ca eṣāṃ yathākramaṃ (18) 
		sattvabhogāv adhiṣṭhānaṃ nāmarūpaṃ ca nāma ca ||71cd || (19) 
sattvādhiṣṭhānā vadhādayaḥ | bhogādhiṣṭhānāḥ parastrīgamanādayaḥ | nāmarūpādhiṣṭhānā mithyādṛṣṭiḥ | (20) nāmakāyādhiṣṭhānā mṛṣāvādādayaḥ | (21) 
	yaḥ paraṃ maraṇe' niyamayya samaṃ tena pūrvaṃ vā miryate, kim asya maulaḥ karmapatho bhavaty utāho na | (22) 
		samaṃ prāk ca mṛtasyāsti, na maulaḥ (72ab) (23) 
ata evocyate, syāt prayogaṃ kuryāt phalaṃ ca paripūrayen na ca prāṇātipātāvadyena spṛśyate | (24) āha | syāt yathāpi tad vyaparopabaḥ' pūrvaṃ saha vā kālaṃ kuryād iti | kiṃ punas tatkāraṇam | [p.243] na hi vadhye jīvati hantuḥ prāṇātipātāvadyenāsti yogo nāpi sahamṛtasya hantuḥ | (2) 
		anyāśrayodayāt | (72b') (3) 
yenāśrayeṇa tatprayogaḥ kṛtaḥ, sa ucchinno 'nya evāśrayo nikāyasabhāgabhinna utpannaḥ | tasyā' (4) prayoktṛtvāt karmapathena yogo na yuktaḥ | 
	yat senāpātamṛgayāvaskandeṣu pareṣāṃ vadhārthaṃ bahavaḥ samagrāḥ (5) patanty ekaś ca prāṇātipātaṃ karoti, kas tena samanvāgato bhavati | (6) 
		senādisv ekakāryatvāt sarve karttṛvad anvitāḥ ||72cd || (7) 
yathaiva hi karttā tathā sarve samanvāgatā bhavanty ekakāryatvāt | arthato hi te 'nyonyaṃ (8) prayoktāro bhavanti | yas tarhi balān nīyate, so 'pi samanvāgato bhavaty anyatra ya evaṃ niścitya (9) yāvat yāvaj jīvitahetor api prāṇinaṃ na haniṣyāmīti | (10) 
	kiyatā punaḥ prāṇātipātaṃ svayaṃ kurvataḥ karmapatho bhavati, kiyatā yāvan mithyādṛṣṭiḥ karmapathaḥ (11) lakṣaṇaṃ vaktavyaṃ tad ucyate | (12) 
		prāṇātipātaḥ saṃcintya parasyābhrāntimāraṇam | (73ab) (13) 
yadi marayiṣyāmy enam iti saṃjñāya paraṃ mārayati tam eva ca mārayati nānyaṃ bhramitvā | iyatā (14) prāṇātipāto bhavati | yas tarhi saṃdigdho mārayati kim ayaṃ prāṇī na prāṇīti sa evānyo (15) veti so 'py avaśyam etaṃ niśrayaṃ labdhvā tatra praharati | yo 'stu so 'stv iti kṛtam evānena (16) tyāgacittaṃ bhavati | kathaṃ kṣaṇikeṣu skandheṣu prāṇātipāto bhavati | prāṇo nāma vāyuḥ (17) kāyacittasaṃniśrito vartate | tam abhipātayati | yathā pradīpaṃ nirodhayati ghaṇṭān vanaṃ vā | (18) jīvitendriyaṃ vā prāṇs tan nirodhayati | yady ekasyāpi jīvitakṣaṇasyotadyamānasyāntarāyaṃ (19) karoti prāṇātipātāvadyena spṛśyate nānyathā | kasya tajjīvitaṃ yas tadabhāvān mṛto bhavati | (20) kasyeti ṣaṣṭhīṃ pudgalavāde vicārayiṣyāmaḥ | uktaṃ tu bhagavatā (21) 
		āyur ūṣmātha vijñānaṃ, yadā kāyaṃ jahagy amī | (22) 
		apaviddhas tadā śete, yathā kāṣṭham acetana || iti | (23) 
tasmāt sendriyaḥ kāyo vīvatīty ucyate anindriyo mṛta iti | abuddhipūrvād api prāṇivadhāt (24) kartur adharmo yathāgnisaṃyogād dāha iti nirgranthāḥ | teṣāṃ [p.244] paradāradarśane 'py eṣa prasaṅgaḥ | nirgranthaśiroluñcane ca kaṣṭatapodeśane ca śāstuḥ tadvisūcikāmaraṇe (2) ca dātur vaidyānāṃ cāturapīḍane maraṇe ca mātṛgarbhasthayorś cānyonyaṃ duḥkhanimittatvāt | (3) vadhyasyāpi ca tatkriyāsaṃbandhād agnisvāśrayadāhavat | kārayataś cāprasaṅgas tadasaṃbandhāt | pareṇāgniṃ (4) sparśayatas tenādāhavat | acetanānāṃ ca kāṣṭhādīnāṃ gṛhapate prāṇivadhāt pāpaprasaṅgo na vā (5) dṛṣṭāntamātrāt siddhir iti | uktaḥ prāṇātipātaḥ | (6) 
		adattādānam anyasvasvīkriyā balacauryataḥ ||73cd || (7) 
abhrāntyeti vartate | yadi balād vā cauryeṇa vā parasvaṃ svīkaroti, yatraiva ca balacauryābhiprāyeṇānyatrā(8)saṃjñāvibhramāt iyatādattādānaṃ bhavati | stūpād apaharato buddhād adattādānam | sarvaṃ hi (9) tad bhagavatā parinirvāṇakāle parigṛhītam iti | yeṣāṃ saṃrakṣyam ity apare | asvāmikaṃ ca (10) nidhim uddharato viṣayasvāmiāt parivartakaṃ harataḥ kṛte karmaṇi sīmāprāptebhyaḥ akṛte (11) sarvabuddhaśaikṣyebhyaḥ | (12) 
		agamyagamanaṃ kāmamithyācāraś caturvidhaḥ | (74ab) (13) 
catuṣprakāram agamyagamanaṃ kāmamithyācāraḥ | agamyāṃ gacchati paraparigṛhītāṃ vā mātaraṃ (14) duhitaraṃ vā mātṛpitṛsaṃbandhinī vā | anaṅge vā gacchati (15) svām api bhāryām apāne mukhe vā | adeśe vā gacchati prakāśe caitye vihāre vā | akāle (16) vā gacchati | kaḥ punaḥ akālaḥ | garbhiṇīṃ vā gacchati pāyayantīṃ vā saniyamāṃ vā | (17) yadi bhartur anujñayā saniyamā bhavatīty eke | abhrāntyeti vartate | yadi svasaṃjñī parastriyaṃ (18) yāyāt na syāt karmapathaḥ | pady anyaḥ parastrīsaṃjñayānyām abhigacchet paradāraprayogād vastuparibhogāc ca (19) syād ity eke | anyatra prayogān na syāt prāṇātipātavad ity apare | bhikṣuṇīṃ gacchataḥ kasyāntikāt (20) kāmamithyācāraḥ | viṣayasvāminaḥ | tasya hi tan na marṣaṇīyam | tasyāpi saniyamā (21) svabhāryeṣu tu sā na gamyā | kumārīṃ gacchato yasya nisṛṣṭā no ced yasya rakṣāntato rājñaḥ | (22) 
		anyathāsaṃjñino vākyam arthābhijñe mṛṣāvacaḥ ||74cd || (23) 
yadi yam arthaṃ bravīti tasmin yathāsaṃjñī bhavati, yaṃ cādhikṛtya bravīti, sa tasya vākyasyārthābhijño [p.245] bhavati tad vākyaṃ mṛṣāvādaḥ | anabhijñe kiṃ syāt | saṃbhinnapralāpaḥ syāt | vākyam ity ucyate | (2) kadācic ca bahavo varṇā vākyaṃ bhavatīti katamaḥ tatra karmapathaḥ | paścimaḥ sahāvijñaptyā yatra (3) cārthābhijño bhavati | pūrve tu varṇāḥ prayogaḥ | arthābhijña ity ucyate | kiṃ tāvad abhijñātārthe (4) śrotari āhosvit abhijñātuṃ samarthe | kiṃ cātaḥ | yady abhijñātārthe (5) manovijñānaviṣayatvād vākyārthasya śrotravijñānena saha virodhāc ca vāgvijñaptir eva syāt (6) karmapathaḥ | athābhijñātuṃ samarthe | evaṃ doṣo na bhavati | kathaṃ cābhijñātuṃ samartho 'py utpannabhāva (7) utpanne śrotravijñāne | yathā na doṣas tathāstu | (8) 
	ṣoḍaśa vyavahārāḥ sūtra uktāḥ | adṛṣte dṛṣṭavāditā aśrute 'mate 'vijñāte vijñātavāditā (9) dṛṣṭe adṛṣtavāditā yāvad vijñāte 'vijñātavāditā itīme 'jāryā aṣṭau vyavahārāḥ | dṛṣṭe (10) dṛṣṭavāditā yāvad vijñāte vijñātavāditā adṛṣte 'dṛṣṭavāditā yāvad avijñāte avijñātavāditā itīme (11) āryā aṣṭau vyavahārāḥ | tatraiṣāṃ dṛṣṭaśrutamatavijñātānāṃ kiṃ lakṣaṇam | (12) 
		cakṣuḥ śrotramanaś cittair nubhūtaṃ tribhiś ca yat | (13) 
		tad dṛṣṭaśrutavijñātaṃ mataṃ coktaṃ yathākramam ||75|| (14) 
yac cakṣurvijñānenānubhūtaṃ tad dṛṣṭam ity uktam | yac chrotravijñānena tac chrutam | yan manovijñānena (15) tat vijñātam | yat ghrāṇajihvākāyavijñānais tan matam | kiṃ kāraṇam | gandharasaspraṣṭavyāny a(16)vyākṛtatvān mṛtakalpāni | ata eteṣu matākhyeti vaibhāṣikāḥ | kim atra jñāpakam | sūtraṃ (17) yuktiś ca | sūtraṃ tāvat: tat kiṃ manyase māhakīmātaḥ yāni tvayā cakṣuṣā rūpāṇi na dṛṣṭāni (18) na dṛṣṭapūrvāṇi na paśyati nāpi te evaṃ bhavati paśyeyam iti | api tu (19) te tannidānam utpannaś chando vā rāgo vā sneho vā yena vā ālayo vā niyatir adhyavasānaṃ (20) vā | no bhadanta | ye tvayā śrotreṇa śabdā na śrutā na śrutapūrvā vistareṇa yāvan manasā (21) dharmā na vijñātā vistareṇa yāvan no bhadanta | atra ca te māhakīmātar dṛṣṭe dṛṣṭamātraṃ bhaviṣyati (22) śrute mate vijñāte vijñātamātram ity uktam | atas triṣu viṣayeṣu dṛṣṭaśrutavijñātaśabdāpadeśād (23) gandhādiṣu matākhyā gamyate | ata evaṃ cāniṣyamāṇe dṛṣṭādibhāvabāhyatvāt gandhādiṣu vyavahāro (24) na syād ity eṣā yuktiḥ | sūtraṃ tāvad ajñāpakam' anyārthatvāt | na hy atra sūtre bhagavān vyavahārāṇāṃ (25) lakṣaṇaṃ śāsti sma | kiṃ tarhi | atra ca te ṣaḍvidhe viṣaye caturṣu vyavahāreṣu dṛṣṭādivyavahāramātraṃ [p.246] bhaviṣyati na priyāpriyanimittādhyāropa ity ayam atra sūtrārtho dṛśyate | kiṃ punar dṛṣṭaṃ kiṃ ca (2) yāvad vijñātam | kecit tāvad āhuḥ | yat pañcabhir indriyaiḥ pratyakṣaṃ tad dṛṣṭam | yat parata (3) āgamitaṃ tac chrutam | yat svayaṃ yuktyanumānato rucitaṃ tan matam | yan manaḥpratyakṣabhāvenādhiṣṭhitaṃ (4) pratyātmavedyaṃ tad vijñātam iti | ete ca pañca viṣayāḥ pratyekaṃ dṛṣṭā iti vā kṛtvā vyavahriyante | (5) śurtā matā vijñātā iti vā | ṣaṣṭho 'nyatra dṛṣṭād iti nāsti (6) gandhādiṣu vyavahārabhāvaprasaṅgaḥ | tasmād yuktir apy eṣā yuktir na bhavati | pūrvācāryā evam āhuḥ | (7) yad dṛṣṭaṃ yat pratyakṣīkṛtaṃ cakṣuṣā | śrutaṃ yac chrotreṇa parataś cākharitam | mataṃ yat svayaṃ cintitam | (8) vijñātaṃ yat pratyātmapratisaṃveditam adhigataṃ cotpannam | (9) 
	prasaṅgena śāstraṃ pravartayatām | yaḥ kāyenānyathātvaṃ prāpayet syān mṛṣāvādaḥ | syāt | ata (10) evocyate | syān na kāyena parākrameta prāṇātipātāvadyena ca spṛśyeta | syād vācā parākrameta (11) syān na vācā parākrameta mṛṣāvādāvadyena ca spṛśyeta | syāt kāyena parākrameta syān na kāyena (12) na vācā parākrameta ubhayāvadyena ca spṛśyeta | syāt ṛṣīṇāṃ manaḥpradoṣeṇa poṣadhanidarśanaṃ (13) cātreti | yady ubhayathāpi na parākrameta na cāvijñaptikāntyavijñaptiḥ kāmāvacarī kathaṃ tayoḥ (14) karmapathaḥ sidhyati | kartavyo 'tra yatnaḥ | gato mṛṣāvādaḥ | (15) 
		paiśunyaṃ kliṣṭacittasya vacanaṃ parabhedane | (76ab) (16) 
yat kliṣṭacittasya pareṣāṃ bhedāya vacanam aprītaye tat paiśunyam | arthābhijña iti vartate (17) abhrantyeti ca | (18) 
		pāruṣyam apriyaṃ (76c) (19) 
vacanam' | kliṣṭacittasyeti vartate | arthābhijñe abhrāntyeti ca | yat kliṣṭacittasya (20) pareṣām apryavacanam arthābhijñe tatraiva ca yatra' vivakṣitatṃ tat pāruṣyam | (21) 
		sarvaṃ kliṣṭaṃ bhinnapralāpitā ||76c'd || (22) 
vākyam ity adhikāro vartate | sarvaṃ kliṣṭaṃ vacanaṃ saṃbhinnapralāpaḥ | saiva saṃbhinnapralāpitā | (23) tadyogena hi saṃbhinnapralāpī bhavati | [p.247] 
		ato 'nyat kliṣṭam ity anye (77a) (2) 
ato mṛṣāvādādikād vākkarmaṇo yad anyat kliṣṭaṃ vacanaṃ tat saṃbhinnapralāpa ity apare (3) 
		lapanāgītanāṭyavat | (4) 
		kuśāstravac ca (77bc) (5) 
tadyathā bhikṣur mithyājīvī lapanāṃ karoti raktāś ca kecit gāyanti nāṭye ca naṭāḥ pareṣāṃ (6) rañjanārthaṃ pralapanti | kuśāstrāṇi ca taddaṣṭayo (taddṛṣṭayo') 'pi gadanti | evaṃ paridevasaṃgaṇikādikam api (7) yad anyan mṛṣāvādādibhyaḥ kliṣṭaṃ vākkarma tat saṃbhinnapralāpaḥ | kathaṃ cakravartikāle gītaṃ cāsti na ca saṃbhinnapralāpaḥ | naiṣkramyopasaṃhitāni hi tadānīṃ gīyante sma na grāmyarasopasaṃhitāni | (9) tadāpy āvāhavivāhādyabhilāpasadbhāvād asti saṃbhinnapralāpo na karmapatha ity apare | (10) 
		abhhyā tu parasvaviṣamaspṛhā ||77c'd || (11) 
aho bata yat pareṣāṃ tan mama syād iti parasvebhyo yā viṣameṇānyāyena spṛhā svīkaraṇecchā (12) balād vā copāyād vā sābhidhyā karmapathaḥ | sarvaiva kāmāvacarī tṛṣṇābhidhyety apare | (13) tathā hi nivaraṇādhikāre kāmacchandam adhikṛtyoktaṃ sūtre: so 'bhidhyāṃ loke prahā(14)yety evamādi | yady api sarvābhidhyā na tu sarvā karmapatha audārika(15)duścaritasaṃgrahād ity apare | mā bhūc cakravartinām apy uttarakauravāṇāṃ cābhidhyā karmapatha iti | (16) 
		vyāpādaḥ sattvavidveṣaḥ (78a) (17) 
sattveṣu vidveṣo vyāpādaḥ parapīḍākārapravṛttaḥ | (18) 
		nāstidṛṣṭiḥ śubhāśubhe | (19) 
		mithyādṛṣṭiḥ (78bc) (20) 
śubhe cāśubhe ca karmaṇi yā nāstīti dṛṣṭiḥ sā mithyādṛṣṭiḥ | tadyathā: nāsti dattaṃ nāstīṣṭaṃ (21) nāsti hutaṃ nāsti sucaritaṃ nāsti duścaritam ityevamādi yāvan na santi loke 'rhanta iti | (22) saiṣā sākalyena karmaphalāryāpavādikā mithyadṛṣṭir bhavati | ādimātraṃ tu śloke darśitam | [p.248] 
	evaṃlakṣaṇā ete daśa karmapathāḥ | karmapathā iti ko 'rthaḥ | (2) 
		trayo hy atra panthānaḥ sapta karma ca ||78c'd || (3) 
abhidhyādayo hi trayaḥ karmaṇaḥ panthāna iti karmapathaḥ | tatsaṃprayogiṇī hi cetanā teṣāṃ (4) vāhena vahita teṣāṃ gatyā gacchati | tadvaśena tathābhisaṃskaraṇāt | sapta tu prāṇātipātādayaḥ (5) karma ca kāyavākkarmatvāt karmaṇaś ca panthāna iti karmapathās tatsamutthanacetanāyās tān adhiṣṭhāya (6) pravṛtter iti | karmapathāś ca karma ca karmapathāś ceti karmapathāḥ | asarūpāṇām apy ekaśeṣasiddheḥ | (7) evam anabhidhyādayaḥ prāṇātipātaviratyādayaś ca jñeyāḥ | prayogapṛṣṭhāni (8) karmāṇi karmapathāḥ yasmāt tadarthaṃ tanmūlikā ca teṣāṃ pravṛttiḥ | yathaudārikaṃ ca grahaṇād ity uktaṃ (9) prāk | yeṣāṃ cotkarṣāpakarṣeṇādhyātmikabāhyānāṃ bhāvānām utkarṣāpakarṣau loke bhavataḥ | (10) ye tarhi dārṣṭāntikā abhidhyādīn eva manaskarmecchanti teṣāṃ te kathaṃ karmapathāḥ | ta eva hi (11) praṣṭavyāḥ | api tu śakyaṃ vaktuṃ karma ca te panthānaś ca sugatidurgatīnām iti karmapathāḥ | (12) itaretarāvāhanād vā | 
	ya ete daśākuśalāḥ karmapathāḥ sarve ete kuśalānāṃ dharmāṇāṃ' (13) samudācāravirodhinaḥ | (14) 
		mūlacchedas tv asaddṛṣṭyā (79a) (15) 
kuśalamūlacchedas tu mithyādṛṣṭyā bhavaty adhimātraparipūrṇayā | yat tarhi śāstra uktaṃ: katamāny a(16)dhimātrāṇy akuśalamūlāni | yair akuśalamūlaiḥ kuśalamūlāni samucchinatti kāmavairāgyaṃ cānuprāpnuvan (17) yāni tatprthamata upalikhatīti | akuśalamūlādhyāhṛtatvāt mithyadṛṣṭes teṣv eva tatparmāpadeśa (18) eṣa kriyate tadyathāgnir eva grāmaṃ dahati, corās tu tasyādhyāhārakā iti caurair grāmo dagdha ity ucyate | (19) 
	katameṣāṃ kuśalamūlānāṃ samucchedo bhavati | (20) 
		kāmāptotpattilābhinām | (78b) (21) 
kāmāvacarāṇi kuśalamūlāni samucchidyante | rūpārūpyāvacarair (22) asamanvāgatatvāt | prajñaptibhāṣyaṃ tarhi kathaṃ nīyate | iyatānena pudgalena traidhātukāni [p.249] kuśalamūlāni samucchinnāni bhavantīti | tatprāptidūrīkaraṇam abhisaṃdhāyaitad uktam | saṃtates (2) tadabhājanatvāpādanāt | upapattipratilābhikāny eva ca samucchidyante | prāyogikebhyaḥ pūrvaṃ (3) parihīṇatvāt | 
	kimālambanayā mithyādṛṣṭyā samucchidyante (4) 
		phalahetvapavādinyā (79c) (5) 
yā ca hetum apavadate nāsti sucaritaṃ nāsti duścaritam iti, yā ca phalaṃ nāsti sucaritaduścaritānāṃ (6) karmaṇāṃ phalavipāka iti | ānantaryavimuktimārgasthānīye ete ity apare | sāsravālambanayaiva (7) nānāsravālambanayā sabhāgadhātvālambanayaiva ca na visabhāgadhātvālambanayā | saṃprayogamātrā(8)nuśāyitvena durbalatvād ity eke | evaṃ tu varṇayanti | (10) 
		sarvayā (79d) (11) 
navaprakārāṇy api kuśalamūlāni sakṛt samucchidyante | darśanaprahātavyavad ity eke | evaṃ tu varṇayanti | (11) 
		kramaśaḥ (79d') (12) 
navaprakārayā mithyādṛṣṭyā navaprakārāṇi kuśalamūlāni samucchidyante | bhāvanāheyakleśavat (13) yāvad adhimātrādhimātrayā mṛdumṛdūnīti | evam ayaṃ granthaḥ pālito bhavati | katamāny aṇusaha(14)gatāni kuśalamūlāni | āha | yair akuśaka'mūlaiḥ kuśalāni samucchinnāni (15) sarvapaścāt vijahāti, yaiḥ vikīrṇaiḥ samucchinakuśalamūla iti saṃkhyāṃ gacchatīti | asya tarhi (16) granthasya ko nayaḥ | katamāny adhimātrādhijātrāṇy akuśalamūlāni | yair akuśalamūlaiḥ kuśalamūlāni (17) samucchinattīti | samāptim etat saṃdhāyoktam | tair niravaśeṣacchedāt | eko 'pi hi (18) prakāras teṣām asamucchinnaḥ sarveṣāṃ punarutpattau hetuḥ syād iti | darśanamārgavad abhyutthānena cchinattīty eke | (19) evaṃ tu varṇayanty ubhayatheti | pūrvaṃ saṃvaraṃ vijahāti paścāt kuśalamūlāni samucchinattīty eke | (20) evaṃ tu varṇayanti | yasya cittasya phalasaṃvaras tattyāgāt tasya tyāga iti |
