	evaṃ tu sādhīyaḥ syād / āsravaty ebhiḥ saṃtatir viṣayeṣv ity āsravāḥ / ''tadyathā āyuṣmanto naur mahadbhir abhisaṃskāraiḥ pratisroto nīyate / sā teṣām eva saṃskārāṇāṃ pratiprasrabdhyālpakṛcchreṇānusrota uhyata'' iti sūtravādānusārāt / adhimātravegatvād oghāḥ / tarhi tadvān uhyate tadanuvidhānāt / nātimatrasamudācāriṇo 'pi yogā vividhaduḥkhasaṃyojanāt / ābhīkṣṇyānuṣaṅgato vā kāmādyupādānād upādānānīti / 
		saṃyojanādibhedena punas te pañcadhoditāḥ / [p.309] 
	ta evānuśayāḥ punaḥ saṃyojanabandhanānuśayopakleśaparyavasthānabhedena pañcadhā bhittvoktāḥ / tatra nava saṃyojanāny anunayapratighamānāvidyādṛṣṭiparāmarśavicikitserṣyāmātsaryasaṃyojanāni / tatrānunayasaṃyojanaṃ traidhātuko rāgaḥ / evam anyāni yathāsaṃbhavaṃ yojyāni / dṛṣṭisaṃyojanaṃ tisro dṛṣṭayaḥ / parāmarśasaṃyojanaṃ dve dṛṣṭī / ata evocyate ''syāt dṛṣṭisaṃprayukteṣu dharmeṣv anunayasaṃyojanena saṃyukto na dṛṣṭisaṃyojanena na ca tatra dṛṣṭyanuśayo nānuśayīta / āha [ /] syāt [ /] samudayajñāne utpanne nirodhajñāne 'nutpanne nirodhamārgadarśanaprahātavyeṣu dṛṣṭiśīlavrataparāmarśasaṃprayukteṣu charmeṣu / teṣv anunayasaṃyojanena saṃyuktas tadālambanena dṛṣṭisaṃyojanenāsaṃyuktaḥ'' / 
	sarvatragasya prahīṇatvād asarvatragasya ca tadālambanasaṃprayogiṇo dṛṣṭisaṃyojanasyābhāvāt / dṛṣṭyanuśayaś ca teṣv anuśete / te eva parāmarśadṛṣṭī saṃprayogataḥ / 
	kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇaṃ saṃyojaneṣu tisro dṛṣṭayo dṛṣṭisaṃprayojanaṃ pṛthag uktaṃ dve punar dṛṣṭī parāmarśasaṃyojanaṃ pṛthak / 
		dravyāmarśanasāmānyād dṛṣṭī saṃyojanāntaram //41cd // 
	aṣṭādaśa dravyāṇi tisro dṛṣṭayaḥ / aṣṭādaśaiva dve parāmarśadṛṣṭī / ataḥ kila dravyasāmānyād ete saṃyojanāntaraṃ kṛte / ete ca dve parāmarśasvabhāvena śeṣā iti parāmarśanasāmānyād apy ete pṛthag vihite grāhyagrāhakabhedāt / 
	atha kasmād īrṣyāmātsarye saṃyojane pṛthak saṃyojanadvayam uktaṃ nānyat paryavasthānam / 
		ekāntākuśalaṃ yasmāt svatantraṃ cobhayaṃ yataḥ / 
		īrṣyamātsaryam eṣūktaṃ pṛthak saṃyojanadvayam //42// 
	na hy anyat paryavasthānam evaṃjātīyakam asti yatraitad ubhayaṃ syād ekāntākuśalatvaṃ svatantratvaṃ ceti / yasyāṣṭau paryavasthānāni tasyaivaṃ syāt / yasya punar daśa tasya krodhamrakṣāv apy ubhayaprakārau / tasmān na bhavaty ayaṃ parihāra ity apare / punar anyatra bhagavatā saṃyojanam uktam, [p.310] 
		pañcadhāvarabhāgīyaṃ_ (43a) 
	tadyathā satkāyadṛṣṭiḥ śīlavrataparāmarśo vicikitsā kāmacchando vyāpāda iti / kasmād etāny avarabhāgīyāny ucyante / avarabhāgahitatvāt / avaro hi bhāgaḥ kāmadhātur etāni ca tasyānuguṇāni / yasmāt, 
		dvābhyāṃ kāmānatikramaḥ / 
		tribhis tu punarāvṛttiḥ (43bc) 
	kāmacchandavyāpādābhyāṃ kāmadhātuṃ nātikramati / satkāyadṛṣṭyādibhir atikrānto 'pi punar āvartate dauvārikānucarasādharmyāt /tribhiḥ sattvāvaratāṃ nātikrāmati pṛthagjanatvaṃ dvābhyāṃ dhātvavaratāṃ kāmadhātum / ata eva tāny avarabhāgīyānīty apare / yadā srotāapannasya paryādāya trisaṃyojanaprahāṇāt ṣaṭ kleśā prahīṇāḥ kim arthaṃ tisro dṛṣṭīr apahāya trayam evāha satkāyadṛṣṭiṃ śīlavrataparāmarśaṃ vicikitsāṃ ca / sarvam etad vaktavyaṃ syāt / kiṃ tūktaṃ, 
		mukhamūlagrahāt trayam //43d // 
	triprakārāḥ kila kleśā ekaprakārā dviprakārāś catuṣprakārāś ca / teṣām ebhis tribhir mukhaṃ gṛhītam iti / api cāntagrāhadṛṣṭiḥ satkāyadṛṣṭipravartitā / dṛṣṭiparāmarśaḥ śīlavrataparāmarśapravartitaḥ / mithyādṛṣṭir vicikitsāpravartitā / ato mūlaṃ gṛhītam iti / 
	apare punar āhuḥ / 
		agantukāmatāmārgavibhramo mārgasaṃśayaḥ / 
		ity antarāyā mokṣasya gamane 'tas trideśanā //44// [p.311] 
	trayo 'ntarāyā deśāntaragamane bhavanti / agantukāmatā mārgavibhramo 'nyamārgasaṃśrayaṇāt mārgasaṃśayaś ca / evaṃ mokṣagamane 'py eta eva trayo 'ntarāyāḥ / tatra satkāyadṛṣṭyā mokṣād uttrāsam āpannasyāgantukāmatā bhavati / śīlavrataparāmarśenānyamārgasaṃśrayaṇān mokṣavibhramaḥ / vicikitsayā mārgasaṃśayaḥ / eṣāṃ mokṣagamanāntarāyāṇāṃ prahāṇaṃ cyotayan bhagavān kleśatrayasyaiva prahāṇaṃ deśitavān / 
	yathā bhagavatā pañcavidham avarabhāgīyaṃ saṃyojanam uktam / evaṃ punaḥ, 
		pañcadhaivordhvabhāgīyaṃ_ (45a) 
	katham ity āha, 
		dvau rāgau rūpyarūpijau / (45b) 
	tau, 
		auddhatyamānamohāś ca (45c) 
	ity etāni pañcordhvabhāgīyāni saṃyojanāni / tadyathā rūparāga ārūpyarāga auddhatyaṃ māno 'vidyā ca / eṣām aprahāṇenordhvadhātvanatikramāt / samāptaḥ saṃyojanaprasaṅgaḥ / 
	bandhanāni katamāni / trīṇi bandhanāni / rāgo bandhanaṃ sarvaḥ, dveṣo bandhanaṃ sarvaḥ, moho bandhanaṃ sarvaḥ / kasmād etad eva trayaṃ bandhanam uktaṃ bhagavatā / 
		vidvaśād bandhanatrayam //45d // [p.312] 
	trivedanāvaśāt trīṇi bandhanāni / sukhāyāṃ hi vedanāyāṃ rāgo 'nuśete ālambanasaṃprayogābhyām / duḥkhāyāṃ dveṣaḥ / aduḥkhāsukhāyāṃ moho na tathā rāgadveṣau / svāsāṃtānikālambanato vā niyamaḥ / anuśayā pūrvam evoktāḥ / 
	upakleśāḥ vaktavyāḥ / tatra ye tāvat kleśā upakleśā api te / cittopakleśanāt / 
		ye 'py anye caitasāḥ kliṣṭāḥ saṃskāraskandhasaṃjñitāḥ / 
		kleśebhyas te 'py upakleśās te tu na kleśasaṃjñitāḥ //46// 
	ye 'py anye kleśebhyaḥ kliṣṭā dharmāḥ saṃskāraskandhasaṃgṛhītāś caitasikās ta upakleśāḥ / te punar ye kṣudravastuke paṭhitāḥ / iha tu paryavasthānakleśamalasaṃgṛhītān eva nirdekṣyāmaḥ / kāni punaḥ paryavasthānānīty āha / kleśo 'pi hi paryavasthānam [ /] ''kāmarāgaparyavasthānapratyayaṃ duḥkham'' iti sūtre vacanāt / 
	prakaraṇaśāstre tu, 
		āhrīkyam anapatrāpyam īrṣyāmātsaryam uddhavaḥ / 
		kaukṛtyaṃ styānamiddhaṃ ca paryavasthānam aṣṭadhā //47// 
	vaibhāṣikanyāyena punar daśa paryavasthānāny etāni cāṣṭau, 
		krodhamrakṣau ca (48a) 
	tatrāhrīkyānapatrāpye vyākhyāte / parasaṃpattau cetaso vyāroṣa īrṣyā / dharmāmiṣakauśalapradānavirodhī cittāgraho mātsaryam / auddhatyaṃ cetaso 'vyupaśamaḥ / kaukṛtyaṃ styānaṃ ca vyākhyāte / kāyasaṃdhāraṇāsamarthaś cittābhisaṃkṣepo middham / tat tu kliṣṭam eva paryavasthānam / kaukṛtyaṃ ca / vyāpādavihiṃsāvarjitaḥ sattvāsattvayor āghātaḥ krodhaḥ / avadyapraddhādanaṃ mrakṣaḥ / eṣāṃ ca daśānāṃ paryavasthānānāṃ, 
		rāgotthā āhrīkyaudhatyamatsarāḥ / (48ab) 
	ete traya upakleśā rāganiḥṣyandāḥ / [p.313] 
		mrakṣe vivādaḥ (48c) 
	tṛṣṇāniḥṣyanda ity eke / avidyāniḥṣyanda ity apare / ubhayor ity anye / yathākramaṃ jñātājñātānām iti / 
		avidyātaḥ styānamiddhānapatrapāḥ //48cd // 
	ete trayo 'vidyāniḥṣyandāḥ / 
		kaukṛtyaṃ vicikitsātaḥ krodherṣye pratighānvaye / (49ab) 
	pratighasamutthe / ity ete ca daśa kleśaniḥṣyandā upakleśāḥ / 
		anye ca ṣaṭ kleśamalāḥ, (49c) 
tadyathā, 
		māyā_ śāṭhyaṃ madas tathā //49d // 
		pradāśa upanāhaś ca vihiṃsā ceti (50ab) 
	tatra paravañcanā māyā / cittakauṭilyaṃ śāṭhyaṃ, yena yathābhūtaṃ nāviṣkaroti vikṣipaty aparisphuṭaṃ cā pratipadyate / madaḥ pūrvoktaḥ / sāvadyavastudṛḍhagrāhitā pradāśo, yena nyāyasaṃjñaptiṃ na gṛhṇāti / āghātavastubahulīkāra upanāhaḥ / viheṣṭhanaṃ vihiṃsā, yena prahārapāruṣyādibhiḥ parān viheṭhayate / 
	eṣāṃ punaḥ ṣaṇṇāṃ kleśamalānāṃ, 
		rāgajau māyāmadau pratighaje upanāhavihiṃsane_ //50bcd // [p.314] 
		dṛṣṭyāmarśāt pradāśas tu śāṭhyaṃ dṛṣṭisamutthitam / (51ab) 
	''kiṃ kuṭilaṃ, pāpikā dṛṣṭir'' iti gāthāvacanād yujyate śāṭhyaṃ dṛṣṭiniḥṣyandaḥ / 
	ka eṣāṃ kiṃprahātavyaḥ / yāni tāvat daśa paryavasthānāny uktāni, 
		tatrāhrīkyānapatrāpyastyānamiddhoddhavā dvidhā //51cd // 
	ete pañca dharmā dvividhā darśanabhāvanāprahātavyā ubhayaprakārakleśasaṃprayogāt / yaś ca yaddarśanaheyasaṃprayuktaḥ, sa taddarśanaprahātavyaḥ / 
		tadanye bhāvnāheyāḥ (52a) 
	tebhyo 'nye paryavasthānasaṃgṛhītā upakleśā bhāvanāheyā eva / īrṣyāmātsaryakaukṛtyakrodhamrakṣāḥ svatantrāś ca / svatantrāś caite pañcopakleśā avidyāmātrasaṃprayogitvāt / 
	yathaite īrṣyādayaḥ pañcopakleśā bhāvanāheyāḥ, 
		svatantrāś ca tathā malāḥ / (52b) 
	ṣaṭ kleśamalās tathaiva / 
	ete punar yathoktā upakleśāḥ, 
		kāme 'śubhāḥ (52c) 
	kāmadhātāv akuśalāḥ / tatrāpi, 
		trayo dvidhā (52c) 
	styānauddhatyamiddhāny akuśalāvyākṛtāni / 
		pareṇāvyākṛtās tataḥ //52d // 
	kāmadhātor ūrdhvam avyākṛtāḥ upakleśā yathāsaṃbhavam / [p.315] 
	kati punar eṣāṃ kutastyā veditavyāḥ / 
		māyā śāṭhyaṃ ca kāmādyadhyānayoḥ (53ab) 
	etau dvau kāmadhātau prathame ca dhyāne / kathaṃ brahmaloke māyāḥ / 
		brahmavañcanāt / (53b) 
	sa hi tatra mahābrahmā vitathātmasaṃdarśanatayā āyuṣmantam aśvajitaṃ vañcayituṃ pravṛttaḥ / uktam api śāṭhyaṃ prasaṅgāgataṃ punar evoktam / 
		styānaudhatyamadā dhatutraye (53cd) 
	ete trayas traidhātukāḥ / 
		anye kāmadhātujāḥ //53d // 
	ṣoḍaśabhyaḥ pañcāpanīyānya ekādaśopakleśāḥ kāmāvacarā eva / uktāḥ kleśā upakleśāś ca / 
	athaiṣām anuśayānāṃ kati manobhūmikāḥ, kati ṣaḍvijñānakāyikāḥ / saṃkṣepataḥ, 
		samānamiddhā dṛggheyā manovijñānabhūmikāḥ / (54ab) 
	darśanaprahātavyāḥ sarve manobhūmikāḥ saha mānamiddhābhyāṃ bhāvanāheyābhyām api / te hi sakale manobhūmike / 
		upakleśāḥ svatantāś ca (54c) 
	ye ca kecid upakleśāḥ svatantrās te bhāvanāheyā api santo manobhūmikā eva draṣṭavyāḥ / 
		ṣaḍvijñānāśrayāḥ pare //54d // 
	anye kleśopakleśāḥ ṣaḍvijñānabhūmikāḥ veditavyāḥ / ke punar anye / bhavanāprahātavyā [p.316] rāgapratighāvidyā upakleśāś ca tatsaṃprayuktāḥ / āhrīkyānapatrāpyastyānauddhatyāni / ye ca kleśamahābhūmikeṣūktāḥ / 
	yānīmāni sukhādīni pañcendriyāṇi eṣāṃ katamenendriyeṇa katamaḥ kleśa upekleśo vā saṃprayūktaḥ / 
		sukhābhyāṃ saṃprayukto hi rāgaḥ (55ab) 
	sukhasaumanasyābhyāṃ rāgaḥ saṃprayuktaḥ / 
		dveṣo viparyayāt / (55b) 
	duḥkhābhyām ity arthaḥ / duḥkhena daurmanasyena ca / harṣadainyākāravartitvāt ṣaḍvijñānabhūmikatvāc ca rāgadveṣayoḥ / 
		mohaḥ sarvaiḥ (55c) 
	avidyāyāḥ sarvakleśasaṃprayogitvāt pañcabhir apīndriyaiḥ saṃprayogaḥ / 
		asaddṛṣṭir mano duḥkhasukhena tu //55cd // 
	manoduḥkhaṃ daurmanasyaṃ manaḥsukhaṃ saumanasyaṃ ca / tābhyāṃ mithādṛṣṭiḥ saṃprayuktā puṇyakarmaṇāṃ pāpakarmaṇāṃ ca yathākramam / 
		daurmanasyena kāṅkṣā (56a) 
	saṃśayito hi niścayenārthī durmanāyate / 
		anye saumanasyena (56ab) 
	anye 'nuśayāḥ saumanasyenaiva saṃprayuktāḥ / ke punar anye / catasro dṛṣṭayo mānaś ca / harṣākāravartitvāt / kiṃpratisaṃyuktā ime 'nuśayā nirdiṣṭāḥ / āha / 
		kāmajāḥ (56b) [p.317] 
	evaṃ pratiniyataṃ saṃprayogam uktvā sāmānyenāha, 
		sarve 'py upekṣayā (56c) 
	sarve 'py ete 'nuśayā upekṣendriyeṇa saṃprayuktāḥ / pravāhacchedakāle kila kleśānām avaśyam upekṣā saṃtiṣṭhate / 
	adhobhūmikāḥ katham ity āha, 
		svaiḥ svair yathābhūmyūrdhvabhūmikāḥ //56cd // 
	svaiḥ svair indriyair ūrdhvabhūmikā anuśayāḥ saṃprayujyante / yasyāṃ bhūmau yāvantīndriyāṇi / tatrāpi cāturvijñānakāyikāś cāturvijñānakāyikair manobhūmikā manobhūmikair eva yathāsaṃbhavam / uktaḥ kleśānām indriyasaṃprayogaḥ / 
	upakleśānāṃ punaḥ, 
		daurmanasyena kaukṛtyam, 
		īrṣyā krodho vihiṃsanam / 
		upanāhaḥ pradāśaś ca (57abc) 
	saṃprayuktānīti vartate / dainyākāravartitvād eṣāṃ manobhūmikatvāc ca / 
		mātsaryaṃ tu viparyayāt //57d // 
	saumanasyenety arthaḥ / lobhānvayatvena harṣākāravartitvāt / 
		māyā śāṭhyam atho mrakṣo, 
		middhaṃ cobhayathā (58ab) 
	saumanasyadaurmanasyābhyāṃ saṃprayujyante / kadācid dhi sumanāḥ paraṃ vañcayate / kadācid durmanāḥ / evaṃ yāvat svapiti / 
		madaḥ / 
		sukhābhyām (58bc) [p.318] 
	tṛtīye dhyāne sukhenādhastāt saumanasyena ūrdhvam upekṣayā / yasmāt, 
		sarvagopekṣā (58c) 
	atas tayā sarve 'nuśayāḥ saṃprayujyante / na hi tasyāḥ kvacit pratiṣedho yathāvidyāyāḥ / 
		catvāry anyāni pañcabhiḥ //58d // 
	āhrīkyam anapatrāpyaṃ styānam auddhatyaṃ caitāni catvāri paryavasthānāni pañcabhir apīndriyaiḥ sapṃprayujyante / akuśalamahābhūmikatvāt kleśamahābhūmikatvāc ca / 
	yāni sūtre pañca nivaraṇāni uktāni kāmacchando vyāpādaḥ styānamiddhauddhatyakaukṛtyaṃ vicikitsā ca / tatra kiṃ traidhātukyaḥ styānauddhatyavicikitsā gṛhyante / atha kāmapratisaṃyuktā eva / ''kevalo 'yaṃ paripūrṇo 'kuśalarāśir yaduta pañca nivaraṇānī''ty ekāntākuśalatvavacanāt sūtre / 
		kāme nivaraṇāni (59a) 
	nānyatra dhātau / kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇaṃ dve styānamiddhe ekaṃ nivaraṇam uktaṃ, cve cauddhatyakaukṛtye ekam / 
		ekavipakṣāhārakṛtyataḥ / 
		dvyekatā_ (59abc) 
	dvayor ekatā dvyekatā / vipakṣaḥ pratipakṣo 'nāhāra ity eko 'rthaḥ / styānamiddhayor eka āhāraḥ sūtre 'nāhāraś ca / kaḥ styānamiddhanivaraṇasyāhāraḥ / pañca dharmāḥ / tandrā aratir vijṛmbhikā bhakte 'samatā cetaso līnatvam iti / kaś ca styānamiddhanivaraṇasyānāhāraḥ ālokasaṃjñeti / kṛtyam anayor apy ekam / ubhe api hy ete cittaṃ layaṃ codayataḥ / [p.319] auddhatyakaukṛtyayor apy eka āhāra uktaḥ ekonāhāraḥ' / kaś cauddhatyakaukṛtyanivaraṇasyāhāraḥ / catvāro dharmāḥ / jñātivitarko janapadavitarko 'maravitarkaḥ paurāṇasya ca hasitakrīḍitaramitaparibhāvitasyānusmartā bhavatīti / caś cauddhatyakaukṛtyanivaraṇasyānāhāraḥ / śamatha iti / kṛtyam apy anayor ekam / ubhe api hy ete cittam avyupaśāntaṃ vartayataḥ / ata ekavipakṣāhārakṛtyatvāt dvayor apy ekatvam uktam / 
	yadā sarvakleśā nivaraṇaṃ kasmāt pañcaivoktāni / 
		pañcatā skandhavighātavicikitsanāt //59cd // 
	kāmacchandavyāpādābhyāṃ śīlaskandhavighātaḥ / sthānamiddhena prajñāskandhasyauddhatyakaukṛṭyena samādhiskandhasya / samādhiprajñayor abhāve satyeṣu vicikitsako bhavatīty ataḥ pañcoktāni / ekasyāṃ tu kalpanāyāṃ samādhiskandhavirodhina auddhatyakaukṛtyanivaraṇasya pūrvaṃ grahaṇaṃ prāpnoti / ato yathāsaṃkyam etābhyāṃ samādhiprajñāsikandhopaghāta ity apare / samādhiprayuktasya hi styānamiddhad bhayam / dharmapravidayaprayuktasyauddhatyakaukṛtyād iti / 
	anye tv anyathā varṇayanti / kathaṃ varṇayanti / cāragatasya priyāpriyarūpeṣu viṣayeṣu nimittagrāhād vihāragatasya tatpūrvakau kāmacchandavyāpādau samādhipraveśasyādito 'ntarāyaṃ kurutaḥ / tataḥ samādhipraviṣṭasyāprayogeṇa śamathaviśaṣyanāsevanāt sthyānamiddham auddhatyakaukṛtyaṃ vicikitsā ca yathākramaṃ śamathavipaśyanayor antarāyaṃ kurutaḥ / vyutthitasyāpi dharmanidhyānakāle vicikitsāntarāyaṃ karoti / ataḥ pañca nivaraṇāny uktāni / 
	idaṃ vicāryate / visabhāgadhātusarvatragāṇāṃ nirodhamārgadarśanaprahātavyānāṃ ca sāsravālambanānāṃ yad ālambanaṃ parijñāyate tadā na prahīyante / yadā prahīyante tadālambanaṃ na parijñāyate iti katham eṣāṃ prahāṇam / nāvaśyam ālambanaparijñānāt kleśānāṃ kṣayo bhavati / kiṃ tarhi / caturbhḥ prakāraiḥ / katamaiś caturbhiḥ / darśanaheyānāṃ tāvat, 
		ālambanaparijñānāt tadālambanasaṃkṣayāt / 
		ālambanaprahāṇāc ca (60abc) 
	tatrālambanaparjñānād duḥkhasamudayadarśanaheyānāṃ svabhūmyālambanānām anāsravālambanānāṃ ca / tadālambanasaṃkṣayād visabhāgadhātusarvatragāṇām / tadālambanā hi sabhāgadhātusarvatragāḥ / [p.320] teṣu prahīṇeṣu te 'pi prahīṇā bhavanti / ālambanaprahāṇān nirodhamārgaheyānāṃ sāsravālambanānām / te hy anāsravālambanās teṣām ālambanam / atas teṣu prahīṇeṣu te 'pi prahīṇā bhavanti / 
	bhāvanāheyānāṃ punaḥ, 
		pratipakṣodayāt kṣayaḥ //60d // 
	yasya hi kleśaprakārasya pratipakṣo mārga utpadyate, sa prahīyate / kasya punaḥ kaḥ pratipakṣaḥ / adhimātrādhimātrasya mṛdumṛdur iti vistareṇa paścāt pravedayiṣyāmaḥ / 
	katividhaś ca pratipakṣa ity āha, 
		prahāṇādhāradūratvadūṣaṇākhyaś caturvidhaḥ / 
		pratipakṣaḥ (61abc) 
	prahāṇapratipakṣaḥ ānantaryamārgaḥ / ādhārapratipakṣas tasmāt pareṇāryo mārgo, yena tatprāpitaṃ prahāṇam ādhāryate / dūrībhāvapratipakṣo vimuktimārgāt pareṇa, yo mārgaś chinnaprāptidūrīkaraṇāt / vimuktimārgo 'pīty apare / so 'pi hi tāṃ prāptiṃ dūrikaroti / vidūṣaṇāpratipakṣo yena mārgeṇa taṃ dhātuṃ doṣato darśanād vidūṣayati / api tv eṣām iyam ānupūrvī sādhvī bhavet / vidūṣaṇāpratipakṣo duḥkhasamudayālambanaḥ prayogamārgaḥ / prahāṇapratipakṣaḥ sarva ānantaryamārgaḥ / ādhārapratipakṣo vimuktimārgaḥ / dūrībhāvapratipakṣo viśeṣamārga iti / 
	prahīyamāṇaḥ kleśaḥ kutaḥ prahātavyaḥ / 
		prahātavyaḥ kleśa ālambanāt mataḥ //61cd // 
	na hi saṃprayogāt kleśo vivecayituṃ śakyate ālambanāc ca śakyate / yasmān na punas tad ālambyotpadyate / anāgatas tāvac chakyetālambanād vivecayitum / atītas tu katham / athālambanaparijñānāt prahātavya ity ayam asyārthaḥ / eṣo 'pi naikāntaḥ / tasmād vaktavyam etat / [p.321] kiyatā kleśaḥ prahīṇo vaktavyaḥ / svāsāṃtānikaḥ prāpticchedāt / pārasāṃtānikas tu kleśaḥ sarvaṃ ca rūpam akliṣṭaś ca dharmas tadālambanasvāsāṃtānikakleśaprahāṇāt / 
	dūrībhāva ity ucyate / katividho dūrībhāvaḥ / caturvidhā kila dūratā / 
		vailakṣaṇyād vipakṣatvād deśavicchedakālataḥ / 
		bhūtaśīlapradeśādhvadvayānām iva dūratā //62// 
	vilakṣaṇadūratā yathā mahābhūtānām / vailakṣaṇyāt sahajānām api dūratā / vipakṣadūratā yathā śīlasya dauḥśīlyam' / deśavicchedadūratā yathā viprakṛṣṭadeśānāṃ deśavicchedāt pūrvapaścimasamudravat / kāladūratā yathā atītānāgatam dūram ucyate / 
	kutas tad dūram / vartamānāt / yad anantarātītam utpadyamānaṃ vā tat kathaṃ dūram / adhvanānātvena tad dūraṃ na cirabhūtabhāvitvena / vartamānam apy evaṃ dūraṃ prāpnoti / akāritrāt tarhi tad dūram / asaṃskṛtasya katham antikatvaṃ sidhyati / sarvatra tatprāpteḥ / atītānāgate 'pi tatprasaṅgaḥ / ākāśaṃ ca katham / evaṃ tarhy atītānāgatam anyonyaṃ vartamānavyavahitatvād dūram / vartamānam ubhayor āsannatvād antikam / asaṃskṛtṃ cāpy avyavahitatvād iti / evam apy atītānāgataṃ vartamānasyāntikatvād ubhayaṃ prāpnoti / 
	evaṃ tu yuktaṃ syāt / dharmasvalakṣaṇād anāgataṃ dūram asaṃprāptatvāt / atītaṃ ca pracyutatvād iti / 
	kiṃ mārgaviśeṣagamanāt kleśānāṃ punaḥ prahāṇaviśeṣo bhavati / naitad asti / sarveṣāṃ hi kleśānāṃ, 
		sakṛt kṣayaḥ (63a) 
	yasya yaḥ prahāṇamārgas tenaiva tasya kṣayaḥ / 
		visaṃyogalābhas teṣāṃ punaḥ punaḥ / (63ab) [p.322] 
	katiṣu kāleṣv ityāha ṣaṭsu kāleṣu / 
		pratipakṣodayaphalaprāptīndriyavivṛddhiṣu //63cd // 
	pratipakṣo vimuktimārga etasminn abhipretaḥ / phalāni catvāri śrāmaṇyaphalāni / indriyavivṛddhir indriyasaṃcāraḥ / eteṣu kāleṣu kleśasya visaṃyogalābhaḥ / sa punar eṣa yathāyogaṃ draṣṭavyaḥ / keṣāṃcit ṣaṭsu kāleṣu keṣāṃcid yāvad dvayoḥ / 
	sa eva visaṃyogas tāsu tāsv avasthāsu parijñāsaṃjñāṃ labhate / dve hi parijñe jñānaparijñā prahāṇaparijñā ca / tatra jñānaparijñā sāsravaṃ jñānam / prahāṇaparijñā prahāṇam eva / phale hetūpacārāt / kim ekaiva parijñā sarvaprahāṇam / nety āha / kiṃ tarhi / 
		parijñā nava (64a) 
	tatra tāvat, 
		kāmādyaprakāradvayasaṃkṣayaḥ / 
		ekā (64abc) 
	kāmadhātāv ādyasya prakāradvayasya duḥkhasamudayadarśanaheyasya prahāṇam ekā parijñā / 
		dvayoḥ kṣayo_ dve te (64c) 
	kāmadhātāv eva nirodhadarśanaheyasya prakārasya prahāṇam ekā parijñā / mārgadarśanaheyasyaikā / yathā kāmāvacarāṇāṃ darśanaprahātavyānāṃ prahāṇaṃ tisraḥ parijñāḥ, 
		tathordhvaṃ tisra eva tāḥ //64d // 
	rūpārūpyāvacarāṇāṃ darśanaprahātavyānāṃ prahāṇaṃ tathaiva tisraḥ parijñā bhavanti / duḥkhasamudayadarśanaprahātavyānāṃ prahāṇam ekā nirodhadarśanaheyānāṃ prahāṇaṃ dvitīyā mārgadarśanaheyānāṃ prahāṇaṃ tṛtīyeti traidhātukānāṃ darśanaheyānāṃ prahāṇaṃ ṣaṭ parijñā bhavanti / [p.323] 
	anyā avarabhāgīyarūpasarvāsravakṣayāḥ / 
		tisraḥ parijñāḥ (65abc) 
	avarabhāgīyaprahāṇam ekā parijñā / rūpāsravaprahāṇam ekā rūparāgakṣayaparijñā / ārūpyāptānāṃ sarvāsravaprahāṇam ekā sarvasaṃyojanaparyādānaparijñā / kasmād rūpārūpyāvacarāṇāṃ bhāvanāheyānāṃ prahāṇaṃ pṛthak parijñā na darśanaheyānām / bhāvanāheyānām atulyapratipakṣatvāt / ity etā nava parijñāḥ / āsāṃ pūrvikāḥ, 
		ṣaṭ kṣāntiphalaṃ (65cd) 
	yā eva darśanaheyaprahāṇasvabhāvāḥ / 
		jñānasya śeṣitāḥ //65// 
	avarabhāgīyaprahāṇādiparijñā bhāvanāmārgaphalatvāt / kathaṃ kṣāntiphalaṃ parijñā bhavanti / kṣāntīnāṃ jñānaparivāratvāt / rājaparivāre rājopacāravat / jñānaikaphalatvāc ca / 
		anāgamyaphalaṃ sarvā dhyānānāṃ pañca vāthavā / 
		aṣṭau (66abc) 
	vaibhāṣikamatena mauladhyānaphalaṃ pañca parijñā yā rūpārūpyāvacarakleśaprahāṇasvabhāvāḥ / kāmāvacarakleśaprahāṇasyānāgamyaphalatvāt / bhadantaghoṣakasya matenāṣṭau / sa hi vītarāgasyāpi kāmāvacarāṇāṃ darśanaheyānāṃ prahāṇaṃ darśanamārgaphalam icchanti / anāsravavisaṃyogaprāptilābhāt / avarabhāgīyaprahāṇaparijñā tv anāgamyaphalam eva / dhyānāntaraṃ dhyānavad draṣṭavyam / ārūpyāṇāṃ tu, 
		sāmantakasyaikā (66c) 
	ākāśānantyāyatanasāmantakasyaikā rūparāgakṣayaparijñā phalam / [p.324] 
		maulārūpyatrayasya ca //66d // 
	maulānāṃ ca trayāṇām ārūpyāṇām ekaiva sarvasaṃyojanaparyādānaparijñā phalam / 
		āryamārgasya sarvāḥ (67a) 
	nava parijñāḥ phalam / 
		dve, laukikasya (67ab) 
	laukikasya mārgasya dve avarabhāgīyarūparāgakṣayaparijñe phalam / 
		anvayasya ca / (67b) 
	anvayajñānasyāpi dve parijñe phalaṃ paścime / 
		dharmajñānasya tisras tu (67c) 
	paścimā eva traidhātukabhāvanāheyapratipakṣatvāt / 
		ṣaṭ tatpakṣasya pañca ca //67d // 
	dharmajñānapakṣasya ṣaṭ parijñāḥ phalaṃ yā eva dharmakṣāntijñānānām / anvayajñānapakṣasya pañca yā evānvāyakṣāntijñānānām / pakṣagrahaṇena hi kṣāntijñānāni gṛhyante / kasmān naikaikaṃ prahāṇaṃ parijñā vyavasthāpyate / yasmāt kṣāntiphalaṃ tāvat prahāṇaṃ vyavasthāpyate / 
		anāsravaviyogāpter bhavāgravikalīkṛteḥ / 
		hetudvayasamudghātāt parijñā (68abc) 
	yatraitāni trīṇi kāraṇāni bhavanti tat prahāṇaṃ parijñocyate / pṛthagjanasya tāvad anāsravā visaṃyogaprāptir nāsti bhavāgravikalīkaraṇaṃ ceti nāsya prahāṇaṃ parijñākhyāṃ labhate / āryasyāpi [p.325] yat kṣāntiphalaṃ tāvat prahāṇam / tatra yāvat duḥkhe 'nvayajñānakṣāntāv anāsravā visaṃyogaprāptir asti na tu bhavāgravikalīkṛtam / duḥkhe 'nvayajñāne ubhayam asti na tu hetudvayasamudghātaḥ / samudayadarśanaprahātavyasya sarvatragahetor aprahīṇatvāt / anyeṣu dharmānvayajñāneṣu sarvaṃ trayam asti / atas tāsu tāsv avasthāsu prahāṇaṃ parijñākhyāṃ labhate / jñānaphalaṃ tu prahāṇam ataś ca kāraṇatrayāt parijñākhyāṃ labhate / caturthāc ca, 
		dhātvatikramāt //68d // 
	yadā dhātuṃ samatikrāmati / kṛtsnadhātuvairāgyāt ubhayasaṃyogaviyogaṃ pañcamaṃ kāraṇam āhur apare / yaḥ prakāraḥ prahīṇo yadi tatrānyena tadālambanena kleśena visaṃyukto bhavatīti / sa tu nāsya ubhayahetusamudghātāt dhātusamatikramāc ceti na brūmaḥ / 
	kaḥ katibhiḥ parijñābhiḥ samanvāgataḥ / 
		naikayā pañcabhir yāvad darśanasthaḥ samanvitaḥ / (69ab) 
	pṛthagjanas tāvan naiva samanvāgataḥ / āryo 'pi darśanamārgastho yāvat samudayadharmajñānakṣāntiṃ naiva samanvāgataḥ / samudaye dharmajñāna ekayā samanvāgataḥ / samudaye 'nvayajñāne dvābhyām / nirodhe dharmajñāne tisṛbhir nirodhānvayajñāne catasṛbhir mārgadharmajñāne pañcabhiḥ / 
		bhāvanāsthaḥ punaḥ ṣaḍbhir ekayā vā dvayena vā //69cd // 
	bhāvanāmārgasthaḥ punar āryapudgalo mārgānvayajñāne ṣaḍbhiḥ parijñābhh sajmanvāgato yāvat kāmavairāgyaṃ na prāptaḥ / parihīṇo vā tataḥ kāmavairāgyaṃ prāptaḥ pūrvaṃ paścād vā ekayāvarabhāgīyaprahāṇaparijñayā / arhattvaṃ prāpta ekayaiva sarvasaṃyojanaparyādānaparijñayā / parihīṇo 'pi rūpāvacareṇa paryavasthānenaikayāvarabhāgīyaprahāṇaparijñayā / rūpavairāgyaṃ prāpto dvābhyām avarabhāgīyaprahāṇarūparāgakṣyaparijñābhyām / prahīṇo 'py ārūpyāvacareṇa paryavasthānenābhyām eva / 
	kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇam anāgāmyarhator ekaiva parijñā vyavasthāpyate na bhūyasyaḥ / yasmāt, [p.326] 
		tāsāṃ saṃkalanaṃ dhātuvairāgyaphalalābhataḥ / (70ab) 
	dvābhyāṃ kāraṇābhyāṃ parijñānāṃ saṃkalanaṃ bhavaty ekatvena vyavasthāpanaṃ dhātuvairāgyāt phalaprāptitaś ca / tayoś cāvasthayor etad ubhayaṃ bhavati / ataḥ sarvaṃ prahāṇaṃ saṃkalayyaikā parijñocyate / 
	atha kaḥ kati parijñās tyajati labhate vā / 
		ekāṃ dve pañca ṣaṭ kaścij jahāty āpnoti pañca na //70cd // 
	ekāṃ tyajati arhattvāt kāmavairāgyād vā parihīyamāṇaḥ / dve parijñe tyajaty anāgāmī rūpavītarāgaḥ kāmavairāgyāt parihīyamāṇaḥ / pañca tyajati vītarāgapūrvī mārgānvayajñāne / sa hy avarabhāgīyaprahāṇaparijñālābhe pūrvikāḥ pañca parijñās tyajati / ṣaṭ parijñās tyajaty anupūrvikaḥ kāmavairāgyāt / lābhe 'py evam eva / kaścid ekāṃ parijñāṃ labhate / yaḥ kaścid apūrvāṃ labhate / kaścid dve yaḥ kevalād ārūpyadhātuvairāgyāt parihīyate / kaścid ṣaṭ yo 'nāgāmiphalāt parihīyate / pañca tu na kaścil labhate / 
	samāptaḥ parijñāprasaṅgaḥ // 
	abhidharmakośabhāṣye 'nuśayanirddeśo nāma 
	pañcamaṃ kośasthānaṃ samāptam iti // 
	śīlāmāvākasya yad atra puṇyam /

 
KAPITEL VI

[p.327] 
ṣaṣṭhaṃ kośashānam mārgapudgalanirdeśaḥ 
			namo buddhāya 
	uktaṃ yathā prahāṇaṃ parijñākhyāṃ labhate / tad api ca, 
		kleśaprahāṇam ākhyātaṃ satyadarśanabhāvanāt / (1ab) 
darśanaheyā bhāvanāheyāś ca kleśā iti vistareṇākhyātam / tāv idānīṃ darśanabhāvanāmārgau kim anāsravau sāsravāv iti vaktavyam / ata idam ucyate, 
		dvividho bhāvanāmārgo darśanākhyas tv anāsravaḥ //1cd // 
dvividho bhāvanāmārgo laukiko lokottaraś ca / darśanamārgas tu lokotara eva traidhātukapratipakṣatvāt, navaprakārāṇāṃ darśanaheyānāṃ sakṛtprahāṇāc ca / na hi laukikasyaiṣā śaktir asti / 
	satyadarśanād ity uktam / kānīmāni satyāni kati ca / 
		satyāny uktāni catvāri (2a) 
kvoktāni / sāsravānāsravadharmanirdeśe / Oanāsravā mārgasatyamO iti svaśabdena, Opratisaṃkhyānirodho yo visaṃyogaO iti nirodhasatyam, Oduḥkhaṃ samudayo lokaO ity atra duḥkhasamudayasatye / 
	kim eṣa evaiṣām anukramaḥ / nety āha / kiṃ tarhi / 
		duḥkhaṃ samudayas tathā / 
		nirodhamārga iti (2bc) 
eṣa eṣām anukramaḥ / svabhāvas" tu yathā pūrvam uktas tathaiveti pradarśanārthas tathāśabdaḥ / 
	sa punar ayam, 
		eṣāṃ yathābhisamayaṃ kramaḥ //2cd // 
yasya hi satyasyābhisamayaḥ pūrvas tasya" pūrvanirdeśaḥ / itarathā hi pūrvaṃ hetunirdeśo *bhaviṣyat paścāt phalanirdeśaḥ / 
	keṣāṃcid utpattyanukūlā deśanā, yathā smṛtyupasthānadhyānādīnām / keṣāṃcit prarūpaṇānukūlā deśanā, yathā samyakprahāṇānām / na hy eṣa niyamo yat pūrvam utpannānāṃ prahānāya cchandaṃ janayati, paścād anutpannānām anutpādāyeti / satyānāṃ tv abhisamayānukūlā deśanā / 
	kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇam evam eṣāṃ satyānām abhisamayaḥ / yatra hi sakto yena ca bādhyate yataś ca mokṣaṃ prārthayate, tad evādau vyavacāraṇāvasthāyāṃ duḥkhasatyaṃ parīṣyate / paścāt ko*sya hetur iti samudayasatyam, ko *sya nirodha iti nirodhasatyam, ko *sya mārga iti mārgasatyam / vyādhiṃ dṛṣṭvā tannidānakṣayabheṣajānveṣaṇavat / 
	sūtre *py eṣa eva satyānāṃ dṛṣṭānto darśitaḥ / katamasmin sūtre / Ocaturbhir aṅgaiḥ samanvāgato bhiṣak śalyāpahartā"O ity atra / 
	yathā ca vyavacāraṇāvasthāyāṃ satyaparīkṣā tathābhisamayāvasthāyāṃ satyābhisamayaḥ pūrvāvedhāt, dṛṣṭabhūminiḥsaṃgāśvadhāvanavat" 
	abhisamaya iti ko *rthaḥ / abhisaṃbodhaḥ / iṇo bodhanārthatvāt / kasmād anāsrava eva na sāsravaḥ / sa hi nirvāṇābhimukhaḥ samyak bodhaḥ / samyag iti tattvena / 
	tatra phalabhūtā upādānaskandhā duḥkhasatyam, hetubhūtāḥ samudayasatyam, samudety asmād iti kṛtvā / ata eva tayoḥ phalahetubhāvān nāmato bhedo, na dravyataḥ / nirodhamārgayos tu dravyato *pi / 
	āryasatyānīti sūtra ucyante / ko *syārthaḥ / Oāryāṇām etāni satyāni tasmād āryasatyānīOti sūtra evoktam / kim anyeṣām etāni mṛṣā / sarveṣām etāni satyāny aviparītatvāt / āryais tu yathaitāni, tathā dṛṣṭāni nānyaiḥ / ata āryāṇām etāni satyāny ucyante na tv anāryāṇāṃ viparītadarśanāt / 
		Oyad āryāḥ sukhataḥ prāhus tat pare duḥkhato viduḥ / 
		yat pare sukhataḥ prāhus tad āryā duḥkhato viduḥO iti gāthā / 
	dve āryāṇāṃ satye, dve ārye cāryāṇāṃ ca satye ity apare / 
	yadā" vedanaikadeśo duḥkhasvabhāvaḥ, kathaṃ sarve sāsravāḥ saṃskārā duḥkham ity ucyante / 
		duḥkhās triduḥkhatāyogād yathāyogam aśeṣataḥ / 
		manāpa amanāpāś ca tadanye caiva sāsravāḥ //3// 
tisro hi duḥkhatā, duḥkhaduḥkhatā saṃskāraduḥkhatā vipariṇāmaduḥkhatā ca / tābhir yathāyogam aśeṣataḥ sarve sāsravāḥ saṃskārā duḥkhāḥ / 
	tatra manāpā vipariṇāmaduḥkhataya, amanāpā duḥkhaduḥkhatayā, tebhyo *nye saṃskāraduḥkhatayā / 
	ke punar manāpāḥ, ke *manāpāḥ, ke nobhayathā / 
	tisro vedanā yathākramam / tadvaśena sukhavedanīyādayo *pi saṃskārā manāpādisaṃjñāṃ labhante / 
		sukhāyā hi vedanāyā vipariṇāmena duḥkhatā / sūtra uktaṃ Osukhā vedanā utpādasukhā sthitisukhā vipariṇāmaduḥkhāO iti / duḥkhāyāḥ duḥkhasvabhāvenaiva duḥkhatā / Oduḥkhā vedanā utpādasuḥkhā sthitiduḥkhāO iti sūtre / aduḥkhāsukhavedanāyāḥ saṃskāreṇaiva duḥkhatā pratyayābhisaṃskaraṇāt , Oyad anityaṃ tad duḥkhamO iti / vedanāvat tadvedanīyā api saṃskārā veditavyā" / 
		duḥkham eva duḥkhatā uḥkhaduḥkhatā / evaṃ yāvat saṃskārā eva duḥkhatety apare / 
2.2.1 b)		asādhāraṇatvāt manāpāmanāpānāṃ vipariṇāmaduḥkhaduḥkahte ukte / sarve tu saṃskārāḥ saṃskāraduḥkhatayā duḥkhāḥ / tāṃs tv āryā eva paśyanti / āha cātra, 
		Oūrṇāpakṣma yathaiva hikaratalasaṃsthaṃ na vedyate pumbhiḥ akṣigataṃ tu tad eva" hijanayaty aratiṃ ca pīḍāṃ ca / karatalasadṛśo bālo na vetti saṃskāraduḥkhatāpakṣma akṣisadṛśas tu vidvāṃstenaivodvejyate gāṭham //O iti / 
na hi bālānām āvīcikeṣv api skandheṣu tathā uḥkhabuddhiḥ pravartate, yathāryāṇāṃ bhāvāgrikeṣv apīti / 
	evaṃ tarhi mārgasyāpi saṃskāraduḥkhatāprasaṅgaḥ saṃskṛtatvāt / 
	pratikūlaṃ hi duḥkham iti lakṣaṇān na mārgo duhkham / na hi tasyotpāda āryāṇāṃ pratikūlaḥ sarvaduḥkhakṣayāvāhanāt / yadāpi te nirvāṇaṃ śāntataḥ paśyanti, tadāpi yad eva duḥkhato dṛṣṭaṃ tasyaiva nirodhaṃ śāntataḥ paśyanti na mārgasya / 
	yadāpi sukham" apy atrāsti, [tadā]" kasmāt" duḥkham evāryasatyam ucyate / 
	sukhasyālpatvād mudgādibhāve *pi māṣarāśy apadeśavad ity eke / ko hi vidvān pariṣekasukhānukena gaṇḍaḥ" sukham iti vyavasyet / 
	āha khalv api, 
			Oduḥkhasya ca hetutvāt duḥkhaiś cānalpakaiḥ samuditatvāt / 
			duḥkhe ca sati tadiṣṭer duḥkhaṃ sukham iti vyavasyantiO iti / 
	sahaiva tu sukhena sarvaṃ bhavam āryā duḥkhataḥ paśyanti saṃskāraduḥkhataikarasatvāt / ato duḥkham evāryasatyaṃ vyavasthāpyate na sukham / 
	katham idānīṃ sukhasvabhāvaṃ vedanāṃ duḥkhataḥ paśyanti / anityatayā pratikūlatvāt / yathā rūpasaṃjñādīny api duḥkhataḥ paśyanti, na ca tāny evaṃ duḥkhāni yathā duḥkhavedaneti / 
	yas tu manyate duḥkhahetutvād iti, tasyāsau samudayākāraḥ syān na duḥkhākāraḥ / āryāṇāṃ ca rūpārūpyopapattau kathaṃ duḥkhasaṃjñā pravarteta / na hi punas teṣāṃ duḥkhavedanāhetuḥ skandhā bhavanti / saṃskāraduḥkhatā ca sūtre kim arthaṃ pṛthag uktā bhavet / 
	yadi tarhy anityatvād duḥkhataḥ paśyanti, pratikūlatvād duḥkham / anityaṃ tu dṛśyamānaṃ pratikūlaṃ bhavatīty anityākāro duḥkhākāram ākarṣati / 
	nāsty eva sukhā vedanety ekīyā duḥkhaiva tu sarvā / katham idaṃ gamyate / sūtrād yuktitaś ca / 
	kathaṃ tāvat sūtrāt / uktaṃ hi bhagavatā Oyat kiṃcid veditam idam atra duḥkhasyaO iti, Osukhā" vedanā duḥkhato draṣṭavyāO iti, Oduḥkhe sukham iti saṃjñāviparyāsaḥO iti / evaṃ tāvat sūtrāt / 
	kathaṃ" yuktitaḥ / sukhahetuvavyavasthānāt / ya eva hi kecit pānabhojanaśītoṣṇādaya iṣyante sukhahetavas ta evātyupayuktā akālopayuktāś ca punar duḥkhahetavaḥ saṃpadyante / na ca yuktā sukhahetuvṛddhyā samena vānyasmin kāle duḥkhotpattir ity ādita eva te duḥkhahetavo na sukhasya / ante tu tad duḥkhaṃ vṛddhim āpannaṃ vyaktim āpadyata iti / 
	evam īryāpathavikalpe *pi vaktavyam / duḥkhapratīkāre dca sukhabuddher duḥkhavikalpe ca / na hi tāvat sukham iti vedyate kiñcid yāvan na duḥhāntarerṇopadruto bhavati kṣutpipāsāśītoṣṇaśramakāmarāgaprabhaveṇa / tasmāt pratīkāra evāviduṣāṃ dukhabuddhir na sukhe / duḥkhavipalpe ca bālāḥ sukhabuddhim utpādayanti, yathāṃsād aṃsaṃ" bhāraṃ saṃcārayantaḥ / tasmān nāsty eva sukham iti / 
	asty evety ābhidhārmikāḥ / eṣa eva ca nyāyaḥ / kathaṃ kṛtvā / idaṃ hi tāvad ayaṃ praṣṭavyaḥ sukhāpavādī, kim idaṃ duḥkhaṃ nāma / yad bādhanātmakaṃ" cet, katham iti vaktavyam / upaghātakaṃ cet, anugrāhakaṃ sukham iti siddham / anabhipretaṃ cet, abhipretaṃ sukham iti siddham / 
	tad eva hy abhipretaṃ punar anabhipretaṃ bhavaty āryāṇāṃ vairāgyakāle" / tasmād aniṣpannam abhipretatvaṃ cet / na, anyathānabhipretatvāt / yā hi vedanā svena lakṣaṇenābhipretā nāsau punas tenaiva jātv anabhipretā bhavati / tathā hy enām ākārāntareṇa vidūṣayanty āryāḥ pramādapadaṃ caināṃ paśyanti, mahābhisaṃskārasādhyāṃ ca vipariṇāminīṃ cānityāṃ ca yenānabhipretā bhavati / na tu khalu svalakṣaṇākāreṇa / 
	yadi cāsau svenātmanābhipretā bhaven, naiva tasyāṃ kasyacid rāgo bhaved yato vairāgyārthaṃ prakārāntareṇāpi doṣavatīṃ paśyeyuḥ / tasmād asty eva svalakṣaṇataḥ sukhā vedanā / 
	yat tu bhagavatoktam Oyat ciṃcid veditam idam atra duḥkhasyaO iti tad bhagavataiva nītārthaṃ Osaṃskārānityatām ānanda mayā saṃdhāya bhāṣitaṃ saṃskāravipariṇāmatāṃ ca yat kiṃcid veditam idam atra duḥkhasyaO iti / ato na duḥkhaduḥkhatāṃ saṃdhāyaitad uktam iti siddhaṃ bhavati / 
	yadi ca svabhāvata eva sarvaṃ veditavyaṃ duḥkham abhaviṣyat, kim artham āryānanda evaṃ bhagavantam aprakṣyat, Otisra ime vedanā uktā bhagavatā sukhā duḥkhāduḥkhāsukhā ca, uktaṃ ceddaṃ bhagavatā, yat kiṃcid veditam idam atra duḥkhasya iti, kiṃ nu saṃdhāya bhagavatā bhāṣitam, yat kiṃcid veditam idam atra duḥkhasyaO iti / 
	evaṃ hi so *prakṣyat, Okiṃ nu saṃdhāya bhagavatā bhāṣitaṃ tisro vedanāO iti / bhagavān apy evaṃ vyākariṣyat, Oidaṃ mayā saṃdhāya bhāṣitaṃ tisro vedanaO iti / na tv evam āha / tasmāt santy eva svabhāvatas tisro vedanāḥ / Oidaṃ tu saṃdhāya mayā bhāṣitaṃ yat kiṃcid veditam idam atra duḥkhasyaO iti ābhiprāyikam etad vākyaṃ darśayati / 
	yad apy uktamm Osukhā vedanā duḥkhato" draṣṭavyāO iti, ubhayaṃ tasyām asti / sukhatvaṃ ca svabhāvato manāpatvād duḥkhatvaṃ ca paryāyato vipariṇāmānityadhamitvāt" / sā tu sukhato dṛśamānā mokṣāya kalpate tadvairāgyād iti / yathā dṛśyamānā mokṣāya kalpate tathaināṃ draṣṭum ājñāpayanti buddhāḥ / katham idaṃ gamyate, svabhāvataḥ sā sukheti / yad āha 
		Osaṃskārānityatāṃ jñātvā, atho vipariṇāmatām / 
		vedanā duḥkhataḥ proktā, sambuddhena prajānatā //O iti / 
	yad api coktam Oduḥkhe sukham iti saṃjñāviparyāsaḥO ity ābhiprāyika eṣa nirdeśaḥ / lokasya hi sukhasaṃjñā vedanāyāṃ kāmaguṇeṣūpapattau ca / tatra sukhāṃ vedanāṃ paryāyeṇa duḥkhāṃ satīṃ ekāntasukhāṃ paśyato viparyāsaḥ / evam upapattim / 
	tasmān nātaḥ sukhavedanābhāvasiddhiḥ / 
	yadi tu svabhāvata eva sarvaṃ veditaṃ duḥkham abhaviṣyat, Otisro vedanāO iti vacane ko guṇo *bhaviṣyat / lokānuvṛttyeti cet, na / sarvaveditaduḥkhatvasya" saṃdhāyabhāṣitavacanāt, yathābhūtavacanāc ca / Oyac ca sukhendriyaṃ yac ca saumanasyendriyaṃ sukhaiṣā vedanā draṣṭavyāO iti vistareṇoktvā Oyenemāni pañcendriyāṇy evaṃ yathābhūtaṃ samyakprajñayā dṛṣṭāni trīṇi cāsya saṃyojanāni prahīṇāni bhavantiO ity evamādi / 
	loko *pi ca kathaṃ duḥkhāṃ vedanāṃ trividhāṃ vyavasyet / mṛduadhimātramadhyāsu yathākramaṃ sukhādibuddhir iti cet, na / sukhasyāpi trividhatvāt mṛdvādiṣu duḥkheṣv adhimātrādisukhabuddhiḥ syāt / 
	yadā ca gandharasaspraṣṭavyaviśeṣajaṃ sukhaṃ vedayate, tadā katamad duḥkhaṃ mṛdubhūtaṃ yatrāsya sukhabuddhir bhavati / anutpannavinaṣṭe ca tasmin mṛduni duḥkhe sutarāṃ sukhabuddhiḥ syād aśeṣaduḥkhāpagamāt / evaṃ kāmasukhasaṃmukhībhāve *pi vaktavyam / 
	kathaṃ ca nāmedaṃ yokṣyate" yan mṛduni vedite suvyaktas tīvro *nubhavo gṛhyate madhye punar avyakta iti / 
	triṣu ca dhyāneṣu sukhavacanād mṛdu duḥkhaṃ syāt, ūrdhvam aduḥkhāsukhavacanān madhyaṃ duḥkham / 
	iti na yujyate mṛdvādiṣu duḥkheṣu sukhādivedanāvyavasthānam / 
	uktaṃ ca bhavagavatā, Orūpaṃ cen mahānāmann ekāntaduḥkham abhaviṣyan, na sukhaṃ na sukhānugatamO ity evamādi / tasmād apy asti kiṃcit sukham / 
	evaṃ tāvan na sūtrāt sukhavedanābhāvaḥ sidhyati / 
	yat punaḥ sukhahetvavyavasthānād ity uktam, hetvaparijñānād idam ucyate / āśrayaviśeṣāpekṣo hi viṣayaḥ sukhahetur vā bhavati duḥkhahetur vā, na kevalo viṣayaḥ / sa yāṃ kāyāvasthāṃ" prāpya sukhahetur bhavati, na tāṃ punaḥ prāpya kadācin na bhavatīti vyavasthita eva sukhahetuḥ / 
	tadyathā sa evāgniḥ pākyabhūtaviśeṣāpekṣaḥ svādupākahetur bhavati, sa evāsvādupākahetuḥ", na tu yāṃ pākyabhūtāvasthāṃ prāpya svādupākahetus tāṃ punaḥ prāpya na hetur ity eṣa dṛṣṭāntaḥ / 
	dhyāneṣu ca kathaṃ na vyavasthitaḥ sukhahetuḥ / 
	yat tu punaḥ duḥkhapratīkāre sukhabuddhir ity uktaṃ tatra vihitaḥ pratīkāraḥ" / yadā gandhādiviśeṣajaṃ sukhaṃ vedayate, tadā kasya pratīkāreṣu sukhabuddhir bhavaty, anutpannavinaṣṭe ca tasmin duḥkhe sutarāṃ sukhabuddhiḥ syāt / dhyānaje sukhe kaḥ" kasy pratīkāra ity evamādi / 
	bhārasaṃcāre" *pi cāvasthāntarajaṃ sukham evotpadyate, yāvad asau tādṛśī kāyāvasthā nāntardhīyate" / anyathā hi paścād bhūyasī sukhabuddhiḥ syāt / evaṃ śrāntasyeryāpathavikalpeṣu veditavyam / 
	Oante kuto duḥkhabuddhir, ārambho yadi nāditaḥO iti cet, kāyapariṇāmaviśeṣān madyādīnām ante mādhuryaśuktatāvat / 
	tasmād asty eva sukhā vedaneti siddham / triduḥkhatāyogād vā sarvaṃ sāsravaṃ duḥkham iti / 
	yat tu samudayasatyaṃ tad eva" ucyate idam utsūtram / sūtre hi tṛṣṇaivoktā / 
	prādhānyād asau sūtra uktā, anye *pi tu samudayaḥ / katham idaṃ pratyetavyam / anyatrānyasyāpi vacanāt / uktaṃ hi bhagavatā, 
	Okarma ca tṛṣṇā ca atho avidyā saṃskārāṇāṃ hetur abhisaṃparāyaO iti / 
punaś coktam, Opañca bījajātānīti sopādānasya vijñānasyaitad adhivacanam / pṛthivīdhātur iti catasṛṇāṃ vijñānasthitīnām etad adhivacanamO iti / 
	tasmād ābhiprāyikaḥ sūtreṣu nirdeśo lākṣaṇikas tv abhharme / 
	api tv abhinirvṛttihetuṃ bruvatā samudayasatyaṃ tṛṣṇaivoktā / upapattyabhinirvṛttihetuṃ sahetukaṃ bruvatā gāthāyāṃ karama ca tṛṣṇā cāvidyoktā / Okarma hetur upapattaye, tṛṣṇā hetur abhirvṛttayeO iti sūtre vadanāt, sahetusapratyayasanidānasūtrakrameṇa vā / bījakṣetrabhāvaṃ pratipādayatā vijñānādayo *py uktāḥ / 
	kā punar upapattiḥ, kā cābhinirvṛttiḥ / #dhātugatiyonyādiprakārabhedenātmabhāvasyopapadanam upapattiḥ / abhedena punarbhavapratisaṃdhānam abhinirvṛttiḥ / tayor yathākramaṃ karma ca bhavatṛṣṇā ca hetuḥ / 
	tadyathā bījaṃ śāliyavādijātiprakārabhedenāṅkuropapadanasya hetuḥ / āpaḥ punar abhedena sarvāṅkuraprarohamātrasyety eva dṛṣṭāntaḥ / 
	tṛṣṇābhinirvṛttihetur iti kātra yuktiḥ / 
	vītatṛṣṇasya janmābhāvāt / ubhaye *pi mriyante satṛṣṇā vītatṛṣṇāś ca, satṛṣṇā eva jātā dṛśyante na vītatṛṣnā iti vinā tṛṣṇayā janmābhāvād bhavasyābhinirvṛttau tṛṣṇāṃ" hetuṃ pratīmaḥ / 
	saṃtatināmanāc ca / yatra ca satṛṣṇā tatrābhīkṣṇaṃ cittasaṃtatiṃ namantīṃ paśyāmaḥ / tasmāt punarbhave *py evam iti vyavasyāmaḥ / 
	na cātmabhāva evaṃ kenacid āgṛhīto yathā tṛṣṇayā / śuṣkamasūropasnānalepāṅgavat / na cānyo hetur evam anuṣakto yathātmasneha ity eṣā yuktiḥ / 
2.2.4	cavāry api satyāny uktāni bhagavatā, dve *pi satye saṃvṛtisatyaṃ paramārthasatyaṃ ca / tayoḥ kiṃ lakṣaṇam / 
		yatra bhinne na" tadbuddhir anyāpohe dhiyā ca tat / 
		ghaṭāmbuvat" saṃvṛtisat paramārthasad anyathā //4// 
2.2.4 a) yasmin avayavaśo bhinne na tadbuddhir bhavati, tat saṃvṛtisat, tadyathā ghaṭaḥ / tatra hi kapālaśo bhinne ghaṭabuddhir na bhavati / 
	yatra cānyān apohya dharmān buddhyā tadbuddhir na bhavati, tac cāpi saṃvṛtisad veditavyam, tadyathāmbu / tatra hi buddhyā rūpādīn dharmān apohyāmbubucchir na bhavati / 
	teṣv eva tu saṃvṛtisaṃjñā kṛteti saṃvṛtivaśāt ghaṭaś cāmbu cāstīti bruvantaḥ satyam evāhur na mṛṣety etat saṃvṛtisatyam / 
2.2.4 b)	ato *nyathā paramārthasatyam / yatra" bhinne *pi tadbuddhir bhavaty eva, anyadharmāpohe *pi buddhā tat paramārthasat, tadyathā rūpam / tatra hi paramānuśo bhinne vastuni rasādīn" api ca dharmān apohya buddhyā rūpasya svabhāvabuddhir bhavaty eva / evaṃ vedanādayo *pi draṣṭavyāḥ / etat paramārthena bhāvāt paramārthasatyam iti / 
	yathā lokottarereṇa jñānena gṛhyate, tatpṛṣṭha labdhena vā laukikena tathā paramārthasatyam, yathānyena tathā saṃvṛtisatyam iti pūrvācāryāḥ / 
	uktāni satyāni / 
3.1	kathaṃ punas teṣāṃ darśanaṃ bhavati, vaktavyam / ata ādiprasthānam ārabhyocyate / 
		vṛttasthaḥ śrutacintāvān bhāvanāyāṃ prayujyate / (5ab) 
satyānīha' draṣṭukāma ādita eva śīlam pālayati / tataḥ satyadarśanasyānulomaṃ śrutam udgṛhṇāty arthaṃ vā śṛṇoti / śrutvā cintayaty aviparītam, cintayitvā bhāvanāyāṃ prayujyate samādahau / tasya śrutamayīṃ prajñāṃ niśritya cintāmayī jāyate / cintāmayīṃ niśritya bhāvanāmayī jāyate / 
3.1.1	kim punar āsāṃ prajñānāṃ lakṣaṇam / 
		nāmobhayārthaviṣayā śrutamayyādikā dhiyaḥ //5cd // 
nāmālambanā kila śrutamayī prajñā / nāmārthālambanā cintāmayī / kadācid vyañjanenārtham ākarṣati, kadācid arthena vyañjanam / arthālambanaiva bhāvanāmayī / sā hi vyañjananirapekṣā arthe pravartate / tadyathāmbhasi plotum aśikṣitaḥ plavaṃ naiva" muñcati / kiyacchikṣitaḥ kadācid muñcet, kadācid ālambate / suśikṣitaḥ plavanirapekṣas" taratīty eṣa dṛṣṭānta iti vaibhāṣikāḥ / 
3.1.2	asyāṃ tu kalpanāyāṃ cintāmayī prajñā na sidhyatīty apare / yā hi nāmālambanā śrutamayīi prāpnoti, yārthālambanā bhavanāmayīti / 
	idaṃ tu lakṣaṇaṃ nāniravadyaṃ vidyate / āptavacanaprāmāṇyajātaniścayā śrutamayī / yuktinidhyānajaś cintāmayī / samādhijā bhāvanāmayīti / hetau mayaṭvidhānāt / tadyathānnamayāḥ prāṇāḥ, tṛṇamayyo gāva iti / 
3.2.1	tasya punar evaṃ bhāvanāyāṃ prayuktasya kathaṃ bhāvanā saṃpadyata ity āha, 
		vyapakarṣadvayavataḥ (6a) 
yadi hi kāyacittābhyāṃ vyapakṛṣṭo bhavati saṃsargākuśalavitarkadūrīkaraṇāt / 
3.2.2	tat tarhi vyapakarṣadvayaṃ kasya sukaraṃ bhavati / yo *lpecchaḥ saṃstuṣṭaś ca / 
		nāsaṃtuṣṭamahecchayoḥ / (6b) 
3.2.2 a1)	kā punar iyam asaṃstuṣṭiḥ, kā ca mahecchatā / 
		labdhe bhūyaḥspṛhātuṣṭir alabdhecchā mahecchatā //6cd // 
labdheṣu kila praṇīteṣu cīvarādiṣu bhūyas"kāmatāsaṃstuṣṭiḥ, alabdheṣu tatkāmatā mahecchatety ābhidhārmikāḥ / 
3.2.2. a2)	nanu ca sātibhūyaskāmatālabdha eva na labdhe bhavatīti ko *nayor viśeṣa iti vaktavyam etad / 
	evaṃ tu yujyate / labdhenāpraṇītenāprabhūtena paritāso" *saṃtuṣṭiḥ, alabdhapraṇītaprabhhūtecchā mahecchatā / 
3.2.2 b)	viparyāsāt tadvipakṣau (7a) 
asaṃtuṣṭimahecchatāviparyayeṇa tatpratipakṣau veditavyau, saṃtuṣṭiś cālpecchatā ceti / 
		tridhātvāptāmalau ca tau / (7b) 
tadvipakṣāv iti vartate / tridhātukau cāpratisaṃyuktau" ca sāsravānāsravatvāt /" asaṃtuṣṭimahecchate ca kāmāvacaryāv eva / 
3.2.2 c)	kaḥ punar anayor alpecchatāsaṃtuṣṭyoḥ svabhāva ity āha / 
		alobhaḥ (7c) 
alobhasvabhāve hy ete / 
3.2.3		āryavaṃśāś ca (7c) 
alobha iti vartate / āryāṇām ebhyaḥ prasavād āryavaṃśāś catvāraḥ / te *py alobhasvabhāvāḥ / 
		teṣāṃ tuṣṭyātmakās trayaḥ //7d // 
āditas trayaḥ" saṃtuṣṭisvabhāvāḥ, cīvarapiṇḍapātaśayanāsanasaṃtuṣṭayaḥ / prahāṇabhāvanārāmatā caturtha āryavaṃśaḥ / katham alobhasvabhāvaḥ / bhavakāmarāgavaimukhyāt / 
3.2.3 a1)	atha caturbhir āryavaṃśaiḥ kiṃ darśitaṃ bhagavatā / 
		karmāntena tribhir vṛttiḥ (8a) 
dharmasvāminā hi bhagavatā parityaktasvavṛttikarmāntebhyaḥ śiṣyebhyo mokṣārtham abhyupagatebhyo dvayaṃ prajñaptaṃ vṛttiś ca karma ca / tribhir āryavaṃśair vṛttiś caturthena karma / Oanayā vṛttyedaṃ karma kurvāṇā bhavanto nacirān mokṣaṃ prāpsyantiO iti / 
3.2.3 a2)	kasmāt punar iyam īdṛśī vṛttir idaṃ ca karma prajñaptam / 
		tṛṣṇotpādavipakṣataḥ / 
catvāras tṛṣṇotpādaḥ sūtra uktāḥ / Ocīvarahetor bhikṣos tṛṣṇotpadyamāṇotpadyate, pratitiṣṭantī pratitiṣṭhati, abhiniviśamānābhiniviśate, piṇḍapātahetoḥ śayyāsanahetor" itibhavavibhavahetor bhikṣos tṛṣṇotpadyamānotpadyateO iti vistaraḥ / eṣāṃ pratipakṣeṇa catvāra āryavaṃśā deśitāḥ / 
3.2.3 b)		mamāhaṃkāravastvicchā 
						tatkālātyantaśantaye //8cd // 
sa evārthaḥ punaḥ paryāyeṇocyate." mamakāravastu cīvarādayo *haṃkāravastv ātmabhāvaḥ / tatrecchā tṛṣṇā / tatra mamakāravastvicchāyās tatkālaśantaye traya āryavaṃśā bhavanti, ubhayecchātyantaśāntaye caturtha iti / 
3.3	uktam idaṃ yathābhūtasya" bhāvanā saṃpadyate / tasya ev evaṃ pātrībhūtasya kathaṃ tasyāṃ bhāvanāyām avatāro bhavati / 
		tatrāvatāro *śubhayā cānāpānasmṛtena ca / 
smṛtir eva smṛtam / 
	keṣāṃ punar aśubhayā keṣām ānāpānasmṛtyā / yathā kramam, 
		adhirāgavitarkāṇām (9c) 
adhiko rāgo vitarkaś caiṣām, ta ime adhirāgavitarkāḥ / yo hi pratyāsannam atyarthaṃ rāgacaritas tasyāśubhayā, yo hi vitarkacaritas tasyānāpānasmṛtyeti / 
	avicitrālambanatvād eṣā" vitarkopacchedāya saṃvartata ity eke / aśubhā tu varṇa"saṃsthānaviśeṣālambanatvād vitarkam āvahatīti / 
	abahirmukhatvād ity apare / aśubhā hi cakṣurvijñānavad bahirmukhī tadviṣayopanidhyānāt / 
3.3.1	tatra punaś caturvidho rāgaḥ, varṇarāgaḥ saṃsthānarāgaḥ sparśarāga upacārarāgaś ca / prathamasya pratipakṣeṇa vinīlakādyālambanām aśubhāṃ varṇayanti,", dvitīyasya vikhāditakavikṣiptālambanām, tṛtīyasya vipaṭumaka"pūyanibaddhāsthyālambanām, caturthasya niśceṣṭamṛtakāyālambanām / 
	abhedena tu śasyate 
		śaṃkalā sarvarāgiṇām //9d // 
asthiśaṃkalāyāṃ hi sarvam etac caturvidhaṃ rāgavastu" nāstīti / 
	adhimuktiprādeśikamanaskāratvād aśubhayā na kleśaprahāṇaṃ viṣkambhaṇaṃ tu / 
3.3.1 a)	sa punar ayam aśubhāṃ bhavayan yogācāras trividha ucyate, ādikarmikaḥ kṛtparijayo *tikrāntamanaskāraś ca / 
3.3.1 a1)	tatra, 
		āsamudrāsthivistārasaṃkṣepād ādikarmikaḥ / (10ab) 
aśubhāṃ bhāvayitukāma ādito yogācāraḥ svāṅgāvayave cittaṃ nibadhnāti pādāṅguṣṭhe lalāṭe yatra vāsyābhiratiḥ" / sa tatra māṃsakledapūty"adhimokṣakrameṇāsthi viśodhayan sakalām asthiśaṃkalāṃ paśyati / tathaiva ca punar dvitīyām adhimucyate, yāvad vihārārāmakṣetrakrameṇa samudraparyantāṃ pṛthivīm asthiśaṃkalāpūrṇām" adhimucyate *dhimokṣābhivardhanārtham / punaś ca saṃkṣipan yāvad ekām eva svām asthiśaṃkalām adhimucyate cittasaṃkṣepārtham / 
	iyatā kila kālenāśubhā pariniṣpannā bhavati / ayam ādikarmiko yogācāraḥ / 
3.3.1 a2)		pādāsthna ā kapālārdhatyāgāt kṛtajayaḥ smṛtaḥ //10cd // 
sa punaḥ cittasaṃkṣepaviśeṣārthaṃ tasyām asthiśaṃkalāyāṃ pādāstīni hitvā śeṣaṃ manasikaroti / evaṃ krameṇa yāvat kapālasyārdhaṃ hitvārdhaṃ manasikaroti / ayaṃ" kṛtaparijayaḥ / 
3.3.1 a3)		atikrāntamanaskāro bhrūmadhye cittadhāraṇāt / (11ab) 
so *rdham api kapālasya muktvā bhruvor madhye cittaṃ dhārayati / ayaṃ kilāśubhāyām atikrāntamanaskāro yogācāraḥ / 
3.3.1 a4)	asty aśubhālambanaparīttatayā parīttā, na vaśitāparīttatayā parītteti catuṣkoṭikam / jitājitamanaskārayor ajitajitamanaskārayoś ca svakāyasamudraparantālambanāt / 
3.3.1 b)	atha kiṃsvabhāveyam aśubhā katibhūmikā kimālambanā kva cotpadyate / yathākramam, 
		alobho daśabhūḥ kāmadṛśyālamba nṛjāśubhā //11cd // 
	alobhasvabhāvā / 
	daśabhūmikā sasāmantakadhyānāntareṣu caturṣu dhyāneṣu kāmadhātau ca / 
	kāmāvacaradṛśyālambanā / kiṃ punar dṛśyuam / varṇasaṃsthāne" / ata evārthālambaneti siddham / 
	manuṣyeṣuv evotpadyate, nānyasyāṃ gatau, kuta eva dhātau / tatrāpi nottarakurau / 
	nāmnaiva siddham aśubhākāreti / 
	yadadhvikā tadadhvālambanā / anutpattidharmiṇī tu tryadhvālambanā / 
	adhimuktimanaskāratvāt sāsravā / 
	vairāgyalābhikī ca prāyogikī ca, ucitānucitatvāt / 
	uktam aśubhāyāḥ saprabhedaṃ lakṣaṇam / 
3.3.2		ānāpānasmṛtiḥ prajñā pañcabhūr vāyugocarā" / 
		kāmāśrayā (12abc) 
ānanam āna āśvāso yo vāyuḥ praviśati / apānanam apānaḥ praśvāso yo vāyuḥ niṣkrāmati / tayoḥ smṛtir ānāpānasmṛtiḥ / 
3.3.2 a)	saiva prajñāsvabhāvā / smṛtivacanaṃ tu smṛtyupasthānavad tadbalādhānavṛttitvāt / 
	pañcasu bhūmiṣu, sāmantakeṣu dhyānāntare kāmadhātau copekṣāsaṃprayogitvāt / vitarkānuguṇatvāt kila sukhaduḥkhayos tatpratipakṣasya tābhyām asaṃprayogaḥ, sukhasaumanasyayoś cāvadhānaparipanthitvāt tasyāś cāvadhāne sādhyatvād iti / ye tu mauleṣv api dhyāneṣu samāpannasyopekṣām icchanti, teṣām aṣṭabhūmikā, pareṇāśvāsapraśvāsānām abhūmitvāt / 
	vāyvālambanā caiṣā / 
	kāmadhātvāśrayā devamanuṣyeṣu / 
	prāyogikī vairāgyalābhikī ca / 
	tattvamanaskāraś caiṣā / 
	idaṃdharmāṇām" eva 
		na bāhyānām (12c) 
upadeśābhāvāt, sarvaṃ ca sūkṣmadharmān abhisaṃbodhāt / 
3.3.2 b)	sāceyaṃ 
		ṣaḍvidhā gaṇanādibhiḥ //12d // 
ṣaṭkāraṇayuktā caiṣā paripūrṇā bhavati / gaṇanayānugamena sthāpanayhopalakṣaṇaya vivarttena pariśuddhyā ca / 
3.3.2 b1)	tatra ca gaṇanā nāmāśvāsapraśvāseṣu cittaṃ dattvānabhisaṃskāreṇa kāyaṃ cittaṃ cādhyupekṣya smṛtimātreṇa gaṇayaty ekaṃ dvau yāvad daśa / cittābhisaṃkṣepavikṣepabhayān nālpabahutarā / 
	tasyāṃ tu trayo doṣāḥ / ūnagaṇanā yadi dvāv ekaṃ gṛhṇāti / adhikagaṇanā yady ekaṃ dvāv iti / saṃkaro yady āśvāsaṃ praśvāsato gṛhṇāti viparyayād vā / ato *nyathā samyaggaṇanā / 
	antarāvikṣepe punar ādito gaṇayitavyaṃ tāvad yāvat samādhiṃ labhate / 
 3.3.2 b2)	anugamo nāmānabhisaṃskāreṇāśvāsapraśvāsānāṃ gatim anugacchati, kiyaddūram ete praviśanti vā niṣkrāmanti vā, kim ete sarvaśarīravyāpina ekadeśacāriṇa iti / tān praviśataḥ kaṇṭhahṛdayanābhkaṭyūru"jaṅghāpraveśakrameṇa yāvat pādāv anugacchati, niṣkrāmato vitastivyāmāntaram / yāvad vāyumaṇḍalaṃ vairambhāś ca vāyava ity apare / tad etat tattvamanasikāratvān na yuktam / 
3.3.2 b3)	sthāpanā nāma nāsikāgre yāvat pādāṅguṣṭhe sthitān paśyati maṇisūtravat / kim anugrāhakā eta upaghātakāḥ" śītā uṣṇā iti" / 
3.3.2 b4)	upalakṣaṇā nāma naite kevalā vāyava eva, catvāry etāni mahābhūtāni mahābhūtābhinirvṛttam upādāyarūpaṃ tadāśritāś cittacaittā iti pañcaskandhān upalakṣayati /

3.4.1	kathaṃ ca punaḥ kuryāt /

3.4.2 a)	atha smṛtyupasthānānāṃ kaḥ svabhāvaḥ / trividhaṃ" smṛtyupasthānaṃ, svabhāvasaṃsargālambanasmṛtyupasthānam /

	smṛtyudrekatvād iti vaibhāṣikāḥ / smṛtibalādhānavṛttitvād ity arthaḥ" / dārupāṭanakīlasaṃdhāraṇavat / 
3.4.3 b)	evaṃ tu yujyate / smṛtir anayopatiṣṭḥata iti smṛty upasthānaṃ prajñā yathādṛṣṭasyābhilapanāt / 
	tathā" hy uktaṃ āyuṣmatāniruddhena, Otasya kāye kāyānupaśyino viharataḥ kāyālambanānusmṛtis tiṣṭhati, saṃtiṣṭhateO iti vistaraḥ / bhagavatāpi coktam, Otasya kāye kāyānupaśyino viharata upasthitā smṛtir bhabaty asaṃmūḍāO iti / 
	yatra tūktam, Okathaṃ bhikṣavaś caturṇāṃ smṛtyupasthānānāṃ samudayaś ca bhavaty astaṃgamaś ca / āhārasamudayāt kāyasya samudayo bhavaty āhāranirodhāt kāyasyāstāṃgamaḥO ity atrālambanam" eva smṛtyupasthānam uktam, smṛtir atropatiṣṭhata iti kṛtvā / 
3.4.4	yathālambanaṃ caiṣāṃ nāma / svaparobhayasaṃtatyālambanatvāt pratyekam eṣāṃ traividhyam / 
3.4.5 a)	kramaḥ / 
		yathotpatti (15bc) 
	kasmāt punar evam utpattiḥ / audārikasya pūrvaṃ darśanāt / yato vā kāmarāgasya kāyo *dhiṣṭhānaṃ sa ca vedanābhilāṣāt sa ca cittasyādāntatvāt tatkleśāprahāṇād iti vaibhāṣikāḥ / 
3.4.5 b)		catuṣkaṃ tu viparyāsavipakṣataḥ //15cd // 
śucisukhanityātmaviparyāsānāṃ caturṇāṃ pratipakṣeṇa catvāri smṛtyupasthānāny uktāni yathākramaṃ nādhikanyūnāni / 
3.4.6	eṣāṃ" ca trīṇy asaṃbhinnālambanāni / caturtham ubhayathā, yadi dharmān eva paśyaty asaṃbhinnālambanam, atha kāyādīnāṃ dve trīṇi catvāri vā samastāni paśyati saṃbhinnālambanam / 
3.5	evaṃ kāyādyālambanāni smṛtyupasthānāny abhyasya, 
		sa dharmasmṛtyupasthāne samastālambane sthitaḥ / 
		anityaduḥkhataḥ śūnyānātmatas tān vipaśyati //16// 
saṃbhinnālambane dharmasmṛtyupasthāne sthitas tān kāyādīn sarvān abhisamasya caturbhir ākāraiḥ paśyati, anityato duḥkhataḥ śūnyato *nātmataś ca / 
3.6.1		tata ūṣmagatotpattiḥ (17a) 
tasmād dharmasmṛtyupasthānād evamabhyastāt krameṇoṣmagataṃ nāma kuśalamūlam utpadyate / ūṣmagatam ivoṣmagatam / kleśendhanadahanasyāryamārgāgneḥ pūrvarūpatvāt / 
		tac catuḥsatyagocaram / 
tad ūṣmagataṃ prākarṣikatvāc catuḥsatyālambanam / 
		ṣoḍaśākāram (17c) 
duḥkhaṃ caturbhir ākāraiḥ paśyaty anityato duḥkhataḥ śūnyato *nātmataś ca / samudayaṃ caturbhir hetutaḥ samudayataḥ prabhavataḥ pratyayataś ca / nirodhaṃ caturbhir nirodhataḥ śāntataḥ praṇītato niḥsaraṇataś ca / mārgaṃ caturbhir mārgato nyāyataḥ pratipattito nairyāṇikataś ca / eṣāṃ tu viśeṣaṇaṃ paścād vakṣyāmaḥ / 
3.6.2 a)		ūṣmabhyo, mūrdhānaḥ (17cd) 
mṛdumadhyādhimātrakramābhivṛddhebhya ūṣmabhyo mūrdhāna utpadyante / 
		te *pi tādṛśāḥ //17d // 
yādṛśā ūṣmāṇaś catuḥsatyālambanāḥ ṣoḍaśākārāś ca / utkṛṣṭataratvāt tu nāmāntaram / calakuśalamūlamūrdhatvād mūrdhānaḥ / ebhyo hi pāto *tikramo vā / 
3.6.2 b)		ubhayākaraṇaṃ dharmeṇa (18a) 
eṣāṃ punar ubhayeṣām ūṣmagatamūrdhnāṃ dharmasmṛtyupasthānenākaraṇam / kim idam ākaraṇam / satyeṣv ākārāṇāṃ prathamato vinyasanam / 
		anyair api tu vardhanam / (18b) 
caturbhir api smṛtyupasthānair eṣāṃ vardhanam / vivardhayataḥ pūrvapratilabdhānām asaṃmukhībhāvo *bahumānatvāt / 
3.6.3	mṛdumadhyādhimātrakramābhivṛddhebhyaḥ punar utpadyate / 
		tebhyaḥ kṣāntiḥ (18c) 
adhimātraṃ satyakṣamaṇād" aparihāṇitaḥ / sāpi triprakārā mṛdvī madhyā *dhimātrā ca / 
3.6.3 a)		dvidhā tadvat (18c)

		kṣāntyā dharmeṇa vardhanam //18// 
dharma"smṛtyupasthānenaiva nānyena / 
3.6.3 b)		kāmāptaduḥkhaviṣayā tv adhimātrā (19ab) 
kṣāntir iti vartate / agradharmasaṃśleṣād asau kāmāvacaraduḥkhālambanaiva / ata evoṣmagatādīnāṃ traidhātukaduḥkhādyālambanatvasiddhir niyamāvacanāt / 
3.6.3 c)	yadā kila rūpārūpyapratipakṣādyekaikasatyākārālambanāpahrāsena" yāvat kāmāvacaram" eva duḥkhaṃ dvābhyāṃ kṣaṇābhyāṃ manasikaroty eṣā sarvaiva madhyā kṣāntiḥ / yadaikam eva kṣaṇaṃ tad" adhimātreti /

kṣaṇikā cāsau na prākarṣikī / 
3.6.4		tathāgradharmāḥ (19c) 
yathaivādhimātrā kṣāntiḥ / te *pi kāmāvacaraduḥkhālambanāḥ kṣaṇikāś ca / laukikāś caite *grāś ca dharmāḥ, sarvalaukikaśreṣṭhatvād iti laukikāgradharmāḥ / vinā sabhāgahetunā mārgasya tatpuruṣakāreṇākarṣaṇāt / 
3.6.5	ta eta ūṣmagatādayaḥ smṛtyupasthānasvabhāvatvāt prajñātmakā ucyante /

saparvāragrahaṇāt / 
		vināptibhiḥ //19d //

3.6.6 a)	tatra trisatyālambanoṣmagatākaraṇe dharmasmṛtyupasthānaṃ pratyutpannam, anāgatāni catvāri bhāvyante / nirodhasatyālambane tad evobhayathā / sarvatrākārāḥ sabhāgāḥ / 
	vivardhane caturṇām anyatamat, anāgatāni catvāri / tatraiva nirodhālambane *ntyam, anāgatāni catvāri / ākārāḥ sarve, labdhatvād gogrāṇām / 
3.6.6 b)	mūrdhākaraṇe catuḥsatyālambane *pi nirodhālambanavardhane cāntyam, anāgatāni catvāri / ākārāḥ sarve / 
	trisatyālambanavivardhane" caturṇām anyatamat, anāgatāni catvāri / ākārāḥ sarve / 
3.6.6 c)	kṣāntīnāṃ sarvatra cāntyam, anāgatāni catvāri / ākārāḥ sarve /

3.6.7		iti nirvedhabhāgīyaṃ, caturdhā (20ab) 
ity etāni catvāri nirvedhabhāgīyāni kuśalamūlāni yad utoṣmagataṃ mūrdhānaḥ kṣāntayo *gradharmāś ca / eṣāṃ cve mṛdūni calatvāt parihāṇitaḥ, kṣāntayo madhyam, agradharmā adhimātram /" 
	nirvedhabhāgīyānīti ko *rthaḥ / vidha vibhāge / niścito vedho nirvedha āryamārgaḥ, tena vicikitsāprahāṇāt satyānāṃ ca vibhajanād idaṃduḥkham ayaṃ yāvat mārga iti / tasya bhāgo darśanamārgaikadeśaḥ / tasyāvāhakatvena hitatvān nirvedhabhāgīyāni / 
3.6.8 a)	tac caitac caturvidham api nirvedhabhāgīyam, 
		bhāvanāmayam / (20b) 
na śrutacintāmayam / 
3.6.8 b1)		anāgamyāntaradhyānabhūmikam (20cd)

3.6.8 b2)	teṣāṃ rūpadhātau pañcaskandhako vipākaḥ / paripūrakāṇy eva nākṣepakāṇi bhavadveṣitvāt /

		kāmāśrayāṇi (21a)

3.6.9 a)	kathaṃ nirvedhabhāgīyānāṃ tyāgaḥ /

3.6.9 b)		anāryas tu mṛtyunā //21d // 
pṛthagjanas tu nikāyasabhāgatyāgenaiva tyajati saty asati vā bhūjisaṃcāre / 
		ādye dve parihāṇyā ca (22a) 
tyajati mṛtyunā ca pṛthagjana eva / āryasya tu nāsti tābhyāṃ parihāṇiḥ / kṣāntyagradharmābhyāṃ tu pṛthagjanasyāpi nāsti parihāṇiḥ / 
3.6.9 c)		maules tatraiva satyadṛk / (22b) 
yo mauladhyānabhūmikāni nirvedhabhāgīyāny utpādayati, sa tatraiva janmani satyāny avaśyaṃ paśyati, tīvrasaṃvegatvāt / 
3.6.9 d)		apūrvāptir vihīneṣu (22c) 
yadā vihīneṣu punarlābho bhavaty apūrvāṇy eva tadā labhyante na pūrvaṃ tyaktāni, prātimokṣa"saṃvaravad, anucitayatnasādhyatvāt / 
3.6.9 e)	sati pratisīmādaiśike pareṇotpādayaty asati mūlād eva / 
3.6.9 )	ete punar vihāniparihāṇī kiṃsvabhāve / 
		hāṇī dve *samanvitiḥ //22d // 
ubhe *pi ete *samanvāgamasvabhāve / parihāṇis tu doṣakṛtā hāvaśyaṃ vihāṇiḥ / guṇaviśeṣakṛtā ca sā /" 
3.6.10 a)	parihīṇo *py ūṣmagatalābhī niyataṃ parinirvāṇadharmā bhavati / mokṣabhāgīyāt ko viśeṣaḥ / satyadarśanāsannataratvam asaty antarāye / 
3.6.10 b)		mūrdhalābhī na mūlacchit (23a)

3.6.10 c)		kṣāntilābhy anapāyagaḥ / (23b) 
vihīnāyām api kṣāntau na punar apāyān yāti tadgatika"karmakleśadūrīkaraṇāt / 
	kṣāntilābhād eva hi gatiyonyupapattyāśrayāṣṭamādi"bhavakleśānāṃ keṣāṃcid anutpattidharmatāṃ " pratilabhate, apāyagatīnām aṇḍajasaṃsvedajayonyor asaṃjñisattvotarakurumahābrahmopapattīnāṃ ṣaṇḍhapaṇḍakobhayavyañjanāśrayāṇām aṣṭamādibhavānāṃ darśanaheyakleśānāṃ ca / tāṃ tu yathāyogaṃ mṛdvadhimātrāyāṃ ca, mṛdvyām apāyagatīnām adhimātrāyām itareṣām /" 
3.6.11 a)	nirvedhabhāgīyāni trigotrāṇi śrāvakādigotrabhedāt / tatra, 
		śiṣyagotrtād vivartya" dve buddhaḥ syāt (23cd) 
ūṣmagataṃ mūrdhānaṃ ca śrāvakagotrād utpannaṃ vyāvartya punar buddhaḥ syād ity asti saṃbhavaḥ / kṣāntau tu labdhāyāṃ nāsty eva saṃbhavaḥ / kiṃ kāraṇam / apāyānāṃ kila vyāvṛttatvāt, bodhisattvāś ca parahitakriyāpāratantryād apāyān apy avagāhanta iti / tasyaiva tu gotrasyāvivartyatvād asaṃbhavaḥ / 
		trīṇy apītaraḥ //23d // 
vivartya syād iti vartate / trīṇy api nirvedhabhāgīyāni śrāvakagotrād vyāvartya buddhād itaraḥ", syāt, pratyekabuddha ity arthaḥ / 
3.6.11 b)	prātyekabuddhagotrāṇi tu vyāvartayitum aśakyāni" / 
		ā bodheḥ sarvam ekatra dhyāne *ntye" śāstṛkhaḍgayoḥ / (24ab) 
śāstā buddhaḥ khaḍgaviṣāṇakalpaḥ pratyekabuddhaḥ / tayor ekatraivāsane caturtham eva dhyānaṃ niśrityāniñjya"paṭusamādhitvān nirvedhabhāgīyāny ārabhya yāvad bodhir utpadyate / kṣayānutpādajñāne hi bodhir iti paścād upapādayiṣyāmaḥ / 
	aśubhām ārabhya yāvad bodhir ity apare / 
	yeṣāṃ tu khaḍgād" anyo *pi pratyekabuddho *sti tadgotrāṇāṃ vyāvartanāpratiṣedhaḥ" / 
3.6.12 a)	kiṃ punaḥ prathama eva janmani kṛtayogo nirvedhabhāgīyāny utpādayet / naitad asti / avaśyaṃ hi, 
		prāk tebhyo mokṣabhāgīyam (25c) 
utādayitavyam / 
3.6.12 b)	sarvasvalpaṃ hi 
		kṣipraṃ mokṣas tribhir bhavaiḥ //24d // 
ekasmin janmani mokṣabhāgīyaṃ kuśalamūlam utpādayet / dvitīye nirvedhabhāgīyāni / tṛtīye āryamārgam / bījāvaropaṇaśasyābhiṛddhiphalotpattikramavat / krameṇa hi santānasyāsyāṃ dharmatāyām avatāraparipākavimuktayo bhavantīti //

		śrutacintāmayam (25a) 
na bhāvanāmayam /

		trīṇi karmāṇi, (25a) 
prādhānyena tu manaskārma / tatpraṇidhānaparigrahaṇāt tu kāyavākkarmāpi mokṣabhāgīyaṃ bhavati / kaścid ekabhikṣām api dattvā, ekaśikṣām api cādāya mokṣābhilāṣabalādhānān mokṣabhāgīyāny ākṣipati /

		ākṣipyate nṛṣu / (25b)

4.	uktaṃ prasaṅgena mokṣabhāgīyam / abhisamayakramas tu vaktum ārabdhaḥ / tatra ca yāvad agradharmā uktāḥ, śeṣaṃ vaktavyam / 
4.1.1	ata idam ucyate / 
		laukikebhyo *gradharmebhyo, 
		dharmakṣāntir anāsravā //25cd // 
laukekāgradharmānantaram anāsravā" dharmajñānakṣāntir utpadyate / kasminn ālambane / 
		kāmaduḥkhe (26a) 
kāmāvacaraduḥkham asyā ālambanam / seyaṃ duḥkhe dharmajñānakṣāntir ity ucyate / anāsravajñāpanārthaṃ niḥṣyandena viśeṣaṇam / dharma"jñānārthaṃ kṣāntiḥ, dharmajñānakṣāntiḥ", puṣpaphalavṛkṣavat / 
4.1.1 a)	saiva ca niyāmāvakrāntir ity ucyate, samyaktvaniyāmāvakramaṇāt / samyaktvaṃ nirvāṇam uktaṃ sūtre / tatra niyamo niyāma ekāntībhāvaḥ / tasyābhigamanam avakramaṇam / 
4.1.1 b)	tasyāṃ cotpannāyām āryapudgala ucyate / anāgatayā pṛthagjanatvaṃ vyāvartyate / etad eva tasyāḥ kāritram anāgatāyām abhyupagamyate, nānyat, pradīpajātivat / 
	laukikāgradharmair ity apare / na, taddharmatvāt / tadvirodhitvād adoṣaḥ, śatruskandhārūḍhatadghātanavat / 
	ubhayair ity apare, ānantaryavimuktimārgasādharmyād iti / 
4.1.2		tato *traiva, dharmajñānaṃ (26ab) 
tataḥ punar duḥkhe dharmajñānakṣānter anantaram atraiva kāmāvacare duḥkhe dharmajñānam utpadyate / tad duḥkhe dharmajñānam ity ucyate / anāsravādhikāraḥ sarvatra veditavyaḥ / 
4.1.3	yathā ca kāmāvacare duḥkhe dharmajñānakṣāntir dharmajñānaṃ cotpadyate, 
		tathā punaḥ / 
		śeṣe duḥkhe *nvayakṣāntijñāne (26bcd) 
duḥkhe dharmajñānānantaraṃ rūpārūpyāvacare duḥkhe samastālambanānvayajñānakṣāntir utpadyate / sā duḥkhe *nvayajñānakṣāntir ity ucyate / 
4.1.4	tato *nvayajñānam utpadyate / tad duḥkhe *nvayajñānam ity ucyate / 
	prathamato dharmatattvajñānād dharma jñānam / tadanvayād ūrdhvaṃ duḥkhālambanam anvayajñānam, tathaivānugamāt" / 
4.1.5	yathā caitāni duḥkhasatye catvāri kṣāntijñānāny utpadyante, 
		satyatraye tathā //26d // 
duḥkhānvayajñānād anantaraṃ kāmāvacare samudaye dharmajñānakṣāntir utpadyate / tataḥ samudaye dharmajñānam / evaṃ samanantorotpattikrameṇa śeṣe samudaye *nvayajñānakṣāntiḥ, samudaye *nvayajñānam, kāmāvacaraduḥkhanirodhe dharmajñānakṣāntiḥ , nirodhe dharmajñānam ,eśeṣe nirodhe *jñvayajñānakṣāntiḥ , nirodhe *nvayajñānam, kāmāvacaraduḥkhapratipakṣamārge dharmajñānakṣāntiḥ, mārge dharmajñānam, śeṣe mārge *nvayajñānakṣāntiḥ , mārge *nvayajñānam / 
4.2		iti ṣoḍaśacitto *yaṃ satyābhisamayaḥ (27ab) 
ity anena krameṇāyaṃ satyānām abhisamayaḥ ṣoḍaśacittako bhavati / 
4.2.1	ye tarhi nikāyāntarīyāḥ satyānām ekābhisamayaṃ varṇayanti, abhiprāya eṣa draṣṭavyaḥ / abhedena hy abhisamaya ucyate /" 
		tridhā / 
		darśanālambakāryākhyaḥ (27bc) 
darśanābhisamayo *nāsravayā prajñayā satyānām /alambanābhisamayas tatsaṃprayuktair vedanādibhir" api / kāryābhisamayo viprayuktair api śīlajāty ādibhiḥ / duḥkhe hi dṛśyamāne tasya trividho *bhisamayaḥ, samudayādīnāṃ kārbyābhisamayaḥ prahāṇasākṣātkaraṇabhāvanāt / 
4.2.1 a)	tad yadi satyānāṃ darśanābhisamayaṃ praty ekābhisamayaṃ brūyāt, ayuktaṃ brūyād ākārabhedāt / 
4.2.1 b)	athāpy anātmākāreṇa sarveṣāṃ darśanam iti bvrūyāt, na tarhi satyānāṃ duḥkhādito darśanaṃ syāt / evaṃ ca sūtravirodhaḥ, Oihāryaśrāvakasya" duḥkhaṃ vā duḥkhato manasikurvataḥ samudayaṃ vā samudayato yāvad mārgaṃ vā mārgato manasikurvato *nāsraveṇa manasikāreṇa saṃprayukto yo" dharmāṇāṃ pravicayaḥO iti / 
	bhāvanāmārgam evam iti cet / na, yathādarśanaṃ bhāvanāt / 
4.2.1 c)	athāpy ekasya darśanāc cheṣeṣu vaśitvalābhād ekābhisamayaṃ brūyān na doṣaḥ syāt / antarā tu vyutthānam asti nāstīti vicāryaṃ syāt / 
	atha punar brūhāt, duḥkham eva parijānan samudayaṃ prajahāti nirodhaṃ sākṣātkaroti mārgaṃ bhāvayati, ata ekābhisamaya iti / evam api na doṣaḥ syād ekasya darśane śeṣāṇāṃ kāryābhisamayavacanāt / 
4.2.1 d)	darśanābhisamayaṃ tu prati sūtre satyānāṃ krameṇābhisamaya ukto lakṣyate / Ona haiva gṛhapate satyānām ekābhisamayo *py anupūrvābhisamayaḥ"O iti vistarreṇa sadṛṣṭāntāni trīṇi sūtrāṇi / 
	Oyo duḥkhe niṣkāṅkṣo nirvicikitso buddhe *pi saḥO iti sūtrād ekābhamaya iti cet / na, asamudācārāvaśyaṃprahāṇābhisaṃdhivacanāt / 
4.2.2	ya eṣa uktaḥ ṣoḍaśacittako *bhisamayaḥ, 
		so *gradharmaikabhūmikaḥ //27d // 
yadbhūmiko *gradharmas tadbhūmikāny etāni ṣoḍaśacittāni / te punaḥ ṣaḍbhūmikā ity uktaṃ prāk / 
4.3	kasmāt punaḥ kṣāntayo jñānāni cāvaśyaṃ bhavanti / yasmāt, 
		kṣāntijñānāny anantarya"muktimārgā yathākramam / (28ab) 
ānantaryamārgāḥ" kṣāntayaḥ kleśaprāptivicchedaṃ praty antarayitum aśakyatvāt / vimuktimārgās tu jñānāni kleśaprāptivimuktān visaṃyogaprāptisahotpādāt / ata ubhayair avaśyaṃ bhavitavyam / dvābhyāṃ cauraniṣkāsanakapāṭapidhānavat / 
4.3.1	yadi punar dvitīyenānantaryamārgeṇaiva saha visaṃyogaprāptir utpadyeta, kiṃ syāt / prahīṇavicikitsaṃ jñānaṃ tatraivālambane notpannaṃ syāt / 
	kṣāntibhiḥ kleśaprahāṇāt Onava saṃyojananikāyā jñānabadhyāḥO iti śāstravirodha iti cet / na, kṣāntīnāṃ jñānaparivāratvāt / rājaparivārakṛtasya rājakṛtavyapadeśavat / 
4.4	kiṃ punaḥ sarvāṇi ṣoḍaśacittāni satyadarśanād darśanamārgaḥ / nety āha / kiṃ tarhi / 
		adṛṣṭadṛṣṭer dṛṅmārgas tatra pañcadaśa kṣaṇāḥ //28cd // 
duḥkhadharmajñānakṣāntim ārabhya yāvad mārge *nvayajñānakṣāntir ete pañcadaśa kṣaṇā darśanamārgaḥ / kiṃ kāraṇam / adṛṣṭasatyadarśanāt / ṣoḍaśe tu nāsty apūrvaṃ draṣṭavyam" iti yathādṛṣṭābhyasanād bhāvanāmārga eva / 
4.4.1 a)	nanu ca tenāpy adṛṣṭaṃ paśyati mārge *nvayajñānakṣāntim / satyaṃ prati cintā na kṣaṇam / na hi kṣaṇenādṛṣṭena satyam adṛṣṭaṃ bhavati / yathā naikena luṅgenālūnena kedāram alūnaṃ bhavati / 
4.4.1 b)	phalatvād aṣṭajñānaṣoḍaśākārabhāvanāt pūrvamārgavihāneḥ prābandhikatvāc ca mārgānvayajñānaṃ bhāvanāmārgaḥ / aparihāṇis tu darśanaheyakleśa" prahāṇasaṃdhāraṇāt / 
4.4.1 c)	ata eva darśanamārga iti cet / na, atiprasaṅgāt / 
	sapta jñānāni kasmād darśanamārgaḥ / darśanasyāsamāptatvāt / na hi sarvaṃ satyadarśanaṃ samāptam iti tadantarālatvāt tāny api darśanamārgaḥ / 
4.5	uktaṃ yathā darśanamārgo bhāvanāmārgaś cotpadyate / yathedānīm utpannāryamārgāṇāṃ pudgalānāṃ vyavasthānaṃ tathā vakṣyāmaḥ / ya ete darśanamārgasvabhāvāḥ pañcadaśa kṣaṇā uktāḥ, veditavyau, 
		mṛdutīkṣṇendriyau teṣu śraddhādharmānusāriṇau / (29ab) 
mṛdvindriyas teṣu vartamānaḥ śraddhānusārīty ucyate, tīkṣṇendriyo dharmānusārīiti / śraddhayānusāraḥ śraddānusāraḥ / so *syāstīti śraddānusārī / śraddhayānusartuṃ śīlam asyeti vā / pūrvaṃ parasaṃpratyayenārthānusaraṇāt / evaṃ dharmānusārī / pūrvaṃ svayam eva sūtrādibhir dharmair arthānusaraṇāt / 
4.5.1	tau punaḥ, 
		ahīnabhāvanāheyau phalādyapratipannakau //29cd // 
phalānām ādyaṃ srotāapattiphalaṃ sarvaphalaprāptau tasya prathamatvāt / tāv eva śraddhādharmānusāriṇau yadi pūrvaṃ laukikena mārgeṇāprahīṇabhāvanāheyau bhavataḥ sakalsbandhanau tau srotāapattiphalapratipannakāv ucyete / 
		yāvat pañcaprakāraghnau (30a) 
yadi pūrvaṃ laukikena mārgeṇa kāmāvacarāṇāṃ bhavanāheyānāṃ yāvat pañcaprakārāḥ prahīṇā bhavanti, tathaiva prathamaphalapratipannakāv ucyete / 
4.5.2		dvitīye *rvāṅ navakṣayāt / 
dvitīyanimittaṃ dvitīye / yadi tayos tasmāt pareṇa ṣaṭ saptāṣṭau vā prakārāḥ pūrvaṃ prahīṇā bhavanti, dvau tau dvitīyaphalapratipannakāv ucyete / katamac ca dvitīyam / sakṛtdāgāmiphalam / 
4.5.3		kāmād viraktāv ūrdhvaṃ vā tṛtīyapratipannakau //30cd / 
yadi punar navamasyāpi prakārasya prahāṇāt kāmadhātor vītarāgau bhavata ūrdhvaṃ vā yāvad ākiñcanyāyatanāt tau tṛtīyapratipannakāv ucyete / katamac ca tṛtīyam / anāgāmiphalam / 
4.6		ṣoḍaśe tu phalasthau tau yatra yaḥ pratipannakaḥ / (31ab) 
ṣoḍaśe tu citta utpanne tau na punaḥ śraddhādharmānusāriṇāv ucyete, nāpi pratipannakau / kiṃ tarhi / yatra phale yaḥ pratipannako bhūtaḥ, sa tadānīṃ tatra phale" sthito bhavati, srotāapattiphale sakṛdāgāmiphale vānāgāmiphale vā / arhattvaṃ tu na śakyam āditaḥ prāptum, darśanamārgeṇa bhāvanāheyānām aprahāṇāt pūrvaṃ ca bhavāgravairāgyāsaṃbhavāt / 
4.6.1		śraddhādhimuktadṛṣṭyāptau mṛdutīkṣṇendriyau tadā //31cd // 
tasmin kāle yo mṛdvindriyaḥ śraddhānusāripūrvī sa śraddhādhimukta ity ucyate, yas tīkṣeṇendriyo dharmānusāripūrvī, sa dṛṣṭiprāpta ity ucyate, śraddhāprajñādhikatvenādhimokṣadṛṣṭiprabhāvitatvāt / 
4.7	kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇaṃ prahīṇapañcaprakāro *pi ṣoḍaśe citte srotāapanna evocyate na sakṛdāgāmiphalapratipannakaḥ / yasmāt, 
		phale phalaviśiṣṭasya lābho mārgasya nāsty ataḥ / 
		nāprayukto viśeṣāya phalasthaḥ pratipannakaḥ //32// 
phale hi labhyamāne phalaviśiṣṭo mārgo na labhyata ity eṣa niyamaḥ / ataḥ phalastho yāvan na viśeṣāya prayujyate phalāntaraprāptau tāvat pratipannako nocyate / evam anyatrāpi phale veditavyam / 
	yas tu tṛtīyadhyānavītarāgo *dharāṃ bhūmiṃ niśritya niyāmam avakrāmati, so *vaśyaṃ phalaviśiṣṭaṃ mārgaṃ saṃmukhīkaroti / anyathā hi sa tasmād ūrdhvopapannaḥ sukhendriyeṇāsamanvāgataḥ syāt / 
5	evaṃ tāvad bhūyaḥkāmavītarāgāṇāṃ niyāmāvakrāntau pudgalavyavasthānam / ānupūrvikaṃ tu vaktavyam / 
5.1	ata idaṃ tāvad vyavasthāpyate / yathaite kāmadhātau navaprakārāḥ kleśā upadiṣṭā evaṃ, 
		navaprakārā doṣā hi bhūmau bhūmau (33ab) 
yāvad bhavāgre / yathā ca doṣāḥ, 
		tathā guṇāḥ / (33b) 
tatpratipakṣā apy ānantaryavimuktimārgākhyā guṇā bhūmau bhūmau navaprakāra" eva / 
5.1.1	kathaṃ kṛtvā / 
		mṛdumadhyādhimātrāṇāṃ punar mṛdvādibhedataḥ //33cd // 
mṛdumadhyādhimātrā hi trayo mūlaprakārāḥ / teṣāṃ punaḥ pratyekaṃ mṛdumadhyādhimātratvena trividhatvāt nava vyavasthāpyante / tadyathā mṛdumṛduḥ prakāro mṛdumadhyo mṛdvadhimātro madhyamṛdur madhyamadhyo madhyādhimātro *dhimātramṛdur adhimātramadhyo *dhimātrādhimātraś ceti / 
5.1.2	tatra mṛdumṛdunā mārgeṇādhimātrādhimātrasya kleśasya prahāṇam / evaṃ yāvad adhimātrādhimātreṇa mṛdumṛdoḥ / ādita evādhijātramārgāsaṃbhavād utpannādhimātramārgasya cādhimātrakleśādaṃbhavāt / audāriko hi malaś celāt pūrvaṃ nirdhūyate paścāt sūkṣmaḥ / audārikaṃ ca tamaḥ sūkṣmeṇālokena hanyate sūkṣmaṃ cādhimātreṇety eṣa dṛṣṭāntayogaḥ / 
	śuklā hi dharmā balavanto durbalās tu kṛṣṇāḥ / kṣaṇikamṛdukenāpy āryamārgeṇānādisaṃsāraparaṃparāpyāyitādhimātrāṇāṃ kleśānām unmūlanāt", bahukālasaṃvarddhitānāṃ doṣāṇāṃ trivṛtkarṣaniṣkarṣaṇavat" kṣaṇikālpapradīpamahātamaupaghātavac ca / 
5.2.1	evaṃ navaprakāreṣu kleśeṣu sarvatra, 
		akṣīṇabhāvanāheyaḥ phalasthaḥ saptakṛtparaḥ / (34ab) 
yasya hi phalasthasyaiko *pi bhāvanāheyaḥ prakāro *prahīṇaḥ, sa srotāapannaḥ / saptajanmāni karotīti saptakṛt / paraḥ sarvāntyaḥ / na hi sarvaḥ saptakṛd iti / OsaptakṛtvaḥparamaḥO iti sūtrapāṭḥaḥ / saptakṛtvaḥ paramaṃ janmāsyety arthaḥ / prakarṣe paramaśabdaḥ / 
5.2.2	nirvāṇasroto hi mārgas tena tatra gamanāt / tad asāv āpanna āgataḥ prāpta iti srotāapannaḥ / 
	katham āpannaḥ / ādyamārgalābhāc cet, aṣṭamako *pi syāt / ādyaphalalābhāc cet, bhūyovītarāgo *pi syāt, kāmavītarāgaḥ" / sarvaphalaprāpiṇam adhikṛtyādyaphalalābhāt / 
	kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇaṃ sa eva nāṣṭamakaḥ / pratipannakaphalamārgalābhād darśanabhāvanāmārgalābhāt sakalasroto*bhisamayāc ca mārgānvayajñāne / 
5.2.3	sa eva tasmād anyān saptopapattibhavān manuṣyeṣu pratisaṃdadhāti, saptāntarābhavān, evaṃ deveṣv ity aṣṭāviṃśatibhavān pratisaṃdadhāti / saptakasāmānyāt tu saptakṛtvaḥparama uktaḥ, saptasthānakauśalasaptaparṇavad iti vaibhāṣikāḥ / 
5.2.3 a)	yat tarhi sūtra uktam, Oasthānam anavakāśo yad dṛṣṭisaṃpannaḥ pudgalo *ṣṭatamaṃ bhavam abhinirvartayiṣyati, nedaṃ sthānaṃ vidyateO iti / ekasyāṃ gatāv ity abhiprāyaḥ / yathārutaṃ vā kalpyamāne *ntarābhavo *pi na syāt / evam apy ūrdhvaṃsrotaso bhavāgraparamasyaikasyāṃ gatāv aṣṭama upapattibhavo na prāpnoti / kāmadhātvabhisaṃdhivacanād adoṣaḥ / 
5.2.3 b)	kim atra jñāpakaṃ sūtraṃ yuktir vā / iha caiva kiṃ jñāpakam, pratyekaṃ devamanuṣyeṣu saptakṛtvo na punar ubhayeṣv eva saptakṛtva iti / evaṃ hi paṭhyate, Osaptakṛtvo devāṃś ca manuṣyāṃś caO iti / nātrābhiniveṣṭavyam / 
	yaś ca manuṣyeṣu srotāapanno bhavati, sa tān evāgamya parinirvāti, yo deveṣu sa tān eva / 
5.2.3 c)	kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇam aṣṭamaṃ bhavaṃ nābhinirvartayati / 
	tāvatā kālenāvaśyaṃ saṃtatiparipākāt / mārgo hi sa tajjātīyaḥ, saptapadāśīviṣadaṣṭavac cāturthakajvaravac ca / 
	saptasaṃyojanāvaśeṣatvāc ca / dve avarabhāgīye pañca cordhvabhāgīyānīti / 
	antareṇāpy āryamārgaṃ saṃmukhīkurvāṇo na parinirvāti, tāvat bhavavedanīyasya karmaṇo balādhānāt / 
5.2.4	asati buddhotpāde gṛhastha" evārhattvaṃ prāpnoti, agāraṃ tu punar nādhyāvasati / dharmatāpratilambhikaṃ tu bhikṣuliṅgaṃ pratilabhati / 
	anyāśramikaṃ liṅgenety apare / 
5.2.5	kasmād avinipātakadharmā bhavati / tadgāmika"karmānupacayād upacitavipākadānavaiguṇyāc ca saṃtater balavatkuśalamūlādhivāsanāt" prayogāśayaśuddhitaḥ / apāyaniyate" tu karmaṇy asau kṣāntim api notpādayet / 
	āha cātra, 
		kṛtvābudho *lpam api pāpam adhaḥ prayāti, 
		kṛtvā budho mahad api prajahāty" anartham, 
		majjaty ayo" *lpam api vāriṇi saṃhataṃ hi, 
		pātrīkṛtaṃ mahad api plavate tad eva 
iti. 
5.2.6	Oduḥkhasyāntaṃ karotiO iti ko duḥkhasyāntaḥ / yasmāt pareṇa duḥkhaṃ nāsti, apratisaṃdhikaṃ duḥkhaṃ karotīty arthaḥ / atha vā nirvāṇam antaḥ / katham nirvāṇaṃ karoti / tatprāptivibandhāpanayanāt / yathākāśaṃ kuru maṇḍapaṃ pātayeti bhavanti vaktāraḥ / 
	anyo *pi ca syāt saptakṛtvaḥparamo na tu niyata iti nocyate / 
5.3	evaṃ tāvad akṣīṇabhāvanāheyaḥ phalasthaḥ saptakṛtvaḥparamo bhavati / 
		tricaturvidhamuktas tu dvitrijanmā kulaṃkulaḥ //34cd // 
sa eva srotāapannas tribhiḥ kāraṇaiḥ kulaṃkulo bhavati, kleśaprahāṇatas tricatuḥprakāraprahīṇatvāt, indriyatas tatpratipakṣānāsravendriyalābhāt, janmato dvitrijanmāvaśeṣatvāt / 
5.3.1 a)	śloke tu dvayor grahaṇaṃ srotāapannasya paścāt prahīṇe sati tatpratipakṣānāsravendriyasyānuktasiddhatvāt / 
	janma tu kadācid alpīyaḥ syāt, pareṇa bhavyatvāt / ato *sya grahaṇam / 
5.3.1 b)	kasmān na pañcaprakāraprahaṇāt tatprahāṇe ṣaṣṭhasyāvaśyaṃ prahāṇāt / na hi tasyaikaḥ prakāraḥ phalaṃ vighnayituṃ samarthaḥ, ekavīcikasyeva dhātvanatikramāt / 
5.3.1 c)	sa eva kulaṃkulo dvividhiaḥ / devakulaṃkulo yo deveṣu dve trīṇi vā kulāni saṃsṛtya" parinirvāti, tatra vānyatra vā devanikāye / manuṣyakulaṃkulo yo manuṣyeṣu tatra vānyatra vādvīpe parinirvāti / 
5.3.2	sa eva punaḥ phalasthaḥ, 
		āpañcamaprakāraghno dvitīyapratipannakaḥ / 
yasya phalasthasyaikaprakāro yāvat pañcamaḥ prahīṇo bhavati asau dvitīyaphalapratipannako veditavyaḥ / 
5.3.3		kṣīṇaṣaṣṭhaprakāras tu sakṛdāgāmy asau punaḥ //35cd // 
dvitīyaphalaprāpto bhavati / devān gatvā sakṛn manuṣyalokāgamanāt sakṛdāgāmī, pareṇa janmābhāvāt / Orāgadveṣamohānāṃ ca tanutvādO ity ucyate, mṛduprakārāvaśeṣatvāt / 
5.4.1	sa eva punaḥ phalasthaḥ, 
		kṣīṇasaptāṣṭadoṣāṃśa ekajanmaikavīcikaḥ / 
		tṛtīyapratipannaś ca (36abc) 
tribhiḥ kāraṇaiḥ sa eva sakṛdāgāmy ekavīciko veditavyaḥ, saptāṣṭaprakāraprahāṇāt, tatpratipakṣānāsravendriyalābhāt, ekajanmāvaśeṣatvāt / 
5.4.1 a)	kathaṃ asyaikaḥ prakāraḥ phalaṃ vighnayituṃ śaknoti / dhātvatikramāt / avasthātraye hi Okarmāṇi vighnāyopatiṣṭhanteO ity uktaṃ prāk / yathā karmāṇy evaṃ kleśā api veditavyā iti, vipākaniḥṣyandaphalabhūmyatrikramāt / 
5.4.1 b)	vīcir nāmāntaraṃ, tasya vaika"janmavyavahitatvād nirvāṇasya, ekakleśaprakāravyavahitatvād vānāgāmiphalasya / eko vīcir asyety ekavīcikaḥ / 
5.4.2	tṛtīyaphalapratipannakaś caikavīciko" veditavyaḥ prahīṇasaptāṣṭaprakāraḥ / 
	pūrvaprahīṇaprakāras tu phalaprāptau na tāvat kulaṃkulo bhavaty ekavīciko vā yāvat phalaviśiṣṭo mārgo na saṃmukhīkṛtaḥ / 
5.4.3		so *nāgāmī navakṣayāt //36d // 
		sa eva punaḥ phalastho navaprakāraprahāṇād anāgāmy upadiṣṭaḥ, kāmadhātvanāgamanāt / Opañcānām avarabhāgīyānāṃ saṃyojanānāṃ prahāṇātO ity ucyate prahāṇasaṃkalanāt" / avaśyaṃ dve trīṇi vā pūrvaṃ prahīṇāni bhavanti / 
5.4.4		so *ntarotpannasaṃskārāsaṃskāraparinirvṛtiḥ / 
		ūrdhvaṃsrotāś ca (37abc) 
antarā parinirvṛtir asyety antarāparinirvṛtiḥ / evam utpannasya" saṃskāreṇāsaṃskāreṇeti yojyam / sa evānāgāmī punaḥ pañcadhā bhavati / 
5.4.4 a)	antarāparinirvāyī yo *ntarābhave parinirvāti / upapadyaparinirvāyī ya upapannamātro nacirāt parinirvāty abhiyuktavāhimārgatvāt sopadhiśeṣanirvāṇena / 
	so *pi nirupadhiśeṣeṇety apare / na, āyurutsargāvaśitvāt / 
5.4.4 b)	sābhisaṃskāraparinirvāyī kilopapadyāpratiprasrabdhaprayogaḥ sābhisaṃskāraṃ parinirvāty abhiyuktāvāhimārgatvāt," anabhisaṃskāraparinirvāyī tv anabhisaṃskāreṇābhiyogavāhimārgābhāvāt / 
	saṃskṛtāsaṃskṛtālambanamārganirvāṇād ity apare / tat tu na, atiprasaṅgāt / 
5.4.4 c)	sūtre tv anabhisaṃskāraparinirvāyī pūrvaṃ paṭhyate / tathaiva ca yujyate, vāhyavāhimārgayor anabhisaṃskārābhisaṃskārasādhyatvād ayatnayatnaprāptitaḥ / 
	upapadyaparinirvāyiṇas tu vāhitaro *dhimātrataraś ca mārgo mṛdutarāś cānuśayā iti / 
5.4.4 d)	ūrdhvaṃsrotā yasyordhvaṃ gatir na tatraiva parinirvāṇaṃ yatropapannaḥ / sroto gatir ity eko *rthaḥ / 
		sa dhyāne vyavakīrṇe *kaniṣṭhagaḥ //37d // 
ūrdhvaṃssrotā dvividho hetutaḥ phalataś ca / hetuto vyavakīrṇāvyavakīrṇadhyānatvāt / phalato *kaniṣṭḥabhavāgraparamatvāt / 
5.4.4 d1)	tatra yena dhyānaṃ vyavakīrṇaṃ so *kaniṣṭhān gatvā parinirvāti / 
		sa pluto *rdhaplutaḥ sarvacyutaś ca (38ab) 
sa punar eko *kaniṣṭhaparama ūrdhvaṃsrotās trividhaḥ plutādibhedāt / 
	tatra pluto nāma ya iha dhyānāni vyavakīrya dhyānatrayāt" parihīṇaḥ prathamaṃ dhyānam āsvādya brahmakāyikeṣūpapannaḥ pūrvābhyāsavaśāc caturthaṃ dhyānaṃ vyavakīrya tasmāt pracyuto *kaniṣṭḥeṣūpapadyate / eṣa hi madhyānimajjanāt plutaḥ / 
	ardhapluto nāma yas tataḥ śuddhāvāseṣūpapadya madhyād ekam api sthānāntaraṃ vilaṅghyākaniṣṭhān praviśati / 
	mahābrahmasv āryo nopapadyate dṛṣṭisthānatvād ekanāyakatvāc ceti / 
	sarvacyuto nāma yaḥ sarvāṇi sthānāntarāṇi saṃcaryākaniṣṭhān praviśati / 
5.4.4 d2)	na ca kadācid anāgāmī tatraivotpattyāyatane dvitīyaṃ janmābhinirvartayati, viśeṣagāmitvāt / evaṃ cāsya paripūrṇam anāgāmitvaṃ bhavati, yatropapannas tasyādhas tatra cātyantam anāgamanāt / 
	evaṃ tāvat Odhyāne vyavakīrṇe *kaniṣṭhagaḥO veditavyaḥ / 
5.4.4 e)	tataḥ, 
		anyo bhavāgragaḥ / (38b) 
avyavakīrṇadhyāna ūrdhvaṃsrotā bhavāgraniṣṭho bhavati / sa hi samāpattyantarāṇy āsvādayan sarvasthānāntareṣūpapadyāpraviśyaiva śuddhāvāsān ārūpyakramotpattito bhavāgraṃ gatvā parinirvāti / 
	śamathacarito hy eṣaḥ, pūrvakas tu vipaśyanācaritaḥ / 
	antarāpi tu parinirvāṇam ūrdhvaṃsrotaso yujyamānaṃ paśyāmaḥ / akaniṣṭḥabhavāgraparamatvaṃ tu pareṇa gatyabhāvāt, yathā saptakṛtvaḥparamatvaṃ srotāapannasyeti / 
	ime tāvat pañca rūpopagā anāgāminaḥ / 
5.4.4 )		ārūpyagaś caturdhānyaḥ, (38c) 
anya ārūpyago *nāgāmī yo rūpavītarāga itaś cyutvārūpyeṣūpapadyate / sa punaś caturvidha upapadyādiparinirvāyibhedāt / ta ete ṣaḍ anāgāmino bhavanti / 
5.4.4 g)	tataḥ, 
		ihanirvāpako *paraḥ //38d // 
ihaiva janmany aparaḥ parinirvāti / sa dṛṣṭadaharmaparinirvāyī saptamaḥ / 
5.4.5 a)		punas trīṃs trividhān kṛtvā nava rūpopagāḥ smṛtāḥ / (39ab) 
punas trayāṇām anāgāmināṃ tridhābhedād rūpopagā navānāgāmino bhavanti / 
	katameṣāṃ trayāṇām / antaropapadyaparinirvāyiṇor ūrdhvaṃsrotasaś ca / antaropapadyaparinirvāyiṇor ūrdhvaṃsrotasaś ca / 
	kathaṃ tridhābhedāt / antarāparinirvāyiṇas tāvad āśvanāśuciraparinirvāṇāt, dṛṣṭāntatrayeṇa / upapadyaparinirvāyiṇa upapadyābhisaṃskārānabhisaṃskāraparinirvāṇāt / sarve hy ete trayo" *py upapannaparinirvāṇād upapadyaparinirvāyiṇaḥ / ūrdhvaṃsrotasaḥ plutādibhedāt / 
	sarveṣāṃ vā trayāṇām āśvanāśuciraparinirvāṇād iti tritvam." / 
5.4.5 b)		tadviśeṣaḥ punaḥ karmakleśendriyaviśeṣataḥ //39cd // 
teṣāṃ punas trayāṇāṃ navānāṃ cānāgāmināṃ karmakleśendriyaviśeṣād viśeṣaḥ / 
	trayāṇāṃ tāvad abhinirvṛttyupapadyāparaparyāyavedanīyakarmopacitatvād yathākramaṃ mṛdumadhyādhimātrakleśasamudācāratvād adhimātramadhyamṛdvindriyatvāc ca / 
	teṣām api" pratyekam ata eva yathāyogaṃ viśeṣaḥ / prathamayos trikayoḥ kleśendriyaviśeṣāt pūrvavat, paścimasya trikasyāparaparyāyavedanīyakarmaviśeṣāc ceti / 
	ta ete navaprakārakleśendriyatvān navānāgāmino bhavanti / 
5.4.6	kathaṃ tarhi sūtre sapta satpuruṣagatayo deśitāḥ / 
		ūrdhvaṃsrotur abhedena sapta sadgatayo matāḥ / (40ab) 
ūrdhvaṃsravaṇadharmā ūrdhvaṃsrotā / tasyābhedanirdeśāt sapta satpuruṣagatayaḥ sūtre *bhihitāḥ / 
5.4.6 a)	kasmāt punar etā eva satpuruṣagatayo nānyāḥ śaikṣagatayaḥ / 
	etā hi gatayo yeṣām teṣāṃ saty eva karmaṇi vṛttiḥ kuśale *saty avṛttir akukśale, etāś ca gatīr gatānāṃ na punaḥ pratyāgatir asti / na tv etad yathoktam anyatrāsti / ataḥ, 
		sadasadvṛttyavṛttibhyāṃ gatāpratyāgateś ca tāḥ //40cd // 
satpuruṣagatayo"nānyā iti / 
5.4.6 b)	yat tarhi sūtra evoktam, Osatpuruṣaḥ katamaḥ / śaikṣa"samyagdṛṣṭyā samanvāgatḥO iti vistaraḥ / 
	anyeṣām apy asti pāryāyikaṃ satpuruṣatvam, pañcavidhasya pāpasyātyantam akaraṇasaṃvarapratilambhāt prāyeṇākuśalaprahāṇāc ca / yeṣāṃ tu niṣparyāyeṇa teṣām ihādhikāraḥ / 
5.4.7 a)	kiṃ punaḥ parivṛttajanmano *py anāgāmina eṣa bhedo *sti / na," yasmāt, 
		na parāvṛtta"janmāryaḥ kāme dhātvantaropagaḥ / (41ab) 
kāmadhātau parāvṛttajanmāntara āryo na dhātvantaraṃ gacchati, anāgāmiphalaṃ prāpya tatraiva janmani parinirvāṇāt / rūpadhātau tu parāvṛttajanmā kadācid ārūpyān praviśati, ya ūrdhvaṃsrotā bhavāgraparamaḥ / 
	yat tarhi śakreṇoktam, Oye te devā akaniṣṭhā iti viśrutāḥ, ante me hīyamānasya tatropapattir bhaviṣyatiO abhidharmānabhijñātvād iti vaibhāṣikāḥ, bhagavatāpy anivāraṇaṃ saṃharṣaṇīyatvād iti / 
5.4.7 b)	sa cordhvajaś ca naivākṣasaṃcāraparihāṇibhāk //41cd // 
sa ca kāmadhātau parivṛttajanmā ūrdhvadhātūpapannaś cāryo naivendriyāṇi saṃcarati, nāpi kathaṃcit parihīyate / 
	kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇaṃ parivṛttajanmāntarasyāryasya rūpārūpyapraveśendriyasaṃvāraparihāṇayo" neṣyante / yasmān na santi / kasmān na santi / janmāntaraparivāsenendriyāṇāṃ paripakvataratvād āśrayaviśeṣalābhāc ca / 
5.4.7 c)	atha kasmād avītarāgaḥ śaikṣo nāntarā"parinirvāyī bhavati / mārgasyājitatvād asaṃmukhībhāvataḥ, anuśayānāṃ ca nātimandatvāt / 
	duḥsamatikramatvāt kāmadhātor iti vaibhāṣikāḥ / bahu hy anena kartavyaṃ bhavati, akuśalāvyākṛtakleśaprahāṇaṃ dvitriśrāmaṇyaphalaprāptis tridhātusamatikramaś ca / tac cāntarābhavastho na śaktaḥ kartum iti / 
5.4.8 a)	yad uktam, Osa dhyāne vyavakīrṇe *kaniṣṭhagaḥO iti / atha katamad dhyānaṃ prathamato vyavakīriyate / 
		ākīryate caturthaṃ prāk (42a) 
sa hi sarvakarmaṇyaḥ samādhiḥ sukhapratipadām agryatvāt / 
5.4.8 a1)	evaṃ ca punaḥ vyavakīryate / 
	arhann anāgāmī vā pravāhayuktam anāsravaṃ caturthaṃ dhyānaṃ samāpadyate / tasmād vyutthāya pravāhayuktaṃ tad eva sāsravaṃ samāpadyate, punaś cānāsravam / 
	evaṃ pravāhāpahrāsena" yadā kila dvau kṣaṇāv anāsravau samāpadyate, dvau sāsravau, punaś cānāsravau / 
	ayaṃ vyavakiraṇasya prayogaḥ / 
5.4.8 a2)		niṣpattiḥ" kṣaṇamiśraṇāt / (42b) 
yadā tv anāsravasya kṣaṇasyānantaraṃ sāsravaṃ saṃmukhīkaroti, sāsravasyānāsravam, evaṃ sāsravasya kṣaṇasyānāsravābhyāṃ miśraṇād vyavakiraṇaṃ niṣpannaṃ bhavatīti vaibhāṣikāḥ / dvau hi kṣaṇāv ānantaryamārgasadṛśau tṛtīyo vimuktimārgasadṛśa iti / 
	evaṃ caturthaṃ dhyānaṃ vyavakīrya tadbalenānyāny api vyavakīryante / 
5.4.8 a3)	kāmadhātau triṣu dvīpeṣu prathamaṃ vyavakīryate, paścāt parihīṇena rūpadhātau / 
5.4.8 a4)	aśakyaṃ tu kṣaṇavyavakiraṇam anyatra buddhāt / ata icchātaḥ pravāhatrayasamāpattito niṣpannaṃ bhavatīti paśyāmaḥ /" 
5.4.8 b)	kim arthaṃ punar dhyānaṃ vyavakīryate / 
		upapattivihārārthaṃ kleśabhīrutayāpi ca //42cd // 
tribhiḥ kāraṇair dhyānaṃ vyavakiranti / 
	tīkṣṇendriyā anāgāminaḥ śuddhāvāsopapattyarthaṃ dṛṣṭadharmasukhavihārārthaṃ ca / mṛdvindriyāḥ kleśabhīrutayā cāsvādanāsaṃprayuktasamādhidūrīkaraṇād aparihīṇārtham / 
	arhantas tu tīkṣṇendriyā dṛṣṭadharmasukhavihārārtham / mṛdvindriyāḥ kleśabhīrutvāc cāparihīṇārtham" / 
5.4.9	atha kasmāt pañcaiva śuddhāvāsopapattayaḥ / yad etad vyavakīrṇabhāvitaṃ caturthaṃ dhyānam uktam, 
		tatpāñcavidhyāt pañcaiva śuddhāvāsopapattayaḥ / (43ab) 
sā hi vyavakīrṇabhāvanā pañcaprakārā mṛdumadhyādhimātrataratamabhedāt / 
	prathamāyāṃ trīṇi cittāni saṃmukhīkriyante, anāsravaṃ sāsravaṃ anāsravaṃ ca / dvitīyāyāṃ ṣaṭ, tṛtīyāyāṃ nava, caturthyāṃ dvādaśa, pañcamyāṃ pañcadaśa / tāsāṃ yathāsaṃkhyaṃ pañca śuddhāvāsāḥ phalam / yat tatra sāsravaṃ tadvaśāt teṣūpapattiḥ / 
	śraddādīndriyādhikyāt pañcety apare / 
5.4.10		nirodhalābhy anāgāmī kāyasākṣī punar mataḥ //43cd // 
nirodhalābho *syāstīti nirodhalābhī / yo hi kaścid anāgāmī nirodhasamāpattilābhīi sa kāyasākṣīty ucyate, nirvāṇasadṛśasya dharmasya kāyena sākṣātkaraṇāt / 
5.4.10 a)	kathaṃ punaḥ kāyena sākṣātkaroti / cittābhāvāt kāyāśrayotpatteḥ / 
	evaṃ tu bhavitavyam / sa hi tasmād vyutthāyāpratilabdhapūrvāṃ savijñānakāṃ kāyaśāntiṃ pratilabhate, yato *syaivaṃ bhavati śāntā vata nirodhasamāpattir nirvāṇasadṛśī vata nirodhasamāpattir iti / evam anena tasyāḥ śāntatvaṃ kāyena sākṣātkṛtaṃ bhavati / prāptijñānasākṣātkriyābhyāṃ pratyakṣīkāro hi sākṣātkriyā /" 
5.4.10 b)	Oaṣṭādaśa śaikṣāḥO ity atra sūtre kiṃ kāraṇaṃ kāyasākṣī noktaḥ / kāraṇābhāvāt / kiṃ punaḥ kāraṇam / anāsravās tisraḥ śikṣās" tatphalaṃ ca / tadviśeṣeṇa hi śaikṣāṇāṃ vyavasthānaṃ / nirodhasamāpattiś ca naiva śikṣā na śikṣāphalam " 
 / ato na tadyogāc chaikṣaviśeṣa uktaḥ / 
5.4.11	eṣa tāvad anāgāmināṃ yathāsthūlaṃ bhedaḥ / sūkṣmaṃ tu bhidyamānāḥ sahasraśo bhidyante /
